using System.CodeDom.Compiler;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Configuration;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Drawing;
using System.Globalization;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Resources;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

using System.Windows.Controls;
using System.Windows.Controls.Primitives;
using System.Windows.Data;
using System.Windows.Documents;
using System.Windows.Input;
using System.Windows.Markup;
using System.Windows.Media;
using System.Windows.Media.Animation;
using System.Windows.Shapes;
using System.Xml;
using IconCtrl.Properties;

namespace IconCtrl
{
	public class BaseObj : Canvas
	{
		public BaseObj()
		{
			base.RenderTransform = new TransformGroup
			{
				Children = { (Transform)new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0) }
			};
		}

		public virtual void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = 0.0;
			y = 0.0;
		}

		public virtual void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			int index = ((this is Group) ? 2 : 0);
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[index];
			translateTransform.X = 0.0;
			translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
		}

		protected void SetAnimation(IconCtrl parent, string registername, Storyboard storyboard, DependencyProperty property, XmlNode node, string conversion)
		{
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation();
			string text = node.Attributes["from"].Value;
			string text2 = node.Attributes["to"].Value;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(conversion))
			{
				Calc calc = new Calc();
				text = (string)calc.Convert(text, null, conversion, null);
				text2 = (string)calc.Convert(text2, null, conversion, null);
			}
			doubleAnimation.From = double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			doubleAnimation.To = double.Parse(text2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			doubleAnimation.Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(node.Attributes["duration"].Value)));
			doubleAnimation.AutoReverse = bool.Parse(node.Attributes["autoreverse"].Value);
			doubleAnimation.RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever;
			Storyboard.SetTargetName(doubleAnimation, registername);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(property));
			storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
			storyboard.Begin(parent, isControllable: true);
			if (node.Attributes["run"] == null || !bool.Parse(node.Attributes["run"].Value))
			{
				storyboard.Pause(parent);
			}
		}

		protected string PropertyXaml(string property)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (property.StartsWith("@"))
			{
				return "{Binding XPath=" + property.Substring(1) + "}";
			}
			if (property == "Default")
			{
				return "{Binding XPath=Color}";
			}
			return property;
		}

		protected void AddAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, string value)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes[name];
			if (xmlAttribute == null)
			{
				xmlAttribute = node.OwnerDocument.CreateAttribute(name);
				node.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			}
			xmlAttribute.Value = value;
		}

		protected void AddElement(XmlNode node, string name, string value)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = node.OwnerDocument.CreateElement(name);
			xmlElement.InnerText = value;
			node.AppendChild(xmlElement);
		}

		protected string GetString(XmlNode node, string attrib)
		{
			if (node.Attributes[attrib] != null)
			{
				return node.Attributes[attrib].Value;
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected double GetDouble(XmlNode node, string attrib)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (node.Attributes[attrib] != null)
			{
				result = double.Parse(node.Attributes[attrib].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected int GetInt(XmlNode node, string attrib)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (node.Attributes[attrib] != null)
			{
				int.TryParse(node.Attributes[attrib].Value, out result);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected System.Windows.Media.Color ColorFromString(string color)
		{
			if (color[0] != '#')
			{
				return (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(color);
			}
			if (color.Length == 9)
			{
				byte a = byte.Parse(color.Substring(1, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
				byte r = byte.Parse(color.Substring(3, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
				byte g = byte.Parse(color.Substring(5, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
				byte b = byte.Parse(color.Substring(7, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
				return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromArgb(a, r, g, b);
			}
			byte r2 = byte.Parse(color.Substring(1, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
			byte g2 = byte.Parse(color.Substring(3, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
			byte b2 = byte.Parse(color.Substring(5, 2), NumberStyles.HexNumber);
			return System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(r2, g2, b2);
		}
	}
	public class Anchor : BaseObj
	{
		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private string _name;

		private string _xparam;

		private string _xconv;

		private string _yparam;

		private string _yconv;

		private List<string> _xparams;

		private List<string> _yparams;

		private int _direction;

		private bool _ashandle;

		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse _ellipse;

		private ScaleTransform _sform;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private List<Handle> _handles;

		private List<Port> _ports;

		private bool _indrag;

		private double _x0;

		private double _y0;

		private Storyboard _xstoryboard;

		private Storyboard _ystoryboard;

		private XmlNode _datanode;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AnchorXProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnchorX", typeof(double), typeof(Anchor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, OnAnchorXChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AnchorYProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("AnchorY", typeof(double), typeof(Anchor), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, OnAnchorYChanged));

		public new string Name => _name;

		public double X => _x;

		public double Y => _y;

		public int Direction
		{
			get
			{
				return _direction;
			}
			set
			{
				_direction = value;
			}
		}

		public bool AsHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return _ashandle;
			}
			set
			{
				_ashandle = value;
			}
		}

		public XmlNode DataNode => _datanode;

		public double AnchorX
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(AnchorXProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AnchorXProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double AnchorY
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(AnchorYProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AnchorYProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Anchor(XmlNode node, IconCtrl parent, string name, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, int elementoffset)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_name = name;
			_x = GetDouble(node, "x");
			_y = GetDouble(node, "y");
			_direction = GetInt(node, "direction");
			_xparams = new List<string>();
			_yparams = new List<string>();
			SetXParam(GetString(node, "xparam"));
			_xconv = GetString(node, "xconv");
			SetYParam(GetString(node, "yparam"));
			_yconv = GetString(node, "yconv");
			bool.TryParse(GetString(node, "handle"), out _ashandle);
			_handles = new List<Handle>();
			_ports = new List<Port>();
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Handle", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string ids = GetString(item, "owner");
				string id = GetString(item, "id");
				Element element = parent.FindElement(ids, elementoffset);
				Handle handle = element.FindHandle(id);
				double dx = GetDouble(item, "dx");
				double dy = GetDouble(item, "dy");
				handle.SetOffset(dx, dy);
				bool reversedirection = false;
				if (item.Attributes["reverse"] != null)
				{
					reversedirection = bool.Parse(item.Attributes["reverse"].Value);
				}
				element.TagAnchor(id, this, reversedirection);
			}
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList2 = node.SelectNodes("y:Port", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNodeList2)
			{
				string name2 = GetString(item2, "name");
				double dx2 = GetDouble(item2, "dx");
				double dy2 = GetDouble(item2, "dy");
				if (item2.Attributes["reverse"] != null)
				{
					bool.Parse(item2.Attributes["reverse"].Value);
				}
				Port port = parent.FindPort(name2);
				if (port != null)
				{
					port.SetOffset(dx2, dy2);
					_ports.Add(port);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_xparam))
			{
				SetValue("xparam", _xparam, _xconv);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_yparam))
			{
				SetValue("yparam", _yparam, _yconv);
			}
			base.MouseEnter += Anchor_MouseEnter;
			base.MouseLeave += Anchor_MouseLeave;
			base.MouseDown += Anchor_MouseDown;
		}

		public Anchor(double x, double y, IconCtrl parent, string name)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_name = name;
			_direction = 0;
			_handles = new List<Handle>();
			_ports = new List<Port>();
			_xparams = new List<string>();
			_yparams = new List<string>();
			base.MouseEnter += Anchor_MouseEnter;
			base.MouseLeave += Anchor_MouseLeave;
			base.MouseDown += Anchor_MouseDown;
		}

		public string GetParam(string name)
		{
			string result = "";
			switch (name)
			{
			case "xparam":
				result = _xparam;
				break;
			case "yparam":
				result = _yparam;
				break;
			case "xconv":
				result = _xconv;
				break;
			case "yconv":
				result = _yconv;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SetParam(string name, string value)
		{
			switch (name)
			{
			case "xparam":
				SetXParam(value);
				break;
			case "yparam":
				SetYParam(value);
				break;
			case "xconv":
				_xconv = value;
				break;
			case "yconv":
				_yconv = value;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void SetXParam(string param)
		{
			_xparam = param;
			_xparams.Clear();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(param))
			{
				string[] array = param.Split(',');
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string item in array2)
				{
					_xparams.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetYParam(string param)
		{
			_yparam = param;
			_yparams.Clear();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(param))
			{
				string[] array = param.Split(',');
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string item in array2)
				{
					_yparams.Add(item);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Run(bool run, string anim)
		{
			if (_xparams.Contains(anim))
			{
				if (run)
				{
					_xstoryboard.Resume(_parent);
				}
				else
				{
					_xstoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				}
			}
			if (_yparams.Contains(anim))
			{
				if (run)
				{
					_ystoryboard.Resume(_parent);
				}
				else
				{
					_ystoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Highlight(bool bOn)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_sform.ScaleX = 1.5;
				_sform.ScaleY = 1.5;
			}
			else
			{
				_sform.ScaleX = 1.0;
				_sform.ScaleY = 1.0;
			}
		}

		private void HighlightTag(bool bOn)
		{
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				handle.Owner.Highlight(bOn, handle);
				handle.Highlight(bOn);
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.Highlight(bOn, bLarge: true);
			}
		}

		private static void OnAnchorXChanged(DependencyObject source, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Anchor anchor = source as Anchor;
			double anchorX = anchor.AnchorX;
			anchor.SetHandles(yvalue: false, anchorX, manually: false);
		}

		private static void OnAnchorYChanged(DependencyObject source, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Anchor anchor = source as Anchor;
			double anchorY = anchor.AnchorY;
			anchor.SetHandles(yvalue: true, anchorY, manually: false);
		}

		private void Anchor_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_indrag && _parent.DragAnchor(this))
			{
				_x0 = _x;
				_y0 = _y;
				_indrag = true;
			}
		}

		private void Anchor_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			Highlight(bOn: false);
			HighlightTag(bOn: false);
		}

		private void Anchor_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_indrag)
			{
				Highlight(bOn: true);
				HighlightTag(bOn: true);
			}
		}

		public XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("Anchor");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", _name);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "direction", _direction.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "directinit", "false");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "xparam", _xparam);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "xconv", _xconv);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "yparam", _yparam);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "yconv", _yconv);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "handle", _ashandle.ToString());
			_datanode = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}

		public void UpdateData(string attrib, string value)
		{
			_datanode.Attributes[attrib].Value = value;
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.SelectSingleNode("y:Anchor[@name='" + _name + "']", nsmgr);
			xmlNode?.ParentNode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
			xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Anchor", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "name", _name);
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "x", _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "y", _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "direction", _direction.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "xparam", _xparam);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "xconv", _xconv);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "yparam", _yparam);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "yconv", _yconv);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "handle", _ashandle.ToString());
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				string elementIndex = _parent.GetElementIndex(handle.Owner);
				handle.Save(xmlNode, elementIndex, nsmgr);
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.SaveAnchor(xmlNode, nsmgr);
			}
		}

		public string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "<anchor ";
			text = text + "name=\"" + _name + "\" ";
			text = text + "x=\"" + _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "direction=\"" + _direction + "\" ";
			text = text + "xparam=\"" + _xparam + "\" ";
			text = text + "xconv=\"" + _xconv + "\" ";
			text = text + "yparam=\"" + _yparam + "\" ";
			text = text + "yconv=\"" + _yconv + "\" ";
			text = text + "handle=\"" + _ashandle + "\">";
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				string elementIndex = _parent.GetElementIndex(handle.Owner);
				text += handle.GenSvg(elementIndex);
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				text += port.GenAnchorSvg();
			}
			return text + "</anchor>";
		}

		public void Render(bool visible)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_sform = new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y));
			base.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			_ellipse = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
			Canvas.SetLeft(_ellipse, -4.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_ellipse, -4.0);
			_ellipse.Width = 8.0;
			_ellipse.Height = 8.0;
			_ellipse.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			base.Children.Add(_ellipse);
			TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock();
			textBlock.Text = _name;
			textBlock.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			Canvas.SetTop(textBlock, -20.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(textBlock, -5.0);
			base.Children.Add(textBlock);
			if (!visible)
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			Panel.SetZIndex(this, 100);
		}

		public void ResetAnimation()
		{
			if (_xstoryboard != null)
			{
				_xstoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = (DoubleAnimation)_xstoryboard.Children[0];
				doubleAnimation.ClearValue(AnchorXProperty);
				_xstoryboard = null;
				SetXParam("");
			}
			if (_ystoryboard != null)
			{
				_ystoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation2 = (DoubleAnimation)_ystoryboard.Children[0];
				doubleAnimation2.ClearValue(AnchorYProperty);
				_ystoryboard = null;
				SetYParam("");
			}
		}

		public void SetValue(string param, string value, string conversion)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = null;
			if (!value.Contains(','))
			{
				xmlNode = _parent.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(value);
			}
			if (param == "xparam")
			{
				SetXParam(value);
				_xconv = conversion;
				if (xmlNode != null && xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "DoubleAnimation")
				{
					SetupAnimation("x", xmlNode, conversion, initial: true);
				}
				else
				{
					SetBinding(AnchorXProperty, value, conversion);
				}
			}
			else if (param == "yparam")
			{
				SetYParam(value);
				_yconv = conversion;
				if (xmlNode != null && xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "DoubleAnimation")
				{
					SetupAnimation("y", xmlNode, conversion, initial: true);
				}
				else
				{
					SetBinding(AnchorYProperty, value, conversion);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ClearBinding()
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_xparam))
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, AnchorXProperty);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_yparam))
			{
				BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, AnchorYProperty);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateAnimation(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (_xstoryboard != null && _xparams.Contains(node.Name))
			{
				bool flag = !_xstoryboard.GetIsPaused(_parent);
				if (flag)
				{
					_xstoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				}
				_xstoryboard.Children.Clear();
				SetupAnimation("x", node, _xconv, initial: false);
				if (flag)
				{
					_xstoryboard.Resume(_parent);
				}
			}
			if (_ystoryboard != null && _yparams.Contains(node.Name))
			{
				bool flag2 = !_ystoryboard.GetIsPaused(_parent);
				if (flag2)
				{
					_ystoryboard.Pause(_parent);
				}
				_ystoryboard.Children.Clear();
				SetupAnimation("y", node, _yconv, initial: false);
				if (flag2)
				{
					_ystoryboard.Resume(_parent);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetupAnimation(string side, XmlNode node, string conversion, bool initial)
		{
			string text = _name + "anchor" + side;
			DependencyProperty property = ((side == "x") ? AnchorXProperty : AnchorYProperty);
			Storyboard storyboard;
			if (side == "x")
			{
				if (_xstoryboard == null)
				{
					_xstoryboard = new Storyboard();
				}
				storyboard = _xstoryboard;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_ystoryboard == null)
				{
					_ystoryboard = new Storyboard();
				}
				storyboard = _ystoryboard;
			}
			if (initial)
			{
				if (_parent.FindName(text) != null)
				{
					_parent.UnregisterName(text);
				}
				_parent.RegisterName(text, this);
			}
			SetAnimation(_parent, text, storyboard, property, node, conversion);
		}

		private void SetBinding(DependencyProperty property, string value, string conversion)
		{
			if (value.Contains(','))
			{
				string[] array = value.Split(',');
				MultiBinding multiBinding = new MultiBinding();
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					Binding binding = new Binding();
					binding.XPath = "/Attributes/" + array[i];
					binding.Mode = BindingMode.OneWay;
					multiBinding.Bindings.Add(binding);
				}
				multiBinding.Converter = new MCalc();
				multiBinding.ConverterParameter = conversion;
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, property, multiBinding);
			}
			else
			{
				Binding binding2 = new Binding();
				binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + value;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(conversion))
				{
					binding2.Converter = new Calc();
					binding2.ConverterParameter = conversion;
				}
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, property, binding2);
			}
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x;
			y = _y;
		}

		public void Shift(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x += dx;
			_y += dy;
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x0 += dx;
			_y0 += dy;
			_tform.X = _x0;
			_tform.Y = _y0;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			if (_xparams.Count > 0)
			{
				SetHandles(yvalue: false, AnchorX, manually: false);
			}
			if (_yparams.Count > 0)
			{
				SetHandles(yvalue: true, AnchorY, manually: false);
			}
		}

		public void SetHandles(bool yvalue, double value, bool manually)
		{
			if (!manually)
			{
				_parent.SmartIconSizeChanged = true;
			}
			double dx = 0.0;
			double dy = 0.0;
			if (yvalue)
			{
				dy = value - _y;
				_y = value;
				_tform.Y = _y;
			}
			else
			{
				dx = value - _x;
				_x = value;
				_tform.X = _x;
			}
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				handle.SetPositionDelta(dx, dy);
				handle.Owner.UpdateClipping();
				handle.Owner.RefreshHandles();
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.SetPosition(yvalue, value);
			}
			if (manually)
			{
				if (yvalue && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_yparam))
				{
					ReverseSet(_yparam, _yconv, value);
				}
				else if (!yvalue && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_xparam))
				{
					ReverseSet(_xparam, _xconv, value);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReverseSet(string param, string conv, double value)
		{
			Calc calc = new Calc();
			double num = (double)calc.ConvertBack(value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat), null, conv, null);
			XmlNode xmlNode = _parent.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(param);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "int")
				{
					xmlNode.InnerText = ((int)num).ToString();
				}
				else
				{
					xmlNode.InnerText = num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Drag(double x, double y)
		{
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				handle.Move(x, y);
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.Drag(x, y);
			}
		}

		public void EndMove()
		{
			_indrag = false;
			_x = _x0;
			_y = _y0;
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				handle.EndMove();
			}
			foreach (Handle handle2 in _handles)
			{
				handle2.Owner.UpdateClip();
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.EndMove();
			}
			_parent.RecalcSize();
		}

		public void Delete()
		{
			int count = _handles.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				_handles[num].UntagAnchor();
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.UntagAnchor(this);
			}
		}

		public void TagHandle(Handle handle)
		{
			if (_handles.IndexOf(handle) == -1)
			{
				_handles.Add(handle);
			}
		}

		public void UntagHandle(Handle handle)
		{
			_handles.Remove(handle);
		}

		public void TagPort(Port port)
		{
			_ports.Add(port);
		}

		public void UntagPort(Port port)
		{
			_ports.Remove(port);
		}
	}
	public class AnchorSelector : UserControl, IComponentConnector
	{
		private IconCtrl _ctrl;

		internal CheckBox Direction;

		internal ComboBox AnchorList;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public AnchorSelector(IconCtrl ctrl)
		{
			_ctrl = ctrl;
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		private void AnchorList_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			if (comboBox.SelectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				_ctrl.AnchorSelected(comboBox.SelectedIndex, Direction.IsChecked.Value);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/IconCtrl;component/anchorselector.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				Direction = (CheckBox)target;
				break;
			case 2:
				AnchorList = (ComboBox)target;
				AnchorList.SelectionChanged += AnchorList_SelectionChanged;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class AnimatedBrush
	{
		private string _name;

		private int _type;

		private IconCtrl _ctrl;

		private Dictionary<string, Storyboard> _storyboards;

		private SolidColorBrush _solidbrush;

		private GradientBrush _gradientbrush;

		private string _color1;

		private string _color2;

		private string _duration;

		private AnimatedBrush[] _animbrushes;

		private Dictionary<string, bool> _animated;

		public string Name => _name;

		public bool IsRunning
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_animated.ContainsKey(_name))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return _animated[_name];
			}
		}

		public System.Windows.Media.Brush Brush
		{
			get
			{
				if (_type == 0)
				{
					return _solidbrush;
				}
				return _gradientbrush;
			}
		}

		public AnimatedBrush(XmlNode node, IconCtrl ctrl)
		{
			_ctrl = ctrl;
			_name = node.Name;
			_type = 0;
			_solidbrush = new SolidColorBrush();
			_color1 = node.Attributes["from"].Value;
			_color2 = node.Attributes["to"].Value;
			_duration = node.Attributes["duration"].Value;
			string name = _name + "animatedbrush";
			if (ctrl.FindName(name) != null)
			{
				ctrl.UnregisterName(name);
			}
			ctrl.RegisterName(name, _solidbrush);
			ColorAnimation colorAnimation = new ColorAnimation
			{
				From = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(_color1),
				To = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(_color2),
				Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(_duration))),
				AutoReverse = bool.Parse(node.Attributes["autoreverse"].Value),
				RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever
			};
			Storyboard.SetTargetName(colorAnimation, name);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(colorAnimation, new PropertyPath(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty));
			_storyboards = new Dictionary<string, Storyboard>();
			Storyboard storyboard = new Storyboard();
			storyboard.Children.Add(colorAnimation);
			storyboard.Begin(ctrl, isControllable: true);
			_storyboards.Add(_name, storyboard);
			_animated = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			if (node.Attributes["run"] == null || !bool.Parse(node.Attributes["run"].Value))
			{
				storyboard.Pause(ctrl);
				_animated.Add(_name, value: false);
			}
			else
			{
				_animated.Add(_name, value: true);
			}
		}

		public AnimatedBrush(string name, GradientBrush brush, AnimatedBrush[] animbrushes, XmlNode[] animparams, IconCtrl ctrl)
		{
			_ctrl = ctrl;
			_name = name;
			if (brush is LinearGradientBrush)
			{
				_type = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				_type = 2;
			}
			_gradientbrush = brush;
			_animbrushes = animbrushes;
			_storyboards = new Dictionary<string, Storyboard>();
			_animated = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			for (int i = 0; i < animbrushes.Length; i++)
			{
				if (animbrushes[i] != null)
				{
					string name2 = _name + "stop" + i;
					if (ctrl.FindName(name2) != null)
					{
						ctrl.UnregisterName(name2);
					}
					GradientStop scopedElement = brush.GradientStops[i];
					ctrl.RegisterName(name2, scopedElement);
					ColorAnimation colorAnimation = new ColorAnimation
					{
						From = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(animbrushes[i]._color1),
						To = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(animbrushes[i]._color2),
						Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(animbrushes[i]._duration))),
						AutoReverse = true,
						RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever
					};
					Storyboard.SetTargetName(colorAnimation, name2);
					Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(colorAnimation, new PropertyPath(GradientStop.ColorProperty));
					string name3 = animbrushes[i].Name;
					Storyboard storyboard = ((!_storyboards.ContainsKey(name3)) ? new Storyboard() : _storyboards[name3]);
					storyboard.Children.Add(colorAnimation);
					if (!_storyboards.ContainsKey(name3))
					{
						_storyboards.Add(name3, storyboard);
						_animated.Add(name3, value: false);
					}
					storyboard.Begin(ctrl, isControllable: true);
					if (!animbrushes[i].IsRunning)
					{
						storyboard.Pause(ctrl);
					}
				}
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < animparams.Length; j++)
			{
				if (animparams[j] != null)
				{
					string name4 = _name + "stopoff" + j;
					if (ctrl.FindName(name4) != null)
					{
						ctrl.UnregisterName(name4);
					}
					GradientStop scopedElement2 = brush.GradientStops[j];
					ctrl.RegisterName(name4, scopedElement2);
					DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation
					{
						From = double.Parse(animparams[j].Attributes["from"].Value),
						To = double.Parse(animparams[j].Attributes["to"].Value),
						Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(double.Parse(animparams[j].Attributes["duration"].Value))),
						AutoReverse = bool.Parse(animparams[j].Attributes["autoreverse"].Value),
						RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever
					};
					Storyboard.SetTargetName(doubleAnimation, name4);
					Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(GradientStop.OffsetProperty));
					Storyboard storyboard2 = new Storyboard
					{
						Children = { (Timeline)doubleAnimation }
					};
					_storyboards.Add(animparams[j].Name, storyboard2);
					storyboard2.Begin(ctrl, isControllable: true);
					storyboard2.Pause(ctrl);
					_animated.Add(animparams[j].Name, value: false);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Run(string name, bool run)
		{
			if (_animated.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				_animated[name] = run;
				if (_animated[name])
				{
					_storyboards[name].Resume(_ctrl);
				}
				else
				{
					_storyboards[name].Pause(_ctrl);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Update(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (_storyboards.ContainsKey(node.Name))
			{
				string value = node.Attributes["from"].Value;
				string value2 = node.Attributes["to"].Value;
				string value3 = node.Attributes["duration"].Value;
				if (_animbrushes == null)
				{
					_color1 = value;
					_color2 = value2;
					_duration = value3;
				}
				Storyboard storyboard = _storyboards[node.Name];
				if (_animated[node.Name])
				{
					storyboard.Pause(_ctrl);
				}
				string targetName = Storyboard.GetTargetName(storyboard.Children[0]);
				storyboard.Children.Clear();
				string value4 = node.Attributes["type"].Value;
				if (value4 == "DoubleAnimation")
				{
					DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation();
					doubleAnimation.From = double.Parse(value);
					doubleAnimation.To = double.Parse(value2);
					doubleAnimation.Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(value3)));
					doubleAnimation.AutoReverse = bool.Parse(node.Attributes["autoreverse"].Value);
					doubleAnimation.RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever;
					Storyboard.SetTargetName(doubleAnimation, targetName);
					Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(GradientStop.OffsetProperty));
					storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
				}
				else
				{
					ColorAnimation colorAnimation = new ColorAnimation();
					colorAnimation.From = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value);
					colorAnimation.To = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value2);
					colorAnimation.Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(value3)));
					colorAnimation.AutoReverse = bool.Parse(node.Attributes["autoreverse"].Value);
					colorAnimation.RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever;
					Storyboard.SetTargetName(colorAnimation, targetName);
					Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(colorAnimation, new PropertyPath(SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty));
					storyboard.Children.Add(colorAnimation);
				}
				storyboard.Begin(_ctrl, isControllable: true);
				if (!_animated[node.Name])
				{
					storyboard.Pause(_ctrl);
				}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateFromChild(AnimatedBrush brush0, bool runflag)
		{
			if (_type == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			string name = brush0.Name;
			if (!_animated.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_animated[name])
			{
				_storyboards[name].Pause(_ctrl);
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _animbrushes.Length; i++)
			{
				AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = _animbrushes[i];
				if (animatedBrush == brush0)
				{
					if (runflag)
					{
						Run(name, brush0._animated[name]);
						break;
					}
					_storyboards[name].Children.Clear();
					string name2 = _name + "stop" + i;
					ColorAnimation colorAnimation = new ColorAnimation();
					colorAnimation.From = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(_animbrushes[i]._color1);
					colorAnimation.To = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(_animbrushes[i]._color2);
					colorAnimation.Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(int.Parse(_animbrushes[i]._duration)));
					colorAnimation.AutoReverse = true;
					colorAnimation.RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever;
					Storyboard.SetTargetName(colorAnimation, name2);
					Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(colorAnimation, new PropertyPath(GradientStop.ColorProperty));
					_storyboards[name].Children.Add(colorAnimation);
				}
			}
			if (!runflag)
			{
				_storyboards[name].Begin(_ctrl, isControllable: true);
			}
			if (!_animated[name])
			{
				_storyboards[name].Pause(_ctrl);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ArrowHead : BaseObj
	{
		private System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon _polygon;

		private bool _start;

		private string _color;

		private string _scale;

		public string Scale => _scale;

		public ArrowHead(double x, double y, double x2, double y2, string color, bool bStart)
		{
			_start = bStart;
			_color = color;
			_polygon = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon();
			_polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0));
			_polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(10.0, -5.0));
			_polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(5.0, 0.0));
			_polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(10.0, 5.0));
			SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush();
			if (color[0] == '@')
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding
				{
					XPath = color.Substring(1)
				};
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(solidColorBrush, SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, binding);
			}
			else
			{
				if (color == "Default")
				{
					color = "Black";
				}
				solidColorBrush.Color = ColorFromString(color);
			}
			_polygon.Fill = solidColorBrush;
			base.Children.Add(_polygon);
			double angle = Math.Atan2(y2 - y, x2 - x) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
			base.RenderTransform = new TransformGroup
			{
				Children = 
				{
					(Transform)new RotateTransform(angle),
					(Transform)new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0),
					(Transform)new TranslateTransform(x, y)
				}
			};
			_scale = "1";
		}

		public ArrowHead(Canvas cv, bool bStart)
		{
			_start = bStart;
			_polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)cv.Children[0];
			cv.Children.Remove(_polygon);
			base.Children.Add(_polygon);
			base.RenderTransform = cv.RenderTransform;
			_color = _polygon.Fill.ToString();
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			if (transformGroup.Children[1] is ScaleTransform)
			{
				_scale = ((ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[1]).ScaleX.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			else
			{
				_scale = "1";
			}
		}

		public void Move(double x, double y, double x2, double y2)
		{
			double angle = Math.Atan2(y2 - y, x2 - x) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			((RotateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0]).Angle = angle;
			((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).X = x;
			((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).Y = y;
		}

		public void ChangeColor(System.Windows.Media.Brush color)
		{
			_polygon.Fill = color;
		}

		public void ChangeColor(string color)
		{
			_color = color;
			SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush();
			if (color[0] == '@')
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding();
				binding.XPath = color.Substring(1);
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(solidColorBrush, SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, binding);
			}
			else
			{
				solidColorBrush.Color = ColorFromString(color);
			}
			_polygon.Fill = solidColorBrush;
		}

		public void ChangeSize(string value)
		{
			_scale = value;
			double num = double.Parse(_scale, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			((ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[1]).ScaleX = num;
			((ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[1]).ScaleY = num;
		}

		public int GetSize()
		{
			double num = double.Parse(_scale, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			return (int)(num + 0.1);
		}

		public string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, string linecolor)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			string text = "";
			text = (bCurrent ? linecolor : ((!_color.StartsWith("@")) ? _color : ("{Binding XPath=" + _color.Substring(1) + "}")));
			string text2 = "<Canvas Tag=\"" + (_start ? "ArrowAtStart" : "ArrowAtEnd") + "\">";
			text2 += "<Canvas.RenderTransform>";
			text2 += "<TransformGroup>";
			text2 = text2 + "<RotateTransform Angle=\"" + ((RotateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0]).Angle.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>";
			string text3 = text2;
			text2 = text3 + "<ScaleTransform ScaleX=\"" + _scale + "\" ScaleY=\"" + _scale + "\"/>";
			string text4 = text2;
			text2 = text4 + "<TranslateTransform X=\"" + ((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" Y=\"" + ((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>";
			text2 += "</TransformGroup>";
			text2 += "</Canvas.RenderTransform>";
			text2 = text2 + "<Polygon Fill=\"" + PropertyXaml(text) + "\" Points=\"0,0 10,-5 5,0 10,5\" />";
			return text2 + "</Canvas>";
		}

		public string GenXmlText()
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			string text = "<Arrow at=\"" + (_start ? "Start" : "End") + "\" ";
			text = text + "angle=\"" + ((RotateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0]).Angle.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "scale=\"" + _scale + "\" ";
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public void Update(XmlNode root)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.SelectSingleNode("Canvas.RenderTransform/TransformGroup");
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in xmlNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				switch (childNode.Name)
				{
				case "RotateTransform":
					((RotateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0]).Angle = GetDouble(childNode, "Angle");
					break;
				case "ScaleTransform":
					_scale = GetString(childNode, "ScaleX");
					break;
				case "TranslateTransform":
					((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).X = GetDouble(childNode, "X");
					((TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2]).Y = GetDouble(childNode, "Y");
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class TagObj : BaseObj
	{
		protected string _visibilitymap = "";

		public TagObj()
		{
		}

		public TagObj(UIElement element)
		{
			Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(element, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			if (binding != null)
			{
				if (binding.Converter == null)
				{
					_visibilitymap = binding.XPath;
				}
				else
				{
					_visibilitymap = binding.XPath + "[" + (string)binding.ConverterParameter + "]";
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void TagAnchor(string id, Anchor anchor, bool reversedirection)
		{
		}

		protected int MatchItem(XmlDocument doc, string element, string value)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = doc.DocumentElement.SelectNodes(element);
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (!(item.InnerText == value))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void ChangeVisibility(string value)
		{
			if (value == "----" || value == "None")
			{
				_visibilitymap = "";
			}
			else
			{
				_visibilitymap = value;
			}
			SetVisibility();
		}

		protected virtual void SetVisibility()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(this, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				if (binding != null)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(this, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				}
				return;
			}
			Binding binding2 = new Binding();
			if (_visibilitymap.Contains('['))
			{
				int num = _visibilitymap.IndexOf('[');
				int num2 = _visibilitymap.IndexOf(']');
				string text = _visibilitymap.Substring(0, num);
				string converterParameter = _visibilitymap.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
				binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + text;
				binding2.Converter = new EnumCalc();
				binding2.ConverterParameter = converterParameter;
			}
			else
			{
				binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _visibilitymap;
			}
			SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding2);
		}

		protected string SaveVisibility()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				return "";
			}
			if (_visibilitymap.Contains('['))
			{
				int num = _visibilitymap.IndexOf('[');
				int num2 = _visibilitymap.IndexOf(']');
				string text = _visibilitymap.Substring(0, num);
				string text2 = _visibilitymap.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
				return "Visibility=\"{Binding XPath=" + text + ",Converter={StaticResource EnumCalc},ConverterParameter=" + text2 + "}\"";
			}
			return "Visibility=\"{Binding XPath=" + _visibilitymap + "}\"";
		}
	}
	public class Element : TagObj
	{
		protected string _name;

		protected XmlNode _xmlnode;

		protected UIElement _element;

		protected List<Handle> _handles;

		protected List<string> _anchored;

		protected List<Element> _clipped;

		protected List<Element> _clipping;

		protected Storyboard _storyboard;

		protected string _stroke;

		protected string _strokethickness;

		protected string _strokedash;

		protected string _dashmotion;

		protected string _fill;

		protected XmlNode _datanode;

		protected XmlNode _positionnode;

		protected ArrowHead _atstart;

		protected ArrowHead _atend;

		protected double _thickness = 4.0;

		private bool _isEnabled = true;

		public Shape Shape
		{
			get
			{
				if (_element is Shape)
				{
					return (Shape)_element;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public new string Name => _name;

		public int ArrowAttached
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (_atstart != null)
				{
					num |= 1;
				}
				if (_atend != null)
				{
					num |= 2;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public string Stroke => _stroke;

		public string Fill => _fill;

		public IconCtrl Control { get; set; }

		public bool OnPfs { get; set; }

		public bool OnLabel { get; set; }

		public Cloud OnCloud { get; set; }

		public int Index { get; set; }

		public XmlNode DataNode
		{
			get
			{
				return _datanode;
			}
			set
			{
				_datanode = value;
			}
		}

		public event IconEditorEventHandler IconEditorEvent;

		public Element()
		{
			OnPfs = false;
			OnLabel = false;
			OnCloud = null;
			_handles = new List<Handle>();
		}

		public Element(UIElement element)
			: base(element)
		{
			_element = element;
			if (element is FrameworkElement)
			{
				_name = ((FrameworkElement)element).Name;
			}
			OnPfs = false;
			OnLabel = false;
			_handles = new List<Handle>();
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += _element_MouseDown;
			if (element is Shape)
			{
				((Shape)element).Loaded += Element_Loaded;
			}
			base.Children.Add(element);
		}

		private void Element_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_clipped != null && _clipped.Count > 0)
			{
				UpdateClip();
			}
		}

		public string ArrowSize(bool start)
		{
			if (start && _atstart != null)
			{
				return _atstart.Scale;
			}
			if (!start && _atend != null)
			{
				return _atend.Scale;
			}
			return "";
		}

		public void SetElement(UIElement element)
		{
			_element = element;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += _element_MouseDown;
			base.Children.Add(element);
		}

		public virtual void ReplaceElement(UIElement element)
		{
			base.Children.Clear();
			SetElement(element);
			AddBack();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public virtual bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = 0.0;
			y = 0.0;
			return false;
		}

		public virtual void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void AddBack()
		{
		}

		public virtual void Enable(bool enable)
		{
			_isEnabled = enable;
			if (_element != null)
			{
				_element.IsEnabled = enable;
				_element.Opacity = (enable ? 1.0 : 0.3);
			}
		}

		public virtual void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
		}

		public virtual void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
		}

		public virtual void RunAnim(bool run, string anim)
		{
			if (_storyboard != null && anim == _dashmotion)
			{
				if (run)
				{
					_storyboard.Resume(this);
				}
				else
				{
					_storyboard.Pause(this);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void UpdateAnimation(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (_storyboard != null && _dashmotion == node.Name)
			{
				bool flag = !_storyboard.GetIsPaused(this);
				if (flag)
				{
					_storyboard.Pause(this);
				}
				_storyboard.Children.Clear();
				SetDashAnimation(node);
				if (flag)
				{
					_storyboard.Resume(this);
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetSelected(bool bOn)
		{
			if (_datanode != null)
			{
				_datanode.Attributes["IsSelected"].Value = (bOn ? "True" : "False");
			}
		}

		public void ChangeName(string name)
		{
			_name = name;
			if (_element is FrameworkElement)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)_element).Name = name;
			}
		}

		private void _element_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (OnPfs)
			{
				FireEvent("Selected", "");
			}
			else if (Control != null && !Control.OnPfs && _isEnabled && Control.DragElement(this))
			{
				ShowHandles(bShow: true);
			}
		}

		public virtual void XmlNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			_xmlnode = node;
			string text = GetType().ToString();
			if (text.StartsWith("IconCtrl."))
			{
				text = text.Replace("IconCtrl.", "");
			}
			if (_element is Shape)
			{
				Shape shape = (Shape)_element;
				if (shape.Fill == null && node.Attributes["Stroke"] == null && node.Attributes["Fill"] == null)
				{
					_stroke = "@Color";
					shape.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
				}
			}
			_ = node.Name != text;
		}

		public virtual XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			if (_fill != null && Control != null)
			{
				if (_fill[0] == '@' && Control.AnimatedBrush(_fill.Substring(1)) != null)
				{
					Shape shape = (Shape)_element;
					shape.Fill = MappedBrush(_fill.Substring(1));
				}
				if (Control.AnimatedBrush(_fill) != null)
				{
					Shape shape2 = (Shape)_element;
					GradientBrush fill = (GradientBrush)Control.AnimatedBrush(_fill).Brush;
					shape2.Fill = fill;
				}
			}
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("Element");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Name))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "Name", Name);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "IsSelected", "false");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "IsChecked", "false");
			int num = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				num = MatchItem(doc, "Visibility", _visibilitymap);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "visibility", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "clipped", "");
			if (_strokethickness != null)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				double num3 = double.Parse(_strokethickness, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "thickness", ((!(num3 < 0.3)) ? ((num3 < 0.6) ? 1 : ((int)(num3 + 0.1) + 1)) : 0).ToString());
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "thickness", "-1");
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_strokedash))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "dash", _strokedash);
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "dash", "");
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dashmotion))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "dashmotion", MatchProperty(doc, "DashMotion", _dashmotion).ToString());
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "checkbox1", "");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "checkbox2", "");
			_datanode = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}

		protected void AddPositionNode()
		{
			if (_datanode != null)
			{
				_positionnode = _datanode.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("position");
				_datanode.AppendChild(_positionnode);
			}
		}

		{
			IconEditorEventArgs e = new IconEditorEventArgs(EventName, Value);
			if (this.IconEditorEvent != null)
			{
				this.IconEditorEvent(this, e);
			}
		}

			_anchored = new List<string>();
			for (int i = 0; i < size; i++)

			}
		}

				_atstart.ChangeColor(color);
				_atstart.ChangeSize(scale);

				_atend.ChangeSize(scale);

			base.Move(dx, dy);
			if (base.Clip != null)
			{
				UpdateClip();

			RefreshHandles();
		}

			if (_clipping != null)
			{
				foreach (Element item in _clipping)
				{
					item.UpdateClip();
				}
			}
			if (_clipped != null)
			{
				UpdateClip();
			}
		}

			{
				((Group)base.Parent).CalcSize(newgroup: false);
			}
		}

		protected string ConvBinding(string value)
		{
			if (value.StartsWith("{Binding ") || value[0] == '@')
			{
				value = "black";
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected string MultiBinding(string paths, string parameter)
		{
			string[] array = paths.Split(',');
			string text = "<MultiBinding Converter=\"{StaticResource MCalc}\" ";
			text = text + "ConverterParameter=\"" + parameter + "\">\r\n";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				text = text + "<Binding XPath=\"" + array[i] + "\" Mode=\"OneWay\"/>";
			}
			return text + "</MultiBinding>";
		}

		public bool Contained(System.Drawing.Rectangle rect)
		{
			double minx = 1E+30;
			double miny = 1E+30;
			double maxx = -1E+30;
			double maxy = -1E+30;
			GetField(ref minx, ref maxx, ref miny, ref maxy);
			if (minx >= (double)rect.Left && maxx <= (double)rect.Right && miny >= (double)rect.Top && maxy <= (double)rect.Bottom)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public virtual void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
		}

		public virtual void ChangeDashMotion(string motion)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = Control.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(motion);
			if (xmlNode != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_strokedash))
			{
				_dashmotion = motion;
				_storyboard = new Storyboard();
				SetDashAnimation(xmlNode);
			}
		}

		private void SetDashAnimation(XmlNode node)
		{
			string value = node.Attributes["direction"].Value;
			int num = int.Parse(node.Attributes["duration"].Value);
			Shape value2 = (Shape)_element;
			DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = new DoubleAnimation();
			string[] array = _strokedash.Split(' ');
			double num2 = 0.0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string s in array2)
			{
				num2 += double.Parse(s, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			if (array.Length == 1)
			{
				num2 *= 2.0;
			}
			if (value == "0")
			{
				doubleAnimation.From = num2;
				doubleAnimation.To = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				doubleAnimation.From = 0.0;
				doubleAnimation.To = num2;
			}
			doubleAnimation.Duration = new Duration(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(num));
			doubleAnimation.RepeatBehavior = RepeatBehavior.Forever;
			Storyboard.SetTarget(doubleAnimation, value2);
			Storyboard.SetTargetProperty(doubleAnimation, new PropertyPath(Shape.StrokeDashOffsetProperty));
			_storyboard.Children.Add(doubleAnimation);
			_storyboard.Begin(this, isControllable: true);
			if (node.Attributes["run"] == null || !bool.Parse(node.Attributes["run"].Value))
			{
				_storyboard.Pause(this);
			}
		}

		public virtual void ChangeProperty(string property, string value, XmlNode gradientsnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Shape shape = (Shape)_element;
			switch (property)
			{
			case "Fill":
			{
				_fill = value;
				if (value == "----" || value == "None")
				{
					shape.Fill = null;
					break;
				}
				if (value[0] == '@')
				{
					shape.Fill = MappedBrush(value.Substring(1));
					break;
				}
				if (value == "Default")
				{
					shape.Fill = MappedBrush("Color");
					break;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if (gradientsnode == null)
				{
					gradientsnode = Control.GradientsNode;
				}
				if (nsmgr == null)
				{
					nsmgr = Control.NSMGR;
				}
				if (gradientsnode != null)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = ((nsmgr != null) ? gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + value + "']", nsmgr) : gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("Gradient[@name='" + value + "']"));
					if (xmlNode != null)
					{
						XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
						xmlDocument.LoadXml(xmlNode.FirstChild.InnerText);
						shape.Fill = Control.CreateGradientBrush(value, xmlDocument.DocumentElement);
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					if (value[0] == '%')
					{
						_fill = value;
						value = value.Substring(1);
					}
					else
					{
						_fill = "%" + value;
					}
					SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush();
					solidColorBrush.Color = ColorFromString(value);
					shape.Fill = solidColorBrush;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Stroke":
				_stroke = value;
				if (value == "----" || value == "None")
				{
					shape.Stroke = null;
				}
				else if (value[0] == '@')
				{
					shape.Stroke = MappedBrush(value.Substring(1));
				}
				else if (value == "Default")
				{
					shape.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
				}
				else
				{
					SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush2 = new SolidColorBrush();
					solidColorBrush2.Color = ColorFromString(value);
					shape.Stroke = solidColorBrush2;
				}
				if (_atstart != null)
				{
					_atstart.ChangeColor(_stroke);
				}
				if (_atend != null)
				{
					_atend.ChangeColor(_stroke);
				}
				break;
			case "StrokeThickness":
				_strokethickness = value;
				shape.StrokeThickness = double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				break;
			case "StrokeDash":
				if (value == "0")
				{
					value = "";
				}
				_strokedash = value;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					shape.StrokeDashArray = null;
				}
				else
				{
					string[] array = value.Split(' ');
					DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						if (array[i] != "" && double.TryParse(array[i], out var result))
						{
							doubleCollection.Add(result);
						}
					}
					shape.StrokeDashArray = doubleCollection;
				}
				if (_datanode != null && _datanode.Attributes["dash"] != null)
				{
					_datanode.Attributes["dash"].Value = _strokedash;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public virtual void ExtractProperty()
		{
			if (_element == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_element is TextBox)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)_element;
				_stroke = textBox.Foreground.ToString();
			}
			else if (_element is Shape)
			{
				Shape shape = (Shape)_element;
				if (shape.Stroke == null)
				{
					_stroke = "None";
					_strokethickness = null;
					_strokedash = null;
				}
				else
				{
					_stroke = shape.Stroke.ToString();
					_strokethickness = shape.StrokeThickness.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					_strokedash = shape.StrokeDashArray.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
				if (shape.Fill == null)
				{
					_fill = "None";
				}
				else
				{
					_fill = shape.Fill.ToString();
				}
			}
		}

		public string GetProperty(string property)
		{
			string text = "";
			switch (property)
			{
			case "Stroke":
				text = _stroke;
				break;
			case "StrokeThickness":
				text = _strokethickness;
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = "1";
				}
				break;
			case "StrokeDash":
				text = _strokedash;
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = "";
				}
				break;
			case "Fill":
				text = _fill;
				break;
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string GetArrowSize(bool bStart)
		{
			string result = "";
			if (bStart && _atstart != null)
			{
				result = _atstart.Scale;
			}
			else if (!bStart && _atend != null)
			{
				result = _atend.Scale;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void ChangeArrowSize(bool bStart, string value, bool bNoHistory)
		{
			if (bStart && _atstart != null)
			{
				if (!bNoHistory && _atstart.Scale != value)
				{
					Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.ArrowSize, this, "Start");
				}
				_atstart.ChangeSize(value);
			}
			else if (!bStart && _atend != null)
			{
				if (_atend.Scale != value)
				{
					Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.ArrowSize, this, "End");
				}
				_atend.ChangeSize(value);
			}
		}

		public void SetArrow(Canvas cv)
		{
			string text = (string)cv.Tag;
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)cv.RenderTransform;
			double scaleX = ((ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[1]).ScaleX;
			if (text == "ArrowAtStart" && _atstart != null)
			{
				_atstart.ChangeSize(scaleX.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			else if (text == "ArrowAtEnd" && _atend != null)
			{
				_atend.ChangeSize(scaleX.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
		}

		public void UpdateGradientBrush(string fillname, XmlNode gradientsnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (_fill == fillname)
			{
				Shape shape = (Shape)_element;
				XmlNode xmlNode = gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + fillname + "']", nsmgr);
				if (xmlNode != null)
				{
					XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
					xmlDocument.LoadXml(xmlNode.FirstChild.InnerText);
					shape.Fill = Control.CreateGradientBrush(fillname, xmlDocument.DocumentElement);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void GenGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			if (Shape != null && !Shape.RenderedGeometry.IsEmpty())
			{
				double top = Canvas.GetTop(Shape);
				double left = Canvas.GetLeft(Shape);
				Geometry renderedGeometry = Shape.RenderedGeometry;
				if (!double.IsNaN(top))
				{
					renderedGeometry.Transform = new TranslateTransform(left, top);
				}
				gg.Children.Add(renderedGeometry);
			}
		}

		public virtual void HandleSelected(string id)
		{
			if (OnPfs)
			{
				FireEvent("HandleSelected", id);
			}
			else
			{
				Control.ResizeElement(this, id);
			}
		}

				Control.HandleQuery(this, id);
			}
		}

		{
		}

		public virtual void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
		}

		public override void TagAnchor(string id, Anchor anchor, bool reversedirection)
		{
			bool flag = this is Line || this is Polygon;
			if (anchor != null && !flag)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _anchored.Count; i++)
				{
					if (_anchored[i] != null && _anchored[i] == anchor.Name)
					{
						_anchored[i] = null;
						_handles[i].UntagAnchor();
					}
				}
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < _handles.Count; j++)
			{
				if (_handles[j].ID == id)
				{
					if (anchor == null)
					{
						_anchored[j] = null;
						_handles[j].UntagAnchor();
					}
					else
					{
						_anchored[j] = anchor.Name;
						_handles[j].TagAnchor(anchor, reversedirection);
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public Handle FindHandle(string id)
		{
			if (_handles.Count == 0)
			{
				AddHandles();
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _handles.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_handles[i].ID == id)
				{
					return _handles[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public virtual void ShowHandles(bool bShow)
		{
			if (OnPfs)
			{
				foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
				{
					if (!handle.Hidden)
					{
						if (bShow)
						{
							((Canvas)base.Parent).Children.Add(handle);
						}
						else
						{
							((Canvas)base.Parent).Children.Remove(handle);
						}
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (Control != null && Control.OnPfs)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Handle handle2 in _handles)
			{
				if (handle2.Hidden)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (bShow)
				{
					if (handle2.Parent == null && Control.CurLayer.Children.IndexOf(handle2) < 0)
					{
						Control.CurLayer.Children.Add(handle2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					Control.CurLayer.Children.Remove(handle2);
				}
			}
		}

		public void RefreshHandles()
		{
			ShowHandles(bShow: false);
			AddHandles();
		}

		protected void AddHandle(string ID, double x, double y, string anchored, int mode = 0)
		{
			ConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				if (handle.ID == ID)
				{
					handle.Update(x, y, anchored != null);
					return;
				}
			}
			Handle item = new Handle(this, ID, x, y, anchored != null, mode);
			_handles.Add(item);
			if (_handles.Count > _anchored.Count)
			{
				_anchored.Add(null);
			}
		}

		protected void RemoveHandle(string ID)
		{
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				if (handle.ID == ID)
				{
					_handles.Remove(handle);
					((Canvas)base.Parent).Children.Remove(handle);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected void ClearHandles()
		{
			foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
			{
				((Canvas)base.Parent).Children.Remove(handle);
			}
			_handles.Clear();
		}

		protected int MatchProperty(XmlDocument doc, string property, string value)
		{
			if (property == "Fill" && value[0] == '%')
			{
				value = value.Substring(1);
			}
			string text = "";
			if (property != "DashMotion")
			{
				text = "Attributes/";
			}
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = doc.DocumentElement.SelectNodes(text + property);
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (!(item.InnerText == value))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public SolidColorBrush MappedBrush(string colorname)
		{
			AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = null;
			if (Control != null)
			{
				animatedBrush = Control.AnimatedBrush(colorname);
			}
			if (animatedBrush != null)
			{
				return (SolidColorBrush)animatedBrush.Brush;
			}
			SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush();
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(solidColorBrush, SolidColorBrush.ColorProperty, GetBinding(colorname));
			return solidColorBrush;
		}

		protected Binding GetBinding(string path)
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.XPath = path;
			return binding;
		}

		public void RevConvCoord(ref double x, ref double y)
		{
			if (base.Parent is Group)
			{
				((Group)base.Parent).RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
				Group obj = (Group)base.Parent;
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
				System.Windows.Point point2 = obj.RenderTransform.Inverse.Transform(point);
				x = point2.X;
				y = point2.Y;
			}
		}

		protected void ConvCoord(ref double x, ref double y)
		{
			if (this is Group)
			{
				Group obj = (Group)this;
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
				System.Windows.Point point2 = obj.RenderTransform.Transform(point);
				x = point2.X;
				y = point2.Y;
			}
			if (base.Parent is Group)
			{
				Group obj2 = (Group)base.Parent;
				obj2.ConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			}
		}

		protected string GetStroke()
		{
			if (_stroke != null)
			{
				return _stroke;
			}
			if (_xmlnode == null)
			{
				_stroke = ((Control == null || !Control.KBase) ? "@Color" : "Default");
			}
			else
			{
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = _xmlnode.Attributes["Stroke"];
				if (xmlAttribute == null)
				{
					xmlAttribute = _xmlnode.Attributes["Foreground"];
				}
				if (xmlAttribute == null)
				{
					_stroke = "None";
				}
				else
				{
					_stroke = xmlAttribute.Value;
					if (xmlAttribute.Value.Contains("{Binding "))
					{
						int num = xmlAttribute.Value.IndexOf("XPath=");
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							_stroke = "@" + Control.ExtractSubstring(xmlAttribute.Value, '=', '}');
							if (Control.KBase && _stroke == "@Color")
							{
								_stroke = "Default";
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						Control.AddLocalColor(_stroke);
					}
				}
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = _xmlnode.Attributes["StrokeThickness"];
				if (xmlAttribute2 == null)
				{
					_strokethickness = null;
				}
				else
				{
					_strokethickness = xmlAttribute2.Value;
				}
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute3 = _xmlnode.Attributes["StrokeDashArray"];
				if (xmlAttribute3 == null)
				{
					_strokedash = null;
				}
				else
				{
					_strokedash = xmlAttribute3.Value;
				}
			}
			return _stroke;
		}

		protected string GetFill(string type)
		{
			if (_fill != null)
			{
				return _fill;
			}
			if (_xmlnode == null)
			{
				_fill = "None";
			}
			else
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _xmlnode.SelectSingleNode(type + ".Fill");
				if (xmlNode != null)
				{
					if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Name == "#comment")
					{
						string value = xmlNode.FirstChild.Value;
						if (value.StartsWith(" Gradient "))
						{
							int num = value.IndexOf('(');
							int num2 = value.IndexOf(')');
							_fill = value.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
						}
					}
					else if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Name == "LinearGradientBrush" && xmlNode.FirstChild.Attributes["StartPoint"] != null)
					{
						if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Attributes["MappingMode"] != null)
						{
							_fill = "Custom";
						}
						else if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Attributes["StartPoint"].Value == "0,0.5")
						{
							_fill = "Vertical";
						}
						else
						{
							_fill = "Horizontal";
						}
					}
					else if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Name == "RadialGradientBrush")
					{
						XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = xmlNode.FirstChild.Attributes["GradientOrigin"];
						if (xmlNode.FirstChild.Attributes["MappingMode"] != null)
						{
							_fill = "Custom";
						}
						else if (xmlAttribute == null)
						{
							_fill = "Sphere";
						}
						else if (xmlAttribute.Value == "0.5,0")
						{
							_fill = "Bottom Half Sphere";
						}
						else if (xmlAttribute.Value == "0.5,1")
						{
							_fill = "Top Half Sphere";
						}
						else if (xmlAttribute.Value == "0,0.5")
						{
							_fill = "Right Half Sphere";
						}
						else if (xmlAttribute.Value == "1,0.5")
						{
							_fill = "Left Half Sphere";
						}
						else
						{
							_fill = "Radial";
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = _xmlnode.Attributes["Fill"];
					if (xmlAttribute2 == null)
					{
						_fill = "None";
					}
					else
					{
						_fill = xmlAttribute2.Value;
						if (xmlAttribute2.Value.Contains("{Binding "))
						{
							int num3 = xmlAttribute2.Value.IndexOf("XPath=");
							if (num3 >= 0)
							{
								_fill = "@" + Control.ExtractSubstring(xmlAttribute2.Value, '=', '}');
								if (Control.KBase && _fill == "@Color")
								{
									_fill = "Default";
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							Control.AddLocalColor(_fill);
							_fill = "%" + _fill;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return _fill;
		}

		protected string SimpleProperty()
		{
			string text = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_name))
			{
				text = text + " Name=\"" + _name + "\"";
			}
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text = text + " Stroke=\"" + PropertyXaml(_stroke) + "\"";
			}
			if (_strokethickness != null)
			{
				text = text + " StrokeThickness=\"" + _strokethickness + "\"";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_strokedash))
			{
				text = text + " StrokeDashArray=\"" + _strokedash + "\"";
			}
			if (_fill != null && (_fill[0] == '@' || _fill[0] == '%' || _fill[0] == '#'))
			{
				string property = _fill;
				if (_fill[0] == '%')
				{
					property = _fill.Substring(1);
				}
				text = text + " Fill=\"" + PropertyXaml(property) + "\"";
			}
			return text;
		}

		private void UpdateSimpleProperty(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (node.Attributes["Stroke"] != null)
			{
				string value = node.Attributes["Stroke"].Value;
				ChangeProperty("Stroke", value, null, null);
			}
			else
			{
				ChangeProperty("Stroke", "None", null, null);
			}
			if (node.Attributes["StrokeThickness"] != null)
			{
				string value2 = node.Attributes["StrokeThickness"].Value;
				ChangeProperty("StrokeThickness", value2, null, null);
			}
			else
			{
				_strokethickness = null;
			}
			if (node.Attributes["StrokeDashArray"] != null)
			{
				string value3 = node.Attributes["StrokeDashArray"].Value;
				ChangeProperty("StrokeDash", value3, null, null);
			}
			else
			{
				ChangeProperty("StrokeDash", "", null, null);
			}
			if (node.Attributes["Fill"] != null)
			{
				string value4 = node.Attributes["Fill"].Value;
				ChangeProperty("Fill", value4, null, null);
			}
			else
			{
				ChangeProperty("Fill", "None", null, null);
			}
		}

		protected string GradientFill(string shape)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (_fill == "Custom")
			{
				text += _xmlnode.InnerXml;
			}
			else if (_fill != null && _fill != "----" && _fill != "None" && _fill[0] != '@' && _fill[0] != '%' && _fill[0] != '#')
			{
				text = text + "<" + shape + ".Fill>";
				text = text + "<!-- Gradient (" + _fill + ") -->";
				text = text + "</" + shape + ".Fill>";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void ClearClipElements()
		{
			if (_clipped == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Element item in _clipped)
			{
				item.RemoveClipping(this);
			}
			_clipped.Clear();
			if (_datanode != null && _datanode.Attributes["clipped"] != null)
			{
				_datanode.Attributes["clipped"].Value = "";
			}
		}

		public void AddClipElement(Element elem)
		{
			if (_clipped == null)
			{
				_clipped = new List<Element>();
			}
			_clipped.Add(elem);
			elem.AddClipping(this);
			if (_datanode != null && _datanode.Attributes["clipped"] != null)
			{
				_datanode.Attributes["clipped"].Value = "yes";
			}
		}

		public void AddClipping(Element elem)
		{
			if (_clipping == null)
			{
				_clipping = new List<Element>();
			}
			_clipping.Add(elem);
		}

		public void RemoveClipping(Element elem)
		{
			if (_clipping != null)
			{
				_clipping.Remove(elem);
			}
		}

		public void ShowClipElements()
		{
			Control.ClearGrouping();
			if (_clipped == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Element item in _clipped)
			{
				Control.AddGrouping(item);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateClip()
		{
			if (_clipped == null || _clipped.Count == 0)
			{
				base.Clip = null;
			}
			else
			{
				base.Clip = GenPathGeometry();
			}
		}

		private PathGeometry GenPathGeometry()
		{
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			geometryGroup.FillRule = FillRule.Nonzero;
			GenCanvasGeometry(geometryGroup);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			path.StrokeThickness = 0.1;
			path.Data = geometryGroup;
			return path.Data.GetOutlinedPathGeometry();
		}

		private void GenCanvasGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			foreach (Element item in _clipped)
			{
				item.GenGeometry(gg);
			}
		}

		public void SaveClip(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (_clipped == null || _clipped.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Clip", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			string elementIndex = Control.GetElementIndex(this);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "index", elementIndex);
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			string text = "";
			foreach (Element item in _clipped)
			{
				if (text != "")
				{
					text += ",";
				}
				text += Control.GetElementIndex(item);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "elements", text);
		}

		public void LoadClip(string elements)
		{
			string[] array = elements.Split(',');
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string ids in array2)
			{
				Element element = Control.FindElement(ids, 0);
				if (element != null)
				{
					AddClipElement(element);
				}
			}
		}

		public void SaveAnimation(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_dashmotion))
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Animation", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				string elementIndex = Control.GetElementIndex(this);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "index", elementIndex);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dashmotion", _dashmotion);
				root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			}
		}

		public void LoadAnimation(XmlNode root)
		{
			if (root.Attributes["dashmotion"] != null)
			{
				string value = root.Attributes["dashmotion"].Value;
				XmlNode xmlNode = Control.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(value);
				if (xmlNode != null)
				{
					_dashmotion = value;
					_storyboard = new Storyboard();
					SetDashAnimation(xmlNode);
					int num = MatchProperty(Control.WholeList, "DashMotion", _dashmotion);
					AddAttribute(_datanode, "dashmotion", num.ToString());
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class Line : Element
	{
		internal double _x1;

		internal double _y1;

		internal double _x2;

		internal double _y2;

		private double _wx1;

		private double _wy1;

		private double _wx2;

		private double _wy2;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Line _back;

		private bool _arrowAtStart;

		private bool _arrowAtEnd;

		public Line(UIElement elem, bool created = false)
			: base(elem)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)elem;
			_x1 = line.X1;
			_x2 = line.X2;
			_y1 = line.Y1;
			_y2 = line.Y2;
			if (created)
			{
				line.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			if ((string)line.Tag == "BothArrows" || (string)line.Tag == "StartArrow")
			{
				_arrowAtStart = true;
				AddArrow("ArrowAtStart", bAdd: true, colorset: false);
			}
			if ((string)line.Tag == "BothArrows" || (string)line.Tag == "EndArrow")
			{
				_arrowAtEnd = true;
				AddArrow("ArrowAtEnd", bAdd: true, colorset: false);
			}
			AddBack();
			InitializeAnchored(3);
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
			_back.X1 = _x1;
			_back.X2 = _x2;
			_back.Y1 = _y1;
			_back.Y2 = _y2;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void AddArrow(string location, bool bAdd, bool colorset = true)
		{
			if (location == "ArrowAtStart")
			{
				if (bAdd)
				{
					if (_atstart == null)
					{
						_atstart = new ArrowHead(_x1, _y1, _x2, _y2, colorset ? GetStroke() : "Default", bStart: true);
						base.Children.Add(_atstart);
					}
					_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (_atstart != null)
				{
					_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(location == "ArrowAtEnd"))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (bAdd)
				{
					if (_atend == null)
					{
						_atend = new ArrowHead(_x2, _y2, _x1, _y1, colorset ? GetStroke() : "Default", bStart: false);
						base.Children.Add(_atend);
					}
					_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (_atend != null)
				{
					_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		public void AddArrow(Canvas cv, bool update)
		{
			string text = (string)cv.Tag;
			if (text == "ArrowAtStart")
			{
				if (_atstart == null || update)
				{
					_atstart = new ArrowHead(cv, bStart: true);
					base.Children.Add(_atstart);
				}
			}
			else if (_atend == null || update)
			{
				_atend = new ArrowHead(cv, bStart: false);
				base.Children.Add(_atend);
			}
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			_wx1 = x[0];
			_wy1 = y[0];
			_wx2 = x[1];
			_wy2 = y[1];
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		public override bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = (_x1 + _x2) / 2.0;
			y = (_y1 + _y2) / 2.0;
			return true;
		}

		public override void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
			switch (command)
			{
			case "RotLeft":
			case "RotRight":
			{
				double num = _x1 - cx;
				double num2 = _y1 - cy;
				double num3 = _x2 - cx;
				double num4 = _y2 - cy;
				if (command == "RotLeft")
				{
					_x1 = cx + num2;
					_y1 = cy - num;
					_x2 = cx + num4;
					_y2 = cy - num3;
				}
				else
				{
					_x1 = cx - num2;
					_y1 = cy + num;
					_x2 = cx - num4;
					_y2 = cy + num3;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "FlipX":
				_x1 = cx - (_x1 - cx);
				_x2 = cx - (_x2 - cx);
				break;
			case "FlipY":
				_y1 = cy - (_y1 - cy);
				_y2 = cy - (_y2 - cy);
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			AddHandle("1", _x1, _y1, _anchored[0]);
			AddHandle("2", _x2, _y2, _anchored[1]);
			AddHandle("Base", (_x1 + _x2) / 2.0, (_y1 + _y2) / 2.0, _anchored[2]);
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			if (_arrowAtStart)
			{
				_arrowAtStart = false;
				_atstart.ChangeColor(stroke);
			}
			if (_arrowAtEnd)
			{
				_arrowAtEnd = false;
				_atend.ChangeColor(stroke);
			}
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Line");
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", "0");
			AddAttribute(xml, "angleactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "fillactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "pointactive", "Collapsed");
			if (_atstart != null && _atstart.IsVisible)
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "atstart", "True");
				AddAttribute(xml, "startarrowsize", (_atstart.GetSize() - 1).ToString());
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "atstart", "False");
				AddAttribute(xml, "startarrowsize", "-1");
			}
			if (_atend != null && _atend.IsVisible)
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "atend", "True");
				AddAttribute(xml, "endarrowsize", (_atend.GetSize() - 1).ToString());
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "atend", "False");
				AddAttribute(xml, "endarrowsize", "-1");
			}
			AddPositionNode();
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var1", "X1");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value1", _x1.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var2", "Y1");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value2", _y1.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var3", "X2");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value3", _x2.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var4", "Y2");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value4", _y2.ToString());
			return xml;
		}

		public override void Update(XmlNode root)
		{
			XmlNode node = root;
			if (_atstart != null || _atend != null)
			{
				node = root.SelectSingleNode("Line");
			}
			_x1 = GetDouble(node, "X1");
			_y1 = GetDouble(node, "Y1");
			_x2 = GetDouble(node, "X2");
			_y2 = GetDouble(node, "Y2");
			Refresh();
			base.Update(node);
			if (_atstart != null)
			{
				node = root.SelectSingleNode("Canvas[@Tag='ArrowAtStart']");
				_atstart.Update(node);
			}
			if (_atend != null)
			{
				node = root.SelectSingleNode("Canvas[@Tag='ArrowAtEnd']");
				_atend.Update(node);
			}
		}

		private void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)_element;
			line.X1 = _x1;
			line.Y1 = _y1;
			line.X2 = _x2;
			line.Y2 = _y2;
			_back.X1 = _x1;
			_back.X2 = _x2;
			_back.Y1 = _y1;
			_back.Y2 = _y2;
			if (!(this is Arrow))
			{
				return;
			}
			line.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(ColorFromString(_stroke));
			line.StrokeThickness = double.Parse(_strokethickness, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			string[] array = _strokedash.Split(' ');
			DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					doubleCollection.Add(double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				}
			}
			line.StrokeDashArray = doubleCollection;
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_positionnode != null)
			{
				_positionnode.Attributes["value1"].Value = (_x1 + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value2"].Value = (_y1 + dy).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value3"].Value = (_x2 + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value4"].Value = (_y2 + dy).ToString();
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			string result = "";
			double result2 = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result2);
			switch (name)
			{
			case "Value1":
				_x1 = result2;
				break;
			case "Value2":
				_y1 = result2;
