				break;
			case "Value3":
				_x2 = result2;
				break;
			case "Value4":
				_y2 = result2;
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			return result;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			bool flag = _atstart != null && _atstart.IsVisible;
			bool flag2 = _atend != null && _atend.IsVisible;
			string text = "";
			if (bCurrent && (flag || flag2))
			{
				text += "<Canvas>";
			}
			text += "<Line";
			text = text + " X1=\"" + _x1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y1=\"" + _y1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " X2=\"" + _x2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y2=\"" + _y2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"BothArrows\"";
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"StartArrow\"";
			}
			else if (flag2)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"EndArrow\"";
			}
			text += ">";
			text += "</Line>";
			if (flag)
			{
				text += _atstart.GenXaml(bCurrent, _stroke);
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				text += _atend.GenXaml(bCurrent, _stroke);
			}
			if (bCurrent && (flag || flag2))
			{
				text += "</Canvas>";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			text += "<line ";
			text = text + "x1=\"" + _x1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y1=\"" + _y1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "x2=\"" + _x2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y2=\"" + _y2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text = text + "style=\"stroke:" + ConvBinding(_stroke) + ";\" ";
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<Line";
			text = text + " X1=\"" + _wx1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y1=\"" + _wy1.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " X2=\"" + _wx2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y2=\"" + _wy2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += "/>";
			if (_atstart != null && _atstart.IsVisible)
			{
				text += _atstart.GenXmlText();
			}
			if (_atend != null && _atend.IsVisible)
			{
				text += _atend.GenXmlText();
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			if (xml.Contains("<Arrow"))
			{
				xml = "<Canvas>" + xml + "</Canvas>";
			}
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode xmlNode = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			if (xmlNode.Name == "Canvas")
			{
				xmlNode = xmlNode.SelectSingleNode("Line");
			}
			_wx1 = GetDouble(xmlNode, "X1");
			_wy1 = GetDouble(xmlNode, "Y1");
			_wx2 = GetDouble(xmlNode, "X2");
			_wy2 = GetDouble(xmlNode, "Y2");
			string text = GetString(xmlNode, "Stroke");
			if (text != null)
			{
				_stroke = text;
			}
			string text2 = GetString(xmlNode, "StrokeThickness");
			if (text2 != null)
			{
				_strokethickness = text2;
			}
			string text3 = GetString(xmlNode, "StrokeDashArray");
			if (text3 == null)
			{
				_strokedash = "";
			}
			else
			{
				_strokedash = text3;
			}
			if (_atstart != null)
			{
				_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			if (_atend != null)
			{
				_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectNodes("Arrow");
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string text4 = GetString(item, "at");
				if (text4 == "Start")
				{
					if (_atstart == null)
					{
						AddArrow("ArrowAtStart", bAdd: true);
					}
					_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_atstart.ChangeSize(GetString(item, "scale"));
					_atstart.ChangeColor(text);
				}
				else if (text4 == "End")
				{
					if (_atend == null)
					{
						AddArrow("ArrowAtEnd", bAdd: true);
					}
					_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					_atend.ChangeSize(GetString(item, "scale"));
					_atend.ChangeColor(text);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x1;
			y = _y1;
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_x1 < minx)
			{
				minx = _x1;
			}
			if (_x1 > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _x1;
			}
			if (_x2 < minx)
			{
				minx = _x2;
			}
			if (_x2 > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _x2;
			}
			if (_y1 < miny)
			{
				miny = _y1;
			}
			if (_y1 > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _y1;
			}
			if (_y2 < miny)
			{
				miny = _y2;
			}
			if (_y2 > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _y2;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x1 += dx;
			_y1 += dy;
			_x2 += dx;
			_y2 += dy;
			Refresh();
			base.Move(dx, dy);
			if (_atstart != null)
			{
				_atstart.Move(_x1, _y1, _x2, _y2);
			}
			if (_atend != null)
			{
				_atend.Move(_x2, _y2, _x1, _y1);
			}
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)_element;
			if (handle == "1")
			{
				_x1 = x;
				_y1 = y;
				line.X1 = x;
				line.Y1 = y;
			}
			else if (handle == "2")
			{
				_x2 = x;
				_y2 = y;
				line.X2 = x;
				line.Y2 = y;
			}
			else if (handle == "Base")
			{
				double num = x - (_x1 + _x2) / 2.0;
				double num2 = y - (_y1 + _y2) / 2.0;
				_x1 += num;
				_x2 += num;
				_y1 += num2;
				_y2 += num2;
				line.X1 = _x1;
				line.Y1 = _y1;
				line.X2 = _x2;
				line.Y2 = _y2;
			}
			_back.X1 = line.X1;
			_back.X2 = line.X2;
			_back.Y1 = line.Y1;
			_back.Y2 = line.Y2;
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void EndResize(string handle)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)_element;
			_x1 = line.X1;
			_y1 = line.Y1;
			_x2 = line.X2;
			_y2 = line.Y2;
			if (_atstart != null)
			{
				_atstart.Move(_x1, _y1, _x2, _y2);
			}
			if (_atend != null)
			{
				_atend.Move(_x2, _y2, _x1, _y1);
			}
			base.EndResize(handle);
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			Resize(ref handle, x, y);
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.WcToDvc(_wx1, _wy1, out _x1, out _y1);
			coeff.WcToDvc(_wx2, _wy2, out _x2, out _y2);
			Refresh();
			if (_atstart != null)
			{
				_atstart.Move(_x1, _y1, _x2, _y2);
			}
			if (_atend != null)
			{
				_atend.Move(_x2, _y2, _x1, _y1);
			}
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.DvcToWc(_x1, _y1, out _wx1, out _wy1);
			coeff.DvcToWc(_x2, _y2, out _wx2, out _wy2);
		}
	}
	public class Arrow : Line
	{
		public Arrow(UIElement elem)
			: base(elem)
		{
		}
	}
	public class PolyElement : Element
	{
		internal List<System.Windows.Point> _points;

		internal List<System.Windows.Point> _wpoints;

		protected List<XmlNode> _pointnodes;

		protected bool _parametric;

		protected double _minx;

		protected double _miny;

		protected double _maxx;

		protected double _maxy;

		protected double _cx;

		protected double _cy;

		private double _px;

		private double _py;

		private double _qx;

		private double _qy;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse _centerPoint;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Line _dragLine;

		private bool _dragStarted;

		public PolyElement()
		{
		}

		public PolyElement(UIElement element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_points[i].X < minx)
				{
					minx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].X > maxx)
				{
					maxx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y < miny)
				{
					miny = _points[i].Y;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y > maxy)
				{
					maxy = _points[i].Y;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point value = _points[i];
				value.X += dx;
				value.Y += dy;
				_points[i] = value;
			}
			Refresh();
			base.Move(dx, dy);
			if (this is Polyline)
			{
				if (_atstart != null)
				{
					_atstart.Move(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y, _points[1].X, _points[1].Y);
				}
				if (_atend != null)
				{
					int num = _points.Count - 1;
					_atend.Move(_points[num].X, _points[num].Y, _points[num - 1].X, _points[num - 1].Y);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			if (handle == "scale")
			{
				Scale(x, y);
				return;
			}
			if (handle == "rotate")
			{
				Rotate(x, y);
				return;
			}
			if (handle == "center")
			{
				Move(x - _cx, y - _cy);
				return;
			}
			if (handle[0] == 'i')
			{
				int num = int.Parse(handle.Substring(1));
				InsertPoint(num, x, y, deletable: true);
				if (this is PolyCurve)
				{
					x = (_points[num - 1].X + _points[num + 1].X) / 2.0;
					y = (_points[num - 1].Y + _points[num + 1].Y) / 2.0;
					InsertPoint(num + 1, x, y, deletable: false);
					ClearHandles();
				}
				handle = handle.Substring(1);
			}
			int num2 = int.Parse(handle);
			System.Windows.Point value = _points[num2];
			value.X = x;
			value.Y = y;
			_points[num2] = value;
			if (this is PolyCurve)
			{
				if (this is Cloud && num2 == _points.Count - 1)
				{
					System.Windows.Point value2 = _points[0];
					value2.X = x;
					value2.Y = y;
					_points[0] = value2;
				}
				Refresh();
			}
			else
			{
				if (this is Polyline)
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_element;
					polyline.Points[num2] = _points[num2];
				}
				else
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_element;
					polygon.Points[num2] = _points[num2];
				}
				base.EndResize(handle);
			}
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void StartResize(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "rotate")
			{
				_dragStarted = true;
				RotateTransform rotateTransform = new RotateTransform();
				rotateTransform.CenterX = _cx;
				rotateTransform.CenterY = _cy;
				_element.RenderTransform = rotateTransform;
			}
			else if (handle == "scale")
			{
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = new ScaleTransform();
				scaleTransform.CenterX = _cx;
				scaleTransform.CenterY = _cy;
				_element.RenderTransform = scaleTransform;
			}
		}

		public override void EndResize(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "scale" || handle == "rotate")
			{
				if (handle == "rotate")
				{
					_dragStarted = false;
					ShowRotateHandle(show: false);
					RotateTransform rotateTransform = (RotateTransform)_element.RenderTransform;
					double num = rotateTransform.Angle * Math.PI / 180.0;
					_element.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity;
					for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
					{
						System.Windows.Point value = _points[i];
						double num2 = ((value.X != _cx) ? Math.Atan((value.Y - _cy) / (value.X - _cx)) : (Math.PI / 2.0));
						value.X = Math.Cos(num2 + num) / Math.Cos(num2) * (value.X - _cx) + _cx;
						value.Y = Math.Sin(num2 + num) / Math.Sin(num2) * (value.Y - _cy) + _cy;
						_points[i] = value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)_element.RenderTransform;
					double scaleX = scaleTransform.ScaleX;
					double scaleY = scaleTransform.ScaleY;
					_element.RenderTransform = Transform.Identity;
					for (int j = 0; j < _points.Count; j++)
					{
						System.Windows.Point value2 = _points[j];
						value2.X = (value2.X - _cx) * scaleX + _cx;
						value2.Y = (value2.Y - _cy) * scaleY + _cy;
						_points[j] = value2;
					}
				}
				if (this is PolyCurve)
				{
					Refresh();
				}
				else if (this is Polyline)
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_element;
					for (int k = 0; k < _points.Count; k++)
					{
						polyline.Points[k] = _points[k];
					}
				}
				else
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_element;
					for (int l = 0; l < _points.Count; l++)
					{
						polygon.Points[l] = _points[l];
					}
				}
			}
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public void ShowRotateHandle(bool show)
		{
			if (_dragStarted)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (show)
			{
				if (_centerPoint == null)
				{
					_centerPoint = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
					_centerPoint.Width = 4.0;
					_centerPoint.Height = 4.0;
					_centerPoint.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				}
				Canvas.SetLeft(_centerPoint, _cx - 2.0);
				Canvas.SetTop(_centerPoint, _cy - 2.0);
				if (_dragLine == null)
				{
					_dragLine = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					_dragLine.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
					DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
					doubleCollection.Add(6.0);
					doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
					_dragLine.StrokeDashArray = doubleCollection;
				}
				_dragLine.X1 = _cx;
				_dragLine.Y1 = _cy;
				_dragLine.X2 = _qx;
				_dragLine.Y2 = _qy;
				base.Children.Add(_centerPoint);
				base.Children.Add(_dragLine);
			}
			else
			{
				base.Children.Remove(_dragLine);
				base.Children.Remove(_centerPoint);
			}
		}

		private void InsertPoint(int ith, double x, double y, bool deletable)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
			_points.Insert(ith, point);
			_anchored.Insert(ith, null);
			if (this is Polyline)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_element;
				polyline.Points.Insert(ith, point);
			}
			else if (this is Polygon)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_element;
				polygon.Points.Insert(ith, point);
			}
			if (_datanode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlElement xmlElement = _datanode.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("point");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", "Point");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "x", "0");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "y", "0");
			if (this is PolyCurve)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "delete", deletable ? "Visible" : "Collapsed");
			}
			else if (_pointnodes.Count <= 2)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _pointnodes.Count; i++)
				{
					_pointnodes[i].Attributes["delete"].Value = "Visible";
				}
			}
			_datanode.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			_pointnodes.Add(xmlElement);
			RefreshPointNodes();
		}

		private void Scale(double x, double y)
		{
			if (!(x <= _cx + 0.01) && !(y <= _cy + 0.01))
			{
				double scaleX = (x - _cx) / (_px - _cx);
				double scaleY = (y - _cy) / (_py - _cy);
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)_element.RenderTransform;
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = scaleX;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = scaleY;
			}
		}

		private void Rotate(double x, double y)
		{
			_dragLine.X2 = x;
			_dragLine.Y2 = y;
			double num;
			if (x == _cx)
			{
				num = ((y < _cy) ? (-90.0) : 90.0);
			}
			else
			{
				num = Math.Atan((y - _cy) / (x - _cx)) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
				if (x < _cx)
				{
					num = 180.0 + num;
				}
			}
			RotateTransform rotateTransform = (RotateTransform)_element.RenderTransform;
			rotateTransform.Angle = num;
		}

		protected virtual void Refresh()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void GetDimension()
		{
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_datanode != null && !_parametric)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = _pointnodes[i];
					xmlNode.Attributes["x"].Value = (_points[i].X + dx).ToString();
					xmlNode.Attributes["y"].Value = (_points[i].Y + dy).ToString();
				}
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			string[] array = name.Split('.');
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result);
			int index = int.Parse(array[1]);
			System.Windows.Point value2 = _points[index];
			if (array[0] == "PointX")
			{
				value2.X = result;
			}
			else
			{
				value2.Y = result;
			}
			_points[index] = value2;
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			return "";
		}

		public override void DeletePoint(XmlNode node0)
		{
			if (_pointnodes.Count <= 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode pointnode in _pointnodes)
			{
				if (pointnode == node0)
				{
					_pointnodes.Remove(pointnode);
					pointnode.ParentNode.RemoveChild(pointnode);
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			for (int i = num; i < _pointnodes.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _pointnodes[i];
				xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value = "P" + i;
			}
			if (_pointnodes.Count <= 2)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < _pointnodes.Count; j++)
				{
					if (_pointnodes[j].Attributes["delete"] == null)
					{
						AddAttribute(_pointnodes[j], "delete", "Collapsed");
					}
					else
					{
						_pointnodes[j].Attributes["delete"].Value = "Collapsed";
					}
				}
			}
			RemovePoint(num);
		}

		protected virtual void RemovePoint(int ith)
		{
			_ = _points.Count;
			_points.RemoveAt(ith);
			_anchored.RemoveAt(ith);
			ClearHandles();
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public void AddPoint(System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			_points.Add(p);
			_anchored.Add(null);
			XmlElement xmlElement = _datanode.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("point");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", "Point");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "x", "0");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "y", "0");
			if (this is PolyCurve)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "delete", "Visible");
			}
			_datanode.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			_pointnodes.Add(xmlElement);
			RefreshPointNodes();
		}

		public void DeletePoint(System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			_points.Remove(p);
			XmlNode xmlNode = _pointnodes[_pointnodes.Count - 1];
			_pointnodes.Remove(xmlNode);
			_datanode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
			RefreshPointNodes();
			ClearHandles();
		}

		public void DeletePoint(string id)
		{
			if (base.Control != null)
			{
				base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, this);
			}
			int num = int.Parse(id);
			if (_pointnodes != null)
			{
				XmlNode node = _pointnodes[num];
				DeletePoint(node);
			}
			else
			{
				RemovePoint(num);
			}
		}

		public void RefreshPointNodes()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _pointnodes[i];
				xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value = "P" + i;
				xmlNode.Attributes["x"].Value = _points[i].X.ToString();
				xmlNode.Attributes["y"].Value = _points[i].Y.ToString();
				if (this is PolyCurve)
				{
					string value = "Visible";
					if (_points.Count <= 3 || i % 2 == 1)
					{
						value = "Collapsed";
					}
					xmlNode.Attributes["delete"].Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public override bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = 0.0;
			y = 0.0;
			if (_points.Count > 0)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
				{
					x += _points[i].X;
					y += _points[i].Y;
				}
				x /= _points.Count;
				y /= _points.Count;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				double num = _points[i].X;
				double num2 = _points[i].Y;
				switch (command)
				{
				case "RotLeft":
				case "RotRight":
				{
					double num3 = num - cx;
					double num4 = num2 - cy;
					if (command == "RotLeft")
					{
						num = cx + num4;
						num2 = cy - num3;
					}
					else
					{
						num = cx - num4;
						num2 = cy + num3;
					}
					break;
				}
				case "FlipX":
					num = cx - (num - cx);
					break;
				case "FlipY":
					num2 = cy - (num2 - cy);
					break;
				}
				System.Windows.Point value = _points[i];
				value.X = num;
				value.Y = num2;
				_points[i] = value;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			GetDimension();
			_cx = (_minx + _maxx) / 2.0;
			_cy = (_miny + _maxy) / 2.0;
			if (!(this is Cloud))
			{
				_px = _maxx + (_maxx - _cx) * 0.2;
				_py = _maxy + (_maxy - _cy) * 0.2;
				_qx = _maxx + (_maxx - _cx) * 0.5;
				_qy = _cy;
				AddHandle("scale", _px, _py, null, 4);
				AddHandle("rotate", _qx, _qy, null, 8);
				AddHandle("center", _cx, _cy, null, 16);
			}
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			if (_wpoints != null)
			{
				int count = _wpoints.Count;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					coeff.WcToDvc(_wpoints[i].X, _wpoints[i].Y, out var xx, out var yy);
					System.Windows.Point value = _points[i];
					value.X = xx;
					value.Y = yy;
					_points[i] = value;
				}
				Refresh();
				RefreshHandles();
			}
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			if (_wpoints != null)
			{
				int count = _wpoints.Count;
				for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
				{
					coeff.DvcToWc(_points[i].X, _points[i].Y, out var x, out var y);
					System.Windows.Point value = _wpoints[i];
					value.X = x;
					value.Y = y;
					_wpoints[i] = value;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class PolyCurve : PolyElement
	{
		private string _data;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _back;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _path;

		public PolyCurve()
		{
		}

		public PolyCurve(System.Windows.Shapes.Path path)
			: base(path)
		{
			ExtractAnchors();
			InitializeAnchored(_points.Count);
			AddBack();
		}

		public PolyCurve(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline elem)
		{
			Initialize(elem);
		}

		protected void Initialize(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline elem)
		{
			_path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			int num = elem.Points.Count;
			if ((num & 1) == 0)
			{
				num--;
			}
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point item = new System.Windows.Point
				{
					X = elem.Points[i].X,
					Y = elem.Points[i].Y
				};
				_points.Add(item);
			}
			PointsToPath(_path);
			_path.Stroke = elem.Stroke;
			_path.Fill = elem.Fill;
			_path.StrokeThickness = elem.StrokeThickness;
			_path.StrokeDashArray = elem.StrokeDashArray;
			SetElement(_path);
			InitializeAnchored(_points.Count);
			AddBack();
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			_back.Data = path.Data;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		protected void PointsToPath(System.Windows.Shapes.Path path)
		{
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry();
			streamGeometry.FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd;
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				List<System.Windows.Point> list = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
				for (int i = 1; i < _points.Count; i++)
				{
					list.Add(_points[i]);
				}
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, this is Cloud);
				streamGeometryContext.PolyQuadraticBezierTo(list, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			path.Data = streamGeometry;
			if (_back != null)
			{
				_back.Data = streamGeometry;
			}
		}

		private void ExtractAnchors()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = (StreamGeometry)path.Data;
			_data = streamGeometry.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			DataToPoints(_data, world: false);
		}

		private void DataToPoints(string data, bool world)
		{
			string[] token = data.Split(',', 'M', 'Q', ' ');
			int num = token.Length / 2;
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			int ith = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point item = new System.Windows.Point
				{
					X = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat),
					Y = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat)
				};
				_points.Add(item);
			}
		}

		private string NextToken(ref string[] token, ref int ith)
		{
			while (string.IsNullOrEmpty(token[ith]))
			{
				ith++;
			}
			return token[ith++];
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			string fill = GetFill("Path");
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Curve");
			AddElement(xml, "Stroke", "None");
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", MatchProperty(doc, "Fill", fill).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "angleactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "valueactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "xyactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "listheight", "80");
			_pointnodes = new List<XmlNode>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("point");
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", "P" + i);
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "x", _points[i].X.ToString());
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "y", _points[i].Y.ToString());
				string value = "Visible";
				if (i % 2 == 1 || _points.Count <= 3)
				{
					value = "Collapsed";
				}
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "delete", value);
				xml.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				_pointnodes.Add(xmlElement);
			}
			return xml;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			string text = "<Path ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			text = text + " Data=\"" + GetData() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			text += GradientFill("Path");
			return text + "</Path>";
		}

		private string GetData()
		{
			string text = "M " + _points[0].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[0].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			int num = (_points.Count - 1) / 2;
			int index = 1;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				string text2 = text;
				text = text2 + " Q " + _points[index].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[index++].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				string text3 = text;
				text = text3 + " " + _points[index].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[index++].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected override void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			PointsToPath(path);
		}

		protected override void GetDimension()
		{
			_minx = 1E+30;
			_miny = 1E+30;
			_maxx = -1E+30;
			_maxy = -1E+30;
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i += 2)
			{
				if (_points[i].X < _minx)
				{
					_minx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y < _miny)
				{
					_miny = _points[i].Y;
				}
				if (_points[i].X > _maxx)
				{
					_maxx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y > _maxy)
				{
					_maxy = _points[i].Y;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					if (Math.Abs(_points[i].X - num) < 1E-05 && Math.Abs(_points[i].Y - num2) < 1E-05)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (i % 2 == 1)
					{
						num3 = (num + _points[i].X) / 2.0;
						num4 = (num2 + _points[i].Y) / 2.0;
					}
					else
					{
						AddHandle("i" + i, (num3 + (num + _points[i].X) / 2.0) / 2.0, (num4 + (num2 + _points[i].Y) / 2.0) / 2.0, null, 2);
					}
				}
				int mode = 0;
				if (i % 2 == 0 && _points.Count > 3)
				{
					mode = 1;
				}
				AddHandle(i.ToString(), num = _points[i].X, num2 = _points[i].Y, _anchored[i], mode);
			}
			base.AddHandles();
		}

		public override void DeletePoint(XmlNode node0)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode pointnode in _pointnodes)
			{
				if (pointnode == node0)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = pointnode.NextSibling;
					if (xmlNode == null)
					{
						xmlNode = pointnode.PreviousSibling;
						num--;
					}
					_pointnodes.Remove(pointnode);
					pointnode.ParentNode.RemoveChild(pointnode);
					_pointnodes.Remove(xmlNode);
					xmlNode.ParentNode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _pointnodes.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = _pointnodes[i];
				xmlNode2.Attributes["name"].Value = "P" + i;
				if (_pointnodes.Count <= 3)
				{
					xmlNode2.Attributes["delete"].Value = "Collapsed";
				}
			}
			RemovePoint(num);
		}

		protected override void RemovePoint(int ith)
		{
			_ = _points.Count;
			_points.RemoveAt(ith);
			_anchored.RemoveAt(ith);
			_points.RemoveAt(ith);
			_anchored.RemoveAt(ith);
			ClearHandles();
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _wpoints.Count; i++)
			{
				coeff.WcToDvc(_wpoints[i].X, _wpoints[i].Y, out var xx, out var yy);
				_points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(xx, yy));
			}
			PointsToPath(_path);
		}
	}
	public class Cloud : PolyCurve
	{
		private static double[] coords = new double[18]
		{
			-10.0, 0.0, -20.0, -20.0, 0.0, -10.0, 20.0, -20.0, 10.0, 0.0,
			20.0, 20.0, 0.0, 10.0, -20.0, 20.0, -10.0, 0.0
		};

		private Text _text;

		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		public Text Text => _text;

		public Cloud(double x, double y)
		{
			double num = 3.0;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline();
			for (int i = 0; i < coords.Length / 2; i++)
			{
				polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x + num * coords[i * 2], y + num * coords[i * 2 + 1]));
			}
			polyline.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			Initialize(polyline);
		}

		public Cloud(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline)
		{
			Initialize(polyline);
		}

		public void AddText(Text text)
		{
			_text = text;
		}

		public void UpdateText(double x, double y)
		{
			_text.EndMove(x - _text._x, y - _text._y, 0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			_x = x[0];
			_y = y[0];
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			base.Move(dx, dy);
			if (_text != null)
			{
				_text.EndMove(dx, dy, 0.0, 0.0);
			}
		}

		public override void ReplaceElement(UIElement element)
		{
			base.Children.Clear();
			Initialize((System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)element);
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<PolyCurve Stroke=\"" + _stroke + "\" Fill=\"" + _fill + "\" StrokeThickness=\"" + _strokethickness + "\" StrokeDashArray=\"" + _strokedash + "\" >";
			for (int i = 0; i < _wpoints.Count; i++)
			{
				string text2 = text;
				text = text2 + "<point x=\"" + _wpoints[i].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" y=\"" + _wpoints[i].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>";
			}
			text += "</PolyCurve>";
			return text + _text.GenXmlText();
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xml = "<Canvas>" + xml + "</Canvas>";
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode xmlNode = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode("PolyCurve");
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlNode.SelectNodes("point");
			_wpoints = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				double x = GetDouble(item, "x");
				double y = GetDouble(item, "y");
				_wpoints.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
			}
			string text = GetString(xmlNode, "Stroke");
			if (text != null)
			{
				_stroke = text;
			}
			string text2 = GetString(xmlNode, "StrokeThickness");
			if (text2 != null)
			{
				_strokethickness = text2;
			}
			string text3 = GetString(xmlNode, "StrokeDashArray");
			if (text3 == null)
			{
				_strokedash = "";
			}
			else
			{
				_strokedash = text3;
			}
			string text4 = GetString(xmlNode, "Fill");
			if (text4 != null)
			{
				_fill = text4;
			}
			XmlNode node2 = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode("Text");
			_text.UpdateXml(node2);
		}

		protected override void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			PointsToPath(path);
			path.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(ColorFromString(_stroke));
			path.StrokeThickness = double.Parse(_strokethickness, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			string[] array = _strokedash.Split(' ');
			DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					doubleCollection.Add(double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				}
			}
			path.StrokeDashArray = doubleCollection;
			if (_fill == "None")
			{
				path.Fill = null;
			}
			else
			{
				path.Fill = new SolidColorBrush(ColorFromString(_fill));
			}
			_text.Refresh();
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			base.WcToDvc(coeff);
			_text.WcToDvc(coeff);
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			_wpoints = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				coeff.DvcToWc(_points[i].X, _points[i].Y, out var x, out var y);
				_wpoints.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
			}
			_text.DvcToWc(coeff);
		}
	}
	public class ColorSelection : UserControl, IComponentConnector, IStyleConnector
	{
		private XmlDocument _doc;

		private XmlNode _curcolor;

		private bool _fill;

		private bool _nonone;

		private string _color;

		private Element _element;

		internal Grid ColorDef;

		internal Grid GradientDef;

		internal Grid ColorField;

		internal Grid FillField;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string Color
		{
			get
			{
				return _color;
			}
			set
			{
				_color = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Fill
		{
			set
			{
				_fill = value;
			}
		}

		public bool NoNone
		{
			set
			{
				_nonone = value;
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler ColorSelected;

		public ColorSelection()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			base.Loaded += ColorSelection_Loaded;
		}

		private void ColorSelection_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_doc = new XmlDocument();
			_doc.LoadXml("<root></root>");
			PropertyInfo[] properties = typeof(Colors).GetProperties();
			for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
			{
				ColumnDefinition columnDefinition = new ColumnDefinition();
				columnDefinition.Width = new GridLength(20.0);
				ColorField.ColumnDefinitions.Add(columnDefinition);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < 12; j++)
			{
				RowDefinition rowDefinition = new RowDefinition();
				rowDefinition.Height = new GridLength(15.0);
				ColorField.RowDefinitions.Add(rowDefinition);
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (!_nonone)
			{
				CreateColorBox(0, num++, "#FFFFFFFF", "None");
			}
			CreateColorBox(0, num++, "#FF000000", "Default");
			PropertyInfo[] array = properties;
			foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in array)
			{
				CreateColorBox(num2, num, ((System.Windows.Media.Color)propertyInfo.GetValue(null, null)/*cast due to .constrained prefix*/).ToString(), propertyInfo.Name);
				if (++num == 12)
				{
					num = 0;
					num2++;
				}
			}
			if (_fill)
			{
				CreateFillBox(0, 0, "FillBox", "Transparent");
				CreateFillBox(0, 1, "LinearBox", "Horizontal");
				CreateFillBox(0, 2, "LinearBox", "Vertical");
				CreateFillBox(0, 3, "SphereBox", "Sphere");
				CreateFillBox(0, 4, "SphereBox", "Top Half Sphere");
				CreateFillBox(0, 5, "SphereBox", "Bottom Half Sphere");
				CreateFillBox(0, 6, "SphereBox", "Left Half Sphere");
				CreateFillBox(0, 7, "SphereBox", "Right Half Sphere");
			}
		}

		public void SetColor(string color, Element element)
		{
			_element = element;
			if (color == "@Color")
			{
				color = "Default";
			}
			Color = color;
			if (_curcolor != null)
			{
				AddAttribute(_curcolor, "border", "Transparent");
			}
			ScanColor(color);
			if (_curcolor != null && _curcolor.Name == "FillColor")
			{
				ShowGradientDef();
				return;
			}
			ColorDef.DataContext = _curcolor;
			ColorDef.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			GradientDef.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
		}

		private void ScanColor(string color)
		{
			if (color[0] == '%')
			{
				color = color.Substring(1);
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in _doc.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if ((color[0] == '#' && childNode.Attributes["color"] != null && childNode.Attributes["color"].Value == color) || childNode.Attributes["tooltip"].Value == color)
				{
					_curcolor = childNode;
					AddAttribute(childNode, "border", "Red");
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void CreateColorBox(int row, int col, string color, string tooltip)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = _doc.CreateElement("Color");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "color", color);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "tooltip", tooltip);
			if (tooltip == Color)
			{
				_curcolor = xmlElement;
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "border", "Red");
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "border", "Transparent");
			}
			if (tooltip != "None")
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "cross", "Hidden");
			}
			_doc.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			Grid grid = new Grid();
			Grid.SetColumn(grid, col);
			Grid.SetRow(grid, row);
			UserControl userControl = new UserControl();
			userControl.DataContext = xmlElement;
			userControl.Style = base.Resources["ColorBox"] as Style;
			grid.Children.Add(userControl);
			ColorField.Children.Add(grid);
		}

		private void CreateFillBox(int row, int col, string style, string tooltip)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = _doc.CreateElement("FillColor");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "style", style);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "tooltip", tooltip);
			if (tooltip == Color)
			{
				_curcolor = xmlElement;
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "border", "Red");
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "border", "Transparent");
			}
			if (style == "LinearBox")
			{
				if (tooltip == "Horizontal")
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "startpoint", "0.5,0");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "endpoint", "0.5,1");
				}
				else
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "startpoint", "0,0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "endpoint", "1,0.5");
				}
			}
			else if (style == "SphereBox")
			{
				if (tooltip == "Sphere")
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "center", "0.5,0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "rx", "0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "ry", "0.5");
				}
				else if (tooltip.StartsWith("Top"))
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "center", "0.5,1");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "rx", "0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "ry", "1.0");
				}
				else if (tooltip.StartsWith("Bottom"))
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "center", "0.5,0");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "rx", "0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "ry", "1.0");
				}
				else if (tooltip.StartsWith("Left"))
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "center", "1,0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "rx", "1.0");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "ry", "0.5");
				}
				else if (tooltip.StartsWith("Right"))
				{
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "center", "0,0.5");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "rx", "1.0");
					AddAttribute(xmlElement, "ry", "0.5");
				}
			}
			_doc.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			Grid grid = new Grid();
			Grid.SetColumn(grid, col);
			Grid.SetRow(grid, row);
			UserControl userControl = new UserControl();
			userControl.DataContext = xmlElement;
			userControl.Style = base.Resources[style] as Style;
			grid.Children.Add(userControl);
			FillField.Children.Add(grid);
		}

		private void AddAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, string value)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = _doc.CreateAttribute(name);
			node.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			xmlAttribute.Value = value;
		}

		private void ColorDef_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			ColorField.Visibility = (ColorField.IsVisible ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			if (_fill)
			{
				FillField.Visibility = (FillField.IsVisible ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			else
			{
				FillField.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
		}

		private void Border_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Border border = (Border)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)border.DataContext;
			Color = xmlNode.Attributes["color"].Value;
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["tooltip"].Value == "None")
			{
				Color = "None";
			}
			if (_curcolor != null)
			{
				AddAttribute(_curcolor, "border", "Transparent");
			}
			_curcolor = xmlNode;
			AddAttribute(_curcolor, "border", "Red");
			ColorDef.DataContext = xmlNode;
			ColorDef.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
			GradientDef.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			ColorField.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			if (_fill)
			{
				FillField.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (_element != null)
			{
				_element.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, _fill ? HistorySubcode.Fill : HistorySubcode.Stroke, _element);
				_element.ChangeProperty(_fill ? "Fill" : "Stroke", Color, null, null);
			}
			if (this.ColorSelected != null)
			{
				this.ColorSelected(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void FillBox_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Border border = (Border)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)border.DataContext;
			Color = xmlNode.Attributes["tooltip"].Value;
			if (Color == "Transparent")
			{
				Color = "#00FFFFFF";
			}
			if (_curcolor != null)
			{
				AddAttribute(_curcolor, "border", "Transparent");
			}
			_curcolor = xmlNode;
			AddAttribute(_curcolor, "border", "Red");
			ShowGradientDef();
			ColorField.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			if (_fill)
			{
				FillField.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (_element != null)
			{
				_element.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.Fill, _element);
				_element.ChangeProperty("Fill", Color, null, null);
			}
			if (this.ColorSelected != null)
			{
				this.ColorSelected(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void ShowGradientDef()
		{
			Border border = (Border)GradientDef.Children[0];
			UserControl userControl = new UserControl();
			XmlNode xmlNode = _doc.ImportNode(_curcolor, deep: true);
			xmlNode.Attributes["border"].Value = "Transparent";
			userControl.DataContext = xmlNode;
			string value = _curcolor.Attributes["style"].Value;
			userControl.Style = base.Resources[value] as Style;
			border.Child = userControl;
			ColorDef.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			GradientDef.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
		}

		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/IconCtrl;component/colorselection.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 5:
				ColorDef = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += ColorDef_MouseDown;
				break;
			case 7:
				GradientDef = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 8:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += ColorDef_MouseDown;
				break;
			case 9:
				ColorField = (Grid)target;
				break;
			case 10:
				FillField = (Grid)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		void IStyleConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += Border_MouseDown;
				break;
			case 2:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += FillBox_MouseDown;
				break;
			case 3:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += FillBox_MouseDown;
				break;
			case 4:
				((Border)target).MouseDown += FillBox_MouseDown;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ColorSelector : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		private List<string> _colornames;

		internal ListView ColorList;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public string Color { get; set; }

		public ColorSelector()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_colornames = new List<string>();
			base.Loaded += ColorSelector_Loaded;
		}

		private void ColorSelector_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			PropertyInfo[] properties = typeof(Colors).GetProperties();
			ColorList.ItemsSource = properties;
			PropertyInfo[] array = properties;
			foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in array)
			{
				_ = (System.Windows.Media.Color)propertyInfo.GetValue(null, null);
				_colornames.Add(propertyInfo.Name);
			}
		}

		private void ColorList_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ListView listView = (ListView)sender;
			int selectedIndex = listView.SelectedIndex;
			if (selectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				Color = _colornames[selectedIndex];
			}
			Close();
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			ColorList.SelectedItem = null;
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/IconCtrl;component/colorselector.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			if (connectionId == 1)
			{
				ColorList = (ListView)target;
				ColorList.SelectionChanged += ColorList_SelectionChanged;
			}
			else
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
			}
		}
	}
	public class Command : BaseObj
	{
		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private Element _element;

		private string _eventname;

		private string _command;

		private Menu _menu;

		private XmlNode _datanode;

		public XmlNode DataNode => _datanode;

		public Menu Menu => _menu;

		public Command(string type, IconCtrl parent)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			if (type == "Menu")
			{
				_menu = new Menu();
			}
		}

		public Command(XmlNode node, IconCtrl parent, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			string text = GetString(node, "Element");
			Element element = null;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				element = parent.FindElement(text);
			}
			_element = element;
			_eventname = GetString(node, "Event");
			_command = GetString(node, "Command");
			if (parent.OnPfs)
			{
				SetupEventHandler();
			}
			if (node.Name == "Menu")
			{
				_menu = new Menu();
				SetupCommandList(node, nsmgr);
			}
		}

		public void Update(XmlNode refnode, string property, string value)
		{
			if (refnode == _datanode)
			{
				switch (property)
				{
				case "ElemName":
					_element = _parent.FindElement(value);
					break;
				case "EventName":
					_eventname = value;
					break;
				case "Command":
					_command = value;
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (_menu == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				MenuItem menuItem = _menu.FindItem(refnode);
				if (menuItem != null)
				{
					if (property == "Header")
					{
						menuItem.Header = value;
					}
					else if (property == "Command")
					{
						menuItem.Name = value;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void AddMenuItem(XmlNode refnode)
		{
			if (_menu != null)
			{
				MenuItem item = new MenuItem();
				_menu.AddItem(item);
				XmlNode itemXml = _menu.GetItemXml(_datanode.OwnerDocument, item);
				refnode.AppendChild(itemXml);
			}
		}

		private void SetupEventHandler()
		{
			switch (_eventname)
			{
			case "MouseLeftButtonDown":
			case "MouseDoubleClick":
				if (_element == null)
				{
					_parent.MouseLeftButtonDown += _element_MouseLeftButtonDown;
				}
				else
				{
					_element.MouseLeftButtonDown += _element_MouseLeftButtonDown;
				}
				break;
			case "MouseRightButtonDown":
				if (_element == null)
				{
					_parent.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown += _element_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
				}
				else
				{
					_element.PreviewMouseRightButtonDown += _element_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void _element_PreviewMouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_command == null && _menu != null)
			{
				_parent.ShowMenu(_menu, _element);
			}
		}

		private void _element_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_command == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_eventname == "MouseDoubleClick")
			{
				if (e.ClickCount > 1)
				{
					_parent.FireEvent("Command", _command);
				}
			}
			else if (e.ClickCount == 1)
			{
				_parent.FireEvent("Command", _command);
			}
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement((_menu == null) ? "Command" : "Menu", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "Element", (_element == null) ? "" : _element.Name);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "Event", _eventname);
			if (_menu == null)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "Command", _command);
			}
			else
			{
				_menu.Save(xmlNode, nsmgr);
			}
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
		}

		private void SetupCommandList(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:MenuItem", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNodeList.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem();
				menuItem.Header = item.Attributes["Header"].Value;
				if (item.HasChildNodes)
				{
					SetupSubmenu(menuItem, item, nsmgr);
				}
				else
				{
					if (item.Attributes["Command"] != null)
					{
						menuItem.Name = item.Attributes["Command"].Value;
					}
					menuItem.Click += item_Click;
				}
				_menu.AddItem(menuItem);
			}
		}

		private void SetupSubmenu(MenuItem parent, XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:MenuItem", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNodeList.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem();
				menuItem.Header = item.Attributes["Header"].Value;
				if (item.HasChildNodes)
				{
					SetupSubmenu(menuItem, item, nsmgr);
				}
				else
				{
					if (item.Attributes["Command"] != null)
					{
						menuItem.Name = item.Attributes["Command"].Value;
					}
					menuItem.Click += item_Click;
				}
				parent.Items.Add(menuItem);
			}
			parent.MouseEnter += item_MouseEnter;
			parent.MouseLeave += item_MouseLeave;
		}

		private void item_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			if (menuItem.HasItems)
			{
				menuItem.IsSubmenuOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void item_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			if (menuItem.HasItems)
			{
				menuItem.IsSubmenuOpen = true;
			}
		}

		private void item_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(menuItem.Name))
			{
				string name = menuItem.Name;
				_parent.FireEvent("Command", name);
				_parent.HideMenu();
			}
		}

		public XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string text = ((_menu == null) ? "Command" : "Menu");
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement(text);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "type", text);
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "element", (_element == null) ? "" : _element.Name);
			int num = -1;
			if (_eventname == "MouseLeftButtonDown")
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			else if (_eventname == "MouseRightButtonDown")
			{
				num = 1;
			}
			else if (_eventname == "MouseDoubleClick")
			{
				num = 2;
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "eventindex", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "command", _command);
			if (_menu != null)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "commandvisible", "Collapsed");
				_menu.GetXml(doc, xmlElement);
			}
			_datanode = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}
	}
	public class Cursor : BaseObj
	{
		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private double _x0;

		private double _y0;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _back;

		private bool _indrag;

		public Cursor(double x, double y, IconCtrl parent)
		{
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_parent = parent;
			base.MouseDown += Cursor_MouseDown;
		}

		private void Cursor_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_indrag && _parent.DragCursor(this))
			{
				_x0 = _x;
				_y0 = _y;
				_indrag = true;
			}
		}

		public void Render(bool visible)
		{
			base.ToolTip = "Cursor";
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y));
			base.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, -6.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, -6.0);
			_back.Width = 12.0;
			_back.Height = 12.0;
			_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Add(_back);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon();
			polygon.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue;
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-6.0, 0.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-1.0, 1.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 6.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(1.0, 1.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(6.0, 0.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(1.0, -1.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, -6.0));
			polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-1.0, -1.0));
			base.Children.Add(polygon);
			if (!visible)
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			Panel.SetZIndex(this, 120);
		}

		public void Relocate(double x, double y)
		{
			_tform.X = x;
			_tform.Y = y;
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x0 += dx;
			_y0 += dy;
			_tform.X = _x0;
			_tform.Y = _y0;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public void EndMove(out double dx, out double dy)
		{
			_indrag = false;
			dx = _x0 - _x;
			dy = _y0 - _y;
			Relocate(_x0, _y0);
			_x0 = 0.0;
			_y0 = 0.0;
		}
	}
	public class IconField
	{
		private bool _kbase;

		private string _xaml;

		private XmlNode _rootnode;

		private XmlNamespaceManager _nsmgr;

		private XmlNode _rangenode;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _path;

		private System.Windows.Media.Pen _pen;

		private List<Rect> _rects;

		private double _minx;

		private double _maxx;

		private double _miny;

		private double _maxy;

		public List<Rect> Rects => _rects;

		public string Xaml => _xaml;

		public IconField(bool kbase, XmlNode rootnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			_kbase = kbase;
			_rootnode = rootnode;
			_nsmgr = nsmgr;
			_rects = new List<Rect>();
		}

		public void Load()
		{
			_rangenode = _rootnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Range", _nsmgr);
			_minx = GetDouble(_rangenode, "MinX");
			_maxy = 0.0 - GetDouble(_rangenode, "MinY");
			_maxx = GetDouble(_rangenode, "MaxX");
			_miny = 0.0 - GetDouble(_rangenode, "MaxY");
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = _rootnode.SelectNodes("y:Field", _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNodeList.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNodeList)
				{
					double num = GetDouble(item2, "MinX");
					double num2 = 0.0 - GetDouble(item2, "MinY");
					double num3 = GetDouble(item2, "MaxX");
					double num4 = 0.0 - GetDouble(item2, "MaxY");
					Rect item = new Rect(num, num4, num3 - num, num2 - num4);
					_rects.Add(item);
				}
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = _rootnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Xaml", _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				_xaml = xmlNode.InnerText;
				while (_xaml.IndexOf("<!-- Gradient") >= 0)
				{
					int num5 = _xaml.IndexOf("<!-- Gradient");
					int num6 = _xaml.Substring(num5).IndexOf("-->") + num5;
					string text = "<SolidColorBrush Color=\"LightGray\"/>";
					_xaml = _xaml.Substring(0, num5) + text + _xaml.Substring(num6 + 3);
				}
			}
		}

		public string FieldNodes()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = text;
			text = text2 + "<Range MinX=\"" + _minx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MinY=\"\"" + (0.0 - _maxy).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MaxX=\"" + _maxx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MaxY=\"" + (0.0 - _miny).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>\r\n";
			foreach (Rect rect in _rects)
			{
				string text3 = text;
				text = text3 + "<Field MinX=\"" + rect.Left.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MinY=\"\"" + (0.0 - rect.Bottom).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MaxX=\"" + rect.Right.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" MaxY=\"" + (0.0 - rect.Top).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>\r\n";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void SaveFields(XmlDocument doc, string ns)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = _rootnode.SelectNodes("y:Field", _nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				_rootnode.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			_rangenode.Attributes["MinX"].Value = _minx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			_rangenode.Attributes["MinY"].Value = (0.0 - _maxy).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			_rangenode.Attributes["MaxX"].Value = _maxx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			_rangenode.Attributes["MaxY"].Value = (0.0 - _miny).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			XmlNode refChild = _rangenode;
			foreach (Rect rect in _rects)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("Field", ns);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = doc.CreateAttribute("MinX");
				xmlAttribute.Value = rect.Left.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				xmlAttribute = doc.CreateAttribute("MinY");
				xmlAttribute.Value = (0.0 - rect.Bottom).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				xmlAttribute = doc.CreateAttribute("MaxX");
				xmlAttribute.Value = rect.Right.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				xmlAttribute = doc.CreateAttribute("MaxY");
				xmlAttribute.Value = (0.0 - rect.Top).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				_rootnode.InsertAfter(xmlElement, refChild);
				refChild = xmlElement;
			}
		}

		private double GetDouble(XmlNode node, string attrib)
		{
			if (node.Attributes[attrib] == null)
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			return double.Parse(node.Attributes[attrib].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
		}

		public void CalcFields()
		{
			_rects.Clear();
			_pen = new System.Windows.Media.Pen(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black, 0.01);
			double num;
			double num2;
			if (_kbase)
			{
				num = 0.0 - GetDouble(_rangenode, "MinY");
				num2 = 0.0 - GetDouble(_rangenode, "MaxY");
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = GetDouble(_rangenode, "MinY");
				num = GetDouble(_rangenode, "MaxY");
			}
			GenPathGeometry(_xaml);
			double num3 = 1E+20;
			double num4 = 1E+20;
			double num5 = -1E+20;
			double num6 = -1E+20;
			double num7 = 0.0;
			double num8 = 0.0;
			int num9 = 0;
			int num10 = 0;
			double num11 = 0.0;
			double num12 = 0.0;
			double minx = 0.0;
			double maxx = 0.0;
			double num13 = 1.0;
			if (num - num2 < 20.0)
			{
				num13 = 0.5;
			}
			int num14 = 6;
			for (double num15 = num2 - 20.0; num15 <= num + 20.0; num15 += num13)
			{
				minx = 1000.0;
				maxx = -1000.0;
				if (!GetIntersection(-1000.0, num15, 1000.0, num15, ref minx, ref maxx, ypos: false))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (minx < num3)
				{
					num3 = minx;
				}
				if (maxx > num5)
				{
					num5 = maxx;
				}
				if (num15 < num4)
				{
					num4 = num15;
				}
				if (num15 > num6)
				{
					num6 = num15;
				}
				if (num9 == 0)
				{
					num7 = minx;
					num8 = maxx;
					num11 = num15;
					num10 = num9;
				}
				else if ((num7 != minx || num8 != maxx) && (Math.Abs(num8 - num7 - (maxx - minx)) > num13 * 10.0 || num9 - num10 >= num14))
				{
					Rect item;
					if (maxx - minx > num8 - num7)
					{
						if (num9 - num10 > num14)
						{
							num15 -= num13;
							item = new Rect(num7, num11, num8 - num7, num15 - num11);
						}
						else
						{
							item = new Rect(minx, num11, maxx - minx, num15 - num11);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						item = new Rect(num7, num11, num8 - num7, num15 - num11);
					}
					_rects.Add(item);
					num7 = minx;
					num8 = maxx;
					num11 = num15;
					num10 = num9;
				}
				num12 = num15;
				num9++;
			}
			if (num9 > 0)
			{
				_minx = num3;
				_maxx = num5;
				_miny = num4;
				_maxy = num6;
			}
			if (Math.Abs(num12 - num11) > 0.01)
			{
				if (num12 < num)
				{
					num12 = num;
				}
				Rect item2 = ((!(maxx - minx > num8 - num7)) ? new Rect(num7, num11, num8 - num7, num12 - num11) : new Rect(minx, num11, maxx - minx, num12 - num11));
				_rects.Add(item2);
			}
		}

		private bool GetIntersection(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, ref double minx, ref double maxx, bool ypos)
		{
			LineGeometry lineGeometry = new LineGeometry();
			lineGeometry.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x1, y1);
			lineGeometry.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x2, y2);
			PathGeometry widenedPathGeometry = _path.Data.GetWidenedPathGeometry(_pen);
			PathGeometry widenedPathGeometry2 = lineGeometry.GetWidenedPathGeometry(_pen);
			CombinedGeometry combinedGeometry = new CombinedGeometry(GeometryCombineMode.Intersect, widenedPathGeometry, widenedPathGeometry2);
			PathGeometry flattenedPathGeometry = combinedGeometry.GetFlattenedPathGeometry();
			if (flattenedPathGeometry.Figures.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < flattenedPathGeometry.Figures.Count; i++)
			{
				Rect bounds = new PathGeometry(new PathFigure[1] { flattenedPathGeometry.Figures[i] }).Bounds;
				if (ypos)
				{
					if (bounds.Top < minx)
					{
						minx = bounds.Top;
					}
					if (bounds.Bottom > maxx)
					{
						maxx = bounds.Bottom;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (bounds.Left < minx)
					{
						minx = bounds.Left;
					}
					if (bounds.Right > maxx)
					{
						maxx = bounds.Right;
					}
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void GenPathGeometry(string xamlcode)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml("<root>" + xamlcode + "</root>");
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				switch (childNode.Name)
				{
				case "Polygon":
					AddPolygon(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				case "Polyline":
					AddPolygon(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				case "Rectangle":
					AddRectangle(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				case "Line":
					AddLine(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				case "Ellipse":
					AddEllipse(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				case "Path":
					AddPath(geometryGroup, childNode);
					break;
				}
			}
			_path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			_path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			_path.StrokeThickness = 0.2;
			_path.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
			_path.Data = geometryGroup;
		}

		private void AddLine(GeometryGroup gg, XmlNode root)
		{
			LineGeometry lineGeometry = new LineGeometry();
			double x = double.Parse(root.Attributes["X1"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double y = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Y1"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double x2 = double.Parse(root.Attributes["X2"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double y2 = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Y2"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			lineGeometry.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
			lineGeometry.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x2, y2);
			gg.Children.Add(lineGeometry);
		}

		private void AddRectangle(GeometryGroup gg, XmlNode root)
		{
			RectangleGeometry rectangleGeometry = new RectangleGeometry();
			double x = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Canvas.Left"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double y = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Canvas.Top"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double width = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Width"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double height = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Height"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			rectangleGeometry.Rect = new Rect(x, y, width, height);
			gg.Children.Add(rectangleGeometry);
		}

		private void AddEllipse(GeometryGroup gg, XmlNode root)
		{
			EllipseGeometry ellipseGeometry = new EllipseGeometry();
			double num = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Canvas.Left"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double num2 = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Canvas.Top"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double num3 = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Width"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) / 2.0;
			double num4 = double.Parse(root.Attributes["Height"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) / 2.0;
			ellipseGeometry.Center = new System.Windows.Point(num + num3, num2 + num4);
			ellipseGeometry.RadiusX = num3;
			ellipseGeometry.RadiusY = num4;
			gg.Children.Add(ellipseGeometry);
		}

		private void AddPolygon(GeometryGroup gg, XmlNode root)
		{
			string value = root.Attributes["Points"].Value;
			string[] array = value.Split(' ');
			double x = 0.0;
			double y = 0.0;
			int num = 0;
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string[] array3 = text.Split(',');
					double num2 = double.Parse(array3[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					double num3 = double.Parse(array3[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					if (num++ > 0)
					{
						LineGeometry lineGeometry = new LineGeometry();
						lineGeometry.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
						lineGeometry.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(num2, num3);
						gg.Children.Add(lineGeometry);
					}
					x = num2;
					y = num3;
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddPath(GeometryGroup gg, XmlNode root)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(root, deep: true);
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["Visibility"] != null)
			{
				xmlNode.Attributes.RemoveNamedItem("Visibility");
			}
			string outerXml = xmlNode.OuterXml;
			string text = "<Canvas xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\">";
			text += outerXml;
			text += "</Canvas>";
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
			streamWriter.Write(text);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			Canvas canvas = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream) as Canvas;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = canvas.Children[0] as System.Windows.Shapes.Path;
			Geometry data = path.Data;
			gg.Children.Add(data);
		}
	}
	public class IconReference : BaseObj
	{
		private string _name;

		private string _iconname;

		private bool _default;

		private bool _current;

		private XmlNode _iconnode;

		public string ID => _name;

		public bool IsDefault => _default;

		public bool IsCurrent
		{
			get
			{
				return _current;
			}
			set
			{
				_current = value;
			}
		}

		public XmlNode IconNode => _iconnode;

		public IconReference(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			_name = GetString(node, "name");
			_iconname = GetString(node, "icon");
			_default = node.Attributes["default"] != null;
			XmlNode parentNode = node.ParentNode.ParentNode;
			XmlNode xmlNode = parentNode.SelectSingleNode("y:Icon[@name='" + _iconname + "']", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				_iconnode = xmlNode;
			}
		}
	}
	public class Menu : Grid
	{
		private List<MenuItem> _items;

		private StackPanel _panel;

		public Menu()
		{
			_items = new List<MenuItem>();
			Border border = new Border
			{
				Background = new SolidColorBrush(System.Windows.Media.Color.FromRgb(240, 240, 240)),
				BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black,
				BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0)
			};
			base.Children.Add(border);
			_panel = new StackPanel();
			_panel.Orientation = Orientation.Vertical;
			border.Child = _panel;
		}

		public void AddItem(MenuItem item)
		{
			_items.Add(item);
			_panel.Children.Add(item);
		}

		public void DeleteItem(XmlNode node)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = FindItem(node);
			if (menuItem != null)
			{
				if (menuItem.Parent is MenuItem)
				{
					((MenuItem)menuItem.Parent).Items.Remove(menuItem);
				}
				else
				{
					_items.Remove(menuItem);
				}
				node.ParentNode.RemoveChild(node);
			}
		}

		public void ChangeItemOrder(XmlNode node, bool up)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = FindItem(node);
			if (menuItem == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (menuItem.Parent is MenuItem)
			{
				MenuItem menuItem2 = (MenuItem)menuItem.Parent;
				int num = menuItem2.Items.IndexOf(menuItem);
				if (up && num > 0)
				{
					menuItem2.Items.Remove(menuItem);
					menuItem2.Items.Insert(num - 1, menuItem);
				}
				else if (!up && num < menuItem2.Items.Count - 1)
				{
					menuItem2.Items.Remove(menuItem);
					menuItem2.Items.Insert(num + 1, menuItem);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = _items.IndexOf(menuItem);
				if (up && num2 > 0)
				{
					_items.Remove(menuItem);
					_items.Insert(num2 - 1, menuItem);
				}
				else if (!up && num2 < _items.Count - 1)
				{
					_items.Remove(menuItem);
					_items.Insert(num2 + 1, menuItem);
				}
			}
			XmlNode parentNode = node.ParentNode;
			if (up)
			{
				XmlNode previousSibling = node.PreviousSibling;
				if (previousSibling != null)
				{
					parentNode.RemoveChild(node);
					parentNode.InsertBefore(node, previousSibling);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				XmlNode nextSibling = node.NextSibling;
				if (nextSibling != null)
				{
					parentNode.RemoveChild(node);
					parentNode.InsertAfter(node, nextSibling);
				}
			}
		}

		public void GetXml(XmlDocument doc, XmlNode root)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in _items)
			{
				XmlElement itemXml = GetItemXml(doc, item);
				root.AppendChild(itemXml);
			}
		}

		public XmlElement GetItemXml(XmlDocument doc, MenuItem item)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("MenuItem");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "type", "MenuItem");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "header", (string)item.Header);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Name))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "command", item.Name);
			}
			if (item.HasItems)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "commandvisible", "Collapsed");
				GetSubmenuXml(doc, xmlElement, item);
			}
			item.Tag = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}

		private void GetSubmenuXml(XmlDocument doc, XmlNode root, MenuItem rootitem)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in (IEnumerable)rootitem.Items)
			{
				XmlElement itemXml = GetItemXml(doc, item);
				root.AppendChild(itemXml);
			}
		}

		public MenuItem FindItem(XmlNode node)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in _items)
			{
				if ((XmlNode)item.Tag == node)
				{
					return item;
				}
				if (item.HasItems)
				{
					MenuItem menuItem = FindSubmenuItem(node, item);
					if (menuItem != null)
					{
						return menuItem;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private MenuItem FindSubmenuItem(XmlNode node, MenuItem rootitem)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in (IEnumerable)rootitem.Items)
			{
				if ((XmlNode)item.Tag == node)
				{
					return item;
				}
				if (item.HasItems)
				{
					MenuItem menuItem2 = FindSubmenuItem(node, item);
					if (menuItem2 != null)
					{
						return menuItem2;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in _items)
			{
				XmlNode newChild = SaveItem(root, nsmgr, item);
				root.AppendChild(newChild);
			}
		}

		private XmlNode SaveItem(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, MenuItem item)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("MenuItem", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "Header", (string)item.Header);
			if (item.HasItems)
			{
				SaveSubmenu(xmlNode, nsmgr, item);
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Name))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "Command", item.Name);
			}
			return xmlNode;
		}

		private void SaveSubmenu(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, MenuItem rootitem)
		{
			foreach (MenuItem item in (IEnumerable)rootitem.Items)
			{
				XmlNode newChild = SaveItem(root, nsmgr, item);
				root.AppendChild(newChild);
			}
		}

		private void AddAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, string value)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes[name];
			if (xmlAttribute == null)
			{
				xmlAttribute = node.OwnerDocument.CreateAttribute(name);
				node.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			}
			xmlAttribute.Value = value;
		}
	}
	public class Path0 : Element
	{
		private Rect _rect;

		private string _xml;

		public Path0(System.Windows.Shapes.Path path, XmlNode node)
			: base(path)
		{
			_xml = node.OuterXml;
			PathGeometry outlinedPathGeometry = path.Data.GetOutlinedPathGeometry();
			_rect = outlinedPathGeometry.Bounds;
			InitializeAnchored(5);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			if (!_rect.IsEmpty)
			{
				AddHandle("NW", _rect.Left, _rect.Top, _anchored[0]);
				AddHandle("NE", _rect.Right, _rect.Top, _anchored[1]);
				AddHandle("SW", _rect.Left, _rect.Bottom, _anchored[2]);
				AddHandle("SE", _rect.Right, _rect.Bottom, _anchored[3]);
			}
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_rect.Left < minx)
			{
				minx = _rect.Left;
			}
			if (_rect.Right > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _rect.Right;
			}
			if (_rect.Top < miny)
			{
				miny = _rect.Top;
			}
			if (_rect.Bottom > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _rect.Bottom;
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Path");
			AddAttribute(xml, "pointactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "valueactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			return xml;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			return _xml.Replace("xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\"", "Tag=\"Generic\"");
		}
	}
	public class Ellipse : Element
	{
		internal double _cx;

		internal double _cy;

		internal double _rx;

		internal double _ry;

		internal double _wcx;

		internal double _wcy;

		internal double _wrx;

		internal double _wry;

		private bool _circle;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse _back;

		public bool Circle
		{
			get
			{
				return _circle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _circle)
				{
					_circle = value;
					if (_circle)
					{
						_ry = (_rx = ((_rx < _ry) ? _ry : _rx));
						Refresh();
						RefreshHandles();
						UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public Ellipse(System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse ellipse)
			: base(ellipse)
		{
			double left = Canvas.GetLeft(ellipse);
			double top = Canvas.GetTop(ellipse);
			double width = ellipse.Width;
			double height = ellipse.Height;
			_rx = width / 2.0;
			_ry = height / 2.0;
			_circle = _rx == _ry;
			_cx = left + _rx;
			_cy = top + _ry;
			AddBack();
			InitializeAnchored(6);
		}

		public Ellipse(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly, bool nomap = false)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse ellipse = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
			double x = poly.Points[0].X;
			double y = poly.Points[0].Y;
			double num = poly.Points[1].X - x;
			double num2 = poly.Points[2].Y - y;
			if (num < 0.0)
			{
				x = poly.Points[1].X;
				num = 0.0 - num;
			}
			if (num2 < 0.0)
			{
				y = poly.Points[2].Y;
				num2 = 0.0 - num2;
			}
			_rx = num / 2.0;
			_ry = num2 / 2.0;
			_circle = _rx == _ry;
			_cx = x + _rx;
			_cy = y + _ry;
			Canvas.SetLeft(ellipse, x);
			Canvas.SetTop(ellipse, y);
			ellipse.Width = num;
			ellipse.Height = num2;
			if (nomap)
			{
				ellipse.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			}
			else
			{
				ellipse.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			SetElement(ellipse);
			AddBack();
			InitializeAnchored(6);
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _cx - _rx - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _cy - _ry - _thickness / 2.0);
			_back.Width = _rx * 2.0 + _thickness;
			_back.Height = _ry * 2.0 + _thickness;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			double num = ((x[0] < x[1]) ? x[0] : x[1]);
			double num2 = ((y[0] > y[1]) ? y[0] : y[1]);
			double num3 = Math.Abs(x[0] - x[1]);
			double num4 = Math.Abs(y[0] - y[1]);
			_wrx = num3 / 2.0;
			_wry = num4 / 2.0;
			_wcx = num + _wrx;
			_wcy = num2 + _wry;
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		public override bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _cx;
			y = _cy;
			return true;
		}

		public override void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
			double cx2 = _cx;
			double cy2 = _cy;
			double num = _cx + _rx;
			double num2 = _cy + _ry;
			switch (command)
			{
			case "RotLeft":
			case "RotRight":
			{
				double num3 = cx2 - cx;
				double num4 = cy2 - cy;
				double num5 = num - cx;
				double num6 = num2 - cy;
				if (command == "RotLeft")
				{
					cx2 = cx + num4;
					cy2 = cy - num3;
					num = cx + num6;
					num2 = cy - num5;
				}
				else
				{
					cx2 = cx - num4;
					cy2 = cy + num3;
					num = cx - num6;
					num2 = cy + num5;
				}
				_cx = cx2;
				_cy = cy2;
				_rx = Math.Abs(num - cx2);
				_ry = Math.Abs(num2 - cy2);
				break;
			}
			case "FlipX":
				_cx = cx - (cx2 - cx);
				break;
			case "FlipY":
				_cy = cy - (cy2 - cy);
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			AddHandle("Top", _cx, _cy - _ry, _anchored[0]);
			AddHandle("Bottom", _cx, _cy + _ry, _anchored[1]);
			AddHandle("Left", _cx - _rx, _cy, _anchored[2]);
			AddHandle("Right", _cx + _rx, _cy, _anchored[3]);
			AddHandle("Center", _cx, _cy, _anchored[4]);
			AddHandle("NE", _cx + _rx, _cy - _ry, _anchored[4]);
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			string fill = GetFill("Ellipse");
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Ellipse");
			int num = MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke);
			int num2 = MatchProperty(doc, "Fill", fill);
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", num2.ToString());
