			AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "angleactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "pointactive", "Collapsed");
			AddPositionNode();
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var1", "CX");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value1", _cx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var2", "CY");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value2", _cy.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var3", "RX");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value3", _rx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var4", "RY");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value4", _ry.ToString());
			xml.Attributes["checkbox2"].Value = "Circle";
			AddAttribute(xml, "check2", _circle.ToString());
			return xml;
		}

		public override void Update(XmlNode node)
		{
			double num = GetDouble(node, "Width");
			double num2 = GetDouble(node, "Height");
			double num3 = GetDouble(node, "Canvas.Left");
			double num4 = GetDouble(node, "Canvas.Top");
			_rx = num / 2.0;
			_ry = num2 / 2.0;
			_cx = num3 + _rx;
			_cy = num4 + _ry;
			Refresh();
			base.Update(node);
		}

		private void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse ellipse = (System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse)_element;
			Canvas.SetLeft(ellipse, _cx - _rx);
			Canvas.SetTop(ellipse, _cy - _ry);
			ellipse.Width = _rx * 2.0;
			ellipse.Height = _ry * 2.0;
			UpdateBack();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_positionnode != null)
			{
				_positionnode.Attributes["value1"].Value = (_cx + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value2"].Value = (_cy + dy).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value3"].Value = _rx.ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value4"].Value = _ry.ToString();
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result);
			if ((name == "Value3" || name == "Value4") && result <= 0.0)
			{
				string text = ((name == "Value3") ? "RX" : "RY");
				UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
				return text + " must be positive.";
			}
			switch (name)
			{
			case "Value1":
				_cx = result;
				break;
			case "Value2":
				_cy = result;
				break;
			case "Value3":
				_rx = result;
				if (_circle)
				{
					_ry = _rx;
				}
				break;
			case "Value4":
				_ry = result;
				if (_circle)
				{
					_rx = _ry;
				}
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			return "";
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			string text = "<Ellipse ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			text = text + " Width=\"" + (_rx * 2.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Height=\"" + (_ry * 2.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Canvas.Top=\"" + (_cy - _ry).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Canvas.Left=\"" + (_cx - _rx).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			text += GradientFill("Ellipse");
			return text + "</Ellipse>";
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			bool flag = _fill.Contains("Sphere");
			text += "<ellipse ";
			text = text + "cx=\"" + _cx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "cy=\"" + _cy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "rx=\"" + _rx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "ry=\"" + _ry.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			if (flag)
			{
				text += "stroke=\"none\" ";
				text += "fill=\"url(#radial)\" ";
			}
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text = text + "style=\"stroke:" + _stroke + ";\" ";
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<Ellipse";
			text = text + " Width=\"" + (_wrx * 2.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Height=\"" + (_wry * 2.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " X=\"" + (_wcx - _wrx).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y=\"" + (_wcy + _wry).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			return text + "</Ellipse>";
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			double num = GetDouble(documentElement, "X");
			double num2 = GetDouble(documentElement, "Y");
			double num3 = GetDouble(documentElement, "Width");
			double num4 = GetDouble(documentElement, "Height");
			_wrx = num3 / 2.0;
			_wry = num4 / 2.0;
			_wcx = num + _wrx;
			_wcy = num2 - _wry;
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _cx;
			y = _cy;
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_cx - _rx < minx)
			{
				minx = _cx - _rx;
			}
			if (_cx + _rx > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _cx + _rx;
			}
			if (_cy - _ry < miny)
			{
				miny = _cy - _ry;
			}
			if (_cy + _ry > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _cy + _ry;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_cx += dx;
			_cy += dy;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
			Refresh();
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				return;
			}
			RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse ellipse = (System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse)_element;
			if (handle == "Top" || handle == "Bottom")
			{
				_ry = Math.Abs(y - _cy);
				if (_circle)
				{
					_rx = _ry;
				}
			}
			else if (handle == "Left" || handle == "Right")
			{
				_rx = Math.Abs(x - _cx);
				if (_circle)
				{
					_ry = _rx;
				}
			}
			else if (handle == "NE")
			{
				double num = Math.Abs(x - _cx);
				double num2 = Math.Abs(y - _cy);
				if (_rx > _ry)
				{
					_ry *= num / _rx;
					_rx = num;
				}
				else
				{
					_rx *= num2 / _ry;
					_ry = num2;
				}
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(ellipse, _cx - _rx);
			Canvas.SetTop(ellipse, _cy - _ry);
			ellipse.Width = _rx * 2.0;
			ellipse.Height = _ry * 2.0;
			UpdateBack();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		private void UpdateBack()
		{
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _cx - _rx - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _cy - _ry - _thickness / 2.0);
			_back.Width = _rx * 2.0 + _thickness;
			_back.Height = _ry * 2.0 + _thickness;
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				translateTransform.X = x - _cx;
				translateTransform.Y = y - _cy;
			}
			else
			{
				Resize(ref handle, x, y);
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}

		public override void GenGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse ellipse = (System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse)_element;
			if (ellipse.RenderedGeometry.IsEmpty())
			{
				return;
			}
			double top = Canvas.GetTop(ellipse);
			double left = Canvas.GetLeft(ellipse);
			Rect bounds = ((EllipseGeometry)ellipse.RenderedGeometry).Bounds;
			if (!bounds.IsEmpty)
			{
				bounds.Width = _rx * 2.0;
				bounds.Height = _ry * 2.0;
				EllipseGeometry ellipseGeometry = new EllipseGeometry(bounds);
				if (!double.IsNaN(top))
				{
					ellipseGeometry.Transform = new TranslateTransform(left, top);
				}
				gg.Children.Add(ellipseGeometry);
			}
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.WcToDvc(_wcx, _wcy, out _cx, out _cy);
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_wrx, out _rx);
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_wry, out _ry);
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.DvcToWc(_cx, _cy, out _wcx, out _wcy);
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_rx, out _wrx);
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_ry, out _wry);
		}
	}
	public class Group : Element
	{
		private int _id;

		private Group _parent;

		private List<Element> _members;

		private List<Canvas> _clones;

		internal double _minx;

		internal double _miny;

		internal double _maxx;

		internal double _maxy;

		internal double _bx;

		internal double _by;

		internal double _cx;

		internal double _cy;

		internal double _scale;

		internal double _scalex;

		internal double _scaley;

		internal double _angle;

		private double _midx;

		private double _midy;

		private string _repeat = "0";

		private string _dx = "0";

		private string _dy = "0";

		private string _offx = "0";

		private string _offy = "0";

		private int _importmode;

		private bool _basemoved;

		private string _anglemap;

		private string _angleconversion;

		private string _repeatanimation;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		internal ScaleTransform _sform;

		internal RotateTransform _rform;

		private TranslateTransform _tform2;

		public new Group Parent
		{
			get
			{
				return _parent;
			}
			set
			{
				_parent = value;
			}
		}

		public List<Element> Members => _members;

		public Group(int id)
		{
			_parent = null;
			_id = id;
			_members = new List<Element>();
			_clones = new List<Canvas>();
			_cx = 0.0;
			_cy = 0.0;
			InitializeAnchored(6);
		}

		public Group(Canvas cv, IconCtrl ctrl, int id, int import = 0)
		{
			_parent = null;
			_id = id;
			_importmode = import;
			if (cv.Tag != null && (string)cv.Tag == "Imported")
			{
				_importmode = 1;
			}
			base.Control = ctrl;
			_name = cv.Name;
			Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(cv, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
			if (binding != null)
			{
				_visibilitymap = binding.XPath;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
				{
					Binding binding2 = new Binding
					{
						XPath = "/Attributes/" + _visibilitymap
					};
					SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding2);
				}
			}
			_members = new List<Element>();
			_clones = new List<Canvas>();
			_cx = 0.0;
			_cy = 0.0;
			InitializeAnchored(6);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			if (cv.RenderTransform is TransformGroup)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup2 = (TransformGroup)cv.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform;
				if (transformGroup2.Children[0] is RotateTransform)
				{
					_rform = (RotateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[0];
					_sform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup2.Children[1];
					translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[2];
					_bx = translateTransform.X;
					_by = translateTransform.Y;
					_cx = _rform.CenterX - _bx;
					_cy = _rform.CenterY - _by;
				}
				else
				{
					_rform = (RotateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[2];
					_sform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup2.Children[0];
					translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[1];
					_bx = translateTransform.X;
					_by = translateTransform.Y;
					translateTransform.X = 0.0;
					translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
					_cx = _rform.CenterX - _bx;
					_cy = _rform.CenterY - _by;
				}
				_angle = _rform.Angle;
				transformGroup.Children.Insert(0, _rform);
				transformGroup.Children.Insert(1, _sform);
				transformGroup.Children.Add(translateTransform);
				_tform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2];
				Binding binding3 = BindingOperations.GetBinding(_rform, RotateTransform.AngleProperty);
				if (binding3 != null)
				{
					_anglemap = binding3.XPath;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty((string)binding3.ConverterParameter))
					{
						string text = (string)binding3.ConverterParameter;
						_angleconversion = text.Replace('_', ' ');
					}
					SetAngleMap(_anglemap);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (cv.RenderTransform is RotateTransform)
				{
					_rform = (RotateTransform)cv.RenderTransform;
					_angle = _rform.Angle;
					_cx = _rform.CenterX;
					_cy = _rform.CenterY;
				}
				else
				{
					_rform = new RotateTransform(0.0);
				}
				_sform = new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0);
				_tform = new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0);
				transformGroup.Children.Insert(0, _rform);
				transformGroup.Children.Insert(1, _sform);
				transformGroup.Children.Insert(2, _tform);
			}
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.Highlight(bOn, handle);
			}
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			if (_importmode != 0)
			{
				AddHandle("Base", _midx, _midy, _anchored[0]);
			}
			else
			{
				AddHandle("Base", 0.0, 0.0, _anchored[0]);
			}
			AddHandle("SW", _minx, _maxy, _anchored[1]);
			AddHandle("SE", _maxx, _maxy, _anchored[2]);
			AddHandle("NE", _maxx, _miny, _anchored[3]);
			AddHandle("NW", _minx, _miny, _anchored[4]);
			if (_importmode != 0)
			{
				AddHandle("Base2", _midx, _midy, _anchored[5], 32);
			}
			else
			{
				AddHandle("Base2", 0.0, 0.0, _anchored[5], 32);
			}
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.AddHandles();
			}
		}

		public void Add(Element element)
		{
			_members.Add(element);
			base.Children.Add(element);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, Element element)
		{
			_members.Insert(index, element);
			base.Children.Insert(index, element);
		}

		public void Remove(Element element)
		{
			XmlNode dataNode = element.DataNode;
			_members.Remove(element);
			base.Children.Remove(element);
			dataNode.ParentNode.RemoveChild(dataNode);
		}

		public void Ungroup(Canvas panel, List<Element> elements, XmlNode groupnode)
		{
			XmlNode parentNode = groupnode.ParentNode;
			int index = elements.IndexOf(this);
			elements.RemoveAt(index);
			XmlNode refChild = groupnode;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				if (member is Group)
				{
					((Group)member).Parent = Parent;
				}
				member.Move(_bx, _by);
				XmlNode xml = member.GetXml(parentNode.OwnerDocument);
				parentNode.InsertAfter(xml, refChild);
				refChild = xml;
				base.Children.Remove(member);
				panel.Children.Add(member);
				elements.Insert(index++, member);
				member.RefreshHandles();
			}
			panel.Children.Remove(this);
			parentNode.RemoveChild(groupnode);
			_members.Clear();
		}

		public override void Enable(bool enable)
		{
			base.Enable(enable);
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.Opacity = (enable ? 1.0 : 0.3);
			}
		}

		public override void ChangeProperty(string property, string value, XmlNode gradientsnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (value != null && property == "AngleMap")
			{
				if (value == "----")
				{
					_anglemap = "";
				}
				else
				{
					_anglemap = value;
				}
				SetAngleMap(_anglemap);
			}
		}

		public void ApplyRepeat(string sRepeat, string sXoff, string sYoff, string sDx, string sDy)
		{
			if (_tform2 == null)
			{
				_tform2 = new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0);
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform2);
			}
			TranslateTransform tform = _tform2;
			double x = (_tform2.Y = 0.0);
			tform.X = x;
			if ((_repeat == "0" && sRepeat == "0") || (sDx == "0" && sDy == "0"))
			{
				return;
			}
			bool flag = false;
			if (sRepeat != _repeat)
			{
				foreach (Canvas clone in _clones)
				{
					base.Children.Remove(clone);
				}
				_clones.Clear();
				flag = true;
			}
			if (sRepeat == "0")
			{
				return;
			}
			int num2 = (int)ConvParam(sRepeat);
			if (sXoff[0] == '@')
			{
				string param = sXoff.Substring(1);
				BindRepeatVariable("offx", param);
			}
			else
			{
				_tform2.X = ConvParam(sXoff);
			}
			if (sYoff[0] == '@')
			{
				string param2 = sYoff.Substring(1);
				BindRepeatVariable("offy", param2);
			}
			else
			{
				_tform2.Y = ConvParam(sYoff);
			}
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			if (sDx[0] != '@')
			{
				num3 = (num4 = ConvParam(sDx));
			}
			double num5 = 0.0;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			if (sDy[0] != '@')
			{
				num5 = (num6 = ConvParam(sDy));
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					string code = GenXaml(bCurrent: false, ResolveAnchor: false, num3, num5, independent: true);
					code = base.Control.ParseGradient(code);
					MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
					StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
					streamWriter.WriteLine(code);
					streamWriter.Flush();
					memoryStream.Position = 0L;
					Canvas canvas = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream) as Canvas;
					base.Children.Add(canvas);
					_clones.Add(canvas);
				}
				if (sDx != _dx || sDy != _dy)
				{
					Canvas canvas2 = _clones[i];
					TransformGroup transformGroup2 = (TransformGroup)canvas2.RenderTransform;
					TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[2];
					if (sDx[0] == '@')
					{
						string param3 = sDx.Substring(1);
						BindRepeatVariable("dx", param3, i);
					}
					else
					{
						translateTransform.X = num3;
					}
					if (sDy[0] == '@')
					{
						string param4 = sDy.Substring(1);
						BindRepeatVariable("dy", param4, i);
					}
					else
					{
						translateTransform.Y = num5;
					}
				}
				num3 += num4;
				num5 += num6;
			}
			_repeat = sRepeat;
			_offx = sXoff;
			_offy = sYoff;
			_dx = sDx;
			_dy = sDy;
		}

		private void BindRepeatVariable(string varname, string param, int ith = 0)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = base.Control.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(param);
			if (xmlNode != null && xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "DoubleAnimation")
			{
				if (!(varname != "offx") || !(varname != "offy"))
				{
					string text = "group" + _id;
					if (_storyboard == null)
					{
						_storyboard = new Storyboard();
					}
					if (base.Control.FindName(text) != null)
					{
						base.Control.UnregisterName(text);
					}
					_repeatanimation = param;
					if (varname == "offx")
					{
						base.Control.RegisterName(text, _tform2);
						SetAnimation(base.Control, text, _storyboard, TranslateTransform.XProperty, xmlNode, null);
					}
					else if (varname == "offy")
					{
						base.Control.RegisterName(text, _tform2);
						SetAnimation(base.Control, text, _storyboard, TranslateTransform.YProperty, xmlNode, null);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.XPath = "/Attributes/" + param;
			if (varname == "offx")
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(_tform2, TranslateTransform.XProperty, binding);
				return;
			}
			if (varname == "offy")
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(_tform2, TranslateTransform.YProperty, binding);
				return;
			}
			string converterParameter = ith + 1 + " *";
			binding.Converter = new Calc();
			binding.ConverterParameter = converterParameter;
			Canvas canvas = _clones[ith];
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)canvas.RenderTransform;
			TranslateTransform target = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2];
			if (varname == "dx")
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(target, TranslateTransform.XProperty, binding);
			}
			else if (varname == "dy")
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(target, TranslateTransform.YProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		private double ConvParam(string value)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			if (value[0] != '@')
			{
				double.TryParse(value, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SaveRepeater(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (!(_repeat == "0"))
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Repeater", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				string elementIndex = base.Control.GetElementIndex(this);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "index", elementIndex);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "count", _repeat);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "offx", _offx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "offy", _offy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dx", _dx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dy", _dy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			}
		}

		public void LoadRepeater(XmlNode node)
		{
			string sRepeat = GetString(node, "count");
			string sXoff = GetString(node, "offx");
			string sYoff = GetString(node, "offy");
			string sDx = GetString(node, "dx");
			string sDy = GetString(node, "dy");
			ApplyRepeat(sRepeat, sXoff, sYoff, sDx, sDy);
			AddAttribute(_datanode, "repeatcount", _repeat.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_datanode, "xoffset", _offx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_datanode, "yoffset", _offy.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_datanode, "dx", _dx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_datanode, "dy", _dy.ToString());
		}

		public void SetAngle(double angle)
		{
			_angle = angle;
			_rform.Angle = angle;
		}

		public void SetAngleConverter(string value)
		{
			_angleconversion = value;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_anglemap))
			{
				SetAngleMap(_anglemap);
			}
		}

		private void SetAngleMap(string varname)
		{
			if (_storyboard != null)
			{
				_storyboard.Pause(base.Control);
				DoubleAnimation doubleAnimation = (DoubleAnimation)_storyboard.Children[0];
				doubleAnimation.ClearValue(RotateTransform.AngleProperty);
				_storyboard = null;
			}
			string text = "";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(varname))
			{
				int num = varname.IndexOf(',');
				if (num >= 0)
				{
					text = varname.Substring(num + 1);
					varname = varname.Substring(0, num);
					_anglemap = varname;
					_angleconversion = text;
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode = base.Control.AttribNode.SelectSingleNode(varname);
				if (xmlNode != null && xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "DoubleAnimation")
				{
					string text2 = "group" + _id;
					if (_storyboard == null)
					{
						_storyboard = new Storyboard();
					}
					if (base.Control.FindName(text2) != null)
					{
						base.Control.UnregisterName(text2);
					}
					base.Control.RegisterName(text2, _rform);
					SetAnimation(base.Control, text2, _storyboard, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, xmlNode, null);
					return;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_anglemap))
			{
				Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(_rform, RotateTransform.AngleProperty);
				if (binding != null)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_rform, RotateTransform.AngleProperty);
				}
				return;
			}
			_rform = new RotateTransform();
			Binding binding2 = new Binding();
			binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + varname;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_angleconversion))
			{
				binding2.Converter = new Calc();
				binding2.ConverterParameter = _angleconversion;
			}
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(_rform, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, binding2);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			transformGroup.Children[0] = _rform;
			CalcSize(newgroup: false);
		}

		public override void RunAnim(bool run, string anim)
		{
			if (_anglemap == anim || _repeatanimation == anim)
			{
				if (run)
				{
					_storyboard.Resume(base.Control);
				}
				else
				{
					_storyboard.Pause(base.Control);
				}
			}
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.RunAnim(run, anim);
			}
		}

		public override void UpdateAnimation(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (_storyboard != null && (_anglemap == node.Name || _repeatanimation == node.Name))
			{
				bool flag = !_storyboard.GetIsPaused(base.Control);
				if (flag)
				{
					_storyboard.Pause(base.Control);
				}
				_storyboard.Children.Clear();
				string registername = "group" + _id;
				if (_anglemap == node.Name)
				{
					SetAnimation(base.Control, registername, _storyboard, RotateTransform.AngleProperty, node, null);
				}
				else if (_offx == "@" + node.Name)
				{
					SetAnimation(base.Control, registername, _storyboard, TranslateTransform.XProperty, node, null);
				}
				else if (_offy == "@" + node.Name)
				{
					SetAnimation(base.Control, registername, _storyboard, TranslateTransform.YProperty, node, null);
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_storyboard.Resume(base.Control);
				}
			}
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.UpdateAnimation(node);
			}
		}

		public void Flip(bool flipy)
		{
			if (flipy)
			{
				_sform.ScaleY = 0.0 - _sform.ScaleY;
				_scaley = 0.0 - _scaley;
			}
			else
			{
				_sform.ScaleX = 0.0 - _sform.ScaleX;
				_scalex = 0.0 - _scalex;
			}
			if (_handles.Count > 0)
			{
				RefreshHandles();
			}
		}

		public void Rotate(bool left)
		{
			double angle = _rform.Angle;
			if (left)
			{
				angle -= 90.0;
				if (angle < 0.0)
				{
					angle += 360.0;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				angle += 90.0;
				if (angle >= 360.0)
				{
					angle -= 360.0;
				}
			}
			_rform.Angle = angle;
			_angle = angle;
			if (_handles.Count > 0)
			{
				RefreshHandles();
			}
		}

		public override void XmlNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			_xmlnode = node;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in node.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (!(childNode is XmlComment) && !(childNode.Name == "Canvas.RenderTransform"))
				{
					if (num < _members.Count)
					{
						_members[num].XmlNode(childNode);
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("Group");
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(base.Name))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "Name", base.Name);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "IsSelected", "false");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "IsChecked", "false");
			int num = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				num = MatchItem(doc, "Visibility", _visibilitymap);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "visibility", num.ToString());
			if (_anglemap == null && _angle != 0.0)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "angle", _angle.ToString());
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_anglemap))
				{
					num2 = MatchItem(doc, "Parameter", _anglemap);
				}
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "anglemapindex", num2.ToString());
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_angleconversion))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "angleconverter", _angleconversion);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "repeatcount", _repeat.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "xoffset", _offx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "yoffset", _offy.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "dx", _dx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "dy", _dy.ToString());
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				XmlNode xml = member.GetXml(doc);
				xmlElement.AppendChild(xml);
			}
			_datanode = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			if (bCurrent && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap) && !base.IsVisible)
			{
				return "";
			}
			bool flag = independent;
			string text = ((!flag) ? _by.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) : offy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			if (ResolveAnchor)
			{
				foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
				{
					if (handle.Anchor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Anchor anchor = handle.Anchor;
					string iD = handle.ID;
					if ((!(iD == "Base") && !(iD == "Base2")) || anchor.Direction != 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string param = anchor.GetParam("yparam");
					string text2 = anchor.GetParam("yconv").Replace(' ', '_');
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						handle.GetOffset(out var _, out var dy);
						if (dy != 0.0)
						{
							text2 = "(" + text2 + ")+" + dy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
						}
						text = "{Binding XPath=" + param + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + text2 + "}";
					}
					else
					{
						text = "{Binding XPath=" + param + "}";
					}
				}
			}
			string text3 = "<Canvas ";
			if (flag)
			{
				text3 += "xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\" xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\" ";
			}
			if (_importmode != 0)
			{
				text3 += "Tag=\"Imported\" ";
			}
			text3 += SaveVisibility();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_name))
			{
				text3 = text3 + " Name=\"" + _name + "\"";
			}
			text3 += ">";
			text3 += "<Canvas.RenderTransform>";
			text3 += "<TransformGroup>";
			text3 += "<RotateTransform Angle=\"";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_anglemap))
			{
				if (bCurrent)
				{
					string attribValue = base.Control.GetAttribValue(_anglemap, _angleconversion);
					text3 += attribValue;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_angleconversion))
				{
					string text4 = _angleconversion.Replace(' ', '_');
					string text5 = text3;
					text3 = text5 + "{Binding XPath=" + _anglemap + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + text4 + "}";
				}
				else
				{
					text3 = text3 + "{Binding XPath=" + _anglemap + "}";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text3 += _angle.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			text3 += "\" ";
			if (_cx != 0.0 || _cy != 0.0)
			{
				string text6 = text3;
				text3 = text6 + "CenterX=\"" + _cx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" CenterY=\"" + _cy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			}
			text3 += "/>";
			string text7 = text3;
			text3 = text7 + "<ScaleTransform ScaleX=\"" + _sform.ScaleX.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ScaleY=\"" + _sform.ScaleY.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" />";
			double num = (flag ? offx : _bx);
			string text8 = text3;
			text3 = text8 + "<TranslateTransform X=\"" + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" Y=\"" + text + "\" />";
			text3 += "</TransformGroup>";
			text3 += "</Canvas.RenderTransform>";
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				text3 += member.GenXaml(bCurrent, ResolveAnchor);
			}
			return text3 + "</Canvas>";
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = text2;
			text2 = text3 + "translate(" + _bx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + " " + _by.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + ") ";
			string text4 = text2;
			text2 = text4 + "scale(" + _sform.ScaleX.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _sform.ScaleY.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + ") ";
			if (_angle != 0.0)
			{
				text2 = text2 + "rotate(" + _angle.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				if (_cx != 0.0 || _cy != 0.0)
				{
					string text5 = text2;
					text2 = text5 + " " + _cx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + " " + _cy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
				text2 += ")";
			}
			text += "<g>";
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				text += "<metadata>";
				text = text + "<visibility context=\"" + _visibilitymap + "\"/>";
				text += "</metadata>";
			}
			text = text + "<g transform=\"" + text2 + "\">";
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				text += member.GenSvg();
			}
			text += "</g>";
			return text + "</g>";
		}

		public Element FindMember(string ids)
		{
			string[] array = ids.Split('.');
			int index = int.Parse(array[0]);
			if (array.Length == 1)
			{
				return _members[index];
			}
			Element element = _members[index];
			if (element is Group)
			{
				string ids2 = ids.Substring(array[0].Length + 1);
				return ((Group)element).FindMember(ids2);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public string GetMemberIndex(Element elem)
		{
			string result = "";
			int num = 0;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				if (member == elem)
				{
					result = num.ToString();
					break;
				}
				if (member is Group)
				{
					string memberIndex = ((Group)member).GetMemberIndex(elem);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(memberIndex))
					{
						result = num + "." + memberIndex;
						break;
					}
				}
				num++;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public XmlNode GetMemberNode(string id)
		{
			int num = id.IndexOf('.');
			string s = ((num >= 0) ? id.Substring(0, num) : id);
			int index = int.Parse(s);
			Element element = _members[index];
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return element.DataNode;
			}
			if (!(element is Group))
			{
				return null;
			}
			id = id.Substring(num + 1);
			return ((Group)element).GetMemberNode(id);
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _bx;
			y = _by;
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(_minx, _miny);
			System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point(_maxx, _maxy);
			System.Windows.Point point3 = base.RenderTransform.Transform(point);
			System.Windows.Point point4 = base.RenderTransform.Transform(point2);
			if (point3.X < minx)
			{
				minx = point3.X;
			}
			if (point4.X < minx)
			{
				minx = point4.X;
			}
			if (point3.X > maxx)
			{
				maxx = point3.X;
			}
			if (point4.X > maxx)
			{
				maxx = point4.X;
			}
			if (point3.Y < miny)
			{
				miny = point3.Y;
			}
			if (point4.Y < miny)
			{
				miny = point4.Y;
			}
			if (point3.Y > maxy)
			{
				maxy = point3.Y;
			}
			if (point4.Y > maxy)
			{
				maxy = point4.Y;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[3];
			_bx += dx;
			_by += dy;
			translateTransform.X = _bx;
			translateTransform.Y = _by;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handlename, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handlename == "Base" || handlename == "Base2")
			{
				if (_importmode == 0)
				{
					_cx = x - _bx;
					_cy = y - _by;
				}
				return;
			}
			Handle handle = FindHandle(handlename);
			Handle handle2 = FindHandle("Base");
			handle2.GetPosition(out var x2, out var y2);
			handle.GetPosition(out var x3, out var y3);
			double num = Math.Abs((x - x2) / (x3 - x2));
			double num2 = Math.Abs((y - y2) / (y3 - y2));
			double num3 = ((num > num2) ? num : num2);
			double scalex = (_sform.ScaleX = ((_sform.ScaleX < 0.0) ? ((0.0 - _scale) * num3) : (_scale * num3)));
			_scalex = scalex;
			double scaley = (_sform.ScaleY = ((_sform.ScaleY < 0.0) ? ((0.0 - _scale) * num3) : (_scale * num3)));
			_scaley = scaley;
			if (_importmode != 0)
			{
				double num6 = _midx * num3 * _scale;
				double num7 = _midy * num3 * _scale;
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				_ = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[transformGroup.Children.Count - 1];
				_tform.X = 0.0 - num6 + _midx * _scale;
				_tform.Y = 0.0 - num7 + _midy * _scale;
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Base" || handle == "Base2")
			{
				if (_importmode != 0)
				{
					_tform.X = x - _bx - _midx * _scale;
					_tform.Y = y - _by - _midy * _scale;
				}
				else
				{
					_tform.X = x - _bx;
					_tform.Y = y - _by;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "Base" || handle == "Base2")
			{
				Move(_tform.X, _tform.Y);
				_tform.X = 0.0;
				_tform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}

		public override void GenGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			geometryGroup.Transform = base.RenderTransform;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.GenGeometry(geometryGroup);
			}
			gg.Children.Add(geometryGroup);
		}

		public override void HandleSelected(string id)
		{
			base.HandleSelected(id);
			_basemoved = id == "Base";
		}

		public override void EndResize(string handle)
		{
			if (_basemoved)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				System.Windows.Point point2 = new System.Windows.Point(_cx, _cy);
				System.Windows.Point point3 = transformGroup.Inverse.Transform(point);
				System.Windows.Point point4 = transformGroup.Inverse.Transform(point2);
				double num = point4.X - point3.X;
				double num2 = point4.Y - point3.Y;
				Move(_cx, _cy);
				_cx = 0.0;
				_cy = 0.0;
				foreach (Element member in _members)
				{
					member.Move(0.0 - num, 0.0 - num2);
				}
				_minx -= num;
				_maxx -= num;
				_miny -= num2;
				_maxy -= num2;
			}
			else
			{
				_scale = _scalex;
				if (_importmode != 0)
				{
					TransformGroup transformGroup2 = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
					TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup2.Children[transformGroup2.Children.Count - 1];
					_bx = (translateTransform.X += _tform.X);
					_by = (translateTransform.Y += _tform.Y);
					TranslateTransform tform = _tform;
					double x = (_tform.Y = 0.0);
					tform.X = x;
				}
			}
		}

		public Element FindElement(XmlNode node)
		{
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				if (member.DataNode.Equals(node))
				{
					return member;
				}
				if (member is Group)
				{
					Element element = ((Group)member).FindElement(node);
					if (element != null)
					{
						return element;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public string ElementID(Element element)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				if (element != member)
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			string text = "";
			if (Parent != null)
			{
				Group parent = Parent;
				text = parent.ElementID(this);
			}
			else
			{
				text = base.Control.Elements.IndexOf(this).ToString();
			}
			return text + "." + num;
		}

		public void ButtonCommand(string command, XmlNode node)
		{
			XmlNode parentNode = node.ParentNode;
			int num = FindMemberIndex(node);
			Element element = _members[num];
			switch (command)
			{
			case "Delete":
				base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Grouping, HistorySubcode.Delete, element);
				base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Grouping, HistorySubcode.Delete, this, null, append: true);
				_members.RemoveAt(num);
				base.Children.Remove(element);
				parentNode.RemoveChild(node);
				CalcSize(newgroup: false);
				break;
			case "Up":
				if (num > 0)
				{
					base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Raise, element);
					base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Raise, this, null, append: true);
					_members.RemoveAt(num);
					_members.Insert(num - 1, element);
					XmlNode previousSibling = node.PreviousSibling;
					parentNode.RemoveChild(node);
					node = element.GetXml(node.OwnerDocument);
					parentNode.InsertBefore(node, previousSibling);
					RefreshMembers();
				}
				break;
			case "Down":
				if (num < _members.Count - 1)
				{
					base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Lower, element);
					base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Lower, this, null, append: true);
					_members.RemoveAt(num);
					_members.Insert(num + 1, element);
					XmlNode nextSibling = node.NextSibling;
					parentNode.RemoveChild(node);
					node = element.GetXml(node.OwnerDocument);
					parentNode.InsertAfter(node, nextSibling);
					RefreshMembers();
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public void CalcSize(bool newgroup, bool initial = false)
		{
			_minx = 10000000000.0;
			_miny = 10000000000.0;
			_maxx = -10000000000.0;
			_maxy = -10000000000.0;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				member.GetField(ref _minx, ref _maxx, ref _miny, ref _maxy);
			}
			if (initial && _importmode != 0)
			{
				_midx = (_minx + _maxx) / 2.0;
				_midy = (_miny + _maxy) / 2.0;
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			if (!newgroup)
			{
				_angle = _rform.Angle;
				if (_handles.Count > 0)
				{
					RefreshHandles();
				}
				_scalex = _sform.ScaleX;
				_scaley = _sform.ScaleY;
				_scale = _scalex;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[transformGroup.Children.Count - 1];
				if (initial && _importmode == 2)
				{
					double bx = (translateTransform.X = 0.0 - _midx);
					_bx = bx;
					double num2 = (translateTransform.Y = 0.0 - _midy);
					_by = num2;
				}
				else
				{
					_bx = translateTransform.X;
					_by = translateTransform.Y;
				}
				if (Parent != null)
				{
					Parent.CalcSize(newgroup: false);
				}
				return;
			}
			_scale = 1.0;
			_scalex = 1.0;
			_scaley = 1.0;
			_angle = 0.0;
			_bx = (_minx + _maxx) / 2.0;
			_by = (_miny + _maxy) / 2.0;
			_rform = new RotateTransform(0.0);
			_sform = new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0);
			_tform = new TranslateTransform(_bx, _by);
			transformGroup.Children.Insert(0, _rform);
			transformGroup.Children.Insert(1, _sform);
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform);
			_minx -= _bx;
			_maxx -= _bx;
			_miny -= _by;
			_maxy -= _by;
			foreach (Element member2 in _members)
			{
				member2.Move(0.0 - _bx, 0.0 - _by);
			}
			AddHandles();
		}

		private int FindMemberIndex(XmlNode node)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				if (!member.DataNode.Equals(node))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void RefreshMembers()
		{
			foreach (Element member in _members)
			{
				base.Children.Remove(member);
			}
			foreach (Element member2 in _members)
			{
				base.Children.Add(member2);
			}
		}
	}
	public class Handle : BaseObj
	{
		private static ContextMenu s_contextMenu;

		private Element _owner;

		private string _id;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _rect;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse _ellipse;

		private ScaleTransform _sform;

		private Anchor _anchor;

		private bool _reversedirection;

		private double _dx;

		private double _dy;

		private bool _insert;

		private bool _deletable;

		private bool _resize;

		private bool _rotate;

		private bool _center;

		private bool _hidden;

		private bool _temporary;

		public string ID => _id;

		public Element Owner => _owner;

		public Anchor Anchor => _anchor;

		public bool Hidden => _hidden;

		public Handle(Element owner, string ID, double x, double y, bool anchored, int mode = 0)
		{
			_owner = owner;
			_id = ID;
			_deletable = (mode & 1) != 0;
			_insert = (mode & 2) != 0;
			_resize = (mode & 4) != 0;
			_rotate = (mode & 8) != 0;
			_center = (mode & 0x10) != 0;
			_hidden = (mode & 0x20) != 0;
			base.Cursor = Cursors.SizeAll;
			_sform = new ScaleTransform();
			Binding binding = new Binding
			{
				XPath = "/Attributes/LabelScale"
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(_sform, ScaleTransform.ScaleXProperty, binding);
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(_sform, ScaleTransform.ScaleYProperty, binding);
			_sform.CenterX = x;
			_sform.CenterY = y;
			base.RenderTransform = _sform;
			if (_rotate || _center)
			{
				_ellipse = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
				Canvas.SetLeft(_ellipse, x - 6.0);
				Canvas.SetTop(_ellipse, y - 6.0);
				_ellipse.Width = 12.0;
				_ellipse.Height = 12.0;
				_ellipse.Stroke = (_rotate ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red);
				_ellipse.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White;
				base.Children.Add(_ellipse);
			}
			else
			{
				_rect = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
				int num = (_resize ? 6 : 4);
				Canvas.SetLeft(_rect, x - (double)num);
				Canvas.SetTop(_rect, y - (double)num);
				_rect.Width = num * 2;
				_rect.Height = num * 2;
				_rect.Fill = (anchored ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Pink : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White);
				if (_resize)
				{
					_rect.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.ForestGreen;
				}
				else
				{
					_rect.Stroke = (_insert ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red);
				}
				base.Children.Add(_rect);
			}
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += Handle_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			base.MouseRightButtonUp += Handle_MouseRightButtonUp;
			base.MouseEnter += Handle_MouseEnter;
			base.MouseLeave += Handle_MouseLeave;
			base.ToolTip = ID;
			if (_deletable)
			{
				if (s_contextMenu == null)
				{
					s_contextMenu = new ContextMenu();
					MenuItem menuItem = new MenuItem();
					menuItem.Header = "Delete";
					menuItem.Click += item_Click;
					s_contextMenu.Items.Add(menuItem);
				}
				base.ContextMenu = s_contextMenu;
			}
		}

		private void item_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			Handle handle = ((ContextMenu)menuItem.Parent).PlacementTarget as Handle;
			PolyElement polyElement = (PolyElement)handle.Owner;
			polyElement.DeletePoint(handle.ID);
		}

		private void Handle_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_anchor != null)
			{
				_anchor.Highlight(bOn: false);
			}
			if (_rotate && _owner is PolyElement)
			{
				((PolyElement)_owner).ShowRotateHandle(show: false);
			}
		}

		private void Handle_MouseEnter(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_anchor != null)
			{
				_anchor.Highlight(bOn: true);
			}
			if (_rotate && _owner is PolyElement)
			{
				((PolyElement)_owner).ShowRotateHandle(show: true);
			}
		}

		private void Handle_MouseRightButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_owner is PolyElement)
			{
				if (_id == "center")
				{
					_owner.HandleQuery(_id);
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
			else if (!_deletable)
			{
				_owner.HandleQuery(_id);
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void Handle_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_owner.HandleSelected(_id);
		}

		public void Update(double x, double y, bool anchored)
		{
			if (_anchor != null)
			{
				_dx = x - _anchor.X;
				_dy = y - _anchor.Y;
			}
			_sform.CenterX = x;
			_sform.CenterY = y;
			if (_rect != null)
			{
				int num = (_resize ? 6 : 4);
				Canvas.SetLeft(_rect, x - (double)num);
				Canvas.SetTop(_rect, y - (double)num);
				_rect.Fill = (anchored ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Pink : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White);
			}
			else if (_ellipse != null)
			{
				Canvas.SetLeft(_ellipse, x - 6.0);
				Canvas.SetTop(_ellipse, y - 6.0);
			}
		}

		public void UpdatePosition(bool yvalue, double value)
		{
			if (yvalue)
			{
				_sform.CenterY = value;
				Canvas.SetTop(_rect, value - 4.0);
			}
			else
			{
				_sform.CenterX = value;
				Canvas.SetLeft(_rect, value - 4.0);
			}
		}

		public void TagAnchor(Anchor anchor, bool reversedirection)
		{
			anchor.TagHandle(this);
			_anchor = anchor;
			_reversedirection = reversedirection;
			_dx = _sform.CenterX - anchor.X;
			_dy = _sform.CenterY - anchor.Y;
		}

		public void UntagAnchor()
		{
			if (_anchor != null)
			{
				_anchor.UntagHandle(this);
			}
			_anchor = null;
		}

		public void GetPosition(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _sform.CenterX;
			y = _sform.CenterY;
		}

		public void SetPositionDelta(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_reversedirection)
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, _sform.CenterX - dx, _sform.CenterY - dy);
			}
			else
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, _sform.CenterX + dx, _sform.CenterY + dy);
			}
			_owner.HandleMoveEnd(_id);
		}

		public void SetPosition(bool yvalue, double value)
		{
			if (yvalue)
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, _sform.CenterX, value + _dy);
			}
			else
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, value + _dx, _sform.CenterY);
			}
			_owner.HandleMoveEnd(_id);
		}

		public new void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_reversedirection)
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, _sform.CenterX - dx, _sform.CenterY - dy);
			}
			else
			{
				_owner.HandleMove(_id, _sform.CenterX + dx, _sform.CenterY + dy);
			}
		}

		public void EndMove()
		{
			_owner.HandleMoveEnd(_id);
			_owner.RefreshHandles();
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, string elementindex, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Handle", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "owner", elementindex);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "id", _id);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dx", _dx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dy", _dy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			if (_reversedirection)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "reverse", "true");
			}
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
		}

		public string GenSvg(string elementindex)
		{
			string text = "<handle ";
			text = text + "owner=\"" + elementindex + "\" ";
			text = text + "id=\"" + _id + "\" ";
			text = text + "dx=\"" + _dx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "dy=\"" + _dy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public void SetOffset(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_dx = dx;
			_dy = dy;
		}

		public void GetOffset(out double dx, out double dy)
		{
			dx = _dx;
			dy = _dy;
		}

		public void Highlight(bool bOn)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				if (_rect != null)
				{
					_rect.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue;
					_rect.StrokeThickness = 3.0;
				}
				else if (_ellipse != null)
				{
					_ellipse.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue;
					_ellipse.StrokeThickness = 3.0;
				}
				if (base.Parent == null)
				{
					_temporary = true;
					_owner.Control.CurLayer.Children.Add(this);
				}
				return;
			}
			if (_temporary)
			{
				_temporary = false;
				_owner.Control.CurLayer.Children.Remove(this);
			}
			if (_rect != null)
			{
				if (_resize)
				{
					_rect.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.ForestGreen;
				}
				else
				{
					_rect.Stroke = (_insert ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red);
				}
				_rect.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
			}
			else if (_ellipse != null)
			{
				_ellipse.Stroke = (_center ? System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red : System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green);
				_ellipse.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum HistoryCode
	{
		Add,
		Delete,
		Move,
		ZIndex,
		Grouping,
		Change,
		Port,
		Anchor,
		Label,
		Group
	}
	public class History
	{
		private HistoryCode m_type;

		private bool m_bHidden;

		private HistoryItem m_item;

		public HistoryItem Item => m_item;

		public string Description
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_item != null)
				{
					return m_item.Description;
				}
				return "";
			}
		}

		public History()
		{
			m_item = null;
		}

		public bool Commit(HistoryCode type, HistorySubcode subcode, BaseObj obj, string description, IconCtrl iconctrl, bool append = false)
		{
			if (append)
			{
				m_item.Append(obj, subcode);
				return true;
			}
			m_type = type;
			switch (type)
			{
			case HistoryCode.Group:
				m_item = new HistoryGroup(description, reverse: false);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Grouping:
				m_item = new HistoryGrouping(obj, subcode);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Add:
			case HistoryCode.Delete:
				m_item = new HistoryAdd(obj, subcode, description);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Move:
				m_item = new HistoryMove(obj, subcode, description);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Change:
				m_item = new HistoryChange(obj, subcode, description);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.ZIndex:
				m_item = new HistoryZIndex(obj, subcode);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Port:
				m_item = new HistoryPort(obj, subcode);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Label:
				m_item = new HistoryLabel(obj, subcode);
				break;
			case HistoryCode.Anchor:
				m_item = new HistoryAnchor(obj, subcode, description);
				break;
			}
			if (m_item != null)
			{
				m_item.Commit(iconctrl, obj, subcode);
			}
			return m_item != null;
		}

		public void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_item != null)
			{
				m_item.Undo(iconctrl, ref modified);
			}
		}

		public void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_item != null)
			{
				m_item.Redo(iconctrl, ref modified);
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryObj
	{
		protected void AddToModified(ref List<string> modified, string spec)
		{
			if (!modified.Contains(spec))
			{
				modified.Add(spec);
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryItem : HistoryObj
	{
		protected int m_class;

		protected string m_name;

		protected BaseObj m_obj;

		protected HistoryObj m_supplement;

		public virtual string Description => null;

		public HistoryItem()
		{
		}

		public HistoryItem(BaseObj obj)
		{
			m_class = 0;
		}

		public virtual void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			if (obj != null)
			{
				m_obj = obj;
				m_name = obj.GetType().ToString();
				if (m_name.StartsWith("IconCtrl."))
				{
					m_name = m_name.Substring(9);
				}
				if (obj is Line)
				{
					HistoryLine historyLine = new HistoryLine();
					historyLine.Save((Line)obj);
					m_supplement = historyLine;
				}
				else if (obj is Rectangle)
				{
					HistoryRectangle historyRectangle = new HistoryRectangle();
					historyRectangle.Save((Rectangle)obj);
					m_supplement = historyRectangle;
				}
				else if (obj is Ellipse)
				{
					HistoryEllipse historyEllipse = new HistoryEllipse();
					historyEllipse.Save((Ellipse)obj);
					m_supplement = historyEllipse;
				}
				else if (obj is PolyElement)
				{
					HistoryPolygon historyPolygon = new HistoryPolygon();
					historyPolygon.Save((PolyElement)obj);
					m_supplement = historyPolygon;
				}
				else if (obj is Path)
				{
					HistoryPath historyPath = new HistoryPath();
					historyPath.Save((Path)obj);
					m_supplement = historyPath;
				}
				else if (obj is Text)
				{
					HistoryText historyText = new HistoryText();
					historyText.Save((Text)obj);
					m_supplement = historyText;
				}
				else if (obj is Group)
				{
					HistoryGroupObj historyGroupObj = new HistoryGroupObj();
					historyGroupObj.Save((Group)obj);
					m_supplement = historyGroupObj;
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void Append(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
		}

		public virtual void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
		}

		public virtual void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
		}

		protected void RestoreText()
		{
			Text text = (Text)m_obj;
			HistoryText historyText = new HistoryText();
			historyText.Save(text);
			HistoryText historyText2 = (HistoryText)m_supplement;
			historyText2.Restore(text);
			m_supplement = historyText;
			text.ResetPosition();
		}

		protected void Swap(ref float x, ref float y)
		{
			float num = x;
			x = y;
			y = num;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryAdd : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private string m_description;

		private HistoryObj m_supplement2;

		private BaseObj m_obj2;

		private int m_childindex;

		private int m_elementindex;

		private int m_elementlistindex;

		private XmlNode m_savednode;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string text = "Add";
				string text2 = m_name;
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Add:
					text = "Add";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Insert:
					text = "Insert";
					text2 = m_description;
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Delete:
					text = "Delete";
					break;
				}
				return text + " " + text2;
			}
		}

		public HistoryAdd(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode, string description)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_description = description;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			base.Commit(iconctrl, obj, subcode);
			if (m_subcode != HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				return;
			}
			Element element = (Element)obj;
			m_childindex = iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.IndexOf(element);
			m_elementindex = iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.IndexOf(element);
			int num = (m_elementlistindex = 0);
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			m_savednode = xmlDocument.ImportNode(element.DataNode, deep: true);
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in iconctrl.ElementList.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode == element.DataNode)
				{
					m_elementlistindex = num;
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
		}

		public override void Append(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			Element element = (Element)m_obj;
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Add)
			{
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Remove(element);
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Remove(element);
				iconctrl.ElementList.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
			}
			else
			{
				if (m_subcode != HistorySubcode.Delete)
				{
					return;
				}
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Insert(m_childindex, element);
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Insert(m_elementindex, element);
				element.DataNode = element.DataNode.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(m_savednode, deep: true);
				int num = 0;
				foreach (XmlNode childNode in iconctrl.ElementList.ChildNodes)
				{
					if (m_elementlistindex == num)
					{
						iconctrl.ElementList.InsertBefore(element.DataNode, childNode);
						return;
					}
					num++;
				}
				iconctrl.ElementList.AppendChild(element.DataNode);
			}
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			Element element = (Element)m_obj;
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Add)
			{
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Add(element);
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Add(element);
				iconctrl.ElementList.AppendChild(element.DataNode);
			}
			else if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Remove(element);
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Remove(element);
				iconctrl.ElementList.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryAnchor : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private double m_x;

		private double m_y;

		private int m_direction;

		private bool m_ashandle;

		private string m_param;

		private string m_value;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string result = "";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Add:
					result = "Add Anchor";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Delete:
					result = "Delete Anchor";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Move:
					result = "Move Anchor";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Motion:
					result = "Set Anchor Direction";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Handle:
					result = "Set Anchor Handle";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Param:
					result = "Change Anchor " + m_param;
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public HistoryAnchor(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode, string description)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_param = description;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_obj = obj;
			Anchor anchor = (Anchor)m_obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
				m_x = anchor.X;
				m_y = anchor.Y;
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Handle:
				m_ashandle = anchor.AsHandle;
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Param:
				m_value = anchor.GetParam(m_param);
				break;
			}
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			DoAnchor(iconctrl, m_subcode);
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			HistorySubcode subcode = m_subcode;
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Add)
			{
				subcode = HistorySubcode.Delete;
			}
			else if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				subcode = HistorySubcode.Add;
			}
			DoAnchor(iconctrl, subcode);
		}

		private void DoAnchor(IconCtrl iconctrl, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			Anchor anchor = (Anchor)m_obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Add:
				iconctrl.Anchors.Remove(anchor);
				iconctrl.AnchorList.RemoveChild(anchor.DataNode);
				iconctrl.IconPanel.Children.Remove(anchor);
				anchor.UpdateData("directinit", "false");
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Delete:
				iconctrl.Anchors.Add(anchor);
				iconctrl.AnchorList.AppendChild(anchor.DataNode);
				iconctrl.IconPanel.Children.Add(anchor);
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
			{
				double x = anchor.X;
				double y = anchor.Y;
				anchor.Move(m_x - x, m_y - y);
				anchor.Drag(m_x - x, m_y - y);
				anchor.EndMove();
				m_x = x;
				m_y = y;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.Motion:
			{
				int direction = anchor.Direction;
				anchor.Direction = m_direction;
				anchor.UpdateData("directinit", "false");
				anchor.UpdateData("direction", m_direction.ToString());
				m_direction = direction;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.Handle:
			{
				bool asHandle = anchor.AsHandle;
				anchor.AsHandle = m_ashandle;
				anchor.UpdateData("handle", m_ashandle.ToString());
				m_ashandle = asHandle;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.Param:
			{
				string param = anchor.GetParam(m_param);
				anchor.SetParam(m_param, m_value);
				anchor.UpdateData(m_param, m_value);
				m_value = param;
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryChange : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private string m_property;

		private string m_value;

		private string m_description;

		private bool m_combo;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string text = "Change";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Stroke:
					text = "Change Stroke";
					m_combo = true;
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Thickness:
					text = "Change Thickness";
					m_combo = true;
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Dash:
					text = "Change Dash";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Fill:
					text = "Change Fill";
					m_combo = true;
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Align:
					text = "Change Alignment";
					m_combo = true;
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.TextType:
					text = "Change Type";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.TextContents:
					text = "Change Contents";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.TextSize:
					text = "Change Size";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.ArrowAdd:
					text = "Add Arrow";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove:
					text = "Remove Arrow";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.ArrowSize:
					text = "Change Arrow Size";
					break;
				}
				return text + " " + m_name;
			}
		}

		public HistoryChange(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode, string description)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_description = description;
			m_combo = false;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_obj = obj;
			m_name = obj.GetType().ToString();
			if (m_name.StartsWith("IconCtrl."))
			{
				m_name = m_name.Substring(9);
			}
			if (obj is Text && subcode != HistorySubcode.Stroke)
			{
				HistoryText historyText = new HistoryText();
				historyText.Save((Text)obj);
				m_supplement = historyText;
				return;
			}
			Element element = (Element)obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.ArrowAdd:
			case HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove:
				return;
			case HistorySubcode.ArrowSize:
				m_property = ((Element)m_obj).GetArrowSize(m_description == "Start");
				return;
			}
			switch (m_subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Stroke:
				m_property = "Stroke";
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Thickness:
				m_property = "StrokeThickness";
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Dash:
				m_property = "StrokeDash";
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Fill:
				m_property = "Fill";
				break;
			}
			m_value = element.GetProperty(m_property);
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			Element element = (Element)m_obj;
			if (element is Text && m_subcode != HistorySubcode.Stroke)
			{
				RestoreText();
			}
			else if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowAdd || m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove)
			{
				element.AddArrow(m_description, m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove);
			}
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowSize)
			{
				string arrowSize = element.GetArrowSize(m_description == "Start");
				element.ChangeArrowSize(m_description == "Start", m_property, bNoHistory: true);
				m_property = arrowSize;
			}
			else
			{
				string property = element.GetProperty(m_property);
				element.ChangeProperty(m_property, m_value, iconctrl.GradientsNode, iconctrl.NSMGR);
				m_value = property;
			}
			XmlNode dataNode = element.DataNode;
			XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(iconctrl.WholeList);
			dataNode.ParentNode.ReplaceChild(xml, dataNode);
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowAdd || m_subcode == HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove)
			{
				Element element = (Element)m_obj;
				element.AddArrow(m_description, m_subcode != HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove);
				XmlNode dataNode = element.DataNode;
				XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(iconctrl.WholeList);
				dataNode.ParentNode.ReplaceChild(xml, dataNode);
			}
			else
			{
				Undo(iconctrl, ref modified);
			}
		}
	}
	public enum HistorySubcode
	{
		Add,
		Insert,
		Delete,
		Move,
		Resize,
		Rename,
		Lower,
		Raise,
		RotateLeft,
		RotateRight,
		FlipX,
		FlipY,
		Rotate180,
		Scale,
		GroupBegin,
		GroupEnd,
		Stroke,
		Thickness,
		Dash,
		Fill,
		Align,
		TextType,
		TextContents,
		TextSize,
		ArrowAdd,
		ArrowRemove,
		ArrowSize,
		IOtype,
		PortType,
		AutoStream,
		Motion,
		Handle,
		Param,
		Group,
		Ungroup
	}
	public class HistoryGrouping : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private List<Element> m_members;

		private Group m_parent;

		private int m_childindex;

		private int m_memberindex;

		private XmlNode m_savednode;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string result = "";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Group:
					result = "Group";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Ungroup:
					result = "Ungroup";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Delete:
					result = "Delete Group Member";
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public HistoryGrouping(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_members = new List<Element>();
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			base.Commit(iconctrl, obj, subcode);
			if (subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				return;
			}
			Group obj2 = (Group)m_obj;
			m_parent = obj2.Parent;
			foreach (Element member in obj2.Members)
			{
				m_members.Add(member);
			}
		}

		public override void Append(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_parent = (Group)obj;
			m_childindex = m_parent.Children.IndexOf((Element)m_obj);
			m_memberindex = m_parent.Members.IndexOf((Element)m_obj);
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			m_savednode = xmlDocument.ImportNode(((Element)m_obj).DataNode, deep: true);
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				Element element = (Element)m_obj;
				m_parent.Children.Insert(m_childindex, element);
				m_parent.Members.Insert(m_memberindex, element);
				m_parent.CalcSize(newgroup: false);
				element.DataNode = element.DataNode.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(m_savednode, deep: true);
				int num = 0;
				foreach (XmlNode childNode in m_parent.DataNode.ChildNodes)
				{
					if (m_memberindex == num)
					{
						m_parent.DataNode.InsertBefore(element.DataNode, childNode);
						return;
					}
					num++;
				}
				m_parent.DataNode.AppendChild(element.DataNode);
			}
			else
			{
				DoGroup(iconctrl, m_subcode);
			}
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				Element element = (Element)m_obj;
				m_parent.Children.Remove(element);
				m_parent.Members.Remove(element);
				m_parent.DataNode.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
				m_parent.CalcSize(newgroup: false);
			}
			else
			{
				DoGroup(iconctrl, (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Group) ? HistorySubcode.Ungroup : HistorySubcode.Group);
			}
		}

		private void DoGroup(IconCtrl iconctrl, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			Group obj = (Group)m_obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Group:
				if (m_parent == null)
				{
					obj.Ungroup(iconctrl.CurLayer, iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements, obj.DataNode);
				}
				else
				{
					obj.Ungroup(m_parent, m_parent.Members, obj.DataNode);
				}
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Ungroup:
			{
				foreach (Element member in m_members)
				{
					if (m_parent == null)
					{
						iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Remove(member);
					}
					else
					{
						m_parent.Children.Remove(member);
					}
					member.Move(0.0 - obj._bx, 0.0 - obj._by);
					obj.Add(member);
					if (m_parent == null)
					{
						iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Remove(member);
						iconctrl.ElementList.RemoveChild(member.DataNode);
					}
					else
					{
						m_parent.Members.Remove(member);
						m_parent.DataNode.RemoveChild(member.DataNode);
					}
				}
				XmlNode xml = obj.GetXml(obj.DataNode.OwnerDocument);
				if (m_parent == null)
				{
					iconctrl.CurLayer.Children.Add(obj);
					iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Insert(0, obj);
					iconctrl.ElementList.InsertBefore(xml, iconctrl.ElementList.FirstChild);
				}
				else
				{
					m_parent.Children.Add(obj);
					m_parent.Members.Add(obj);
					m_parent.DataNode.InsertBefore(xml, m_parent.DataNode.FirstChild);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryZIndex : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private Group m_group;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string text = "";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Raise:
					text = "Raise";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Lower:
					text = "Lower";
					break;
				}
				return text + " " + m_name;
			}
		}

		public HistoryZIndex(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_group = null;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_obj = obj;
			m_name = obj.GetType().ToString();
			if (m_name.StartsWith("IconCtrl."))
			{
				m_name = m_name.Substring(9);
			}
		}

		public override void Append(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_group = (Group)obj;
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			DoMove(iconctrl, m_subcode);
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			DoMove(iconctrl, (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Lower) ? HistorySubcode.Raise : HistorySubcode.Lower);
		}

		private void DoMove(IconCtrl iconctrl, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			Element element = (Element)m_obj;
			if (m_group != null)
			{
				int num = m_group.Members.IndexOf(element);
				m_group.Members.RemoveAt(num);
				XmlNode parentNode = element.DataNode.ParentNode;
				XmlNode refChild;
				if (subcode == HistorySubcode.Raise)
				{
					m_group.Members.Insert(num + 1, element);
					refChild = element.DataNode.NextSibling;
				}
				else
				{
					m_group.Members.Insert(num - 1, element);
					refChild = element.DataNode.PreviousSibling;
				}
				parentNode.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
				XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(iconctrl.WholeList);
				if (subcode == HistorySubcode.Raise)
				{
					parentNode.InsertAfter(xml, refChild);
				}
				else
				{
					parentNode.InsertBefore(xml, refChild);
				}
				m_group.RefreshMembers();
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.IndexOf(element);
				iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.RemoveAt(num2);
				XmlNode refChild2;
				if (subcode == HistorySubcode.Raise)
				{
					iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Insert(num2 + 1, element);
					refChild2 = iconctrl.NextElementNode(element.DataNode);
				}
				else
				{
					iconctrl.CurLayer.Elements.Insert(num2 - 1, element);
					refChild2 = iconctrl.PreviousElementNode(element.DataNode);
				}
				iconctrl.ElementList.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
				XmlNode xml2 = element.GetXml(iconctrl.WholeList);
				if (subcode == HistorySubcode.Raise)
				{
					iconctrl.ElementList.InsertAfter(xml2, refChild2);
				}
				else
				{
					iconctrl.ElementList.InsertBefore(xml2, refChild2);
				}
				iconctrl.RefreshElements();
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryGroup : HistoryItem
	{
		private List<History> m_list;

		private bool m_reverse;

		public override string Description => m_name;

		public bool Empty => m_list.Count == 0;

		public History GetLast
		{
			get
			{
				if (m_list.Count == 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return m_list[m_list.Count - 1];
			}
		}

		public HistoryGroup(string name, bool reverse)
		{
			m_name = name;
			m_reverse = reverse;
			m_list = new List<History>();
		}

		public void SetDescription(string description)
		{
			m_name = description;
		}

		public void Add(History history)
		{
			m_list.Add(history);
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_reverse)
			{
				foreach (History item in m_list)
				{
					item.Undo(iconctrl, ref modified);
				}
				return;
			}
			int count = m_list.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				History history = m_list[num];
				history.Undo(iconctrl, ref modified);
			}
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			foreach (History item in m_list)
			{
				item.Redo(iconctrl, ref modified);
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryLabel : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private double m_x;

		private double m_y;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string result = "";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Add:
					result = "Add LabelAt";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Move:
					result = "Move LabelAt";
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public HistoryLabel(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_obj = obj;
			LabelAt labelAt = (LabelAt)m_obj;
			labelAt.GetLocation(out m_x, out m_y);
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			LabelAt labelAt = (LabelAt)m_obj;
			switch (m_subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Add:
				iconctrl.RemoveLabel();
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
			{
				labelAt.GetLocation(out var x, out var y);
				labelAt.SetLocation(m_x, m_y);
				m_x = x;
				m_y = y;
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			LabelAt labelAt = (LabelAt)m_obj;
			switch (m_subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Add:
				iconctrl.RestoreLabel(labelAt);
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
			{
				labelAt.GetLocation(out var x, out var y);
				labelAt.SetLocation(m_x, m_y);
				m_x = x;
				m_y = y;
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryMove : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private float m_x;

		private float m_y;

		private string m_description;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string text = "Move";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Resize:
					text = ((m_description == null) ? "Resize" : m_description);
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Scale:
					text = "Scale";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.FlipX:
					text = "Flip X";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.FlipY:
					text = "Flip Y";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.RotateLeft:
					text = "Rotate Left";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.RotateRight:
					text = "Rotate Right";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Rotate180:
					text = "Rotate 180 Degrees";
					break;
				}
				return text + " " + m_name;
			}
		}

		public HistoryMove(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode, string description)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
			m_x = (m_y = 0f);
			m_description = description;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			base.Commit(iconctrl, obj, subcode);
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			if (m_obj is Line)
			{
				HistoryLine historyLine = new HistoryLine();
				historyLine.Save((Line)m_obj);
				HistoryLine historyLine2 = (HistoryLine)m_supplement;
				historyLine2.Restore((Line)m_obj);
				m_supplement = historyLine;
				((Line)m_obj).Move(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			else if (m_obj is Rectangle)
			{
				HistoryRectangle historyRectangle = new HistoryRectangle();
				historyRectangle.Save((Rectangle)m_obj);
				HistoryRectangle historyRectangle2 = (HistoryRectangle)m_supplement;
				historyRectangle2.Restore((Rectangle)m_obj);
				m_supplement = historyRectangle;
				((Rectangle)m_obj).Move(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			else if (m_obj is Ellipse)
			{
				HistoryEllipse historyEllipse = new HistoryEllipse();
				historyEllipse.Save((Ellipse)m_obj);
				HistoryEllipse historyEllipse2 = (HistoryEllipse)m_supplement;
				historyEllipse2.Restore((Ellipse)m_obj);
				m_supplement = historyEllipse;
				((Ellipse)m_obj).Move(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			else if (m_obj is PolyElement)
			{
				HistoryPolygon historyPolygon = new HistoryPolygon();
				historyPolygon.Save((PolyElement)m_obj);
				HistoryPolygon historyPolygon2 = (HistoryPolygon)m_supplement;
				historyPolygon2.Restore((PolyElement)m_obj);
				m_supplement = historyPolygon;
				((PolyElement)m_obj).Move(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			else if (m_obj is Path)
			{
				HistoryPath historyPath = new HistoryPath();
				historyPath.Save((Path)m_obj);
				HistoryPath historyPath2 = (HistoryPath)m_supplement;
				historyPath2.Restore((Path)m_obj);
				m_supplement = historyPath;
				((Path)m_obj).Move(0.0, 0.0);
			}
			else if (m_obj is Text)
			{
				RestoreText();
			}
			else if (m_obj is Group)
			{
				Group obj = (Group)m_obj;
				HistoryGroupObj historyGroupObj = new HistoryGroupObj();
				historyGroupObj.Save(obj);
				HistoryGroupObj historyGroupObj2 = (HistoryGroupObj)m_supplement;
				historyGroupObj2.Restore(obj);
				m_supplement = historyGroupObj;
				obj.Move(0.0, 0.0);
				if (m_subcode != HistorySubcode.Move)
				{
					obj._rform.Angle = obj._angle;
					obj._sform.ScaleX = obj._scalex;
					obj._sform.ScaleY = obj._scaley;
					obj.AddHandles();
				}
			}
			if (m_obj.Parent is Group)
			{
				((Group)m_obj.Parent).CalcSize(newgroup: false);
			}
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			Undo(iconctrl, ref modified);
		}
	}
	public class HistoryLine : HistoryObj
	{
		private double _x1;

		private double _y1;

		private double _x2;

		private double _y2;

		public void Save(Line line)
		{
			_x1 = line._x1;
			_y1 = line._y1;
			_x2 = line._x2;
			_y2 = line._y2;
		}

		public void Restore(Line line)
		{
			line._x1 = _x1;
			line._y1 = _y1;
			line._x2 = _x2;
			line._y2 = _y2;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryRectangle : HistoryObj
	{
		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private double _width;

		private double _height;

		public void Save(Rectangle rect)
		{
			_x = rect._x;
			_y = rect._y;
			_width = rect._width;
			_height = rect._height;
		}

		public void Restore(Rectangle rect)
		{
			rect._x = _x;
			rect._y = _y;
			rect._width = _width;
			rect._height = _height;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryEllipse : HistoryObj
	{
		private double _cx;

		private double _cy;

		private double _rx;

		private double _ry;

		public void Save(Ellipse ellipse)
		{
			_cx = ellipse._cx;
			_cy = ellipse._cy;
			_rx = ellipse._rx;
			_ry = ellipse._ry;
		}

		public void Restore(Ellipse ellipse)
		{
			ellipse._cx = _cx;
			ellipse._cy = _cy;
			ellipse._rx = _rx;
			ellipse._ry = _ry;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryPolygon : HistoryObj
	{
		private List<System.Windows.Point> _points;

		public void Save(PolyElement polygon)
		{
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
