			for (int i = 0; i < polygon._points.Count; i++)
			{
				_points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(polygon._points[i].X, polygon._points[i].Y));
			}
		}

		public void Restore(PolyElement polygon)
		{
			int count = polygon._points.Count;
			for (int i = _points.Count; i < count; i++)
			{
				polygon.DeletePoint(polygon._points[polygon._points.Count - 1]);
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < _points.Count; j++)
			{
				if (j < polygon._points.Count)
				{
					System.Windows.Point value = polygon._points[j];
					value.X = _points[j].X;
					value.Y = _points[j].Y;
					polygon._points[j] = value;
				}
			}
			if (_points.Count > count)
			{
				for (int k = count; k < _points.Count; k++)
				{
					polygon.AddPoint(_points[k]);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				polygon.RefreshPointNodes();
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryPath : HistoryObj
	{
		private System.Windows.Point[] _points;

		private double _rx;

		private double _ry;

		private double _angle;

		private int _startangle;

		private int _endangle;

		public void Save(Path path)
		{
			_points = new System.Windows.Point[path._points.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < path._points.Length; i++)
			{
				ref System.Windows.Point reference = ref _points[i];
				reference = new System.Windows.Point(path._points[i].X, path._points[i].Y);
			}
			_rx = path._rx;
			_ry = path._ry;
			_angle = path._angle;
			_startangle = path._startangle;
			_endangle = path._endangle;
		}

		public void Restore(Path path)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < path._points.Length; i++)
			{
				path._points[i].X = _points[i].X;
				path._points[i].Y = _points[i].Y;
			}
			path._rx = _rx;
			path._ry = _ry;
			path._angle = _angle;
			path._startangle = _startangle;
			path._endangle = _endangle;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryText : HistoryObj
	{
		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private string _text;

		private double _size;

		private double _width;

		private double _height;

		private int _alignment;

		private bool _bold;

		private bool _italic;

		private bool _underline;

		public void Save(Text text)
		{
			_x = text._x;
			_y = text._y;
			_text = text._text;
			_size = text._size;
			_width = text._width;
			_height = text._height;
			_alignment = text._alignment;
			_bold = text._bold;
			_italic = text._italic;
			_underline = text._underline;
		}

		public void Restore(Text text)
		{
			text._x = _x;
			text._y = _y;
			if (text._text != _text)
			{
				text.TextContent(_text);
			}
			if (text._size != _size)
			{
				text.TextSize(_size.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			text._width = _width;
			text._height = _height;
			if (text._alignment != _alignment)
			{
				text._alignment = _alignment;
				string value = "Left";
				if (_alignment == 1)
				{
					value = "Center";
				}
				else if (_alignment == 2)
				{
					value = "Right";
				}
				text.ChangeProperty("Alignment", value, null, null);
			}
			if (text._bold != _bold)
			{
				text.TextStyle("BoldText", _bold);
			}
			if (text._italic != _italic)
			{
				text.TextStyle("ItalicText", _italic);
			}
			if (text._underline != _underline)
			{
				text.TextStyle("UnderText", _underline);
			}
		}
	}
	public class HistoryGroupObj : HistoryObj
	{
		private double _minx;

		private double _miny;

		private double _maxx;

		private double _maxy;

		private double _bx;

		private double _by;

		private double _cx;

		private double _cy;

		private double _scalex;

		private double _scaley;

		private double _angle;

		public void Save(Group group)
		{
			_minx = group._minx;
			_miny = group._miny;
			_maxx = group._maxx;
			_maxy = group._maxy;
			_bx = group._bx;
			_by = group._by;
			_cx = group._cx;
			_cy = group._cy;
			_scalex = group._scalex;
			_scaley = group._scaley;
			_angle = group._angle;
		}

		public void Restore(Group group)
		{
			group._minx = _minx;
			group._miny = _miny;
			group._maxx = _maxx;
			group._maxy = _maxy;
			group._bx = _bx;
			group._by = _by;
			group._cx = _cx;
			group._cy = _cy;
			group._scalex = _scalex;
			group._scaley = _scaley;
			group._angle = _angle;
		}
	}
	public class HistoryPort : HistoryItem
	{
		private HistorySubcode m_subcode;

		private double m_x;

		private double m_y;

		private int m_side;

		private new string m_name;

		private string m_type;

		private string m_porttype;

		private int m_direction;

		public override string Description
		{
			get
			{
				string result = "";
				switch (m_subcode)
				{
				case HistorySubcode.Add:
					result = "Add Port";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Delete:
					result = "Delete Port";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Move:
					result = "Move Port";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.Rename:
					result = "Rename Port";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.IOtype:
					result = "Port IOtype Change";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.PortType:
					result = "Port Type Change";
					break;
				case HistorySubcode.AutoStream:
					result = "Port Auto Stream Set";
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public HistoryPort(BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
			: base(obj)
		{
			m_subcode = subcode;
		}

		public override void Commit(IconCtrl iconctrl, BaseObj obj, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			m_obj = obj;
			Port port = (Port)m_obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
				port.GetLocation(out m_x, out m_y, out m_side);
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Rename:
				m_name = port.Name;
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.IOtype:
				m_type = port.IOtype;
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.PortType:
				m_porttype = port.PortType;
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.AutoStream:
				m_direction = port.StreamDirection;
				break;
			}
		}

		public override void Undo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			DoPort(iconctrl, m_subcode);
		}

		public override void Redo(IconCtrl iconctrl, ref List<string> modified)
		{
			HistorySubcode subcode = m_subcode;
			if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Add)
			{
				subcode = HistorySubcode.Delete;
			}
			else if (m_subcode == HistorySubcode.Delete)
			{
				subcode = HistorySubcode.Add;
			}
			DoPort(iconctrl, subcode);
		}

		private void DoPort(IconCtrl iconctrl, HistorySubcode subcode)
		{
			Port port = (Port)m_obj;
			switch (subcode)
			{
			case HistorySubcode.Add:
				iconctrl.Ports.Remove(port);
				iconctrl.PortList.RemoveChild(port.DataNode);
				iconctrl.IconPanel.Children.Remove(port);
				port.UpdateData("autostreaminit", "false");
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Delete:
				iconctrl.Ports.Add(port);
				iconctrl.PortList.AppendChild(port.DataNode);
				iconctrl.IconPanel.Children.Add(port);
				break;
			case HistorySubcode.Move:
			{
				port.GetLocation(out var x, out var y, out var side);
				port.Move(m_x, m_y);
				port.AccessSide = m_side;
				m_x = x;
				m_y = y;
				m_side = side;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.Rename:
			{
				string name = port.Name;
				port.ChangeName(m_name, standalone: false);
				port.UpdateData("name", m_name);
				m_name = name;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.IOtype:
			{
				string iOtype = port.IOtype;
				port.ChangeIOType(m_type);
				port.UpdateData("inport", (m_type == "0") ? "true" : "false");
				port.UpdateData("outport", (m_type == "1") ? "true" : "false");
				port.UpdateData("biport", (m_type == "-1") ? "true" : "false");
				m_type = iOtype;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.PortType:
			{
				string portType = port.PortType;
				port.ChangePortType(m_porttype, standalone: false);
				port.UpdateData("porttype", iconctrl.PortTypeDisplayName(m_porttype));
				m_porttype = portType;
				break;
			}
			case HistorySubcode.AutoStream:
			{
				string value = port.DataNode.Attributes["direction"].Value;
				port.StreamDirection = m_direction;
				port.UpdateData("autostreaminit", "false");
				port.UpdateData("direction", m_direction.ToString());
				m_direction = int.Parse(value);
				break;
			}
			}
		}
	}
	public class IconEditorEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		private string m_EventName;

		private string m_EventValue;

		public string Event => m_EventName;

		public string Value => m_EventValue;

		public IconEditorEventArgs(string EventName, string EventValue)
		{
			m_EventName = EventName;
			m_EventValue = EventValue;
		}
	}
	public delegate void IconEditorEventHandler(object sender, IconEditorEventArgs e);
	public class LabelAt : BaseObj
	{
		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _back;

		private bool _indrag;

		private double _x0;

		private double _y0;

		public LabelAt(XmlNode node, IconCtrl parent)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			double num = 100.0;
			if (_parent.KBase)
			{
				num = 2500.0;
			}
			_x = GetDouble(node, "x") * num;
			_y = GetDouble(node, "y") * num;
			if (!_parent.HysysIcon)
			{
				_y = 0.0 - _y;
			}
			base.MouseDown += LabelAt_MouseDown;
		}

		public LabelAt(double x, double y, IconCtrl parent)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			base.MouseDown += LabelAt_MouseDown;
		}

		private void LabelAt_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!_indrag && _parent.DragLabelAt(this))
			{
				_x0 = _x;
				_y0 = _y;
				_indrag = true;
			}
		}

		public void Render(bool visible)
		{
			base.ToolTip = "Label Position";
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new ScaleTransform(1.0, 1.0));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y));
			base.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, -6.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, -6.0);
			_back.Width = 12.0;
			_back.Height = 12.0;
			_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Add(_back);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline();
			polyline.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Blue;
			polyline.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-6.0, 0.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, -6.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(6.0, 0.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 6.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(-6.0, 0.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(6.0, 0.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, -6.0));
			polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 6.0));
			base.Children.Add(polyline);
			if (!visible)
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
			Panel.SetZIndex(this, 100);
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x0 += dx;
			_y0 += dy;
			_tform.X = _x0;
			_tform.Y = _y0;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public void EndMove()
		{
			_indrag = false;
			_x = _x0;
			_y = _y0;
			_x0 = 0.0;
			_y0 = 0.0;
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.SelectSingleNode("y:LabelAt", nsmgr);
			xmlNode?.ParentNode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
			xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("LabelAt", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			double num = 100.0;
			if (_parent.KBase)
			{
				num = 2500.0;
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "x", (_x / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			if (!_parent.HysysIcon)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "y", ((0.0 - _y) / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "y", (_y / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
		}

		public void GetLocation(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x;
			y = _y;
		}

		public void SetLocation(double x, double y)
		{
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_tform.X = _x;
			_tform.Y = _y;
		}
	}
	public class Layer : Canvas
	{
		private List<Element> _elements;

		private int _type;

		private Canvas _parent;

		private double _relative;

		private double _repeat;

		private string _visibility = "";

		public List<Element> Elements => _elements;

		public int Type
		{
			get
			{
				return _type;
			}
			set
			{
				_type = value;
			}
		}

		public double Relative
		{
			get
			{
				return _relative;
			}
			set
			{
				_relative = value;
			}
		}

		public double Repeat
		{
			get
			{
				return _repeat;
			}
			set
			{
				_repeat = value;
			}
		}

		public string visibility
		{
			get
			{
				return _visibility;
			}
			set
			{
				_visibility = value;
				Binding binding = new Binding();
				binding.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _visibility;
				SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		public string growthmoniker { get; set; }

		public string labelinfo { get; set; }

		public Layer(Canvas parent)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			parent.Children.Add(this);
			_elements = new List<Element>();
		}

		public void Setup()
		{
			if (_type == 1 || _type == 2)
			{
				base.RenderTransform = new TranslateTransform(0.0, _relative);
			}
		}

		public void GetField(ref double minx0, ref double miny0, ref double maxx0, ref double maxy0)
		{
			double minx1 = 10000000000.0;
			double miny1 = 10000000000.0;
			double maxx1 = -10000000000.0;
			double maxy1 = -10000000000.0;
			foreach (Element element in Elements)
			{
				element.GetField(ref minx1, ref maxx1, ref miny1, ref maxy1);
			}
			if (_type == 1 || _type == 2)
			{
				miny1 += _relative;
				maxy1 += _relative;
			}
			if (minx1 < minx0)
			{
				minx0 = minx1;
			}
			if (miny1 < miny0)
			{
				miny0 = miny1;
			}
			if (maxx1 > maxx0)
			{
				maxx0 = maxx1;
			}
			if (maxy1 > maxy0)
			{
				maxy0 = maxy1;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ParameterEditor
	{
		private XmlDocument _context;

		private XmlDocument _adsmap;

		private Grid _field;

		private ComboBox _modelmap;

		private int _count;

		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private List<ComboBox> _adsmaps;

		public ParameterEditor(Grid field, ComboBox modelmap, XmlDocument attribdoc, XmlDocument adsmap, IconCtrl parent)
		{
			_field = field;
			_modelmap = modelmap;
			_context = attribdoc;
			_adsmap = adsmap;
			_parent = parent;
			if (_modelmap != null)
			{
				_modelmap.SelectionChanged += _modelmap_SelectionChanged;
				_modelmap.DataContext = _adsmap;
				Binding binding = new Binding
				{
					XPath = "/ADSMap/Model/@name"
				};
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(_modelmap, ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty, binding);
				_adsmaps = new List<ComboBox>();
			}
			if (attribdoc == null || field == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in attribdoc.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Attributes["type"] != null)
				{
					AddItem(count++, childNode);
				}
			}
			_count = count;
		}

		private void _modelmap_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			string text = (string)comboBox.SelectedValue;
			foreach (ComboBox adsmap in _adsmaps)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding();
				binding.XPath = "/ADSMap/Model[@name='" + text + "']/Variable/@id";
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(adsmap, ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		public void Reload(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			_field.Children.Clear();
			_field.RowDefinitions.Clear();
			_modelmap.SelectedIndex = -1;
			_count = 0;
			int count = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in doc.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Attributes["type"] == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (childNode.Attributes["global"].Value == "True")
				{
					if (childNode.Attributes["override"] != null)
					{
						list.Add(childNode.Name);
					}
					else if (list.Contains(childNode.Name))
					{
						continue;
					}
				}
				AddItem(count++, childNode);
			}
			_count = count;
		}

		public void Add(XmlNode node)
		{
			AddItem(_count++, node);
		}

		private void AddItem(int ith, XmlNode node)
		{
			RowDefinition rowDefinition = new RowDefinition();
			rowDefinition.Height = new GridLength(30.0);
			_field.RowDefinitions.Add(rowDefinition);
			string[] array = null;
			if (node.Attributes["adsmap"] != null)
			{
				string value = node.Attributes["adsmap"].Value;
				array = value.Split('.');
				if (_modelmap.SelectedIndex < 0)
				{
					_modelmap.SelectedValue = array[0];
				}
			}
			bool flag = true;
			if (node.Attributes["global"] != null && node.Attributes["global"].Value == "False")
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			bool flag2 = false;
			TextBlock textBlock = new TextBlock();
			textBlock.Text = node.Name;
			if (flag)
			{
				textBlock.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			}
			Grid.SetColumn(textBlock, 0);
			Grid.SetRow(textBlock, ith);
			_field.Children.Add(textBlock);
			StackPanel stackPanel = new StackPanel();
			stackPanel.Orientation = Orientation.Horizontal;
			Grid.SetColumn(stackPanel, 1);
			Grid.SetRow(stackPanel, ith);
			string value2 = node.Attributes["type"].Value;
			switch (value2)
			{
			case "string":
			case "COLOR":
			{
				TextBox textBox4 = new TextBox();
				textBox4.Width = 200.0;
				textBox4.Text = node.InnerText;
				textBox4.Tag = node;
				textBox4.KeyUp += tb_KeyUp;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox4);
				if (value2 == "string")
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "boolean":
			case "Visibility":
			{
				string text = ((value2 == "Visibility") ? "Visible,Hidden" : ((node.Attributes["value"] != null) ? node.Attributes["value"].Value : "On,Off"));
				string[] array2 = text.Split(',');
				RadioButton radioButton = new RadioButton();
				radioButton.Margin = new Thickness(5.0);
				radioButton.GroupName = "radio" + ith;
				radioButton.Content = array2[0];
				if (array2[0] == node.InnerText || string.IsNullOrEmpty(node.InnerText))
				{
					radioButton.IsChecked = true;
				}
				radioButton.Tag = node;
				radioButton.Checked += rb_Checked;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(radioButton);
				RadioButton radioButton2 = new RadioButton();
				radioButton2.Margin = new Thickness(5.0);
				radioButton2.GroupName = "radio" + ith;
				radioButton2.Content = array2[1];
				if (array2[1] == node.InnerText)
				{
					radioButton2.IsChecked = true;
				}
				radioButton2.Tag = node;
				radioButton2.Checked += rb_Checked;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(radioButton2);
				flag2 = true;
				break;
			}
			case "double":
			case "int":
			{
				double result2 = 0.0;
				if (node.Attributes["min"] != null)
				{
					double.TryParse(node.Attributes["min"].Value, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result2);
				}
				double result3 = 1.0;
				if (node.Attributes["max"] != null)
				{
					double.TryParse(node.Attributes["max"].Value, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result3);
				}
				TextBlock textBlock6 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock6.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock6.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock6.Text = result2.ToString();
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock6);
				Slider slider2 = new Slider();
				slider2.Width = 250.0;
				slider2.AutoToolTipPlacement = AutoToolTipPlacement.TopLeft;
				slider2.Minimum = result2;
				slider2.Maximum = result3;
				double result4 = 0.0;
				double.TryParse(node.InnerText, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result4);
				slider2.Value = result4;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(slider2);
				slider2.Tag = node;
				slider2.ValueChanged += slider_ValueChanged;
				TextBlock textBlock7 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock7.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock7.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock7.Text = result3.ToString();
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock7);
				flag2 = true;
				break;
			}
			case "XYList":
			{
				TextBlock textBlock4 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock4.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 5.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				textBlock4.Width = 60.0;
				textBlock4.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock4.Text = "X values";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock4);
				TextBox textBox = new TextBox();
				textBox.Name = "xvalues";
				textBox.Width = 200.0;
				textBox.Tag = node;
				textBox.KeyUp += xylist_KeyUp;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox);
				TextBlock textBlock5 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock5.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 5.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				textBlock5.Width = 60.0;
				textBlock5.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock5.Text = "Y values";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock5);
				textBox = new TextBox();
				textBox.Name = "yvalues";
				textBox.Width = 200.0;
				textBox.Tag = node;
				textBox.KeyUp += xylist_KeyUp;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox);
				break;
			}
			case "enum":
			{
				string value3 = node.Attributes["values"].Value;
				string[] array3 = value3.Split(',');
				ComboBox comboBox2 = new ComboBox();
				comboBox2.SelectedValue = node.InnerText;
				comboBox2.Width = 100.0;
				comboBox2.Height = 25.0;
				string[] array4 = array3;
				foreach (string newItem in array4)
				{
					comboBox2.Items.Add(newItem);
				}
				stackPanel.Children.Add(comboBox2);
				comboBox2.SelectionChanged += enumvalues_SelectionChanged;
				comboBox2.DataContext = node;
				flag2 = true;
				break;
			}
			case "Gradient":
			{
				TextBlock textBlock12 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock12.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
				textBlock12.Text = node.Attributes["base"].Value;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock12);
				TextBlock textBlock13 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock13.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				textBlock13.Text = "Colors";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock13);
				int num = 1;
				while (true)
				{
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute9 = node.Attributes["color" + num];
					if (xmlAttribute9 == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					TextBox textBox5 = new TextBox();
					textBox5.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
					textBox5.Width = 80.0;
					textBox5.Text = xmlAttribute9.InnerText;
					textBox5.Tag = xmlAttribute9;
					textBox5.KeyUp += Gradient_KeyUp;
					stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox5);
					num++;
				}
				TextBlock textBlock14 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock14.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
				textBlock14.Text = "Offsets";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock14);
				num = 1;
				while (true)
				{
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute10 = node.Attributes["offset" + num];
					if (xmlAttribute10 == null)
					{
						break;
					}
					TextBox textBox6 = new TextBox();
					textBox6.Margin = new Thickness(5.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
					textBox6.Width = 80.0;
					textBox6.Text = xmlAttribute10.InnerText;
					textBox6.Tag = xmlAttribute10;
					textBox6.KeyUp += Gradient_KeyUp;
					stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox6);
					num++;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "ColorAnimation":
			case "DoubleAnimation":
			{
				TextBlock textBlock8 = new TextBlock();
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock8);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute4 = node.Attributes["from"];
				TextBox textBox2 = new TextBox();
				textBox2.Width = 60.0;
				textBox2.Text = xmlAttribute4.InnerText;
				textBox2.Tag = xmlAttribute4;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox2);
				TextBlock textBlock9 = new TextBlock();
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock9);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute5 = node.Attributes["to"];
				TextBox textBox3 = new TextBox();
				textBox3.Width = 60.0;
				textBox3.Text = xmlAttribute5.InnerText;
				textBox3.Tag = xmlAttribute5;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBox3);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute6 = node.Attributes["duration"];
				TextBlock textBlock10 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock10.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock10.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock10.Text = "0";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock10);
				Slider slider3 = new Slider();
				slider3.Width = 100.0;
				slider3.AutoToolTipPlacement = AutoToolTipPlacement.TopLeft;
				slider3.Minimum = 0.0;
				slider3.Maximum = 4.0;
				double result5 = 0.0;
				double.TryParse(xmlAttribute6.InnerText, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result5);
				if (result5 > 0.0)
				{
					result5 = 1000.0 / result5;
				}
				slider3.Value = result5;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(slider3);
				slider3.Tag = xmlAttribute6;
				slider3.ValueChanged += speed_ValueChanged;
				TextBlock textBlock11 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock11.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock11.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock11.Text = "4";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock11);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute7 = node.Attributes["autoreverse"];
				CheckBox checkBox2 = new CheckBox();
				checkBox2.Content = "Auto\nReverse";
				checkBox2.IsChecked = bool.Parse(xmlAttribute7.Value);
				checkBox2.Tag = xmlAttribute7;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(checkBox2);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute8 = node.Attributes["run"];
				CheckBox checkBox3 = new CheckBox();
				checkBox3.Content = "Run";
				checkBox3.Tag = node;
				if (xmlAttribute8 != null)
				{
					checkBox3.IsChecked = bool.Parse(xmlAttribute8.Value);
				}
				stackPanel.Children.Add(checkBox3);
				if (value2 == "ColorAnimation")
				{
					textBlock8.Text = "Color1";
					textBlock9.Text = "Color2";
					textBox2.KeyUp += ColorAnim_KeyUp;
					textBox3.KeyUp += ColorAnim_KeyUp;
					checkBox3.Click += ColorAnim_Click;
					checkBox2.Click += ColorAnim_Click2;
				}
				else
				{
					textBlock8.Text = "From";
					textBlock9.Text = "To";
					textBox2.KeyUp += DoubleAnim_KeyUp;
					textBox3.KeyUp += DoubleAnim_KeyUp;
					checkBox3.Click += DoubleAnim_Click;
					checkBox2.Click += DoubleAnim_Click2;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "DashAnimation":
			{
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes["direction"];
				ComboBox comboBox = new ComboBox();
				comboBox.Width = 100.0;
				comboBox.Height = 25.0;
				comboBox.Items.Add("Forward");
				comboBox.Items.Add("Backward");
				comboBox.SelectedIndex = int.Parse(xmlAttribute.Value);
				comboBox.SelectionChanged += direction_SelectionChanged;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(comboBox);
				comboBox.Tag = xmlAttribute;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = node.Attributes["duration"];
				TextBlock textBlock2 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock2.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock2.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock2.Text = "0";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock2);
				Slider slider = new Slider();
				slider.Width = 100.0;
				slider.AutoToolTipPlacement = AutoToolTipPlacement.TopLeft;
				slider.Minimum = 0.0;
				slider.Maximum = 4.0;
				double result = 0.0;
				double.TryParse(xmlAttribute2.InnerText, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out result);
				if (result > 0.0)
				{
					result = 1000.0 / result;
				}
				slider.Value = result;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(slider);
				slider.Tag = xmlAttribute2;
				slider.ValueChanged += speed_ValueChanged;
				TextBlock textBlock3 = new TextBlock();
				textBlock3.Width = 30.0;
				textBlock3.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
				textBlock3.Text = "4";
				stackPanel.Children.Add(textBlock3);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute3 = node.Attributes["run"];
				CheckBox checkBox = new CheckBox();
				checkBox.Content = "Run";
				checkBox.Tag = node;
				if (xmlAttribute3 != null)
				{
					checkBox.IsChecked = bool.Parse(xmlAttribute3.Value);
				}
				checkBox.Click += DoubleAnim_Click;
				stackPanel.Children.Add(checkBox);
				break;
			}
			}
			if (!flag && flag2)
			{
				ComboBox comboBox3 = new ComboBox();
				comboBox3.Width = 75.0;
				comboBox3.Height = 25.0;
				comboBox3.DataContext = _adsmap;
				if (array != null)
				{
					Binding binding = new Binding();
					binding.XPath = "/ADSMap/Model[@name='" + array[0] + "']/Variable/@id";
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(comboBox3, ItemsControl.ItemsSourceProperty, binding);
					comboBox3.SelectedValue = array[1];
				}
				comboBox3.ToolTip = "ADS vaiable map";
				Grid.SetColumn(comboBox3, 2);
				Grid.SetRow(comboBox3, ith);
				_field.Children.Add(comboBox3);
				_adsmaps.Add(comboBox3);
				comboBox3.Tag = node;
				comboBox3.SelectionChanged += adsmap_SelectionChanged;
			}
			else
			{
				Grid.SetColumnSpan(stackPanel, 2);
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				Button button = new Button();
				button.Template = _parent.Resources["DeleteButton"] as ControlTemplate;
				button.Width = 16.0;
				button.Height = 16.0;
				button.DataContext = node;
				button.Click += deletebutton_Click;
				Grid.SetColumn(button, 3);
				Grid.SetRow(button, ith);
				_field.Children.Add(button);
			}
			else
			{
				CheckBox checkBox4 = new CheckBox();
				checkBox4.Content = "Override";
				checkBox4.IsChecked = node.Attributes["override"] != null;
				checkBox4.Tag = node;
				checkBox4.Click += over_Click;
				Grid.SetColumn(checkBox4, 2);
				Grid.SetRow(checkBox4, ith);
				_field.Children.Add(checkBox4);
			}
			_field.Children.Add(stackPanel);
		}

		private void direction_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)comboBox.Tag;
			xmlAttribute.Value = comboBox.SelectedIndex.ToString();
			_parent.UpdateAnimation(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
		}

		private void enumvalues_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
			xmlNode.InnerText = (string)comboBox.SelectedValue;
			if (_parent.ReferenceIcon)
			{
				_parent.SelectIcon(xmlNode.InnerText);
			}
		}

		private void over_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)checkBox.Tag;
			if (checkBox.IsChecked.Value)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = _context.ImportNode(xmlNode, deep: true);
				xmlNode2.Attributes["global"].Value = "False";
				XmlAttribute node = _context.CreateAttribute("override");
				xmlNode2.Attributes.Append(node);
				_context.DocumentElement.InsertBefore(xmlNode2, xmlNode);
				ReplaceTag(xmlNode, xmlNode2);
			}
			else
			{
				XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = _context.DocumentElement.SelectNodes(xmlNode.Name);
				if (xmlNodeList.Count == 2)
				{
					_context.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(xmlNodeList[0]);
					ReplaceTag(xmlNodeList[0], xmlNodeList[1]);
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReplaceTag(XmlNode oldtag, XmlNode newtag)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in _field.Children)
			{
				if (!(child is StackPanel))
				{
					continue;
				}
				StackPanel stackPanel = (StackPanel)child;
				foreach (UIElement child2 in stackPanel.Children)
				{
					if (child2 is FrameworkElement)
					{
						FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)child2;
						if (frameworkElement.Tag == oldtag)
						{
							frameworkElement.Tag = newtag;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void adsmap_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.Tag;
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = xmlNode.Attributes["adsmap"];
			if (xmlAttribute == null)
			{
				xmlAttribute = _context.CreateAttribute("adsmap");
				xmlNode.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			}
			xmlAttribute.Value = (string)_modelmap.SelectedValue + "." + comboBox.SelectedValue;
		}

		private void ColorAnim_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)checkBox.Tag;
			AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = _parent.AnimatedBrush(xmlNode.Name);
			animatedBrush.Run(xmlNode.Name, checkBox.IsChecked.Value);
			_parent.UpdateAnimatedGradient(animatedBrush, runflag: true);
		}

		private void ColorAnim_Click2(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)checkBox.Tag;
			xmlAttribute.InnerText = checkBox.IsChecked.ToString();
			AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = _parent.AnimatedBrush(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement.Name);
			animatedBrush.Update(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
			_parent.UpdateAnimatedGradient(animatedBrush, runflag: false);
		}

		private void ColorAnim_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)textBox.Tag;
				xmlAttribute.InnerText = textBox.Text;
				AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = _parent.AnimatedBrush(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement.Name);
				animatedBrush.Update(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
				_parent.UpdateAnimatedGradient(animatedBrush, runflag: false);
			}
		}

		private void DoubleAnim_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)checkBox.Tag;
			_parent.ToggleAnimation(checkBox.IsChecked.Value, xmlNode.Name);
		}

		private void DoubleAnim_Click2(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)checkBox.Tag;
			xmlAttribute.InnerText = checkBox.IsChecked.ToString();
			_parent.UpdateAnimation(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
		}

		private void DoubleAnim_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)textBox.Tag;
				xmlAttribute.InnerText = textBox.Text;
				_parent.UpdateAnimation(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
			}
		}

		private void deletebutton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Button button = sender as Button;
			XmlNode oldChild = button.DataContext as XmlNode;
			_context.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(oldChild);
			Reload(_context);
		}

		private void tb_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)textBox.Tag;
				xmlNode.InnerText = textBox.Text;
				_parent.UpdateTextParam(xmlNode.Name);
			}
		}

		private void Gradient_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)textBox.Tag;
				xmlAttribute.InnerText = textBox.Text;
				XmlNode ownerElement = xmlAttribute.OwnerElement;
				_parent.UpdateDerivedGradient(ownerElement);
			}
		}

		private void xylist_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Return)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)textBox.Tag;
				xmlNode.Attributes[textBox.Name].Value = textBox.Text;
			}
		}

		private void slider_ValueChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			Slider slider = (Slider)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)slider.Tag;
			string value = xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value;
			if (value == "int")
			{
				xmlNode.InnerText = ((int)(slider.Value + 0.4)).ToString();
			}
			else
			{
				xmlNode.InnerText = slider.Value.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
		}

		private void speed_ValueChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<double> e)
		{
			Slider slider = (Slider)sender;
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = (XmlAttribute)slider.Tag;
			double value = slider.Value;
			if (value > 0.01)
			{
				xmlAttribute.Value = ((int)(1000.0 / value)).ToString();
				string value2 = xmlAttribute.OwnerElement.Attributes["type"].Value;
				if (value2 == "ColorAnimation")
				{
					AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = _parent.AnimatedBrush(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement.Name);
					animatedBrush.Update(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
					_parent.UpdateAnimatedGradient(animatedBrush, runflag: false);
				}
				else
				{
					_parent.UpdateAnimation(xmlAttribute.OwnerElement);
				}
			}
		}

		private void rb_Checked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RadioButton radioButton = (RadioButton)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)radioButton.Tag;
			xmlNode.InnerText = (string)radioButton.Content;
		}
	}
	public class Path : Element
	{
		private string _data;

		internal System.Windows.Point[] _points;

		internal System.Windows.Point[] _wpoints;

		private bool _vertical;

		private bool _nonstandard;

		internal double _rx;

		internal double _ry;

		internal double _wrx;

		internal double _wry;

		internal double _angle;

		internal int _startangle;

		internal int _endangle;

		private int _large;

		private int _direction;

		private bool _closed;

		private bool _largearc;

		private bool _circular;

		private bool _circle;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _back;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path _path;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse _shadow;

		private double _rxSaved;

		private double _rySaved;

		private Rect _rect;

		public bool Closed
		{
			get
			{
				return _closed;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _closed)
				{
					_closed = value;
					Refresh();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Circle
		{
			get
			{
				return _circle;
			}
			set
			{
				_circle = value;
				if (!_circle)
				{
					return;
				}
				double num = (_ry = (_rx = ((_rx < _ry) ? _ry : _rx)));
				if (_vertical)
				{
					if (_points[0].Y < _points[3].Y)
					{
						_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y - num;
						_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y + num;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y + num;
						_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y - num;
					}
					if (_points[2].X < _points[3].X)
					{
						_points[2].X = _points[3].X - num;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[2].X = _points[3].X + num;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (_points[0].X < _points[3].X)
					{
						_points[0].X = _points[3].X - num;
						_points[1].X = _points[3].X + num;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].X = _points[3].X + num;
						_points[1].X = _points[3].X - num;
					}
					if (_points[2].Y < _points[3].Y)
					{
						_points[2].Y = _points[3].Y - num;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[2].Y = _points[3].Y + num;
					}
				}
				Refresh();
				RefreshHandles();
				UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
			}
		}

		public Path(System.Windows.Shapes.Path path)
			: base(path)
		{
			PathGeometry outlinedPathGeometry = path.Data.GetOutlinedPathGeometry();
			_rect = outlinedPathGeometry.Bounds;
			InitializeAnchored(5);
			ExtractAnchors();
			if (_data.EndsWith("z"))
			{
				_closed = true;
			}
			AddBack();
		}

		public Path(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly)
		{
			_path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			_points = new System.Windows.Point[4];
			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
			{
				_points[i] = default(System.Windows.Point);
			}
			_points[2].X = poly.Points[0].X;
			_points[2].Y = poly.Points[0].Y;
			_points[0].X = poly.Points[2].X;
			_points[0].Y = poly.Points[2].Y;
			_points[1].X = poly.Points[3].X;
			_points[1].Y = poly.Points[3].Y;
			_rx = Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[2].X);
			_ry = Math.Abs(_points[0].Y - _points[2].Y);
			_circle = _rx == _ry;
			_angle = 180.0;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry
			{
				FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd
			};
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, isClosed: false);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X, _points[1].Y), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			_path.Data = streamGeometry;
			_path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			base.Children.Add(_path);
		}

		public Path(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly, bool closed, bool nomap = false)
		{
			_closed = closed;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			_points = new System.Windows.Point[4];
			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
			{
				_points[i] = default(System.Windows.Point);
			}
			_points[2].X = poly.Points[0].X;
			_points[2].Y = poly.Points[0].Y;
			_points[0].X = poly.Points[2].X;
			_points[0].Y = poly.Points[2].Y;
			_points[1].X = poly.Points[3].X;
			_points[1].Y = poly.Points[3].Y;
			_rx = Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[2].X);
			_ry = Math.Abs(_points[0].Y - _points[2].Y);
			_circle = _rx == _ry;
			_angle = 180.0;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry
			{
				FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd
			};
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, closed);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X, _points[1].Y), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			path.Data = streamGeometry;
			if (nomap)
			{
				path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			}
			else
			{
				path.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			SetElement(path);
			InitializeAnchored(5);
			AddBack();
			PathGeometry outlinedPathGeometry = path.Data.GetOutlinedPathGeometry();
			_rect = outlinedPathGeometry.Bounds;
		}

		private void GetAngle()
		{
			double num = 1.0;
			if (_rx != _ry)
			{
				num = _ry / _rx;
			}
			double num2 = _points[0].X - _points[3].X;
			double num3 = _points[3].Y - _points[0].Y;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			if (Math.Abs(num3) < 1.0)
			{
				num4 = Math.Atan(num3 / num2) / Math.PI * 180.0;
				if (num2 < 0.0)
				{
					num4 = 180.0 - num4;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num3 /= num;
				num4 = 90.0 - Math.Atan(num2 / num3) / Math.PI * 180.0;
				if (num3 < 0.0)
				{
					num4 = 180.0 + num4;
				}
			}
			_startangle = (int)(num4 + 0.5);
			num2 = _points[1].X - _points[3].X;
			num3 = _points[3].Y - _points[1].Y;
			if (Math.Abs(num3) < 1.0)
			{
				num4 = Math.Atan(num3 / num2) / Math.PI * 180.0;
				if (num2 < 0.0)
				{
					num4 = 180.0 - num4;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num3 /= num;
				num4 = 90.0 - Math.Atan(num2 / num3) / Math.PI * 180.0;
				if (num3 < 0.0)
				{
					num4 = 180.0 + num4;
				}
			}
			_endangle = (int)(num4 + 0.5);
			_angle = Math.Abs(_endangle - _startangle);
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			_back.Data = path.Data;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void ShowHandles(bool bShow)
		{
			base.ShowHandles(bShow);
			if (!_nonstandard || _shadow == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (bShow)
			{
				if (_shadow.Parent == null)
				{
					base.Children.Add(_shadow);
				}
				_rxSaved = _rx;
				_rySaved = _ry;
			}
			else if (base.Children.IndexOf(_shadow) >= 0)
			{
				base.Children.Remove(_shadow);
			}
		}

		private void AddShadow()
		{
			if (_shadow == null)
			{
				_shadow = new System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse();
				_shadow.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				DoubleCollection doubleCollection = new DoubleCollection();
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				doubleCollection.Add(3.0);
				_shadow.StrokeDashArray = doubleCollection;
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(_shadow, _points[3].X - _rx);
			Canvas.SetTop(_shadow, _points[3].Y - _ry);
			_shadow.Width = _rx * 2.0;
			_shadow.Height = _ry * 2.0;
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			_wpoints = new System.Windows.Point[4];
			for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
			{
				ref System.Windows.Point reference = ref _wpoints[i];
				reference = new System.Windows.Point(x[i], y[i]);
			}
			_wpoints[3] = default(System.Windows.Point);
			_wrx = Math.Abs(_wpoints[0].X - _wpoints[2].X);
			_wry = Math.Abs(_wpoints[0].Y - _wpoints[2].Y);
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		private void ExtractAnchors()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = (StreamGeometry)path.Data;
			_data = streamGeometry.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			_points = new System.Windows.Point[4];
			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
			{
				_points[i] = default(System.Windows.Point);
			}
			DataToPoints(_data, ref _points, world: false);
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		private void DataToPoints(string data, ref System.Windows.Point[] points, bool world)
		{
			string[] token = data.Split(',', 'M', 'A', 'z');
			int ith = 0;
			points[0].X = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			points[0].Y = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double num;
			double num2;
			if (world)
			{
				num = (_wrx = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				num2 = (_wry = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			else
			{
				num = (_rx = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				num2 = (_ry = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			double num3 = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (num3 != 0.0 && _angle == 0.0)
			{
				_angle = num3;
			}
			_large = int.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith));
			_largearc = _large == 1;
			_direction = int.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith));
			points[1].X = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			points[1].Y = double.Parse(NextToken(ref token, ref ith), CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (Math.Abs(points[0].Y - points[1].Y) < 0.01 && (_angle == 180.0 || _circular))
			{
				_vertical = false;
				_nonstandard = false;
				points[2].X = (points[0].X + points[1].X) / 2.0;
				points[3].X = points[2].X;
				if (_circular)
				{
					double num4 = num2 * Math.Cos(_angle * 0.5 * Math.PI / 180.0);
					if (_large == 1)
					{
						num4 = 0.0 - num4;
					}
					if (points[1].X > points[0].X)
					{
						points[3].Y = points[0].Y - num4;
						points[2].Y = points[3].Y + num2;
					}
					else
					{
						points[3].Y = points[0].Y + num4;
						points[2].Y = points[3].Y - num2;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					points[2].Y = ((points[0].X < points[1].X) ? (points[0].Y + num2) : (points[0].Y - num2));
					points[3].Y = points[0].Y;
				}
			}
			else if (Math.Abs(points[0].X - points[1].X) < 0.01 && (_angle == 180.0 || _circular))
			{
				_vertical = true;
				_nonstandard = false;
				points[2].Y = (points[0].Y + points[1].Y) / 2.0;
				points[3].Y = points[2].Y;
				if (_circular)
				{
					double num5 = num * Math.Cos(_angle * 0.5 * Math.PI / 180.0);
					if (_large == 1)
					{
						num5 = 0.0 - num5;
					}
					if (points[1].Y < points[0].Y)
					{
						points[3].X = points[0].X - num5;
						points[2].X = points[3].X + num;
					}
					else
					{
						points[3].X = points[0].X + num5;
						points[2].X = points[3].X - num;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					points[2].X = ((points[0].Y > points[1].Y) ? (points[0].X + num) : (points[0].X - num));
					points[3].X = points[0].X;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!_nonstandard)
				{
					ClearHandles();
				}
				_nonstandard = true;
				CalcCenterPoint();
				AddShadow();
			}
			GetAngle();
		}

		private string NextToken(ref string[] token, ref int ith)
		{
			while (string.IsNullOrEmpty(token[ith]))
			{
				ith++;
			}
			return token[ith++];
		}

		private void CalcCenterPoint()
		{
			double rx = _rx;
			double num = 1.0;
			if (_rx != _ry)
			{
				num = _ry / _rx;
			}
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[1].X) < 1E-05)
			{
				num2 = ((!(_points[0].Y < _points[1].Y)) ? (-90.0) : 90.0);
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = Math.Atan((_points[1].Y - _points[0].Y) / num / (_points[1].X - _points[0].X)) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
				if (_points[0].X > _points[1].X)
				{
					num2 += 180.0;
				}
			}
			double num3;
			if (_angle != 0.0)
			{
				num3 = 90.0 - (360.0 - _angle) / 2.0;
			}
			else
			{
				double num4 = Math.Sqrt((_points[1].X - _points[0].X) * (_points[1].X - _points[0].X) + (_points[1].Y - _points[0].Y) / num * ((_points[1].Y - _points[0].Y) / num)) / 2.0;
				num3 = (0.0 - Math.Acos(num4 / rx)) * 180.0 / Math.PI;
			}
			double num5 = num3 + num2;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			double num7 = 0.0;
			if (Math.Abs(Math.Abs(num5) - 90.0) < 0.01)
			{
				num6 = 0.0;
				num7 = ((num5 < 0.0) ? ((0.0 - rx) * num) : (rx * num));
			}
			else
			{
				num6 = rx * Math.Cos(num5 * Math.PI / 180.0);
				num7 = rx * Math.Sin(num5 * Math.PI / 180.0) * num;
			}
			_points[3].X = _points[0].X + num6;
			_points[3].Y = _points[0].Y + num7;
		}

		public override bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _points[3].X;
			y = _points[3].Y;
			return true;
		}

		public override void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
			{
				double num = _points[i].X;
				double num2 = _points[i].Y;
				switch (command)
				{
				case "RotLeft":
				case "RotRight":
				{
					double num3 = num - cx;
					double num4 = num2 - cy;
					if (command == "RotLeft")
					{
						num = cx + num4;
						num2 = cy - num3;
					}
					else
					{
						num = cx - num4;
						num2 = cy + num3;
					}
					break;
				}
				case "FlipX":
					num = cx - (num - cx);
					break;
				case "FlipY":
					num2 = cy - (num2 - cy);
					break;
				}
				_points[i].X = num;
				_points[i].Y = num2;
			}
			switch (command)
			{
			case "RotLeft":
			case "RotRight":
			{
				double rx = _rx;
				_rx = _ry;
				_ry = rx;
				break;
			}
			case "FlipX":
			case "FlipY":
			{
				double x = _points[0].X;
				double y = _points[0].Y;
				_points[0].X = _points[1].X;
				_points[0].Y = _points[1].Y;
				_points[1].X = x;
				_points[1].Y = y;
				break;
			}
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			ExtractAnchors();
			if (!_nonstandard)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
				{
					if (!_circular || i != 2)
					{
						AddHandle(i.ToString(), _points[i].X, _points[i].Y, _anchored[i]);
					}
				}
			}
			AddHandle("Base", _points[3].X, _points[3].Y, _anchored[3]);
			if (_nonstandard)
			{
				AddHandle("0", _points[3].X + _rx, _points[3].Y, _anchored[0]);
				AddHandle("1", _points[3].X, _points[3].Y - _ry, _anchored[1]);
				AddHandle("4", _points[3].X + _rx, _points[3].Y - _ry, _anchored[4]);
			}
			else if (_vertical)
			{
				if (_points[0].Y < _points[1].Y)
				{
					AddHandle("4", _points[2].X, _points[0].Y, _anchored[4]);
				}
				else
				{
					AddHandle("4", _points[2].X, _points[1].Y, _anchored[4]);
				}
			}
			else if (_points[0].X > _points[1].X)
			{
				AddHandle("4", _points[0].X, _points[2].Y, _anchored[4]);
			}
			else
			{
				AddHandle("4", _points[1].X, _points[2].Y, _anchored[4]);
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			string fill = GetFill("Path");
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Arc");
			AddElement(xml, "Stroke", "None");
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", MatchProperty(doc, "Fill", fill).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "pointactive", "Collapsed");
			AddPositionNode();
			AddAttribute(xml, "startangle", _startangle.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "endangle", _endangle.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var1", "CX");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value1", _points[3].X.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var2", "CY");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value2", _points[3].Y.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var3", "RX");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value3", _rx.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var4", "RY");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value4", _ry.ToString());
			xml.Attributes["checkbox1"].Value = "Closed";
			AddAttribute(xml, "check1", _closed.ToString());
			xml.Attributes["checkbox2"].Value = "Circle";
			AddAttribute(xml, "check2", _circle.ToString());
			return xml;
		}

		public override void Update(XmlNode node)
		{
			_data = GetString(node, "Data");
			_data = _data.Replace(' ', ',');
			DataToPoints(_data, ref _points, world: false);
			Refresh();
			base.Update(node);
		}

		private void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry();
			streamGeometry.FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd;
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, _closed);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X, _points[1].Y), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			path.Data = streamGeometry;
			_back.Data = streamGeometry;
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_positionnode != null && _datanode != null)
			{
				_datanode.Attributes["startangle"].Value = _startangle.ToString();
				_datanode.Attributes["endangle"].Value = _endangle.ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value1"].Value = (_points[3].X + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value2"].Value = (_points[3].Y + dy).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value3"].Value = _rx.ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value4"].Value = _ry.ToString();
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result);
			if ((name == "Value3" || name == "Value4") && result <= 0.0)
			{
				string text = ((name == "Value3") ? "RX" : "RY");
				UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
				return text + " must be positive.";
			}
			switch (name)
			{
			case "Value1":
			{
				double num7 = result - _points[3].X;
				_points[0].X += num7;
				_points[1].X += num7;
				break;
			}
			case "Value2":
			{
				double num5 = result - _points[3].Y;
				_points[0].Y += num5;
				_points[1].Y += num5;
				break;
			}
			case "Value3":
			{
				double num6 = result - _rx;
				if (Math.Abs(_points[0].Y - _points[1].Y) < 1E-05)
				{
					if (_points[0].X < _points[1].X)
					{
						_points[0].X -= num6;
						_points[1].X += num6;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].X += num6;
						_points[1].X -= num6;
					}
				}
				_rx = result;
				if (!_circle)
				{
					break;
				}
				_ry = result;
				if (Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[1].X) < 1E-05)
				{
					if (_points[0].Y < _points[1].Y)
					{
						_points[0].Y -= num6;
						_points[1].Y += num6;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].Y += num6;
						_points[1].Y -= num6;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Value4":
			{
				double num4 = result - _ry;
				if (Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[1].X) < 1E-05)
				{
					if (_points[0].Y < _points[1].Y)
					{
						_points[0].Y -= num4;
						_points[1].Y += num4;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].Y += num4;
						_points[1].Y -= num4;
					}
				}
				_ry = result;
				if (!_circle)
				{
					break;
				}
				_rx = result;
				if (Math.Abs(_points[0].Y - _points[1].Y) < 1E-05)
				{
					if (_points[0].X < _points[1].X)
					{
						_points[0].X -= num4;
						_points[1].X += num4;
					}
					else
					{
						_points[0].X += num4;
						_points[1].X -= num4;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			case "StartAngle":
			case "EndAngle":
			{
				for (; result < 0.0; result += 360.0)
				{
				}
				while (result >= 360.0)
				{
					result -= 360.0;
				}
				int num = (int)(result + 0.5);
				if (name == "StartAngle")
				{
					_startangle = num;
				}
				else
				{
					_endangle = num;
				}
				_angle = _endangle - _startangle;
				if (_angle < 0.0)
				{
					_angle += 360.0;
				}
				_largearc = _angle > 180.0;
				if (name == "StartAngle")
				{
					double num2 = (double)_startangle / 180.0 * Math.PI;
					_points[0].X = _points[3].X + _rx * Math.Cos(num2);
					_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y - _ry * Math.Sin(num2);
				}
				else
				{
					double num3 = (double)_endangle / 180.0 * Math.PI;
					_points[1].X = _points[3].X + _rx * Math.Cos(num3);
					_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y - _ry * Math.Sin(num3);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			return "";
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			string text = "";
			bool flag = false;
			string paths = "";
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (ResolveAnchor)
			{
				string text2 = "0";
				string text3 = "0";
				string text4 = null;
				foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
				{
					if (handle.Anchor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					flag = true;
					Anchor anchor = handle.Anchor;
					string iD = handle.ID;
					if (iD == "Base")
					{
						if (anchor.Direction == 1)
						{
							string param = anchor.GetParam("yparam");
							string text5 = anchor.GetParam("yconv").Replace(' ', '_');
							if (_points[3].Y != 0.0)
							{
								text5 = "(" + text5 + ")+" + (0.0 - _points[3].Y).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
							}
							text3 = "{Binding XPath=" + param + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + text5 + "}";
						}
						else if (anchor.Direction == 2)
						{
							string param2 = anchor.GetParam("xparam");
							string text6 = anchor.GetParam("xconv").Replace(' ', '_');
							if (_points[3].X != 0.0)
							{
								text6 = "(" + text6 + ")+" + _points[3].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
							}
							text2 = "{Binding XPath=" + param2 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + text6 + "}";
						}
					}
					else if (iD == "4" && anchor.Direction == 2)
					{
						string param3 = anchor.GetParam("xparam");
						string text7 = anchor.GetParam("xconv").Replace(' ', '_');
						handle.GetOffset(out var dx, out var _);
						if (dx != 0.0)
						{
							text7 = dx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "+" + text7;
						}
						if (param3.Contains(','))
						{
							flag2 = true;
							paths = param3;
							text4 = "(" + text7 + ")/" + _rx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
							continue;
						}
						text4 = "{Binding XPath=" + param3 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=(" + text7 + ")/" + _rx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "}";
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					text += "<Canvas>";
					text += "<Canvas.RenderTransform>";
					text += "<TransformGroup>";
					if (text4 != null)
					{
						if (flag2)
						{
							string text8 = text;
							text = text8 + "<ScaleTransform CenterX=\"" + _points[3].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" CenterY=\"" + _points[3].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\">";
							text += "<ScaleTransform.ScaleX>";
							text += MultiBinding(paths, text4);
							text += "</ScaleTransform.ScaleX>";
							text += "<ScaleTransform.ScaleY>";
							text += MultiBinding(paths, text4);
							text += "</ScaleTransform.ScaleY>";
							text += "</ScaleTransform>";
						}
						else
						{
							string text9 = text;
							text = text9 + "<ScaleTransform ScaleX=\"" + text4 + "\" ScaleY=\"" + text4 + "\" CenterX=\"" + _points[3].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" CenterY=\"" + _points[3].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"/>";
						}
					}
					string text10 = text;
					text = text10 + "<TranslateTransform X=\"" + text2 + "\" Y=\"" + text3 + "\"/>";
					text += "</TransformGroup>";
					text += "</Canvas.RenderTransform>";
				}
			}
			text += "<Path ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			text = text + " Data=\"" + GetData() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			text += GradientFill("Path");
			text += "</Path>";
			if (flag)
			{
				text += "</Canvas>";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = null;
			if (_fill.Contains("Bottom"))
			{
				text2 = "bottomradial";
			}
			else if (_fill.Contains("Top"))
			{
				text2 = "topradial";
			}
			else if (_fill.Contains("Left"))
			{
				text2 = "leftradial";
			}
			else if (_fill.Contains("Right"))
			{
				text2 = "rightradial";
			}
			text += "<path ";
			text = text + "d=\"" + GetData() + "\" ";
			if (text2 != null)
			{
				text += "stroke=\"none\" ";
				text = text + "fill=\"url(#" + text2 + ")\" ";
			}
			else
			{
				text += "stroke=\"black\" ";
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<Path";
			text = text + " Data=\"" + GetWData() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			return text + "</Path>";
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			string value = documentElement.Attributes["Data"].Value;
			value = value.Replace(' ', ',');
			_wpoints = new System.Windows.Point[4];
			for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
			{
				_wpoints[i] = default(System.Windows.Point);
			}
			DataToPoints(value, ref _wpoints, world: true);
		}

		private string GetData()
		{
			string text = "M " + _points[0].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[0].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			string text2 = text;
			text = text2 + " A " + _rx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _ry.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (_angle != 180.0)
			{
				string text3 = text;
				text = text3 + " 0 " + _large + " " + _direction + " ";
			}
			else
			{
				text += " 0 0 0 ";
			}
			text = text + _points[1].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[1].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (_data.EndsWith("Z") || _closed)
			{
				text += " z";
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string GetWData()
		{
			string text = "M " + _wpoints[0].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _wpoints[0].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			string text2 = text;
			text = text2 + " A " + _wrx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _wry.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (_angle != 180.0)
			{
				string text3 = text;
				text = text3 + " " + _angle.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + " " + _large + " " + _direction + " ";
			}
			else
			{
				text += " 180 0 0 ";
			}
			text = text + _wpoints[1].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _wpoints[1].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (_data.EndsWith("Z") || _closed)
			{
				text += " z";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _points[0].X;
			y = _points[0].Y;
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_rect.Left < minx)
			{
				minx = _rect.Left;
			}
			if (_rect.Right > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _rect.Right;
			}
			if (_rect.Top < miny)
			{
				miny = _rect.Top;
			}
			if (_rect.Bottom > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _rect.Bottom;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry();
			streamGeometry.FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd;
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X + dx, _points[0].Y + dy), isFilled: true, _closed);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X + dx, _points[1].Y + dy), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			path.Data = streamGeometry;
			_back.Data = streamGeometry;
			for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
			{
				_points[i].X += dx;
				_points[i].Y += dy;
			}
			AddHandles();
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (_nonstandard)
			{
				ResizeNonStandard(ref handle, x, y);
				return;
			}
			RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			if (handle == "Base")
			{
				_circular = true;
				_circle = false;
				RemoveHandle("2");
				RemoveHandle("4");
				double num;
				double num2;
				if (_vertical)
				{
					_largearc = Math.Abs(x - _points[2].X) < Math.Abs(_points[2].X - _points[0].X);
					num = Math.Abs(_points[2].Y - _points[0].Y);
					num2 = Math.Abs(x - _points[0].X);
				}
				else
				{
					_largearc = Math.Abs(y - _points[2].Y) < Math.Abs(_points[2].Y - _points[0].Y);
					num = Math.Abs(_points[2].X - _points[0].X);
					num2 = Math.Abs(y - _points[0].Y);
				}
				double num3 = Math.Atan(num2 / num);
				_rx = num / Math.Cos(num3);
				_ry = _rx;
				_angle = (90.0 - num3 / Math.PI * 180.0) * 2.0;
			}
			else
			{
				int num4 = int.Parse(handle);
				switch (num4)
				{
				case 2:
					if (_circular)
					{
						break;
					}
					if (_vertical)
					{
						_rx = Math.Abs(x - _points[0].X);
						if (_circle)
						{
							_ry = _rx;
							if (_points[0].Y < _points[3].Y)
							{
								_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y - _ry;
								_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y + _ry;
							}
							else
							{
								_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y + _rx;
								_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y - _ry;
							}
						}
						break;
					}
					_ry = Math.Abs(y - _points[0].Y);
					if (_circle)
					{
						_rx = _ry;
						if (_points[0].X < _points[3].X)
						{
							_points[0].X = _points[3].X - _rx;
							_points[1].X = _points[3].X + _rx;
						}
						else
						{
							_points[0].X = _points[3].X + _rx;
							_points[1].X = _points[3].X - _rx;
						}
					}
					break;
				case 4:
					if (_vertical)
					{
						double num9 = Math.Abs(y - _points[2].Y);
						if (_circle)
						{
							_rx = num9;
						}
						_ry = num9;
						if (_points[0].Y < _points[1].Y)
						{
							_points[0].Y = y;
							_points[1].Y = y + _ry * 2.0;
						}
						else
						{
							_points[1].Y = y;
							_points[0].Y = y + _ry * 2.0;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						double num10 = Math.Abs(x - _points[2].X);
						if (_circle)
						{
							_ry = num10;
						}
						_rx = num10;
						if (_points[0].X > _points[1].X)
						{
							_points[0].X = x;
							_points[1].X = x - _rx * 2.0;
						}
						else
						{
							_points[1].X = x;
							_points[0].X = x - _rx * 2.0;
						}
					}
					break;
				default:
					if (_vertical)
					{
						_points[num4].Y = y;
						_ry = Math.Abs(_points[1].Y - _points[0].Y) / 2.0;
						if (_circle)
						{
							_rx = _ry;
						}
						else if (_circular)
						{
							double num5 = Math.Abs(_points[3].X - _points[0].X);
							double num6 = Math.Atan(num5 / _ry);
							_rx = Math.Sqrt(_ry * _ry + num5 * num5);
							_ry = _rx;
							_angle = (90.0 - num6 / Math.PI * 180.0) * 2.0;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						_points[num4].X = x;
						_rx = Math.Abs(_points[1].X - _points[0].X) / 2.0;
						if (_circle)
						{
							_ry = _rx;
						}
						else if (_circular)
						{
							double num7 = Math.Abs(_points[3].Y - _points[0].Y);
							double num8 = Math.Atan(num7 / _rx);
							_ry = Math.Sqrt(_rx * _rx + num7 * num7);
							_rx = _ry;
							_angle = (90.0 - num8 / Math.PI * 180.0) * 2.0;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry();
			streamGeometry.FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd;
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, _closed);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X, _points[1].Y), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			path.Data = streamGeometry;
			_back.Data = streamGeometry;
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		private void ResizeNonStandard(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (!(handle == "Base"))
			{
				RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
				if (handle == "0")
				{
					_rx = x - _points[3].X;
				}
				else if (handle == "1")
				{
					_ry = _points[3].Y - y;
				}
				else if (handle == "4")
				{
					_rx = x - _points[3].X;
					_ry = _points[3].Y - y;
				}
				Canvas.SetLeft(_shadow, _points[3].X - _rx);
				Canvas.SetTop(_shadow, _points[3].Y - _ry);
				_shadow.Width = _rx * 2.0;
				_shadow.Height = _ry * 2.0;
			}
		}

		public override void EndResize(string handle)
		{
			if (_nonstandard)
			{
				_points[0].X = _points[3].X + (_points[0].X - _points[3].X) * _rx / _rxSaved;
				_points[0].Y = _points[3].Y + (_points[0].Y - _points[3].Y) * _ry / _rySaved;
				_points[1].X = _points[3].X + (_points[1].X - _points[3].X) * _rx / _rxSaved;
				_points[1].Y = _points[3].Y + (_points[1].Y - _points[3].Y) * _ry / _rySaved;
				Refresh();
			}
		}

		public void Resize(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly)
		{
			_points[2].X = poly.Points[0].X;
			_points[2].Y = poly.Points[0].Y;
			_points[0].X = poly.Points[2].X;
			_points[0].Y = poly.Points[2].Y;
			_points[1].X = poly.Points[3].X;
			_points[1].Y = poly.Points[3].Y;
			_rx = Math.Abs(_points[0].X - _points[2].X);
			_ry = Math.Abs(_points[0].Y - _points[2].Y);
			StreamGeometry streamGeometry = new StreamGeometry();
			streamGeometry.FillRule = FillRule.EvenOdd;
			using (StreamGeometryContext streamGeometryContext = streamGeometry.Open())
			{
				streamGeometryContext.BeginFigure(new System.Windows.Point(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y), isFilled: true, _closed);
				streamGeometryContext.ArcTo(new System.Windows.Point(_points[1].X, _points[1].Y), new System.Windows.Size(_rx, _ry), 0.0, _largearc, SweepDirection.Counterclockwise, isStroked: true, isSmoothJoin: false);
			}
			streamGeometry.Freeze();
			_path.Data = streamGeometry;
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Base")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				if (_vertical)
				{
					translateTransform.X = x - _points[0].X;
					translateTransform.Y = y - _points[2].Y;
				}
				else
				{
					translateTransform.X = x - _points[2].X;
					translateTransform.Y = y - _points[0].Y;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Resize(ref handle, x, y);
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "Base")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}

		public override void GenGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)_element;
			Geometry data = path.Data;
			gg.Children.Add(data);
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			int num = _wpoints.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				coeff.WcToDvc(_wpoints[i].X, _wpoints[i].Y, out var xx, out var yy);
				_points[i].X = xx;
				_points[i].Y = yy;
			}
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_wrx, out _rx);
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_wry, out _ry);
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			int num = _wpoints.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				coeff.DvcToWc(_points[i].X, _points[i].Y, out var x, out var y);
				_wpoints[i].X = x;
				_wpoints[i].Y = y;
			}
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_rx, out _wrx);
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_ry, out _wry);
		}
	}
	public class Polygon : PolyElement
	{
		private System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon _back;

		private bool _arrowAtStart;

		private bool _arrowAtEnd;

		public Polygon(System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon elem, bool created = false)
			: base(elem)
		{
			int count = elem.Points.Count;
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				_points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(elem.Points[i].X, elem.Points[i].Y));
			}
			if (created)
			{
				elem.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			InitializeAnchored(_points.Count);
			AddBack();
		}

		public Polygon(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline elem, bool created = false)
			: base(elem)
		{
			int count = elem.Points.Count;
			switch (count)
			{
			case 0:
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			case 1:
				_parametric = true;
				break;
			}
			_points = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				_points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(elem.Points[i].X, elem.Points[i].Y));
			}
			if (created)
			{
				elem.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			if ((string)elem.Tag == "BothArrows" || (string)elem.Tag == "StartArrow")
			{
				_arrowAtStart = true;
				AddArrow("ArrowAtStart", bAdd: true, colorset: false);
			}
			if ((string)elem.Tag == "BothArrows" || (string)elem.Tag == "EndArrow")
			{
				_arrowAtEnd = true;
				AddArrow("ArrowAtEnd", bAdd: true, colorset: false);
			}
			InitializeAnchored(_points.Count);
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_element;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon();
			_back.Points = polygon.Points;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			_wpoints = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			for (int i = 0; i < x.Count; i++)
			{
				_wpoints.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x[i], y[i]));
			}
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		public override void AddArrow(string location, bool bAdd, bool colorset = true)
		{
			if (!(this is Polyline))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (location == "ArrowAtStart")
			{
				if (bAdd)
				{
					if (_atstart == null)
					{
						_atstart = new ArrowHead(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y, _points[1].X, _points[1].Y, colorset ? GetStroke() : "Default", bStart: true);
						base.Children.Add(_atstart);
					}
					_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (_atstart != null)
				{
					_atstart.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(location == "ArrowAtEnd"))
				{
					return;
				}
				if (bAdd)
				{
					if (_atend == null)
					{
						int num = _points.Count - 1;
						_atend = new ArrowHead(_points[num].X, _points[num].Y, _points[num - 1].X, _points[num - 1].Y, colorset ? GetStroke() : "Default", bStart: false);
						base.Children.Add(_atend);
					}
					_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (_atend != null)
				{
					_atend.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			string fill = GetFill((this is Polyline) ? "Polyline" : "Polygon");
			if (_arrowAtStart)
			{
				_arrowAtStart = false;
				_atstart.ChangeColor(stroke);
			}
			if (_arrowAtEnd)
			{
				_arrowAtEnd = false;
				_atend.ChangeColor(stroke);
			}
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", (this is Polyline) ? "Polyline" : "Polygon");
			int num = MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke);
			int num2 = MatchProperty(doc, "Fill", fill);
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", num2.ToString());
			if (this is Polyline)
			{
				if (_atstart != null && _atstart.IsVisible)
				{
					AddAttribute(xml, "atstart", "True");
					AddAttribute(xml, "startarrowsize", (_atstart.GetSize() - 1).ToString());
				}
				else
				{
					AddAttribute(xml, "atstart", "False");
					AddAttribute(xml, "startarrowsize", "-1");
				}
				if (_atend != null && _atend.IsVisible)
				{
					AddAttribute(xml, "atend", "True");
					AddAttribute(xml, "endarrowsize", (_atend.GetSize() - 1).ToString());
				}
				else
				{
					AddAttribute(xml, "atend", "False");
					AddAttribute(xml, "endarrowsize", "-1");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			}
			AddAttribute(xml, "angleactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "valueactive", "Collapsed");
			if (!_parametric)
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "xyactive", "Collapsed");
				AddAttribute(xml, "listheight", "80");
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xml, "listheight", "25");
			}
			_pointnodes = new List<XmlNode>();
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("point");
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", "P" + i);
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "x", _points[i].X.ToString());
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "y", _points[i].Y.ToString());
				string value = "Visible";
				if (_points.Count <= 2 && !_parametric)
				{
					value = "Collapsed";
				}
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "delete", value);
				xml.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				_pointnodes.Add(xmlElement);
			}
			return xml;
		}

		public override void Update(XmlNode node)
		{
			string data = GetString(node, "Points");
			DataToPoints(data);
			Refresh();
			base.Update(node);
		}

		protected override void Refresh()
		{
			if (this is Polyline)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_element;
				polyline.Points.Clear();
				for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
				{
					polyline.Points.Add(_points[i]);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_element;
				polygon.Points.Clear();
				for (int j = 0; j < _points.Count; j++)
				{
					polygon.Points.Add(_points[j]);
				}
			}
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		private void DataToPoints(string data)
		{
			string[] array = data.Split(',', ' ');
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point value = _points[i];
				value.X = double.Parse(array[num++], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				value.Y = double.Parse(array[num++], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				_points[i] = value;
			}
		}

		private void DataToWPoints(string data)
		{
			string[] array = data.Split(',', ' ');
			int num = array.Length / 2;
			if (_wpoints == null)
			{
				_wpoints = new List<System.Windows.Point>();
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				System.Windows.Point item = new System.Windows.Point
				{
					X = double.Parse(array[num2++], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat),
					Y = double.Parse(array[num2++], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat)
				};
				_wpoints.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			string text = "<Polygon ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			text = text + " Points=\"" + GetPoints() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			text += GradientFill("Polygon");
			return text + "</Polygon>";
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
