			if (_fill != "----" && _fill != "None" && _fill[0] != '@' && _fill[0] != '#')
			{
				if (_fill.Contains("Vertical"))
				{
					text2 = "lineary";
				}
				else if (_fill.Contains("Horizontal"))
				{
					text2 = "linearx";
				}
				if (_fill.Contains("Bottom"))
				{
					text2 = "bottomradial";
				}
				else if (_fill.Contains("Top"))
				{
					text2 = "topradial";
				}
				else if (_fill.Contains("Left"))
				{
					text2 = "leftradial";
				}
				else if (_fill.Contains("Right"))
				{
					text2 = "rightradial";
				}
			}
			text += "<polygon ";
			text = text + "points=\"" + GetPoints() + "\" ";
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text += "stroke=\"black\" ";
			}
			else if (text2 != "")
			{
				text += "stroke=\"none\" ";
			}
			if (text2 != "")
			{
				text = text + "fill=\"url(#" + text2 + ")\" ";
			}
			else if (_fill != null && (_fill[0] == '@' || _fill[0] == '#'))
			{
				text = text + "fill=\"" + ConvBinding(_fill) + "\" ";
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<" + GetType().Name;
			text = text + " Points=\"" + GetWPoints() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			return text + "</Polygon>";
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			string data = documentElement.Attributes["Points"].Value.TrimEnd();
			DataToWPoints(data);
		}

		protected string GetPoints()
		{
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				string text2 = text;
				text = text2 + _points[i].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _points[i].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + " ";
			}
			return text;
		}

		protected string GetWPoints()
		{
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _wpoints.Count; i++)
			{
				string text2 = text;
				text = text2 + _wpoints[i].X.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "," + _wpoints[i].Y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + " ";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _points[0].X;
			y = _points[0].Y;
		}

		public override void EndResize(string handle)
		{
			if (this is Polyline)
			{
				if (_atstart != null)
				{
					_atstart.Move(_points[0].X, _points[0].Y, _points[1].X, _points[1].Y);
				}
				if (_atend != null)
				{
					int num = _points.Count - 1;
					_atend.Move(_points[num].X, _points[num].Y, _points[num - 1].X, _points[num - 1].Y);
				}
			}
			base.EndResize(handle);
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				translateTransform.X = x - _cx;
				translateTransform.Y = y - _cy;
			}
			else
			{
				Resize(ref handle, x, y);
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}

		protected override void GetDimension()
		{
			_minx = 1E+30;
			_miny = 1E+30;
			_maxx = -1E+30;
			_maxy = -1E+30;
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				if (_points[i].X < _minx)
				{
					_minx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y < _miny)
				{
					_miny = _points[i].Y;
				}
				if (_points[i].X > _maxx)
				{
					_maxx = _points[i].X;
				}
				if (_points[i].Y > _maxy)
				{
					_maxy = _points[i].Y;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < _points.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					if (Math.Abs(_points[i].X - num) < 1E-05 && Math.Abs(_points[i].Y - num2) < 1E-05)
					{
						continue;
					}
					AddHandle("i" + i, (num + _points[i].X) / 2.0, (num2 + _points[i].Y) / 2.0, null, 2);
				}
				AddHandle(i.ToString(), num = _points[i].X, num2 = _points[i].Y, _anchored[i], 1);
			}
			if (!(this is Polyline))
			{
				AddHandle("i" + _points.Count, (num + _points[0].X) / 2.0, (num2 + _points[0].Y) / 2.0, null, 2);
			}
			base.AddHandles();
		}
	}
	public class Polyline : Polygon
	{
		private System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline _back;

		private string _xyparam = "";

		private string _xconv = "";

		private string _yconv = "";

		private double _bx;

		private double _by;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PlotXProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PlotX", typeof(string), typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("", OnPlotXChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty PlotYProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("PlotY", typeof(string), typeof(Polyline), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata("", OnPlotYChanged));

		public string PlotX
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PlotXProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PlotXProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public string PlotY
		{
			get
			{
				return (string)GetValue(PlotYProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(PlotYProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Polyline(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline elem, bool created = false)
			: base(elem, created)
		{
			AddBack();
		}

		private new void AddBack()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_element;
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline();
			_back.Points = polyline.Points;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "xyparam", _xyparam);
			AddAttribute(xml, "xconv", _xconv);
			AddAttribute(xml, "yconv", _yconv);
			_bx = _points[0].X;
			_by = _points[0].Y;
			return xml;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			bool flag = _atstart != null && _atstart.IsVisible;
			bool flag2 = _atend != null && _atend.IsVisible;
			string text = "<Polyline ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			text = text + " Points=\"" + GetPoints() + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += GradientFill("Polyline");
			if (flag && flag2)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"BothArrows\"";
			}
			else if (flag)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"StartArrow\"";
			}
			else if (flag2)
			{
				text += " Tag=\"EndArrow\"";
			}
			text += ">";
			text += "</Polyline>";
			if (flag)
			{
				text += _atstart.GenXaml(bCurrent, _stroke);
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				text += _atend.GenXaml(bCurrent, _stroke);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			text += "<polyline ";
			text = text + "points=\"" + GetPoints() + "\" ";
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None" && !_stroke.Contains("{Binding"))
			{
				text = text + "style=\"stroke:" + _stroke + ";\" ";
			}
			else if (_fill != null)
			{
				text = text + "style=\"stroke:none;fill:" + _fill + ";\" ";
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public void SavePlot(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (_parametric)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Plot", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				string elementIndex = base.Control.GetElementIndex(this);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "index", elementIndex);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "param", _xyparam);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "xconv", _xconv);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "yconv", _yconv);
				root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			}
		}

		public void LoadPlot(XmlNode node)
		{
			_xyparam = GetString(node, "param");
			_xconv = GetString(node, "xconv");
			_yconv = GetString(node, "yconv");
			_datanode.Attributes["xyparam"].Value = _xyparam;
			_datanode.Attributes["xconv"].Value = _xconv;
			_datanode.Attributes["yconv"].Value = _yconv;
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _xyparam + "/@xvalues";
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, PlotXProperty, binding);
			Binding binding2 = new Binding();
			binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _xyparam + "/@yvalues";
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, PlotYProperty, binding2);
		}

		public void SetPlot(string param, string value)
		{
			switch (param)
			{
			case "XYParam":
				_xyparam = value;
				break;
			case "XConverter":
				_xconv = value;
				break;
			case "YConverter":
				_yconv = value;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void RefreshPlot()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(PlotX) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(PlotY))
			{
				_points.Clear();
				Refresh();
				return;
			}
			string[] array = PlotX.Trim().Split(' ');
			string[] array2 = PlotY.Trim().Split(' ');
			if (array.Length != array2.Length)
			{
				return;
			}
			_points.Clear();
			Calc calc = new Calc();
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = array[i];
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_xconv))
				{
					text = (string)calc.Convert(text, null, _xconv, null);
				}
				double num = double.Parse(text);
				string text2 = array2[i];
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_yconv))
				{
					text2 = (string)calc.Convert(text2, null, _yconv, null);
				}
				double num2 = double.Parse(text2);
				System.Windows.Point item = new System.Windows.Point(_bx + num, _by + num2);
				_points.Add(item);
			}
			Refresh();
		}

		private static void OnPlotXChanged(DependencyObject source, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Polyline polyline = source as Polyline;
			polyline.RefreshPlot();
		}

		private static void OnPlotYChanged(DependencyObject source, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Polyline polyline = source as Polyline;
			polyline.RefreshPlot();
		}
	}
	public class Port : TagObj
	{
		private int _index;

		private string _name;

		private string _oldname;

		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private double _x2;

		private double _y2;

		private string _type;

		private string _attrib;

		private string _moniker;

		private string _porttype;

		private bool _bNoRange;

		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _back;

		private Canvas _canvas;

		private double _anchordx;

		private double _anchordy;

		private int _side;

		private RotateTransform _rform;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private Anchor _anchor;

		private bool _autostream;

		private int _streamdirection;

		private XmlNode _datanode;

		private string _group;

		private bool _kbase;

		private bool _abe;

		public new string Name => _name;

		public int AccessSide
		{
			set
			{
				if (value != _side)
				{
					Rotate(value);
				}
				_side = value;
			}
		}

		public IconCtrl Ctrl => _parent;

		public XmlNode DataNode => _datanode;

		public string IOtype => _type;

		public string PortType => _porttype;

		public int StreamDirection
		{
			get
			{
				return _streamdirection;
			}
			set
			{
				_streamdirection = value;
			}
		}

		public bool Visible => _canvas.IsVisible;

		public event EventHandler PortMoved;

		public event EventHandler PortVisibilityChanged;

		public Port(XmlNode node, IconCtrl parent, int index, bool kbaseAutoStream)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_kbase = parent.KBase;
			_abe = parent.ABE;
			_index = index;
			_name = GetString(node, "name");
			_oldname = _name;
			_x = GetDouble(node, "x");
			_y = GetDouble(node, "y");
			if (!parent.ABE)
			{
				_x *= 100.0;
				_y *= 100.0;
			}
			if (node.Attributes["x2"] == null)
			{
				_bNoRange = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_x2 = GetDouble(node, "x2");
				_y2 = GetDouble(node, "y2");
			}
			if (parent.ABE)
			{
				string text = _name.ToLower();
				if (text.StartsWith("inlet") || text.EndsWith("in"))
				{
					_type = "0";
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("outlet") || text.EndsWith("out"))
				{
					_type = "1";
				}
				else
				{
					_type = "-1";
				}
				int num = GetInt(node, "angle");
				_attrib = " ";
				if (num == 0)
				{
					_attrib = "r";
				}
				if (num == 180)
				{
					_attrib = "l";
				}
				if (num == 90)
				{
					_attrib = "u";
				}
				if (num == 270)
				{
					_attrib = "d";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_type = GetString(node, "type");
				if (_type == null)
				{
					string text2 = GetString(node, "direction");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						_type = (2 - int.Parse(text2)).ToString();
					}
				}
				_porttype = GetString(node, "portType");
				_attrib = GetString(node, "attrib");
				if (_attrib == null)
				{
					int num2 = GetInt(node, "dx");
					int num3 = GetInt(node, "dy");
					if (num3 == 0)
					{
						_attrib = ((num2 < 0) ? "l" : "r");
					}
					if (num2 == 0)
					{
						_attrib = ((num3 > 0) ? "u" : "d");
					}
					_x *= 25.0;
					_y *= 25.0;
				}
			}
			_moniker = GetString(node, "moniker");
			if ((!_parent.HysysIcon || _moniker == null) && !parent.ABE)
			{
				_y = 0.0 - _y;
			}
			string text3 = GetString(node, "def");
			if (text3 != null)
			{
				_autostream = true;
			}
			if (kbaseAutoStream && (_name == "Inlet" || _name == "Outlet"))
			{
				_autostream = true;
				text3 = _attrib;
			}
			if (text3 != null)
			{
				switch (text3)
				{
				case "l":
					_streamdirection = 1;
					break;
				case "r":
					_streamdirection = 2;
					break;
				case "u":
					_streamdirection = 3;
					break;
				case "d":
					_streamdirection = 4;
					break;
				}
			}
			_group = GetString(node, "group");
			_visibilitymap = GetString(node, "visibility");
			switch (char.ToLower(_attrib[0]))
			{
			case 'l':
				_side = 1;
				break;
			case 'r':
				_side = 2;
				break;
			case 'u':
				_side = 3;
				break;
			case 'd':
				_side = 4;
				break;
			}
			if (!parent.OnPfs)
			{
				base.MouseLeftButtonDown += Port_MouseDown;
				base.MouseRightButtonDown += Port_MouseRightButtonDown;
			}
		}

		public Port(double x, double y, IconCtrl parent, string name, int index)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_kbase = parent.KBase;
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_attrib = "l";
			_side = 1;
			_type = "0";
			_name = name;
			_oldname = name;
			_porttype = "PIP_L_PR";
			_index = index;
			_moniker = null;
			_bNoRange = true;
			if (!parent.OnPfs)
			{
				base.MouseLeftButtonDown += Port_MouseDown;
				base.MouseRightButtonDown += Port_MouseRightButtonDown;
			}
		}

		private void Port_MouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_parent.DragPort = this;
			Highlight(bOn: true, bLarge: false);
		}

		private void Port_MouseRightButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			_parent.HandleQuery(this, "");
		}

		public void GetLocation(out double x, out double y, out int side)
		{
			x = _x;
			y = _y;
			side = _side;
		}

		public void Save(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = ((!_kbase) ? root.SelectSingleNode("y:Port[@name='" + _name + "']", nsmgr) : root.SelectSingleNode("y:Port[@name='" + _oldname + "']", nsmgr));
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Port", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "name", _name);
				root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			}
			else if (_oldname != _name)
			{
				xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value = _name;
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "updated", "true");
			double num = 100.0;
			if (_kbase)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "portType", _porttype);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "direction", (2 - int.Parse(_type)).ToString());
				int num2 = 0;
				int num3 = 0;
				switch (GetAttrib((int)_rform.Angle, _type))
				{
				case "l":
					num2 = -1;
					break;
				case "r":
					num2 = 1;
					break;
				case "u":
					num3 = 1;
					break;
				case "d":
					num3 = -1;
					break;
				}
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dx", num2.ToString());
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dy", num3.ToString());
				num *= 25.0;
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "type", _type);
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "attrib", GetAttrib((int)_rform.Angle, _type));
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "x", (_x / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			if (!_parent.HysysIcon || _moniker == null)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "y", ((0.0 - _y) / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			else
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "y", (_y / num).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			string autoStreamDirection = GetAutoStreamDirection();
			_autostream = autoStreamDirection != "";
			if (_autostream)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "def", autoStreamDirection);
			}
			else if (xmlNode.Attributes["def"] != null)
			{
				xmlNode.Attributes.Remove(xmlNode.Attributes["def"]);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlNode, "visibility", _visibilitymap);
			}
		}

		public string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "<port ";
			text = text + "id=\"" + Name + "\" ";
			text = text + "iotype=\"" + IOtype + "\" ";
			string autoStreamDirection = GetAutoStreamDirection();
			if (autoStreamDirection != "")
			{
				text = text + "def=\"" + autoStreamDirection + "\" ";
			}
			if (_group != null)
			{
				text = text + "group=\"" + _group + "\" ";
			}
			text = text + "x=\"" + (_x / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y=\"" + (_y / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "angle=\"" + GetAngle() + "\"";
			return text + "/>";
		}

		private int GetAngle()
		{
			if (IOtype == "0")
			{
				if (_attrib == "u")
				{
					return 90;
				}
				if (_attrib == "d")
				{
					return -90;
				}
				if (_attrib == "r")
				{
					return 180;
				}
				return 0;
			}
			if (_attrib == "d")
			{
				return 90;
			}
			if (_attrib == "u")
			{
				return -90;
			}
			if (_attrib == "l")
			{
				return 180;
			}
			return 0;
		}

		private string GetAutoStreamDirection()
		{
			string result = "";
			if (_datanode != null)
			{
				switch (_datanode.Attributes["direction"].Value)
				{
				case "1":
					result = "l";
					break;
				case "2":
					result = "r";
					break;
				case "3":
					result = "u";
					break;
				case "4":
					result = "d";
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SaveAnchor(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Port", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "name", _name);
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dx", _anchordx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			AddAttribute(xmlNode, "dy", _anchordy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
		}

		public string GenAnchorSvg()
		{
			string text = "<port ";
			text = text + "name=\"" + _name + "\" ";
			text = text + "dx=\"" + _anchordx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "dy=\"" + _anchordy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public void SetOffset(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_anchordx = dx;
			_anchordy = dy;
		}

		public void ChangeName(string name, bool standalone)
		{
			if (standalone)
			{
				_parent.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Rename, this);
			}
			_name = name;
			base.ToolTip = _name;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_name))
			{
				_porttype = "";
			}
		}

		public void ChangeIOType(string value)
		{
			if (!(_type == value))
			{
				_type = value;
				int angle = GetAngle(_attrib[0], _type, bHysys: false);
				_rform.Angle = angle;
				base.Children.Remove(_back);
				base.Children.Remove(_canvas);
				Draw(onPfs: false);
			}
		}

		public void ChangeAttrib(int modifier)
		{
			int num = (int)_rform.Angle;
			switch (modifier)
			{
			case 0:
				num += 90;
				break;
			case 1:
				num -= 90;
				break;
			}
			num %= 360;
			_rform.Angle = num;
			_attrib = GetAttrib(num, _type);
		}

		public override void TagAnchor(string id, Anchor anchor, bool reversedirection)
		{
			if (anchor != null)
			{
				anchor.TagPort(this);
				_anchor = anchor;
				_anchordx = _x - anchor.X;
				_anchordy = _y - anchor.Y;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_anchor != null)
				{
					_anchor.UntagPort(this);
				}
				_anchor = null;
			}
		}

		public void UntagAnchor(Anchor anchor)
		{
			if (_anchor != null)
			{
				_anchor.UntagPort(this);
			}
			_anchor = null;
		}

		public new void Move(double x, double y)
		{
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_tform.X = x;
			_tform.Y = y;
		}

		public void Drag(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_tform.X = _x + dx;
			_tform.Y = _y + dy;
		}

		public void GetPosition(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x;
			y = _y;
		}

		public void SetPosition(bool yvalue, double value)
		{
			if (yvalue)
			{
				_tform.Y = value + _anchordy;
			}
			else
			{
				_tform.X = value + _anchordx;
			}
			EndMove();
			if (this.PortMoved != null)
			{
				this.PortMoved(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		public void EndMove()
		{
			_x = _tform.X;
			_y = _tform.Y;
		}

		public void ChangePortType(string name, bool standalone)
		{
			if (standalone)
			{
				_parent.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.PortType, this);
			}
			_porttype = name;
		}

		private void Rotate(int side)
		{
			int num = 0;
			switch (side)
			{
			case 1:
				num = ((_type == "0") ? 180 : 0);
				break;
			case 2:
				num = ((!(_type == "0")) ? 180 : 0);
				break;
			case 3:
				num = ((_type == "0") ? (-90) : 90);
				break;
			case 4:
				num = ((_type == "0") ? 90 : (-90));
				break;
			}
			_rform.Angle = num;
		}

		public XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = doc.CreateElement("Port");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "IsChecked", "false");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "index", _index.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "name", _name);
			if (_kbase)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "porttype", _parent.PortTypeDisplayName(_porttype));
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "inport", (_type == "0") ? "True" : "False");
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "outport", (_type == "1") ? "True" : "False");
			if (_kbase || _abe)
			{
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "biport", (_type == "-1") ? "True" : "False");
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "direction", _streamdirection.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "autostreaminit", "false");
			int num = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				num = MatchItem(doc, "Visibility", _visibilitymap);
			}
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "visibility", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xmlElement, "update", "false");
			_datanode = xmlElement;
			return xmlElement;
		}

		public void UpdateData(string attrib, string value)
		{
			if (_datanode.Attributes[attrib] != null)
			{
				_datanode.Attributes["update"].Value = "true";
				_datanode.Attributes[attrib].Value = value;
				_datanode.Attributes["update"].Value = "false";
			}
		}

		public void Render(bool onPfs, bool visible)
		{
			int angle = GetAngle(_attrib[0], _type, bHysys: false);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_rform = new RotateTransform(angle));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new ScaleTransform(0.3, 0.3));
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y));
			base.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			Draw(onPfs);
			SetVisibility();
			if (!visible)
			{
				base.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
			}
		}

		public void SetAttrib()
		{
			_attrib = GetAttrib((int)_rform.Angle, _type);
		}

		private void Draw(bool onPfs)
		{
			base.ToolTip = _name;
			int num = ((!(_type == "0")) ? 50 : 0);
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, -10.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, -num);
			_back.Width = 50.0;
			_back.Height = 20.0;
			_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetTop(rectangle, -3.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(rectangle, 20 - num);
			if (_type == "-1")
			{
				rectangle.Width = 20.0;
			}
			else
			{
				rectangle.Width = 30.0;
			}
			rectangle.Height = 6.0;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			PathGeometry pathGeometry = new PathGeometry();
			PathFigureCollection pathFigureCollection = new PathFigureCollection();
			PathFigure pathFigure = new PathFigure();
			pathFigure.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(-num, 0.0);
			PathSegmentCollection pathSegmentCollection = new PathSegmentCollection();
			if (_type == "-1")
			{
				pathSegmentCollection.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(20 - num, -10.0), isStroked: false));
				pathSegmentCollection.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(20 - num, 10.0), isStroked: false));
			}
			else
			{
				pathSegmentCollection.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(30 - num, -10.0), isStroked: false));
				pathSegmentCollection.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(20 - num, 0.0), isStroked: false));
				pathSegmentCollection.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(30 - num, 10.0), isStroked: false));
			}
			pathFigure.Segments = pathSegmentCollection;
			pathFigureCollection.Add(pathFigure);
			if (_type == "-1")
			{
				PathFigure pathFigure2 = new PathFigure();
				pathFigure2.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(0.0, 0.0);
				PathSegmentCollection pathSegmentCollection2 = new PathSegmentCollection();
				pathSegmentCollection2.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(-20.0, -10.0), isStroked: false));
				pathSegmentCollection2.Add(new LineSegment(new System.Windows.Point(-20.0, 10.0), isStroked: false));
				pathFigure2.Segments = pathSegmentCollection2;
				pathFigureCollection.Add(pathFigure2);
			}
			pathGeometry.Figures = pathFigureCollection;
			if (onPfs)
			{
				rectangle.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
				path.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
			else
			{
				rectangle.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green;
				path.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Green;
			}
			path.Data = pathGeometry;
			_canvas = new Canvas();
			_canvas.Children.Add(rectangle);
			_canvas.Children.Add(path);
			base.Children.Add(_back);
			base.Children.Add(_canvas);
		}

		public void Highlight(bool bOn, bool bLarge)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
			if (bOn)
			{
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = 0.5;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = 0.5;
				if (bLarge)
				{
					_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = 0.3;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = 0.3;
				if (bLarge)
				{
					_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void SetVisibility()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_visibilitymap))
			{
				Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(_canvas, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				if (binding != null)
				{
					BindingOperations.ClearBinding(_canvas, UIElement.VisibilityProperty);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Binding binding2 = new Binding();
				binding2.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _visibilitymap;
				_canvas.SetBinding(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, binding2);
				_canvas.IsVisibleChanged += Port_IsVisibleChanged;
			}
		}

		private void Port_IsVisibleChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.PortVisibilityChanged != null)
			{
				this.PortVisibilityChanged(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private int GetAngle(char attrib, string type, bool bHysys)
		{
			int result = 0;
			if (bHysys)
			{
				switch (attrib)
				{
				case 'd':
					attrib = 'u';
					break;
				case 'u':
					attrib = 'd';
					break;
				}
			}
			if (type == "0")
			{
				switch (attrib)
				{
				case 'l':
					result = 180;
					break;
				case 'd':
					result = 90;
					break;
				case 'u':
					result = -90;
					break;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				switch (attrib)
				{
				case 'r':
					result = 180;
					break;
				case 'u':
					result = 90;
					break;
				case 'd':
					result = -90;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string GetAttrib(int angle, string type)
		{
			if (angle < 0)
			{
				angle += 360;
			}
			string text = "";
			if (type == "0")
			{
				return angle switch
				{
					180 => "l", 
					90 => "d", 
					270 => "u", 
					_ => "r", 
				};
			}
			return angle switch
			{
				180 => "r", 
				90 => "u", 
				270 => "d", 
				_ => "l", 
			};
		}

		public System.Drawing.Point GetDirection()
		{
			System.Drawing.Point result = default(System.Drawing.Point);
			if (_attrib == "l")
			{
				result.X = -1;
			}
			else if (_attrib == "r")
			{
				result.X = 1;
			}
			else if (_attrib == "u")
			{
				result.Y = 1;
			}
			else if (_attrib == "d")
			{
				result.Y = -1;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class PortLocator
	{
		private Canvas _panel;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Path m_path;

		private Port m_port;

		private System.Windows.Media.Pen m_pen;

		private TransformGroup m_transformgroup;

		private bool _generated;

		public PortLocator(Canvas root)
		{
			_panel = root;
			m_pen = new System.Windows.Media.Pen(System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black, 0.1);
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			_generated = false;
		}

		public void Selected(Port port)
		{
			if (!_generated)
			{
				GenGeometry();
				_generated = true;
			}
			m_port = port;
		}

		public void MovePort(System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			double mindelta = 1000.0;
			double p2 = p.X;
			bool side;
			bool intersection = GetIntersection(-1000.0, p.Y, 1000.0, p.Y, ref p2, out side, ypos: false, ref mindelta);
			double p3 = p.Y;
			bool side2;
			bool intersection2 = GetIntersection(p.X, 1000.0, p.X, -1000.0, ref p3, out side2, ypos: true, ref mindelta);
			if (intersection2 || intersection)
			{
				if (intersection2)
				{
					m_port.Move(p.X, p3);
					m_port.AccessSide = (side2 ? 3 : 4);
				}
				else if (intersection)
				{
					m_port.Move(p2, p.Y);
					m_port.AccessSide = (side ? 1 : 2);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool GetIntersection(double x1, double y1, double x2, double y2, ref double p, out bool side, bool ypos, ref double mindelta)
		{
			side = false;
			LineGeometry lineGeometry = new LineGeometry();
			lineGeometry.StartPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x1, y1);
			lineGeometry.EndPoint = new System.Windows.Point(x2, y2);
			PathGeometry widenedPathGeometry = m_path.Data.GetWidenedPathGeometry(m_pen);
			widenedPathGeometry.Transform = m_transformgroup;
			PathGeometry widenedPathGeometry2 = lineGeometry.GetWidenedPathGeometry(m_pen);
			CombinedGeometry combinedGeometry = new CombinedGeometry(GeometryCombineMode.Intersect, widenedPathGeometry, widenedPathGeometry2);
			PathGeometry flattenedPathGeometry = combinedGeometry.GetFlattenedPathGeometry();
			if (flattenedPathGeometry.Figures.Count == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			double num = double.NaN;
			double num2 = 1000.0;
			double num3 = -1000.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < flattenedPathGeometry.Figures.Count; i++)
			{
				Rect bounds = new PathGeometry(new PathFigure[1] { flattenedPathGeometry.Figures[i] }).Bounds;
				double num4 = ((!ypos) ? bounds.Left : bounds.Top);
				if (num4 > num3)
				{
					num3 = num4;
				}
				if (num4 < num2)
				{
					num2 = num4;
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num4 - p) < mindelta)
				{
					num = num4;
					mindelta = Math.Abs(num4 - p);
				}
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(num))
			{
				return false;
			}
			p = num;
			side = Math.Abs(num - num2) < Math.Abs(num - num3);
			return true;
		}

		public void GenGeometry()
		{
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			geometryGroup.Transform = _panel.RenderTransform;
			GenCanvasGeometry(_panel, geometryGroup);
			m_path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			m_path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			m_path.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
			m_path.Data = geometryGroup;
		}

		public void ShowPath()
		{
			_panel.Children.Add(m_path);
		}

		private void GenCanvasGeometry(Canvas root, GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in root.Children)
			{
				if (child is Port)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (child is Layer)
				{
					Layer layer = (Layer)child;
					foreach (UIElement child2 in layer.Children)
					{
						if (child2 is Element)
						{
							Element element = (Element)child2;
							element.GenGeometry(gg);
						}
					}
				}
				else if (child is Element)
				{
					Element element2 = (Element)child;
					element2.GenGeometry(gg);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class Rectangle : Element
	{
		internal double _x;

		internal double _y;

		internal double _width;

		internal double _height;

		internal double _bx;

		internal double _by;

		private double _wx;

		private double _wy;

		private double _widthw;

		private double _heightw;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _back;

		private bool _proportional;

		private double _ratio;

		public bool Proportional
		{
			set
			{
				_proportional = value;
				if (_proportional)
				{
					_ratio = _height / _width;
				}
			}
		}

		public Rectangle(System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rect, bool textboundary = false)
			: base(rect)
		{
			_x = Canvas.GetLeft(rect);
			_y = Canvas.GetTop(rect);
			_width = rect.Width;
			_height = rect.Height;
			InitializeAnchored(7);
			if (textboundary)
			{
				Proportional = true;
				_thickness = 4.0;
			}
			AddBack();
		}

		public Rectangle(System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rect, double x, double y, bool textboundary = false)
			: base(rect)
		{
			_bx = x;
			_by = y;
			_x = Canvas.GetLeft(rect);
			_y = Canvas.GetTop(rect);
			_width = rect.Width;
			_height = rect.Height;
			InitializeAnchored(7);
			if (textboundary)
			{
				Proportional = true;
			}
			AddBack();
		}

		public Rectangle(System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly, bool nomap = false)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			_x = poly.Points[0].X;
			_y = poly.Points[0].Y;
			_width = poly.Points[1].X - _x;
			_height = poly.Points[2].Y - _y;
			if (_width < 0.0)
			{
				_x = poly.Points[1].X;
				_width = 0.0 - _width;
			}
			if (_height < 0.0)
			{
				_y = poly.Points[2].Y;
				_height = 0.0 - _height;
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(rectangle, _x);
			Canvas.SetTop(rectangle, _y);
			rectangle.Width = _width;
			rectangle.Height = _height;
			if (nomap)
			{
				rectangle.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			}
			else
			{
				rectangle.Stroke = MappedBrush("Color");
			}
			SetElement(rectangle);
			InitializeAnchored(7);
			AddBack();
		}

		protected override void AddBack()
		{
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _x - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _y - _thickness / 2.0);
			_back.Width = _width + _thickness;
			_back.Height = _height + _thickness;
			_back.StrokeThickness = _thickness;
			_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Insert(0, _back);
		}

		public override void SetCoordinate(ref List<double> x, ref List<double> y)
		{
			_wx = ((x[0] < x[1]) ? x[0] : x[1]);
			_wy = ((y[0] > y[1]) ? y[0] : y[1]);
			_widthw = Math.Abs(x[0] - x[1]);
			_heightw = Math.Abs(y[0] - y[1]);
		}

		public override void Highlight(bool bOn, Handle handle)
		{
			if (bOn)
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
			else
			{
				_back.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		public void SetBase(double bx, double by)
		{
			_bx = bx;
			_by = by;
		}

		public override bool GetCenterPoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x + _width / 2.0;
			y = _y + _height / 2.0;
			return true;
		}

		public override void Modify(string command, double cx, double cy)
		{
			double x = _x;
			double y = _y;
			double num = _x + _width;
			double num2 = _y + _height;
			switch (command)
			{
			case "RotLeft":
			case "RotRight":
			{
				double num3 = x - cx;
				double num4 = y - cy;
				double num5 = num - cx;
				double num6 = num2 - cy;
				if (command == "RotLeft")
				{
					x = cx + num4;
					y = cy - num3;
					num = cx + num6;
					num2 = cy - num5;
				}
				else
				{
					x = cx - num4;
					y = cy + num3;
					num = cx - num6;
					num2 = cy + num5;
				}
				_x = ((x < num) ? x : num);
				_y = ((y < num2) ? y : num2);
				double width = _width;
				_width = _height;
				_height = width;
				break;
			}
			case "FlipX":
				x = cx - (x - cx);
				num = cx - (num - cx);
				_x = ((x < num) ? x : num);
				break;
			case "FlipY":
				y = cy - (y - cy);
				num2 = cy - (num2 - cy);
				_y = ((y < num2) ? y : num2);
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			AddHandle("SE", _x + _width, _y + _height, _anchored[0]);
			if (!_proportional)
			{
				AddHandle("NW", _x, _y, _anchored[1]);
				AddHandle("NE", _x + _width, _y, _anchored[2]);
				AddHandle("SW", _x, _y + _height, _anchored[3]);
				AddHandle("Center", _x + _width / 2.0, _y + _height / 2.0, _anchored[4]);
				AddHandle("Top", _x + _width / 2.0, _y, _anchored[5]);
				AddHandle("Side", _x + _width, _y + _height / 2.0, _anchored[6]);
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			string fill = GetFill("Rectangle");
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Rectangle");
			int num = MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke);
			int num2 = MatchProperty(doc, "Fill", fill);
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", num.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "fill", num2.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "arrowactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "angleactive", "Collapsed");
			AddAttribute(xml, "pointactive", "Collapsed");
			AddPositionNode();
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var1", "Left");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value1", _x.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var2", "Top");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value2", _y.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var3", "Width");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value3", _width.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var4", "Height");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value4", _height.ToString());
			return xml;
		}

		public override void Update(XmlNode node)
		{
			_width = GetDouble(node, "Width");
			_height = GetDouble(node, "Height");
			_x = GetDouble(node, "Canvas.Left");
			_y = GetDouble(node, "Canvas.Top");
			Refresh();
			base.Update(node);
		}

		private void Refresh()
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)_element;
			rectangle.Width = _width;
			rectangle.Height = _height;
			Canvas.SetLeft(rectangle, _x);
			Canvas.SetTop(rectangle, _y);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _x - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _y - _thickness / 2.0);
			_back.Width = _width + _thickness;
			_back.Height = _height + _thickness;
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_positionnode != null)
			{
				_positionnode.Attributes["value1"].Value = (_x + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value2"].Value = (_y + dy).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value3"].Value = _width.ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value4"].Value = _height.ToString();
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result);
			if ((name == "Value3" || name == "Value4") && result <= 0.0)
			{
				string text = ((name == "Value3") ? "Width" : "Height");
				UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
				return text + " must be positive.";
			}
			switch (name)
			{
			case "Value1":
				_x = result;
				break;
			case "Value2":
				_y = result;
				break;
			case "Value3":
				_width = result;
				break;
			case "Value4":
				_height = result;
				break;
			}
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
			return "";
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			string text = "<Rectangle";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			string text4 = "";
			string text5 = "";
			string paths = "";
			string text6 = "";
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (ResolveAnchor)
			{
				string text7 = "";
				string text8 = "";
				string text9 = "";
				string text10 = "";
				string text11 = "";
				string text12 = "";
				foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
				{
					if (handle.Anchor == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					Anchor anchor = handle.Anchor;
					string iD = handle.ID;
					if (iD == "NE" && anchor.Direction == 1)
					{
						text8 = anchor.GetParam("yparam");
						text7 = anchor.GetParam("yconv").Replace(' ', '_');
						text4 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) ? ("{Binding XPath=" + text8 + "}") : ("{Binding XPath=" + text8 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + text7 + "}"));
					}
					else if (iD == "SE" && anchor.Direction == 1)
					{
						text10 = anchor.GetParam("yparam");
						text9 = anchor.GetParam("yconv").Replace(' ', '_');
					}
					else if (iD == "Side" && anchor.Direction == 2)
					{
						text12 = anchor.GetParam("xparam");
						text11 = anchor.GetParam("xconv").Replace(' ', '_');
						handle.GetOffset(out var dx, out var _);
						if (dx != 0.0)
						{
							text11 = dx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "+" + text11;
						}
						if (text12.Contains(','))
						{
							flag = true;
							flag2 = true;
							paths = text12;
							text6 = text11;
							continue;
						}
						text2 = "{Binding XPath=" + text12 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=(" + text11 + ")*2}";
						text5 = "{Binding XPath=" + text12 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=(" + text11 + ")*-1}";
					}
				}
				if (text7 != "")
				{
					if (text9 != "")
					{
						if (text8 == text10)
						{
							text3 = "{Binding XPath=" + text8 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=(" + text9 + ")-(" + text7 + ")}";
						}
					}
					else
					{
						text3 = "{Binding XPath=" + text8 + ",Converter={StaticResource Calc},ConverterParameter=" + (_y + _height).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "-(" + text7 + ")}";
					}
				}
			}
			if (text2 == "")
			{
				text2 = _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			if (text3 == "")
			{
				text3 = _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			if (text4 == "")
			{
				text4 = _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			if (text5 == "")
			{
				text5 = _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			text = "<Rectangle ";
			text += SaveVisibility();
			if (!flag)
			{
				text = text + " Width=\"" + text2 + "\"";
			}
			text = text + " Height=\"" + text3 + "\"";
			text = text + " Canvas.Top=\"" + text4 + "\"";
			if (!flag2)
			{
				text = text + " Canvas.Left=\"" + text5 + "\"";
			}
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			if (flag)
			{
				text += "<Rectangle.Width>";
				text += MultiBinding(paths, "(" + text6 + ")*2");
				text += "</Rectangle.Width>";
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				text += "<Canvas.Left>";
				text += MultiBinding(paths, "(" + text6 + ")*-1");
				text += "</Canvas.Left>";
			}
			text += GradientFill("Rectangle");
			return text + "</Rectangle>";
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			text += "<rect ";
			text = text + "x=\"" + _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "width=\"" + _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "height=\"" + _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			int num = 0;
			if (_fill != "----" && _fill != "None" && _fill[0] != '@' && _fill[0] != '#')
			{
				if (_fill.Contains("Vertical"))
				{
					num = 2;
				}
				else if (_fill.Contains("Horizontal"))
				{
					num = 1;
				}
			}
			if (_stroke != null && _stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text = text + "stroke=\"" + ConvBinding(_stroke) + "\" ";
			}
			else if (num > 0)
			{
				text += "stroke=\"none\" ";
			}
			switch (num)
			{
			case 1:
				text += "fill=\"url(#lineary)\" ";
				break;
			case 2:
				text += "fill=\"url(#linearx)\" ";
				break;
			default:
				if (_fill != null && (_fill[0] == '@' || _fill[0] == '#'))
				{
					text += "fill=\"black\" ";
				}
				break;
			}
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<Rectangle";
			text = text + " Width=\"" + _widthw.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Height=\"" + _heightw.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " X=\"" + _wx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Y=\"" + _wy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text += SimpleProperty();
			text += ">";
			return text + "</Rectangle>";
		}

		public override void LoadXmlText(string xml)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(xml);
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			_wx = GetDouble(documentElement, "X");
			_wy = GetDouble(documentElement, "Y");
			_widthw = GetDouble(documentElement, "Width");
			_heightw = GetDouble(documentElement, "Height");
		}

		public override void GetBasePoint(out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = _x;
			y = _y;
		}

		public void SetDimension(double width, double height)
		{
			_width = width;
			_height = height;
			if (_proportional)
			{
				_ratio = _height / _width;
			}
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)_element;
			rectangle.Width = width;
			rectangle.Height = height;
			_back.Width = width + _thickness;
			_back.Height = height + _thickness;
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public void GetDimension(out double width, out double height)
		{
			width = _width;
			height = _height;
		}

		public void SetPosition(double x, double y)
		{
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle element = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)_element;
			Canvas.SetLeft(element, _x);
			Canvas.SetTop(element, _y);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _x - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _y - _thickness / 2.0);
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_bx + _x < minx)
			{
				minx = _bx + _x;
			}
			if (_bx + _x + _width > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _bx + _x + _width;
			}
			if (_by + _y < miny)
			{
				miny = _by + _y;
			}
			if (_by + _y + _height > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _by + _y + _height;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x += dx;
			_y += dy;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
			Refresh();
		}

		public override void Resize(ref string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				return;
			}
			RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)_element;
			if (handle == "Top")
			{
				double num3 = _y + _height;
				double num4 = y - _by - _y;
				_y = y - _by;
				num = num3 - _y - num4;
				num2 = _width;
			}
			else if (handle == "Side")
			{
				double num5 = _x + _width;
				double num6 = x - _bx - num5;
				num2 = _width + num6 * 2.0;
				_x = x - num2;
				num = _height;
			}
			else if (handle[0] == 'N')
			{
				double num7 = _y + _height;
				_y = y - _by;
				num = num7 - _y;
			}
			else if (handle[0] == 'S')
			{
				num = _height + y - (_by + _y + _height);
			}
			if (handle[1] == 'W')
			{
				double num8 = _x + _width;
				_x = x - _bx;
				num2 = num8 - _x;
			}
			else if (handle[1] == 'E')
			{
				num2 = _width + x - (_bx + _x + _width);
			}
			if (_proportional)
			{
				if (num2 * _ratio > num)
				{
					num = num2 * _ratio;
				}
				else
				{
					num2 = num / _ratio;
				}
			}
			if (num2 > 0.0)
			{
				_width = num2;
			}
			if (num >= 0.0)
			{
				_height = num;
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(rectangle, _x);
			Canvas.SetTop(rectangle, _y);
			rectangle.Width = _width;
			rectangle.Height = _height;
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, _x - _thickness / 2.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, _y - _thickness / 2.0);
			_back.Width = _width + _thickness;
			_back.Height = _height + _thickness;
			UpdateValues(0.0, 0.0);
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				translateTransform.X = x - (_x + _width * 0.5);
				translateTransform.Y = y - (_y + _height * 0.5);
			}
			else
			{
				Resize(ref handle, x, y);
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "Center")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}

		public override void GenGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)_element;
			double top = Canvas.GetTop(rectangle);
			double left = Canvas.GetLeft(rectangle);
			Rect bounds = ((RectangleGeometry)rectangle.RenderedGeometry).Bounds;
			if (!bounds.IsEmpty)
			{
				bounds.Width = _width;
				bounds.Height = _height;
				RectangleGeometry rectangleGeometry = new RectangleGeometry(bounds);
				if (!double.IsNaN(top))
				{
					rectangleGeometry.Transform = new TranslateTransform(left, top);
				}
				gg.Children.Add(rectangleGeometry);
			}
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.WcToDvc(_wx, _wy, out _x, out _y);
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_widthw, out _width);
			coeff.WcToDvcDelta(_heightw, out _height);
			Refresh();
			RefreshHandles();
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.DvcToWc(_x, _y, out _wx, out _wy);
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_width, out _widthw);
			coeff.DvcToWcDelta(_height, out _heightw);
		}
	}
	public class AnySelector
	{
	}
	public class ElementTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Name == "Group")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("GroupTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			if (xmlNode.Name == "Element")
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "TextBox")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextEntryTemplate") as DataTemplate;
				}
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "Text")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextTemplate") as DataTemplate;
				}
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "Viewbox")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextTemplate") as DataTemplate;
				}
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("ElementTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class ElementTemplateSelector2 : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Name == "Group")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("GroupTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
			}
			if (xmlNode.Name == "Element")
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "TextBox")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextEntryTemplate") as DataTemplate;
				}
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "Text")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
				}
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value == "Viewbox")
				{
					return frameworkElement.FindResource("TextTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
				}
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("ElementTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class EditTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["Edit"] != null)
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("EditTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return frameworkElement.FindResource("ModelTemplate") as DataTemplate;
		}
	}
	public class EditTemplateSelector2 : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["Edit"] != null)
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("EditTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return frameworkElement.FindResource("ModelTemplate2") as DataTemplate;
		}
	}
	public class PortTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["inport"].Value == "True")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("InPortTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["outport"].Value == "True")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("OutPortTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			if (xmlNode.Attributes["biport"] != null && xmlNode.Attributes["biport"].Value == "True")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("BiPortTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public class CommandTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = container as FrameworkElement;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)item;
			if (xmlNode.Name == "MenuItem")
			{
				return frameworkElement.FindResource("MenuTemplate") as DataTemplate;
			}
			return frameworkElement.FindResource("CommandTemplate") as DataTemplate;
		}
	}
	public class Text : Element
	{
		private IconCtrl _parent;

		private Viewbox _vb;

		private TextBox _textbox;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _rectbox;

		private Rectangle _rectangle;

		private bool _new;

		internal double _x;

		internal double _y;

		internal double _wx;

		internal double _wy;

		internal string _text;

		internal double _size;

		internal double _width;

		internal double _height;

		internal int _alignment;

		internal bool _bold;

		internal bool _italic;

		internal bool _underline;

		internal string _font;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private XmlNode _attribnode;

		private double _xmargin = 2.0;

		private double _ymargin = 1.0;

		private bool _empty;

		private List<string> _params;

		public bool _valueset;

		public TextBox TextBox => _textbox;

		public Rectangle Boundary => _rectangle;

		public bool Entered { get; set; }

		public bool Locked { get; set; }

		public Text(IconCtrl parent, TextBlock tb, bool hysysicon, Viewbox vb = null)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			base.Control = parent;
			_text = tb.Text;
			_valueset = true;
			if (tb.Inlines != null)
			{
				_text = "";
				foreach (Inline inline in tb.Inlines)
				{
					if (inline is Run)
					{
						Run run = (Run)inline;
						Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(run, Run.TextProperty);
						if (binding != null)
						{
							_text = _text + "@" + binding.XPath + " ";
						}
						else
						{
							_text += run.Text;
						}
					}
					else if (inline is LineBreak)
					{
						_text += "\r\n";
					}
				}
			}
			base.Loaded += Text_Loaded;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += Text_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			base.GotKeyboardFocus += Text_GotKeyboardFocus;
			if (vb != null)
			{
				_vb = vb;
				_width = vb.Width;
				_height = vb.Height;
				_x = Canvas.GetLeft(vb);
				_y = Canvas.GetTop(vb);
			}
			else
			{
				GetDimension(tb);
			}
			InitializeAnchored(1);
			if (tb.RenderTransform is TransformGroup)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)tb.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				_x = translateTransform.X;
				_y = translateTransform.Y;
				if (hysysicon)
				{
					_y = 0.0 - _y;
				}
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
			_size = tb.FontSize;
			switch (tb.TextAlignment)
			{
			case TextAlignment.Center:
				_alignment = 1;
				_x += _width * 0.5;
				break;
			case TextAlignment.Right:
				_alignment = 2;
				_x += _width;
				break;
			default:
				_alignment = 0;
				break;
			}
			Entered = false;
			_rectbox = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			_rectbox.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			_rectbox.Height = _height;
			_rectbox.Width = _width;
			if (_vb != null)
			{
				Canvas.SetTop(_rectbox, _y - _ymargin);
				Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, NormalizeXPosition() - _xmargin);
			}
			else
			{
				Canvas.SetTop(_rectbox, 0.0 - _height / 2.0);
				Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, 0.0 - _width / 2.0);
			}
			base.Children.Add(_rectbox);
			_textbox = new TextBox();
			_textbox.Loaded += _textbox_Loaded;
			_textbox.LostKeyboardFocus += _textbox_LostKeyboardFocus;
			_textbox.KeyUp += _textbox_KeyUp;
			_textbox.FontSize = tb.FontSize;
			_textbox.FontWeight = tb.FontWeight;
			_textbox.FontStyle = tb.FontStyle;
			_textbox.Foreground = tb.Foreground;
			_textbox.TextAlignment = tb.TextAlignment;
			_textbox.TextDecorations = tb.TextDecorations;
			_textbox.Text = _text;
			_textbox.IsReadOnly = true;
			_textbox.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			_textbox.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
			_textbox.AcceptsReturn = true;
			GetDimension();
			if (_vb != null)
			{
				_size = _textbox.FontSize;
			}
			base.Children.Add(_textbox);
			_element = _textbox;
			if (tb.FontWeight == FontWeights.Bold)
			{
				_bold = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bold = false;
			}
			if (tb.FontStyle == FontStyles.Italic)
			{
				_italic = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_italic = false;
			}
			_underline = false;
			if (tb.TextDecorations.Count > 0)
			{
				TextDecoration textDecoration = tb.TextDecorations[0];
				if (textDecoration.Location == TextDecorationLocation.Underline)
				{
					_underline = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public Text(TextBlock tb, bool hysysicon, Viewbox vb = null)
		{
			_text = tb.Text;
			if (tb.Inlines != null)
			{
				_text = "";
				foreach (Inline inline in tb.Inlines)
				{
					if (inline is Run)
					{
						Run run = (Run)inline;
						Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(run, Run.TextProperty);
						if (binding != null)
						{
							_text = _text + "@" + binding.XPath + " ";
						}
						else
						{
							_text += run.Text;
						}
					}
					else if (inline is LineBreak)
					{
						_text += "\r\n";
					}
				}
			}
			GetDimension(tb);
			InitializeAnchored(1);
			if (tb.RenderTransform is TransformGroup)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)tb.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				_x = translateTransform.X;
				_y = translateTransform.Y;
				if (hysysicon)
				{
					_y = 0.0 - _y;
				}
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
			_size = tb.FontSize;
			Setup(tb, hysysicon);
			if (vb != null)
			{
				_vb = vb;
				_width = vb.Width;
				_height = vb.Height;
				_x = Canvas.GetLeft(vb);
				_y = Canvas.GetTop(vb);
				base.Children.Add(vb);
			}
			else
			{
				base.Children.Add(tb);
			}
			_element = tb;
			switch (tb.TextAlignment)
			{
			case TextAlignment.Center:
				_alignment = 1;
				break;
			case TextAlignment.Right:
				_alignment = 2;
				break;
			default:
				_alignment = 0;
				break;
			}
			if (tb.FontWeight == FontWeights.Bold)
			{
				_bold = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_bold = false;
			}
			if (tb.FontStyle == FontStyles.Italic)
			{
				_italic = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_italic = false;
			}
			_underline = false;
			if (tb.TextDecorations.Count > 0)
			{
				TextDecoration textDecoration = tb.TextDecorations[0];
				if (textDecoration.Location == TextDecorationLocation.Underline)
				{
					_underline = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void Setup(TextBlock tb, bool hysysicon)
		{
			if (hysysicon)
			{
				tb.RenderTransform = new ScaleTransform(1.0, -1.0);
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y);
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform);
		}

		private double NormalizeXPosition()
		{
			if (_alignment == 1)
			{
				return _x - _width * 0.5;
			}
			if (_alignment == 2)
			{
				return _x - _width;
			}
			return _x;
		}

		private void _textbox_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_textbox.Text = ConvertParam(_text);
			if (_rectbox != null)
			{
				_rectbox.Width = _textbox.ActualWidth;
				_rectbox.Height = _textbox.ActualHeight;
			}
			if (_rectangle != null)
			{
				_rectangle.SetDimension(_textbox.ActualWidth, _textbox.ActualHeight);
			}
		}

		private void vb_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Control.DragElement(this);
		}

		private void tb_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = (TextBlock)_element;
			double num = _vb.ActualWidth / textBlock.ActualWidth;
			_size = textBlock.FontSize * num;
			if (_attribnode != null)
			{
				_attribnode.Attributes["size"].Value = _size.ToString("G3");
			}
		}

		public Text(IconCtrl parent, TextBox tb, double x, double y, bool hysysicon, bool centered = false)
		{
			_parent = parent;
			_new = true;
			base.Loaded += Text_Loaded;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += Text_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			base.GotKeyboardFocus += Text_GotKeyboardFocus;
			InitializeAnchored(1);
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_bold = false;
			_italic = false;
			_underline = false;
			if (tb != null)
			{
				_stroke = tb.Foreground.ToString();
				_size = tb.FontSize;
				_font = tb.FontFamily.ToString();
				_bold = tb.FontWeight == FontWeights.Bold;
				_italic = tb.FontStyle == FontStyles.Italic;
				if (tb.TextDecorations.Count > 0 && tb.TextDecorations[0].Location == TextDecorationLocation.Underline)
				{
					_underline = true;
				}
			}
			Entered = false;
			_rectbox = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			_rectbox.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			_rectbox.Height = 30.0;
			_rectbox.Width = 30.0;
			_width = _rectbox.Width;
			_height = _rectbox.Height;
			Canvas.SetTop(_rectbox, -15.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, -5.0);
			base.Children.Add(_rectbox);
			if (tb == null)
			{
				_text = "(Empty)";
				_textbox = new TextBox();
				_textbox.Text = "(Empty)";
				_textbox.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
				_textbox.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Beige;
				_empty = true;
			}
			else
			{
				_textbox = tb;
				_text = tb.Text;
				_textbox.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
			_textbox.LostKeyboardFocus += _textbox_LostKeyboardFocus;
			_textbox.PreviewKeyDown += _textbox_PreviewKeyDown;
			_textbox.KeyUp += _textbox_KeyUp;
			_textbox.BorderThickness = new Thickness(0.0);
			_textbox.TextAlignment = (centered ? TextAlignment.Center : TextAlignment.Left);
			_textbox.AcceptsReturn = true;
			_size = _textbox.FontSize;
			base.Children.Add(_textbox);
			_element = _textbox;
			_alignment = (centered ? 1 : 0);
			GetDimension();
		}

		private void _textbox_KeyUp(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_alignment != 0)
			{
				ShiftPosition();
			}
		}

		private void _textbox_PreviewKeyDown(object sender, KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_empty)
			{
				_textbox.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
				_textbox.Text = "";
				_empty = false;
			}
		}

		public void SetFocus()
		{
			_textbox.Focus();
		}

		private void _textbox_LostKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewFocus != null && e.NewFocus.GetType().Name == "EditorContextMenu")
			{
				return;
			}
			_textbox.IsReadOnly = true;
			_textbox.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			if (_text != _textbox.Text)
			{
				if (_valueset && _parent != null)
				{
					_parent.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.TextContents, this);
				}
				_valueset = true;
				_text = _textbox.Text;
			}
			_textbox.Text = ConvertParam(_text);
			if (_valueset)
			{
				if (_rectbox != null)
				{
					_rectbox.Width = _textbox.ActualWidth;
					_rectbox.Height = _textbox.ActualHeight;
					_width = _rectbox.Width;
					_height = _rectbox.Height;
				}
				if (_rectangle != null)
				{
					_rectangle.SetDimension(_textbox.ActualWidth, _textbox.ActualHeight);
				}
				if (_attribnode != null)
				{
					_attribnode.Attributes["text"].Value = _text;
				}
			}
		}

		private void Text_GotKeyboardFocus(object sender, KeyboardFocusChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (!Locked && _textbox != null && _textbox.IsReadOnly)
			{
				_textbox.IsReadOnly = false;
				_textbox.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Beige;
				_textbox.Text = _text;
			}
		}

		private void Text_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Locked)
			{
				return;
			}
			Entered = true;
			if (_rectangle != null)
			{
				_rectangle.Highlight(bOn: true, null);
				return;
			}
			int count = base.Children.Count;
			if (count > 1 && base.Children[count - 2] is System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)base.Children[count - 2];
				rectangle.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gold;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Locked)
			{
				return;
			}
			Entered = false;
			if (_rectangle != null)
			{
				_rectangle.Highlight(bOn: false, null);
				return;
			}
			int count = base.Children.Count;
			if (count > 1 && base.Children[count - 2] is System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle rectangle = (System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)base.Children[count - 2];
				rectangle.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			}
		}

		private void Text_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_text.Contains("\\n"))
			{
				_textbox.Text = "";
				string text = _text;
				while (true)
				{
					int num = text.IndexOf("\\n");
					if (num < 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					string textData = text.Substring(0, num);
					_textbox.AppendText(textData);
					_textbox.AppendText(Environment.NewLine);
					text = text.Substring(num + 2);
				}
				_textbox.AppendText(text);
				_textbox.UpdateLayout();
				_text = _textbox.Text;
				GetDimension();
			}
			if (_rectbox != null && _rectangle == null)
			{
				int index = base.Children.IndexOf(_rectbox);
				base.Children.Remove(_rectbox);
				_rectangle = new Rectangle(_rectbox, textboundary: true);
				_rectangle.Control = base.Control;
				_rectangle.OnLabel = true;
				_rectangle.OnPfs = base.OnPfs;
				_rectangle.AddHandles();
				base.Children.Insert(index, _rectangle);
			}
		}

		public void ChangeAttrib(string color, double size, string font, bool bold, bool italic, bool underscore)
		{
			_stroke = color;
			_size = size;
			_font = font;
			_bold = bold;
			_italic = italic;
			_underline = underscore;
		}

		public override string GenXmlText()
		{
			string text = "<Text x=\"" + _wx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" y=\"" + _wy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			if (_stroke != null)
			{
				text = text + "Foreground=\"" + _stroke + "\" ";
			}
			text = text + "FontSize=\"" + _size.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			if (_font != null)
			{
				text = text + "FontFamily=\"" + _font + "\" ";
			}
			if (_bold)
			{
				text += "FontWeight=\"Bold\" ";
			}
			if (_italic)
			{
				text += "FontStyle=\"Italic\" ";
			}
			if (_underline)
			{
				text += "TextDecorations=\"underline\" ";
			}
			text += ">";
			text += _text;
			return text + "</Text>";
		}

		private void GetDimension()
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(_textbox.Width) || _textbox.Width == 0.0)
			{
				string textToFormat = _text;
				if (_text.Contains('@'))
				{
					textToFormat = ConvertParam(_text);
				}
				FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(textToFormat, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(_textbox.FontFamily, _textbox.FontStyle, _textbox.FontWeight, _textbox.FontStretch), _textbox.FontSize, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black);
				if (_vb == null)
				{
					if (_alignment != 1)
					{
					}
				}
				else if (formattedText.Height > _height && _height != 0.0)
				{
					double num = _height / formattedText.Height;
					_textbox.FontSize *= num;
					formattedText = new FormattedText(textToFormat, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(_textbox.FontFamily, _textbox.FontStyle, _textbox.FontWeight, _textbox.FontStretch), _textbox.FontSize, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black);
				}
				_width = formattedText.Width;
				_height = formattedText.Height;
				ResetPosition();
			}
			else
			{
				_width = _textbox.Width;
				_height = _textbox.Height;
			}
		}

		public void ResetPosition()
		{
			double num = NormalizeXPosition();
			Canvas.SetLeft(_textbox, num - _xmargin);
			Canvas.SetTop(_textbox, _y - _ymargin);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, num - _xmargin);
			Canvas.SetTop(_rectbox, _y - _ymargin);
			_rectbox.Width = _width;
			_rectbox.Height = _height;
			if (_rectangle != null)
			{
				_rectangle.SetPosition(num, _y);
				_rectangle.SetDimension(_width, _height);
			}
		}

		private void GetDimension(TextBlock tb)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(tb.Width) || tb.Width == 0.0)
			{
				FormattedText formattedText = new FormattedText(_text, CultureInfo.CurrentUICulture, FlowDirection.LeftToRight, new Typeface(tb.FontFamily, tb.FontStyle, tb.FontWeight, tb.FontStretch), tb.FontSize, System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black);
				_width = formattedText.Width;
				_height = formattedText.Height;
			}
			else
			{
				_width = tb.Width;
				_height = tb.Height;
			}
		}

		public void TextContent(string content)
		{
			_text = content;
			if (_textbox.IsReadOnly)
			{
				_textbox.Text = ConvertParam(_text);
			}
			else
			{
				_textbox.Text = _text;
			}
			GetDimension();
		}

		public void TextSize(string size)
		{
			double num = double.Parse(size, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			if (!(num <= 0.0))
			{
				if (_vb != null)
				{
					double num2 = num / _size;
					_width = (_vb.Width *= num2);
					_height = (_vb.Height *= num2);
					_size = num;
					_textbox.FontSize *= num2;
				}
				else
				{
					_size = num;
					_textbox.FontSize = _size;
				}
				GetDimension();
			}
		}

		public void TextStyle(string name, bool bOn)
		{
			TextBox textBox = (TextBox)_element;
			switch (name)
			{
			case "BoldText":
				_bold = bOn;
				if (_bold)
				{
					textBox.FontWeight = FontWeights.Bold;
				}
				else
				{
					textBox.FontWeight = FontWeights.Regular;
				}
				break;
			case "ItalicText":
				_italic = bOn;
				if (_italic)
				{
					textBox.FontStyle = FontStyles.Italic;
				}
				else
				{
					textBox.FontStyle = FontStyles.Normal;
				}
				break;
			case "UnderText":
				_underline = bOn;
				if (_underline)
				{
					textBox.TextDecorations = TextDecorations.Underline;
				}
				else
				{
					textBox.TextDecorations = null;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		public void EndMove(IconCtrl ctrl)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
			if (translateTransform.X != 0.0 || translateTransform.Y != 0.0)
			{
				ctrl?.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Move, this);
				_x += translateTransform.X;
				_y += translateTransform.Y;
				double left = Canvas.GetLeft(_textbox);
				double top = Canvas.GetTop(_textbox);
				Canvas.SetLeft(_textbox, left + translateTransform.X);
				Canvas.SetTop(_textbox, top + translateTransform.Y);
				_rectangle.Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				double x = (translateTransform.Y = 0.0);
				translateTransform.X = x;
			}
		}

		public void EndMove(double dx, double dy, double ddx, double ddy)
		{
			_x += dx;
			_y += dy;
			double left = Canvas.GetLeft(_textbox);
			double top = Canvas.GetTop(_textbox);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_textbox, left + dx + ddx);
			Canvas.SetTop(_textbox, top + dy + ddy);
			_rectangle.Move(dx + ddx, dy + ddy);
		}

		public void Resized()
		{
			_rectangle.GetBasePoint(out var x, out var y);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, x);
			Canvas.SetTop(_rectbox, y);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_textbox, x);
			Canvas.SetTop(_textbox, y);
			_rectangle.GetDimension(out var width, out var height);
			_rectbox.Width = width;
			_rectbox.Height = height;
			double num = width / _width;
			_textbox.FontSize *= num;
			_width = width;
			_height *= num;
			_x = x;
			if (_alignment == 1)
			{
				_x = x + _width * 0.5;
			}
			else if (_alignment == 2)
			{
				_x = x + _width;
			}
			_y = y;
			if (_vb != null)
			{
				GetDimension();
			}
			_size = _textbox.FontSize;
			if (_attribnode != null)
			{
				_attribnode.Attributes["size"].Value = _size.ToString("G3");
			}
		}

		private void ShiftPosition()
		{
			double num = _textbox.ActualWidth;
			if (_alignment == 1)
			{
				num *= 0.5;
			}
			Canvas.SetLeft(_textbox, _x - num);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_rectbox, _x - num);
			_rectangle.SetPosition(_x - num, _y);
		}

		public override void XmlNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			if (_vb == null)
			{
				base.XmlNode(node);
			}
			else
			{
				base.XmlNode(node.FirstChild);
			}
			if (_stroke == null && _parent != null)
			{
				GetStroke();
				ChangeProperty("Stroke", _stroke, null, null);
			}
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			string stroke = GetStroke();
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "Text");
			AddAttribute(xml, "text", _text);
			AddAttribute(xml, "size", _size.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "stroke", MatchProperty(doc, "Stroke", stroke).ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "align", _alignment.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "bold", _bold.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "italic", _italic.ToString());
			AddAttribute(xml, "underline", _underline.ToString());
			AddPositionNode();
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var1", "X");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value1", _x.ToString());
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "var2", "Y");
			AddAttribute(_positionnode, "value2", _y.ToString());
			_attribnode = xml;
			return xml;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			double num = _x;
			string text = _text;
			if (text.Contains('&'))
			{
				text = text.Replace("&", "&amp;");
			}
			string text2 = "Left";
			if (_alignment == 1)
			{
				text2 = "Center";
				num = _x - _width * 0.5;
			}
			else if (_alignment == 2)
			{
				text2 = "Right";
				num = _x - _width;
			}
			string text3 = "";
			if (_vb != null)
			{
				text3 += "<Viewbox";
				text3 += " StretchDirection=\"Both\" Stretch=\"Uniform\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Canvas.Left=\"" + _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Canvas.Top=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Width=\"" + _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Height=\"" + _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 += ">";
				text3 += "<TextBlock";
				text3 = text3 + " FontSize=\"" + _size.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			}
			else if (_new && _textbox != null)
			{
				text3 += "<Viewbox";
				text3 += " StretchDirection=\"Both\" Stretch=\"Uniform\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Canvas.Left=\"" + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Canvas.Top=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Width=\"" + _textbox.ActualWidth.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Height=\"" + _textbox.ActualHeight.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 += ">";
				text3 += "<TextBlock";
				text3 = text3 + " FontSize=\"" + _size.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			}
			else
			{
				text3 += "<TextBlock";
				text3 = text3 + " Width=\"" + _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " Height=\"" + _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
				text3 = text3 + " FontSize=\"" + _size.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			}
			if (_stroke != "----" && _stroke != "None")
			{
				text3 = text3 + " Foreground=\"" + PropertyXaml(_stroke) + "\"";
			}
			text3 = text3 + " TextAlignment=\"" + text2 + "\"";
			if (_bold)
			{
				text3 += " FontWeight=\"Bold\"";
			}
			if (_italic)
			{
				text3 += " FontStyle=\"Italic\"";
			}
			if (_underline)
			{
				text3 += " TextDecorations=\"Underline\"";
			}
			text3 += ">";
			if (_vb == null && (_textbox == null || !_new))
			{
				text3 += "<TextBlock.RenderTransform>";
				text3 += "<TransformGroup>";
				string text4 = text3;
				text3 = text4 + "<TranslateTransform X=\"" + num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" Y=\"" + (base.Control.HysysIcon ? (0.0 - _y) : _y).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" />";
				text3 += "</TransformGroup>";
				text3 += "</TextBlock.RenderTransform>";
			}
			if (!text.Contains('@') && !text.Contains("\r\n"))
			{
				text3 += text;
			}
			else
			{
				bool flag = false;
				text3 += "<TextBlock.Inlines>";
				string text5 = text;
				while (true)
				{
					int num2 = text5.IndexOf('@');
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					flag = true;
					string str = text5.Substring(0, num2);
					str = EmbedLineBreak(ref text3, str);
					text3 = text3 + "<Run>" + str + "</Run>";
					text5 = text5.Substring(num2 + 1);
					num2 = text5.IndexOf(' ');
					string text6 = ((num2 >= 0) ? text5.Substring(0, num2) : text5);
					if (bCurrent)
					{
						string attribValue = base.Control.GetAttribValue(text6, null);
						text3 = text3 + "<Run>" + attribValue + "</Run>";
					}
					else
					{
						text3 = text3 + "<Run Text=\"{Binding XPath=" + text6 + "}\" />";
					}
					if (num2 < 0)
					{
						text5 = "";
						break;
					}
					text5 = text5.Substring(num2);
				}
				if (text5.Length > 0)
				{
					text5 = EmbedLineBreak(ref text3, text5);
					text3 = ((!flag) ? (text3 + text5) : (text3 + "<Run>" + text5 + "</Run>"));
				}
				text3 += "</TextBlock.Inlines>";
			}
			text3 += "</TextBlock>";
			if (_vb != null || (_textbox != null && _new))
			{
				text3 += "</Viewbox>";
			}
			return text3;
		}

		private string ConvertParam(string text)
		{
