			_params = new List<string>();
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = text;
			while (true)
			{
				int num = text3.IndexOf('@');
				if (num < 0)
				{
					text2 += text3;
					break;
				}
				string text4 = text3.Substring(0, num);
				text2 += text4;
				text3 = text3.Substring(num + 1);
				num = text3.IndexOf(' ');
				string text5 = ((num >= 0) ? text3.Substring(0, num) : text3);
				_params.Add(text5);
				string attribValue = base.Control.GetAttribValue(text5, null);
				text2 += attribValue;
				if (num < 0)
				{
					text3 = "";
					break;
				}
				text3 = text3.Substring(num);
			}
			return text2;
		}

		public void UpdateParam(string param)
		{
			if (_params != null && _params.Contains(param) && _textbox.IsReadOnly)
			{
				_textbox.Text = ConvertParam(_text);
				GetDimension();
			}
		}

		private string EmbedLineBreak(ref string code, string str)
		{
			while (str.Contains("\r\n"))
			{
				int num = str.IndexOf("\r\n");
				string text = str.Substring(0, num);
				code += text;
				code += "<LineBreak />";
				str = str.Substring(num + 2);
			}
			return str;
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = _text;
			if (text2.Contains('&'))
			{
				text2 = text2.Replace("&", "&amp;");
			}
			if (text2.Contains('@'))
			{
				text += "<g>";
				text += "<metadata>";
				string text3 = text2;
				while (true)
				{
					int num = text3.IndexOf('@');
					if (num < 0)
					{
						break;
					}
					text3 = text3.Substring(num + 1);
					num = text3.IndexOf(' ');
					string text4 = text3.Substring(0, num);
					text = text + "<text context=\"" + text4 + "\"/>";
					text3 = text3.Substring(num);
				}
				text += "</metadata>";
			}
			text += "<text fill=\"black\" ";
			object obj = text;
			text = string.Concat(obj, "x=\"", _x, "\" ");
			object obj2 = text;
			text = string.Concat(obj2, "y=\"", _y, "\">");
			text += text2;
			text += "</text>";
			if (text2.Contains('@'))
			{
				text += "</g>";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override void GetField(ref double minx, ref double maxx, ref double miny, ref double maxy)
		{
			if (_x < minx)
			{
				minx = _x;
			}
			if (_y < miny)
			{
				miny = _y;
			}
			if (_x + _width > maxx)
			{
				maxx = _x + _width;
			}
			if (_y + _height > maxy)
			{
				maxy = _y + _height;
			}
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x += dx;
			_y += dy;
			if (_vb != null)
			{
				Canvas.SetLeft(_vb, _x);
				Canvas.SetTop(_vb, _y);
			}
			else if (_tform != null)
			{
				_tform.X = _x;
				_tform.Y = _y;
			}
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public override void ChangeProperty(string property, string value, XmlNode gradientsnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (property)
			{
			case "Stroke":
			case "TextColor":
			{
				_stroke = value;
				TextBox textBox2 = (TextBox)_element;
				if (value[0] == '@')
				{
					textBox2.Foreground = MappedBrush(value.Substring(1));
				}
				else if (value == "Default")
				{
					textBox2.Foreground = MappedBrush("Color");
				}
				else if (value != "None")
				{
					SolidColorBrush solidColorBrush = new SolidColorBrush();
					solidColorBrush.Color = ColorFromString(value);
					textBox2.Foreground = solidColorBrush;
				}
				break;
			}
			case "Alignment":
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)_element;
				switch (value)
				{
				case "Left":
					if (_alignment != 0)
					{
						base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.Align, this);
					}
					_alignment = 0;
					textBox.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Left;
					break;
				case "Center":
					if (_alignment != 1)
					{
						base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.Align, this);
					}
					_alignment = 1;
					textBox.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center;
					break;
				case "Right":
					if (_alignment != 2)
					{
						base.Control.AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.Align, this);
					}
					_alignment = 2;
					textBox.TextAlignment = TextAlignment.Right;
					break;
				}
				ResetPosition();
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		public void UpdateXml(XmlNode node)
		{
			_wx = GetDouble(node, "x");
			_wy = GetDouble(node, "y");
			_text = node.InnerText;
			_stroke = GetString(node, "Foreground");
			_size = GetDouble(node, "FontSize");
			_font = GetString(node, "FontFamily");
			_bold = GetString(node, "FontWeight") == "Bold";
			_italic = GetString(node, "FontStyle") == "Italic";
			_underline = GetString(node, "TextDecorations") == "underline";
		}

		public void Refresh()
		{
			_textbox.Foreground = new SolidColorBrush(ColorFromString(_stroke));
			_textbox.FontSize = _size;
			if (_font != null)
			{
				_textbox.FontFamily = new System.Windows.Media.FontFamily(_font);
			}
			_textbox.FontWeight = (_bold ? FontWeights.Bold : FontWeights.Normal);
			_textbox.FontStyle = (_italic ? FontStyles.Italic : FontStyles.Normal);
			_textbox.TextDecorations = (_underline ? TextDecorations.Underline : null);
		}

		public override void UpdateValues(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_positionnode != null)
			{
				_positionnode.Attributes["value1"].Value = (_x + dx).ToString();
				_positionnode.Attributes["value2"].Value = (_y + dy).ToString();
			}
		}

		public override string ChangeValue(string name, string value)
		{
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(value, out result);
			if (name == "Value1")
			{
				_x = result;
			}
			else if (name == "Value2")
			{
				_y = result;
			}
			GetDimension();
			RefreshHandles();
			return "";
		}

		public void SizeChange()
		{
			if (_rectangle != null)
			{
				_rectangle.SetDimension(_textbox.ActualWidth, _textbox.ActualHeight);
				_rectbox.Width = _textbox.ActualWidth;
				_rectbox.Height = _textbox.ActualHeight;
				_width = _rectbox.Width;
			}
		}

		public override void WcToDvc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.WcToDvc(_wx, _wy, out var xx, out var yy);
			bool flag = _textbox.Text != _text;
			_textbox.Text = _text;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (flag)
			{
				_textbox.UpdateLayout();
				_rectbox.Width = _textbox.ActualWidth;
				_rectbox.Height = _textbox.ActualHeight;
				_rectangle.SetDimension(_textbox.ActualWidth, _textbox.ActualHeight);
				num = _width - _rectbox.Width;
				_width = _rectbox.Width;
				if (_alignment == 1)
				{
					num *= 0.5;
				}
				num2 = _height - _rectbox.Height;
				if (_alignment == 1)
				{
					num2 *= 0.5;
				}
				_height = _rectbox.Height;
			}
			EndMove(xx - _x, yy - _y, num, num2);
		}

		public override void DvcToWc(ViewCoeff coeff)
		{
			coeff.DvcToWc(_x, _y, out _wx, out _wy);
		}
	}
	public class TextEntry : Element
	{
		private double _x;

		private double _y;

		private double _width;

		private double _height;

		private System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle _back;

		private TranslateTransform _tform;

		private string _textmap = "";

		private bool _readonly;

		public bool ReadOnly
		{
			set
			{
				_readonly = value;
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)_element;
				textBox.IsReadOnly = _readonly;
			}
		}

		public TextEntry(TextBox tb)
		{
			Binding binding = BindingOperations.GetBinding(tb, TextBox.TextProperty);
			if (binding != null)
			{
				_textmap = binding.XPath;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_textmap))
				{
					Binding binding2 = new Binding
					{
						XPath = "/Attributes/" + _textmap,
						Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay,
						UpdateSourceTrigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged
					};
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(tb, TextBox.TextProperty, binding2);
				}
			}
			_readonly = tb.IsReadOnly;
			_x = Canvas.GetLeft(tb);
			_y = Canvas.GetTop(tb);
			Canvas.SetLeft(tb, 0.0);
			Canvas.SetTop(tb, 0.0);
			_width = 20.0;
			_height = 20.0;
			InitializeAnchored(1);
			Setup();
			tb.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
			base.Children.Add(tb);
			_element = tb;
		}

		public TextEntry(double x, double y)
		{
			_x = x;
			_y = y;
			_width = 20.0;
			_height = 20.0;
			InitializeAnchored(1);
			Setup();
			TextBox element = new TextBox
			{
				Width = _width,
				Height = _height,
				Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray
			};
			base.Children.Add(element);
			_element = element;
		}

		private void Setup()
		{
			_tform = new TranslateTransform(_x, _y);
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
			transformGroup.Children.Add(_tform);
			_back = new System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle();
			Canvas.SetTop(_back, -5.0);
			Canvas.SetLeft(_back, -5.0);
			_back.Width = 30.0;
			_back.Height = 30.0;
			_back.Fill = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Transparent;
			base.Children.Add(_back);
		}

		private void SetTextMap()
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding();
			binding.XPath = "/Attributes/" + _textmap;
			binding.Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay;
			binding.UpdateSourceTrigger = UpdateSourceTrigger.PropertyChanged;
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(_element, TextBox.TextProperty, binding);
		}

		public override XmlNode GetXml(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			XmlNode xml = base.GetXml(doc);
			AddAttribute(xml, "type", "TextBox");
			AddAttribute(xml, "readonly", _readonly.ToString());
			int num = 0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_textmap))
			{
				num = MatchItem(doc, "Parameter", _textmap);
			}
			AddAttribute(xml, "parameterindex", num.ToString());
			return xml;
		}

		public override string GenXaml(bool bCurrent, bool ResolveAnchor, double offx = 0.0, double offy = 0.0, bool independent = false)
		{
			if (ResolveAnchor)
			{
				foreach (Handle handle in _handles)
				{
					if (handle.Anchor != null)
					{
						Anchor anchor = handle.Anchor;
						string iD = handle.ID;
						if (iD == "Base" && anchor.Direction == 1)
						{
							anchor.GetParam("yparam");
							anchor.GetParam("yconv").Replace(' ', '_');
						}
					}
				}
			}
			string text = "<TextBox";
			text = text + " Width=\"" + _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Height=\"" + _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Canvas.Top=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			text = text + " Canvas.Left=\"" + _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\"";
			if (_readonly)
			{
				text += " IsReadOnly=\"True\"";
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_textmap))
			{
				text = text + " Text=\"{Binding XPath=" + _textmap + "}\"";
			}
			text += ">";
			return text + "</TextBox>";
		}

		public override string GenSvg()
		{
			string text = "";
			text += "<rect ";
			text = text + "x=\"" + _x.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "y=\"" + _y.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "width=\"" + _width.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text = text + "height=\"" + _height.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "\" ";
			text += "stroke=\"black\" ";
			text += "fill=\"#CCCCCC\" ";
			return text + "/>";
		}

		public override void ChangeProperty(string property, string value, XmlNode gradientsnode, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (value != null && property == "TextMap")
			{
				if (value == "----" || value == "None")
				{
					_textmap = "";
					return;
				}
				_textmap = value;
				SetTextMap();
			}
		}

		public override void AddHandles()
		{
			AddHandle("Base", _x, _y, _anchored[0]);
		}

		public override void Move(double dx, double dy)
		{
			_x += dx;
			_y += dy;
			_tform.X = _x;
			_tform.Y = _y;
			base.Move(dx, dy);
		}

		public override void HandleMove(string handle, double x, double y)
		{
			if (handle == "Base")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				translateTransform.X = x - _x;
				translateTransform.Y = y - _y;
			}
		}

		public override void HandleMoveEnd(string handle)
		{
			if (handle == "Base")
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)base.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				Move(translateTransform.X, translateTransform.Y);
				translateTransform.X = 0.0;
				translateTransform.Y = 0.0;
			}
		}
	}
	public class EnumCalc : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = (string)parameter;
			if (text == (string)value)
			{
				return Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return Visibility.Collapsed;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class Calc : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = (string)parameter;
			double num;
			if (text.Contains("$"))
			{
				Calculator calculator = new Calculator();
				num = calculator.Calculate(null, (string)value, text);
			}
			else
			{
				RPNCalculator rPNCalculator = new RPNCalculator();
				num = rPNCalculator.Calculate(null, (string)value, (string)parameter);
			}
			return num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			RPNCalculator rPNCalculator = new RPNCalculator();
			double num = rPNCalculator.ReverseCalculate(null, (string)value, (string)parameter);
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class MCalc : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(values[0] is string))
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			string text = (string)parameter;
			if (text.Contains("$"))
			{
				Calculator calculator = new Calculator();
				return calculator.Calculate(values, "", text);
			}
			RPNCalculator rPNCalculator = new RPNCalculator();
			return rPNCalculator.Calculate(values, "", text);
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class RPNCalculator
	{
		private double[] _stack;

		private int _sp;

		public RPNCalculator()
		{
			_stack = new double[4];
			_sp = -1;
		}

		public double Calculate(object[] values, string value, string expr)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (values == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				_stack[++_sp] = double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				num = _stack[0];
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(expr))
			{
				return 0.0;
			}
			string[] array = expr.Split(' ');
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				switch (array[i])
				{
				case "*":
				{
					double num3 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] *= num3;
					continue;
				}
				case "+":
				{
					double num4 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] += num4;
					continue;
				}
				case "-":
				{
					double num2 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] -= num2;
					continue;
				}
				case "/":
				{
					double num5 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] /= num5;
					continue;
				}
				}
				if (array[i] == "@")
				{
					_stack[++_sp] = num;
				}
				else if (array[i].StartsWith("@"))
				{
					int num6 = int.Parse(array[i].Substring(1));
					if (num6 > 0 && num6 <= values.Length)
					{
						string text = (string)values[num6 - 1];
						double num7 = 0.0;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							num7 = double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
						}
						_stack[++_sp] = num7;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_stack[++_sp] = double.Parse(array[i], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
			}
			return _stack[_sp];
		}

		public double ReverseCalculate(object[] values, string value, string expr)
		{
			if (values == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				_stack[++_sp] = double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			string op = "";
			string[] array = expr.Split(' ');
			for (int num = array.Length - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				switch (array[num])
				{
				case "*":
				case "+":
				case "-":
				case "/":
					op = array[num];
					break;
				default:
				{
					double value2 = 0.0;
					if (array[num].StartsWith("@"))
					{
						int num2 = int.Parse(array[num].Substring(1));
						if (num2 > 0 && num2 <= values.Length)
						{
							string text = (string)values[num2 - 1];
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
							{
								value2 = double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						value2 = double.Parse(array[num], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					}
					ApplyReverseOp(value2, op);
					op = "";
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			return _stack[_sp];
		}

		private void ApplyReverseOp(double value, string op)
		{
			double num = _stack[_sp];
			switch (op)
			{
			case "*":
				_stack[_sp] = num / value;
				break;
			case "+":
				_stack[_sp] = num - value;
				break;
			case "-":
				_stack[_sp] = num + value;
				break;
			case "/":
				_stack[_sp] = num * value;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class Calculator
	{
		private double[] _stack;

		private string[] _opstack;

		private int _sp;

		private int _opsp;

		public Calculator()
		{
			_stack = new double[4];
			_opstack = new string[4];
		}

		public double Calculate(object[] values, string value, string expr)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			if (values == null)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					return 0.0;
				}
				num = double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
			_sp = -1;
			_opsp = -1;
			List<string> list = Split(expr);
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				switch (list[i])
				{
				case "+":
				case "-":
				case "*":
				case "/":
				case "(":
					_opstack[++_opsp] = list[i];
					continue;
				case ")":
					Process();
					continue;
				}
				if (list[i] == "$")
				{
					_stack[++_sp] = num;
				}
				else if (list[i].StartsWith("$"))
				{
					int num2 = int.Parse(list[i].Substring(1));
					if (num2 > 0 && num2 <= values.Length)
					{
						string text = (string)values[num2 - 1];
						double num3 = 0.0;
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
						{
							num3 = double.Parse(text, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
						}
						_stack[++_sp] = num3;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_stack[++_sp] = double.Parse(list[i], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
			}
			Process();
			return _stack[_sp];
		}

		private void Process()
		{
			while (_opsp >= 0)
			{
				switch (_opstack[_opsp--])
				{
				case "*":
				{
					double num4 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] *= num4;
					break;
				}
				case "+":
				{
					double num3 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] += num3;
					break;
				}
				case "-":
				{
					double num2 = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] -= num2;
					break;
				}
				case "/":
				{
					double num = _stack[_sp--];
					_stack[_sp] /= num;
					break;
				}
				case "(":
					return;
				}
			}
		}

		private List<string> Split(string expr)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < expr.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = expr[i];
				if (char.IsDigit(c) || c == '.' || (text == "" && c == '-'))
				{
					text += c;
					continue;
				}
				if (text != "")
				{
					list.Add(text);
					text = "";
				}
				switch (c)
				{
				case '$':
					text = "$";
					break;
				default:
					list.Add(c.ToString());
					break;
				case ' ':
				case '_':
					break;
				}
			}
			if (text != "")
			{
				list.Add(text);
			}
			return list;
		}
	}
	public class ViewCoeff
	{
		private double m_c1;

		private double m_c2;

		private double m_c3;

		private double m_c4;

		public void SetCoeff(double a1, double a2, double a3, double a4)
		{
			m_c1 = a1;
			m_c2 = a2;
			m_c3 = a3;
			m_c4 = a4;
		}

		public void WcToDvc(double x, double y, out double xx, out double yy)
		{
			xx = m_c1 * x - m_c2;
			yy = m_c3 * y - m_c4;
		}

		public void WcToDvcDelta(double dx, out double dxx)
		{
			double num = ToDvcX(0.0);
			double num2 = ToDvcX(dx);
			dxx = ((num2 > num) ? (num2 - num) : (num - num2));
		}

		public void DvcToWc(double xx, double yy, out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = ToWcX(xx);
			y = ToWcY(yy);
		}

		public void DvcToWcDelta(double idx, out double dx)
		{
			double num = ToWcX(0.0);
			double num2 = ToWcX(idx);
			dx = Math.Abs(num - num2);
		}

		private double ToDvcX(double x)
		{
			return m_c1 * x - m_c2;
		}

		private double ToDvcY(double y)
		{
			return m_c3 * y - m_c4;
		}

		private double ToWcX(double x)
		{
			return (x + m_c2) / m_c1;
		}

		private double ToWcY(double y)
		{
			return (y + m_c4) / m_c3;
		}
	}

		public event EventHandler LayerReset;

		public event IconEditorEventHandler IconEditorEvent;

		public IconCtrl()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			_layers = new List<Layer>();
			_curLayer = new Layer(IconPanel);
			_layers.Add(_curLayer);
			_iconrefs = new List<IconReference>();
			_ports = new List<Port>();
			_anchors = new List<Anchor>();
			_commands = new List<Command>();
			_animatedbrushes = new List<AnimatedBrush>();
			_grouping = new List<Element>();
			_animparams = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			base.Loaded += IconCtrl_Loaded;
			base.PreviewMouseWheel += IconCtrl_PreviewMouseWheel;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonDown;
			base.MouseLeftButtonUp += IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonUp;
			base.MouseRightButtonUp += IconCtrl_MouseRightButtonUp;
			base.MouseMove += IconCtrl_MouseMove;
			_current = null;
			_curport = null;
			_dragport = null;
			_hysysicon = false;
			SetupWholeList();
			m_history = new List<History>();
			m_redoList = new List<History>();
			m_historyGroup = null;
			m_historyGroupDepth = 0;
		}

		public IconCtrl(bool onPfs)
		{
			_onPfs = onPfs;
			InitializeComponent();
			_layers = new List<Layer>();
			_curLayer = new Layer(IconPanel);
			_layers.Add(_curLayer);
			_iconrefs = new List<IconReference>();
			_ports = new List<Port>();
			_anchors = new List<Anchor>();
			_commands = new List<Command>();
			_animatedbrushes = new List<AnimatedBrush>();
			_animparams = new Dictionary<string, bool>();
			base.Loaded += IconCtrl_Loaded;
			base.MouseLeftButtonDown += IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonDown2;
			base.MouseLeftButtonUp += IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonUp2;
			SetupWholeList();
			if (!onPfs)
			{
				m_history = new List<History>();
				m_redoList = new List<History>();
			}
		}

		public void SelectIcon(string iconname)
		{
			foreach (IconReference iconref in _iconrefs)
			{
				if (iconref.ID == iconname)
				{
					if (!iconref.IsCurrent)
					{
						UpdateIconReference(iconref.IconNode, _nsmgr);
						iconref.IsCurrent = true;
					}
				}
				else if (iconref.IsCurrent)
				{
					iconref.IsCurrent = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetPosition(double bx, double by, double scale)
		{
			if (_bLoaded)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = scale;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = scale;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
				translateTransform.X = bx;
				translateTransform.Y = by;
			}
			_bx = bx;
			_by = by;
			_scale = scale;
		}

		public void GetPosition(out double x, out double y, out double scale)
		{
			x = _bx;
			y = _by;
			scale = _scale;
		}

		public void GetPortPosition(Port port, out double x, out double y, out System.Drawing.Point direction)
		{
			x = (y = 0.0);
			port.GetPosition(out var x2, out var y2);
			x = _bx + _scale * x2;
			y = _by + _scale * y2;
			direction = port.GetDirection();
		}

		public void SetSize(double minx, double miny, double maxx, double maxy)
		{
			_minx = minx;
			_miny = miny;
			_maxx = maxx;
			_maxy = maxy;
		}

		public void GetSize(out double minx, out double maxx, out double miny, out double maxy)
		{
			if (SmartIcon && SmartIconSizeChanged)
			{
				_smartIconSizeChanged = false;
				Rect rect = GenPathGeometry();
				_minx = rect.Left;
				_maxx = rect.Right;
				_miny = rect.Top;
				_maxy = rect.Bottom;
			}
			minx = _minx;
			maxx = _maxx;
			miny = _miny;
			maxy = _maxy;
		}

		private Rect GenPathGeometry()
		{
			GeometryGroup geometryGroup = new GeometryGroup();
			geometryGroup.Transform = base.RenderTransform;
			GenCanvasGeometry(geometryGroup);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = new System.Windows.Shapes.Path();
			path.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			path.StrokeThickness = 0.1;
			path.Data = geometryGroup;
			PathGeometry outlinedPathGeometry = path.Data.GetOutlinedPathGeometry();
			return outlinedPathGeometry.Bounds;
		}

		private void GenCanvasGeometry(GeometryGroup gg)
		{
			foreach (UIElement child in _curLayer.Children)
			{
				if (!(child is Port) && child is Element)
				{
					Element element = (Element)child;
					element.GenGeometry(gg);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Highlight(string color)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attrib.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode("CColor");
			xmlNode.InnerText = color;
		}

		public void RefreshAnchors()
		{
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				anchor.Refresh();
			}
		}

		internal void FireEvent(string EventName, string Value)
		{
			switch (EventName)
			{
			case "History":
			{
				IconEditorEventArgs e2 = new IconEditorEventArgs(EventName, Value);
				if (this.IconEditorEvent != null)
				{
					this.IconEditorEvent(this, e2);
				}
				if (e2.Value == "UndoClear" && m_undolist != null)
				{
					m_undolist.Items.Clear();
				}
				else if (e2.Value == "RedoClear" && m_redolist != null)
				{
					m_redolist.Items.Clear();
				}
				break;
			}
			case "AddHistory":
				AddToUndoList(Value);
				break;
			case "Undo":
				m_undolist.Items.Remove(Value);
				if (m_undolist.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					FireEvent("History", "UndoClear");
				}
				if (m_redolist.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					FireEvent("History", "RedoSet");
				}
				m_redolist.Items.Insert(0, Value);
				break;
			case "Redo":
				m_redolist.Items.Remove(Value);
				if (m_redolist.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					FireEvent("History", "RedoClear");
				}
				if (m_undolist.Items.Count == 0)
				{
					FireEvent("History", "UndoSet");
				}
				AddToUndoList(Value);
				break;
			default:
			{
				IconEditorEventArgs e = new IconEditorEventArgs(EventName, Value);
				if (this.IconEditorEvent != null)
				{
					this.IconEditorEvent(this, e);
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}

		public void KeyEvent(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Key == Key.Escape)
			{
				if (_inInsert != null && _dragElement == null)
				{
					CancelInsert();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (e.Key != Key.Delete)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (_current != null)
				{
					BaseObj current = _current;
					DeleteObject(_current);
					if (current == _current)
					{
						_current = null;
					}
				}
				else if (_curport != null)
				{
					DeletePort(_curport.DataNode, _curport);
					_curport = null;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool IsChanged()
		{
			if (_iconnode != null && m_history.Count > 0)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void AddToUndoList(string value)
		{
			m_undolist.Items.Insert(0, value);
		}

		public AnimatedBrush AnimatedBrush(string name)
		{
			foreach (AnimatedBrush animatedbrush in _animatedbrushes)
			{
				if (animatedbrush.Name == name)
				{
					return animatedbrush;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private XmlNode AnimatedParameter(string param)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attrib.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode(param);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				string value = xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value;
				if (value.EndsWith("Animation"))
				{
					return xmlNode;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void _anchorlistview_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void _portlistview_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_curport != null)
			{
				_curport.Highlight(bOn: false, bLarge: false);
				_curport = null;
			}
			int selectedIndex = _portlistview.SelectedIndex;
			if (selectedIndex >= 0)
			{
				Port port = _ports[selectedIndex];
				port.Highlight(bOn: true, bLarge: false);
				_curport = port;
			}
		}

		private void _elementtreeview_SelectedItemChanged(object sender, RoutedPropertyChangedEventArgs<object> e)
		{
			TreeView treeView = (TreeView)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)treeView.SelectedItem;
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Element element = FindElement(xmlNode);
			if (element == _current || element == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (e.OldValue != null)
			{
				XmlNode node = (XmlNode)e.OldValue;
				if (FindElement(node) == null)
				{
					TreeViewItem treeViewItem = treeView.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(xmlNode) as TreeViewItem;
					treeViewItem.IsSelected = false;
					return;
				}
			}
			element.ShowHandles(bShow: true);
			SetCurrent(element);
		}

		public string GetAttribValue(string param, string conversion)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attrib.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode(param, _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				return "";
			}
			string text = xmlNode.InnerText;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(conversion))
			{
				Calc calc = new Calc();
				text = (string)calc.Convert(text, null, conversion, null);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void DragMove(double dx, double dy)
		{
			if (_onPfs)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2];
				translateTransform.X = dx;
				translateTransform.Y = dy;
			}
		}

		public void EndDrag()
		{
			if (_onPfs)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
				TranslateTransform translateTransform2 = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[2];
				translateTransform.X += translateTransform2.X;
				translateTransform.Y += translateTransform2.Y;
				double x = (translateTransform2.Y = 0.0);
				translateTransform2.X = x;
				_bx = translateTransform.X;
				_by = translateTransform.Y;
			}
		}

		public string PortType(string name)
		{
			if (_porttypes.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				return _porttypes[name];
			}
			return "";
		}

		public void ModifyPort(int modifycode)
		{
			if (_curport != null)
			{
				_curport.ChangeAttrib(modifycode);
			}
		}

		public Port FindPort(string name)
		{
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				if (port.Name == name)
				{
					return port;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private Port FindPort(XmlNode node)
		{
			XmlNode parentNode = node.ParentNode;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in parentNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (!childNode.Equals(node))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return _ports[num];
		}

		private Anchor FindAnchor(XmlNode node)
		{
			XmlNode parentNode = node.ParentNode;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in parentNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (!childNode.Equals(node))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return _anchors[num];
		}

		public Element FindElement(string ids, int elementoffset)
		{
			string[] array = ids.Split('.');
			int index = int.Parse(array[0]) + elementoffset;
			if (array.Length == 1)
			{
				return _elements[index];
			}
			Element element = _elements[index];
			if (element is Group)
			{
				string ids2 = ids.Substring(array[0].Length + 1);
				return ((Group)element).FindMember(ids2);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public Element FindElement(string name)
		{
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (element.Name == name)
				{
					return element;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public string GetElementIndex(Element elem)
		{
			string result = "";
			int num = 0;
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (element == elem)
				{
					result = num.ToString();
					break;
				}
				if (element is Group)
				{
					string memberIndex = ((Group)element).GetMemberIndex(elem);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(memberIndex))
					{
						result = num + "." + memberIndex;
						break;
					}
				}
				num++;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public XmlNode GetElementNode(int index)
		{
			Element element = _elements[index];
			return element.DataNode;
		}

		public XmlNode GetElementNode(string id)
		{
			int num = id.IndexOf('.');
			string s = ((num >= 0) ? id.Substring(0, num) : id);
			int index = int.Parse(s);
			Element element = _elements[index];
			if (num < 0)
			{
				return element.DataNode;
			}
			if (!(element is Group))
			{
				return null;
			}
			id = id.Substring(num + 1);
			return ((Group)element).GetMemberNode(id);
		}

		public XmlNode GetPortNode(int index)
		{
			Port port = _ports[index];
			return port.DataNode;
		}

		public Command FindCommand(XmlNode node)
		{
			foreach (Command command in _commands)
			{
				if (command.DataNode == node)
				{
					return command;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public Element FindElement(XmlNode node)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = node;
			bool flag = false;
			while (xmlNode.ParentNode != null && xmlNode.ParentNode.Name == "Group")
			{
				flag = true;
				xmlNode = xmlNode.ParentNode;
			}
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (element.DataNode != null && element.DataNode.Equals(xmlNode))
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						return ((Group)element).FindElement(node);
					}
					return element;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private int FindElementIndex(XmlNode node)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (!element.DataNode.Equals(node))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void ClearGrouping()
		{
			foreach (Element item in _grouping)
			{
				item.DataNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "false";
			}
			_grouping.Clear();
		}

		public void AddGrouping(Element elem)
		{
			elem.DataNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "true";
			_grouping.Add(elem);
		}

		public void ElementListViewButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is TextBox)
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)sender;
				XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)textBox.DataContext;
				if (xmlNode == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (textBox.Name == "TextContent")
				{
					Text text = (Text)FindElement(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.TextContents, text);
					text.TextContent(textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "TextSize")
				{
					Text text2 = (Text)FindElement(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.TextSize, text2);
					text2.TextSize(textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "AngleConverter")
				{
					Group obj = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode);
					obj.SetAngleConverter(textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "StrokeDash")
				{
					Element element = FindElement(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.Dash, element);
					element.ChangeProperty("StrokeDash", textBox.Text, GradientsNode, _nsmgr);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "PortName")
				{
					Port port = FindPort(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Rename, port);
					port.ChangeName(textBox.Text, standalone: false);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "xparam" || textBox.Name == "xconv" || textBox.Name == "yparam" || textBox.Name == "yconv")
				{
					Anchor anchor = FindAnchor(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Param, anchor, textBox.Name);
					anchor.SetParam(textBox.Name, textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name.StartsWith("Value"))
				{
					Element element2 = FindElement(xmlNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, element2);
					string text3 = element2.ChangeValue(textBox.Name, textBox.Text);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						MessageBox.Show(text3);
					}
				}
				else if (textBox.Name.StartsWith("Point"))
				{
					Element element3 = FindElement(xmlNode.ParentNode);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, element3);
					string value = xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value;
					element3.ChangeValue(textBox.Name + "." + value.Substring(1), textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "StartAngle" || textBox.Name == "EndAngle")
				{
					Element element4 = FindElement(xmlNode);
					if (element4 is Path)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, element4, textBox.Name);
						((Path)element4).ChangeValue(textBox.Name, textBox.Text);
					}
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "ElemName")
				{
					Element element5 = FindElement(xmlNode);
					string.IsNullOrEmpty(element5.Name);
					element5.ChangeName(textBox.Text);
					XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
					xmlElement.InnerText = textBox.Text;
					_namelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "Command" || textBox.Name == "Header")
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode2 = xmlNode;
					while (xmlNode2.Name == "MenuItem")
					{
						xmlNode2 = xmlNode2.ParentNode;
					}
					FindCommand(xmlNode2)?.Update(xmlNode, textBox.Name, textBox.Text);
				}
				else if (textBox.Name == "XYParam" || textBox.Name == "XConverter" || textBox.Name == "YConverter")
				{
					Element element6 = FindElement(xmlNode);
					if (element6 is Polyline)
					{
						Polyline polyline = (Polyline)element6;
						polyline.SetPlot(textBox.Name, textBox.Text);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (sender is CheckBox)
			{
				CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)sender;
				XmlNode xmlNode3 = (XmlNode)checkBox.DataContext;
				if (xmlNode3 == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (checkBox.Name == "Grouping")
				{
					Element element7 = FindElement(xmlNode3);
					if (checkBox.IsChecked.Value)
					{
						int count = _grouping.Count;
						for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
						{
							Element element8 = _grouping[num];
							if (element8.Parent != element7.Parent)
							{
								element8.DataNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "false";
								_grouping.Remove(element8);
							}
						}
						_grouping.Add(element7);
					}
					else
					{
						_grouping.Remove(element7);
					}
				}
				else if (checkBox.Name == "BoldText" || checkBox.Name == "ItalicText" || checkBox.Name == "UnderText")
				{
					Text text4 = (Text)FindElement(xmlNode3);
					if (text4 != null)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.TextType, text4);
						text4.TextStyle(checkBox.Name, checkBox.IsChecked.Value);
					}
				}
				else if (checkBox.Name == "ArrowAtStart" || checkBox.Name == "ArrowAtEnd")
				{
					Element element9 = FindElement(xmlNode3);
					if (checkBox.IsChecked == true)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.ArrowAdd, element9, checkBox.Name);
					}
					else
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, HistorySubcode.ArrowRemove, element9, checkBox.Name);
					}
					element9.AddArrow(checkBox.Name, checkBox.IsChecked.Value);
				}
				else if (checkBox.Name == "AnchorHandle")
				{
					Anchor anchor2 = FindAnchor(xmlNode3);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Handle, anchor2);
					anchor2.AsHandle = checkBox.IsChecked.Value;
				}
				else
				{
					if (!(checkBox.Name == "CheckBox1") && !(checkBox.Name == "CheckBox2"))
					{
						return;
					}
					Element element10 = FindElement(xmlNode3);
					if (element10 is Path)
					{
						Path path = (Path)element10;
						if (checkBox.Name == "CheckBox1")
						{
							path.Closed = checkBox.IsChecked.Value;
						}
						else
						{
							path.Circle = checkBox.IsChecked.Value;
						}
					}
					else if (element10 is Ellipse)
					{
						Ellipse ellipse = (Ellipse)element10;
						ellipse.Circle = checkBox.IsChecked.Value;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (sender is ComboBox)
			{
				ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
				XmlNode xmlNode4 = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
				if (comboBox.Name == "PortName")
				{
					Port port2 = FindPort(xmlNode4);
					if (port2.Name != comboBox.Text)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Rename, port2);
						port2.ChangeName(comboBox.Text, standalone: false);
						if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(comboBox.Text) && xmlNode4.Attributes["porttype"] != null)
						{
							xmlNode4.Attributes["porttype"].Value = "";
						}
					}
				}
				else if (comboBox.Name == "AngleMap")
				{
					Group obj2 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode4);
					if (double.TryParse(comboBox.Text, NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result))
					{
						obj2.SetAngle(result);
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			ContentControl contentControl = (ContentControl)sender;
			if (contentControl.Name == "NewColor")
			{
				TextBox textBox2 = (TextBox)_elementtreeview.FindName("NewColorName");
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(textBox2.Text))
				{
					TextBlock textBlock = (TextBlock)_elementtreeview.FindName("MessageOut");
					if (ValidColor(textBox2.Text))
					{
						textBlock.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
						AddLocalColor(textBox2.Text);
					}
					else
					{
						textBlock.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "AddLayer")
			{
				Layer item = new Layer(IconPanel);
				_layers.Add(item);
				return;
			}
			Canvas curLayer = _curLayer;
			if (contentControl.Name == "Group" || contentControl.Name == "GroupElements")
			{
				if (_grouping.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				Group obj3 = new Group(++_groupcount);
				obj3.Control = this;
				Group obj4 = null;
				foreach (Element item2 in _grouping)
				{
					item2.DataNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "False";
					if (item2.Parent is Group)
					{
						obj4 = (Group)item2.Parent;
						obj4.Remove(item2);
					}
					else
					{
						curLayer.Children.Remove(item2);
						_elements.Remove(item2);
						_elementlist.RemoveChild(item2.DataNode);
					}
					if (item2 is Group)
					{
						((Group)item2).Parent = obj3;
					}
					obj3.Insert(0, item2);
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
				if (obj4 != null)
				{
					obj3.Parent = obj4;
				}
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Grouping, HistorySubcode.Group, obj3);
				XmlNode xml = obj3.GetXml(_wholelist);
				if (obj4 != null)
				{
					obj4.Insert(0, obj3);
					obj4.DataNode.InsertBefore(xml, obj4.DataNode.FirstChild);
				}
				else
				{
					curLayer.Children.Add(obj3);
					_elements.Insert(0, obj3);
					_elementlist.InsertBefore(xml, _elementlist.FirstChild);
				}
				obj3.CalcSize(newgroup: true);
				RefreshElements();
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode5 = (XmlNode)contentControl.DataContext;
			if (contentControl.Name == "Ungroup")
			{
				Group obj5 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode5);
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Grouping, HistorySubcode.Ungroup, obj5);
				XmlNode parentNode = xmlNode5.ParentNode;
				if (parentNode.Name == "Group")
				{
					Group obj6 = (Group)FindElement(parentNode);
					obj5.Ungroup(obj6, obj6.Members, xmlNode5);
				}
				else
				{
					obj5.Ungroup(curLayer, _elements, xmlNode5);
				}
				RefreshElements();
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "ApplyRepeat")
			{
				Group obj7 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode5);
				StackPanel stackPanel = (StackPanel)contentControl.Parent;
				TextBox textBox3 = (TextBox)stackPanel.FindName("RepeatCount");
				string text5 = textBox3.Text;
				TextBox textBox4 = (TextBox)stackPanel.FindName("XOffset");
				string text6 = textBox4.Text;
				TextBox textBox5 = (TextBox)stackPanel.FindName("YOffset");
				string text7 = textBox5.Text;
				TextBox textBox6 = (TextBox)stackPanel.FindName("DX");
				string text8 = textBox6.Text;
				TextBox textBox7 = (TextBox)stackPanel.FindName("DY");
				string text9 = textBox7.Text;
				obj7.ApplyRepeat(text5, text6, text7, text8, text9);
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "RotateLeft" || contentControl.Name == "RotateRight")
			{
				Group obj8 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode5);
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, (contentControl.Name == "RotateLeft") ? HistorySubcode.RotateLeft : HistorySubcode.RotateRight, obj8);
				obj8.Rotate(contentControl.Name == "RotateLeft");
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "FlipX" || contentControl.Name == "FlipY")
			{
				Group obj9 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode5);
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, (contentControl.Name == "FlipX") ? HistorySubcode.FlipX : HistorySubcode.FlipY, obj9);
				obj9.Flip(contentControl.Name == "FlipY");
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "ClipButton")
			{
				Element element11 = FindElement(xmlNode5);
				element11.ShowClipElements();
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "UpdateButton")
			{
				Element element12 = FindElement(xmlNode5);
				element12.ClearClipElements();
				foreach (Element item3 in _grouping)
				{
					element12.AddClipElement(item3);
				}
				element12.UpdateClip();
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "TextReadOnly")
			{
				CheckBox checkBox2 = (CheckBox)contentControl;
				TextEntry textEntry = (TextEntry)FindElement(xmlNode5);
				textEntry.ReadOnly = checkBox2.IsChecked.Value;
				return;
			}
			if (xmlNode5 != null && xmlNode5.ParentNode.Name == "Group")
			{
				Group obj10 = (Group)FindElement(xmlNode5.ParentNode);
				obj10.ButtonCommand(contentControl.Name, xmlNode5);
				return;
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "DeletePoint")
			{
				int index = FindElementIndex(xmlNode5.ParentNode);
				Element element13 = _elements[index];
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, element13);
				element13.DeletePoint(xmlNode5);
				return;
			}
			Element element14 = null;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (xmlNode5 != null)
			{
				num2 = FindElementIndex(xmlNode5);
				element14 = _elements[num2];
			}
			if (contentControl.Name == "Delete")
			{
				if (element14 != null)
				{
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Delete, HistorySubcode.Delete, element14);
					element14.Delete();
					element14.ShowHandles(bShow: false);
					_elements.RemoveAt(num2);
					curLayer.Children.Remove(element14);
					_elementlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode5);
				}
				foreach (Element item4 in _grouping)
				{
					item4.Delete();
					_elements.Remove(item4);
					curLayer.Children.Remove(item4);
					_elementlist.RemoveChild(item4.DataNode);
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
			}
			else if (contentControl.Name == "Up")
			{
				if (xmlNode5 != null && num2 > 0)
				{
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Raise, element14);
					_elements.RemoveAt(num2);
					_elements.Insert(num2 - 1, element14);
					XmlNode refChild = PreviousElementNode(xmlNode5);
					_elementlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode5);
					xmlNode5 = element14.GetXml(_wholelist);
					_elementlist.InsertBefore(xmlNode5, refChild);
					RefreshElements();
				}
			}
			else if (contentControl.Name == "Down" && xmlNode5 != null && num2 < _elements.Count - 1)
			{
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Lower, element14);
				_elements.RemoveAt(num2);
				_elements.Insert(num2 + 1, element14);
				XmlNode refChild2 = NextElementNode(xmlNode5);
				_elementlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode5);
				xmlNode5 = element14.GetXml(_wholelist);
				_elementlist.InsertAfter(xmlNode5, refChild2);
				RefreshElements();
			}
		}

		public void ElementListViewButtonClick2(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e, TreeView tree)
		{
			ButtonBase buttonBase = (ButtonBase)sender;
			if (buttonBase.Name == "GroupElements")
			{
				if (_grouping.Count == 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				Group obj = new Group(++_groupcount);
				obj.Control = this;
				Group obj2 = null;
				foreach (Element item in _grouping)
				{
					item.DataNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "False";
					if (item.Parent is Group)
					{
						obj2 = (Group)item.Parent;
						obj2.Remove(item);
					}
					else
					{
						_curLayer.Children.Remove(item);
						_elements.Remove(item);
						_elementlist.RemoveChild(item.DataNode);
					}
					if (item is Group)
					{
						((Group)item).Parent = obj;
					}
					obj.Add(item);
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					obj.Parent = obj2;
				}
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Grouping, HistorySubcode.Group, obj);
				XmlNode xml = obj.GetXml(_wholelist);
				if (obj2 != null)
				{
					obj2.Insert(0, obj);
					obj2.DataNode.InsertBefore(xml, obj2.DataNode.FirstChild);
				}
				else
				{
					_curLayer.Children.Add(obj);
					_elements.Insert(0, obj);
					_elementlist.InsertBefore(xml, _elementlist.FirstChild);
				}
				obj.CalcSize(newgroup: true);
				RefreshElements();
			}
			else if (buttonBase.Name == "Up")
			{
				foreach (Element item2 in _grouping)
				{
					XmlNode dataNode = item2.DataNode;
					if (item2.Parent is Group)
					{
						Group obj3 = (Group)item2.Parent;
						obj3.ButtonCommand("Up", dataNode);
						continue;
					}
					int num = FindElementIndex(dataNode);
					if (num > 0)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Raise, item2);
						_elements.RemoveAt(num);
						_elements.Insert(num - 1, item2);
						XmlNode refChild = PreviousElementNode(dataNode);
						_elementlist.RemoveChild(dataNode);
						dataNode = item2.GetXml(_wholelist);
						_elementlist.InsertBefore(dataNode, refChild);
					}
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
				RefreshElements();
			}
			else if (buttonBase.Name == "Down")
			{
				foreach (Element item3 in _grouping)
				{
					XmlNode dataNode2 = item3.DataNode;
					if (item3.Parent is Group)
					{
						Group obj4 = (Group)item3.Parent;
						obj4.ButtonCommand("Down", dataNode2);
						continue;
					}
					int num2 = FindElementIndex(dataNode2);
					if (num2 < _elements.Count - 1)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.ZIndex, HistorySubcode.Lower, item3);
						_elements.RemoveAt(num2);
						_elements.Insert(num2 + 1, item3);
						XmlNode refChild2 = NextElementNode(dataNode2);
						_elementlist.RemoveChild(dataNode2);
						dataNode2 = item3.GetXml(_wholelist);
						_elementlist.InsertAfter(dataNode2, refChild2);
					}
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
				RefreshElements();
			}
			else if (buttonBase.Name == "Delete")
			{
				foreach (Element item4 in _grouping)
				{
					XmlNode dataNode3 = item4.DataNode;
					dataNode3.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value = "False";
					if (item4.Parent is Group)
					{
						Group obj5 = (Group)item4.Parent;
						obj5.ButtonCommand("Delete", dataNode3);
						continue;
					}
					int index = FindElementIndex(dataNode3);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Delete, HistorySubcode.Delete, item4);
					item4.Delete();
					item4.ShowHandles(bShow: false);
					_elements.RemoveAt(index);
					_curLayer.Children.Remove(item4);
					_elementlist.RemoveChild(dataNode3);
				}
				_grouping.Clear();
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(buttonBase.Name == "RotLeft") && !(buttonBase.Name == "RotRight") && !(buttonBase.Name == "FlipX") && !(buttonBase.Name == "FlipY"))
				{
					return;
				}
				double num3 = 0.0;
				double num4 = 0.0;
				int num5 = 0;
				foreach (Element item5 in _grouping)
				{
					if (item5.GetCenterPoint(out var x, out var y))
					{
						num3 += x;
						num4 += y;
						num5++;
					}
				}
				num3 /= (double)num5;
				num4 /= (double)num5;
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Group, HistorySubcode.GroupBegin, null, (string)buttonBase.ToolTip);
				foreach (Element item6 in _grouping)
				{
					XmlNode dataNode4 = item6.DataNode;
					FindElementIndex(dataNode4);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Move, item6);
					item6.Modify(buttonBase.Name, num3, num4);
				}
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Group, HistorySubcode.GroupEnd, null);
			}
		}

		private void DeleteObject(BaseObj obj)
		{
			if (obj is Element)
			{
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Delete, HistorySubcode.Delete, obj);
				Element element = (Element)obj;
				element.Delete();
				element.ShowHandles(bShow: false);
				_elements.Remove(element);
				_curLayer.Children.Remove(element);
				_elementlist.RemoveChild(element.DataNode);
			}
		}

		private bool ValidColor(string colorname)
		{
			try
			{
				_ = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(colorname);
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal XmlNode NextElementNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			while (node != null)
			{
				XmlNode nextSibling = node.NextSibling;
				if (nextSibling.Name == "Element" || nextSibling.Name == "Group")
				{
					return nextSibling;
				}
				node = node.NextSibling;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal XmlNode PreviousElementNode(XmlNode node)
		{
			while (node != null)
			{
				XmlNode previousSibling = node.PreviousSibling;
				if (previousSibling.Name == "Element" || previousSibling.Name == "Group")
				{
					return previousSibling;
				}
				node = node.PreviousSibling;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void PortListViewButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ButtonBase buttonBase = (ButtonBase)sender;
			Port port = null;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)buttonBase.DataContext;
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				port = FindPort(xmlNode);
			}
			if (buttonBase is RadioButton && port != null)
			{
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.IOtype, port);
				RadioButton radioButton = (RadioButton)buttonBase;
				string text = (string)radioButton.Content;
				text = ((text == "In") ? "0" : ((!(text == "Both")) ? "1" : "-1"));
				port.ChangeIOType(text);
				XmlNode nextSibling = xmlNode.NextSibling;
				_portlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				if (nextSibling != null)
				{
					_portlist.InsertBefore(xmlNode, nextSibling);
				}
				else
				{
					_portlist.AppendChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
			else if (buttonBase.Name == "DeletePort")
			{
				DeletePort(xmlNode, port);
			}
		}

		public void PortListViewButtonClick2(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e, ListView list)
		{
			ButtonBase buttonBase = (ButtonBase)sender;
			foreach (XmlNode item in (IEnumerable)list.Items)
			{
				if (!(item.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value == "True"))
				{
					continue;
				}
				Port port = FindPort(item);
				if (buttonBase.Name == "DeletePort")
				{
					DeletePort(item, port);
					break;
				}
				if (buttonBase.Name == "RotateLeft" || buttonBase.Name == "RotateRight")
				{
					int modifycode = 0;
					if (buttonBase.Name == "RotateLeft")
					{
						modifycode = 1;
					}
					else if (buttonBase.Name == "RotateRight")
					{
						modifycode = 0;
					}
					ModifyPort(modifycode);
				}
			}
		}

		private void DeletePort(XmlNode node, Port port)
		{
			if (port != null)
			{
				if (port == _curport)
				{
					SetCurrent(null);
				}
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Delete, port);
				_ports.Remove(port);
				IconPanel.Children.Remove(port);
				_portlist.RemoveChild(node);
			}
			int count = _portlist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _portlist.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["IsChecked"].Value == "True")
				{
					Port port2 = FindPort(xmlNode);
					if (port2 != null)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Delete, port2);
						_ports.Remove(port2);
						IconPanel.Children.Remove(port2);
						_portlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void AnchorListViewButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			ButtonBase buttonBase = (ButtonBase)sender;
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)buttonBase.DataContext;
			XmlNode parentNode = xmlNode.ParentNode;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in parentNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (!childNode.Equals(xmlNode))
				{
					num++;
					continue;
				}
				break;
			}
			Anchor anchor = _anchors[num];
			if (buttonBase.Name == "Delete")
			{
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Delete, anchor);
				anchor.Delete();
				_anchors.RemoveAt(num);
				IconPanel.Children.Remove(anchor);
				_anchorlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
			}
			else if (buttonBase.Name == "Apply")
			{
				anchor.ResetAnimation();
				string value = xmlNode.Attributes["xparam"].Value;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
				{
					string value2 = xmlNode.Attributes["xconv"].Value;
					anchor.SetValue("xparam", value, value2);
				}
				string value3 = xmlNode.Attributes["yparam"].Value;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value3))
				{
					string value4 = xmlNode.Attributes["yconv"].Value;
					anchor.SetValue("yparam", value3, value4);
				}
			}
		}

		public void ElementListSelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_colorselector != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			if (comboBox.Name == "LayerList")
			{
				SwitchLayer(comboBox.SelectedIndex);
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "LayerType")
			{
				if (_curLayer != null)
				{
					_curLayer.Type = comboBox.SelectedIndex;
				}
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "LayerVisibility")
			{
				if (_curLayer != null && comboBox.SelectedIndex >= 0)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.Items[comboBox.SelectedIndex];
					_curLayer.visibility = xmlNode.InnerText;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(comboBox.DataContext is XmlNode))
				{
					return;
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
				if (xmlNode2.ParentNode == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				Element element = FindElement(xmlNode2);
				if (comboBox.Name != "Alignment" && !comboBox.Name.EndsWith("ArrowSize"))
				{
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Change, GetChangeCode(comboBox.Name), element);
				}
				if (comboBox.Name == "Visibility")
				{
					element.ChangeVisibility((string)comboBox.SelectedValue);
					return;
				}
				if (comboBox.Name == "StrokeThickness")
				{
					element.ChangeProperty(comboBox.Name, (string)comboBox.SelectedValue, GradientsNode, _nsmgr);
					return;
				}
				if (comboBox.Name == "Alignment")
				{
					ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem = (ComboBoxItem)comboBox.SelectedItem;
					if (comboBoxItem != null)
					{
						element.ChangeProperty(comboBox.Name, (string)comboBoxItem.Content, GradientsNode, _nsmgr);
					}
					return;
				}
				if (comboBox.Name == "StartArrowSize" || comboBox.Name == "EndArrowSize")
				{
					element.ChangeArrowSize(comboBox.Name == "StartArrowSize", (comboBox.SelectedIndex + 1).ToString(), bNoHistory: false);
					return;
				}
				if (comboBox.Name == "DashMotion")
				{
					element.ChangeDashMotion((string)comboBox.SelectedValue);
					return;
				}
				string text = (string)comboBox.SelectedValue;
				if (text == "Other...")
				{
					string color = "";
					if (comboBox.Name == "Stroke" || comboBox.Name == "TextColor")
					{
						color = element.Stroke;
					}
					else if (comboBox.Name == "Fill")
					{
						color = element.Fill;
					}
					text = OpenColorSelector(color, comboBox.Name == "Fill");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						AddWholeList(text);
						comboBox.SelectedValue = text;
					}
					_colorselector = null;
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				element.ChangeProperty(comboBox.Name, text, GradientsNode, _nsmgr);
			}
		}

		private string OpenColorSelector(string color, bool fill)
		{
			_colorselector = new ColorSelector();
			_colorselector.Owner = OwnerWindow;
			_colorselector.ShowDialog();
			return _colorselector.Color;
		}

		private HistorySubcode GetChangeCode(string property)
		{
			HistorySubcode result = HistorySubcode.Stroke;
			switch (property)
			{
			case "StrokeThickness":
				result = HistorySubcode.Thickness;
				break;
			case "StrokeDash":
				result = HistorySubcode.Dash;
				break;
			case "Fill":
				result = HistorySubcode.Fill;
				break;
			case "StartArrowSize":
			case "EndArrowSize":
				result = HistorySubcode.ArrowSize;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void SwitchLayer(int layer)
		{
			if (layer < 0 || layer >= _layers.Count)
			{
				return;
			}
			SetCurrent(null);
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				element.Enable(enable: false);
			}
			_curLayer = _layers[layer];
			CleanElementList();
			foreach (Element element2 in _elements)
			{
				element2.Enable(enable: true);
				XmlNode xml = element2.GetXml(_wholelist);
				_elementlist.AppendChild(xml);
			}
		}

		public void PortListSelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			if (!(comboBox.DataContext is XmlNode) || _ports.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
			Port port = FindPort(xmlNode);
			if (comboBox.Name == "AutoStreamDir")
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["autostreaminit"].Value == "false")
				{
					xmlNode.Attributes["autostreaminit"].Value = "true";
				}
				else if (port.StreamDirection != comboBox.SelectedIndex)
				{
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.AutoStream, port);
					port.StreamDirection = comboBox.SelectedIndex;
				}
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "PortVisibility")
			{
				port.ChangeVisibility((string)comboBox.SelectedValue);
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "PortTypeName")
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["update"].Value == "true")
				{
					port.ChangePortType((string)comboBox.SelectedValue, standalone: false);
				}
				else
				{
					port.ChangePortType((string)comboBox.SelectedValue, standalone: true);
				}
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "PortName" && comboBox.SelectedValue != null)
			{
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["update"].Value == "true")
				{
					port.ChangeName((string)comboBox.SelectedValue, standalone: false);
				}
				else
				{
					port.ChangeName((string)comboBox.SelectedValue, standalone: true);
				}
			}
		}

		public void AnchorListSelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			if (!(comboBox.DataContext is XmlNode))
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
			string value = xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value;
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				if (anchor.Name == value)
				{
					if (xmlNode.Attributes["directinit"].Value == "false")
					{
						xmlNode.Attributes["directinit"].Value = "true";
						break;
					}
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Motion, anchor);
					anchor.Direction = int.Parse(xmlNode.Attributes["direction"].Value);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void CommandListSelectionChange(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)sender;
			if (!(comboBox.DataContext is XmlNode))
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)comboBox.DataContext;
			Command command = FindCommand(xmlNode);
			if (command == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string value = "";
			if (comboBox.Name == "ElemName")
			{
				value = (string)comboBox.SelectedValue;
			}
			else if (comboBox.Name == "EventName")
			{
				ComboBoxItem comboBoxItem = (ComboBoxItem)comboBox.SelectedValue;
				if (comboBoxItem != null)
				{
					value = (string)comboBoxItem.Content;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
			{
				command.Update(xmlNode, comboBox.Name, value);
			}
		}

		public void CommandListButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Button button = (Button)sender;
			if (!(button.DataContext is XmlNode))
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)button.DataContext;
			if (button.Name == "DeleteCommand")
			{
				Command item = FindCommand(xmlNode);
				_commands.Remove(item);
				xmlNode.ParentNode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
			}
			else if (xmlNode.Name == "MenuItem")
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = xmlNode;
				while (xmlNode2.Name != "Menu")
				{
					xmlNode2 = xmlNode2.ParentNode;
				}
				Command command = FindCommand(xmlNode2);
				if (button.Name == "DeleteMenu")
				{
					command.Menu.DeleteItem(xmlNode);
				}
				else if (button.Name == "Up" || button.Name == "Down")
				{
					command.Menu.ChangeItemOrder(xmlNode, button.Name == "Up");
				}
			}
		}

		internal void RefreshElements()
		{
			Canvas curLayer = _curLayer;
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				curLayer.Children.Remove(element);
			}
			foreach (Element element2 in _elements)
			{
				curLayer.Children.Add(element2);
			}
		}

		public void RecalcSize()
		{
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (element is Group)
				{
					Group obj = element as Group;
					obj.CalcSize(newgroup: false);
					obj.RefreshHandles();
				}
			}
		}

		public void ParameterFieldButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Button button = (Button)sender;
			StackPanel stackPanel = (StackPanel)button.Parent;
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			foreach (UIElement child in stackPanel.Children)
			{
				if (child is TextBox)
				{
					text = ((TextBox)child).Text;
					text = text.Replace(' ', '_');
				}
				else if (child is ComboBox)
				{
					ComboBox comboBox = (ComboBox)child;
					if (comboBox.SelectionBoxItem != null)
					{
						text2 = (string)comboBox.SelectionBoxItem;
					}
				}
				else if (child is CheckBox)
				{
					_ = ((CheckBox)child).IsChecked.Value;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (text2)
			{
			case "COLOR":
			case "ColorAnimation":
				AddColorToAttributeList(text);
				break;
			case "Visibility":
				AddItemToList(text2, text);
				break;
			case "string":
			case "int":
			case "double":
			case "DoubleAnimation":
				AddItemToList("Parameter", text);
				break;
			case "DashAnimation":
				AddItemToList("DashMotion", text);
				break;
			default:
				_ = text2 == "enum";
				break;
			}
			XmlElement xmlElement = _attrib.CreateElement(text);
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = _attrib.CreateAttribute("type");
			xmlAttribute.Value = text2;
			xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			CheckBox checkBox = (CheckBox)_parameterfield.FindName("GlobalParam");
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("global");
			xmlAttribute2.Value = checkBox.IsChecked.Value.ToString();
			xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute2);
			switch (text2)
			{
			case "string":
			case "COLOR":
			{
				TextBox textBox15 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("TextDefaultValue");
				xmlElement.InnerText = textBox15.Text;
				break;
			}
			case "boolean":
			{
				TextBox textBox11 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("TrueValue");
				TextBox textBox12 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("FalseValue");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute18 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("value");
				xmlAttribute18.Value = textBox11.Text + "," + textBox12.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute18);
				xmlElement.InnerText = textBox11.Text;
				break;
			}
			case "Visibility":
			{
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute22 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("value");
				xmlAttribute22.Value = "Visible,Hidden";
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute22);
				xmlElement.InnerText = "Visible";
				break;
			}
			case "Gradient":
			{
				ComboBox comboBox4 = (ComboBox)_parameterfield.FindName("BaseGradient");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute19 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("base");
				string value = (string)comboBox4.SelectedValue;
				xmlAttribute19.Value = value;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute19);
				StackPanel stackPanel2 = (StackPanel)_parameterfield.FindName("ColorValues");
				for (int i = 0; i < stackPanel2.Children.Count; i++)
				{
					TextBox textBox13 = (TextBox)stackPanel2.Children[i];
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute20 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("color" + (i + 1));
					xmlAttribute20.Value = textBox13.Text;
					xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute20);
				}
				StackPanel stackPanel3 = (StackPanel)_parameterfield.FindName("OffsetValues");
				for (int j = 0; j < stackPanel3.Children.Count; j++)
				{
					TextBox textBox14 = (TextBox)stackPanel3.Children[j];
					XmlAttribute xmlAttribute21 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("offset" + (j + 1));
					xmlAttribute21.Value = textBox14.Text;
					xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute21);
				}
				AddDerivedGradient(xmlElement);
				break;
			}
			case "enum":
			{
				ComboBox comboBox3 = (ComboBox)_parameterfield.FindName("EnumCollection");
				string text3 = "";
				foreach (string item in (IEnumerable)comboBox3.Items)
				{
					if (text3 != "")
					{
						text3 += ",";
					}
					text3 += item;
				}
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute17 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("values");
				xmlAttribute17.Value = text3;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute17);
				break;
			}
			case "ColorAnimation":
			{
				TextBox textBox8 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimColor1");
				TextBox textBox9 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimColor2");
				TextBox textBox10 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimColorDuration");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute13 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("from");
				xmlAttribute13.Value = textBox8.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute13);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute14 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("to");
				xmlAttribute14.Value = textBox9.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute14);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute15 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("duration");
				xmlAttribute15.Value = textBox10.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute15);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute16 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("autoreverse");
				xmlAttribute16.Value = "True";
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute16);
				xmlElement.InnerText = textBox8.Text;
				AddAnimatedBrush(xmlElement);
				break;
			}
			case "DoubleAnimation":
			{
				TextBox textBox5 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimFrom");
				TextBox textBox6 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimTo");
				TextBox textBox7 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimDuration");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute9 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("from");
				xmlAttribute9.Value = textBox5.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute9);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute10 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("to");
				xmlAttribute10.Value = textBox6.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute10);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute11 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("duration");
				xmlAttribute11.Value = textBox7.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute11);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute12 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("autoreverse");
				xmlAttribute12.Value = "True";
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute12);
				xmlElement.InnerText = textBox5.Text;
				break;
			}
			case "DashAnimation":
			{
				ComboBox comboBox2 = (ComboBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimDashDirection");
				TextBox textBox4 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("AnimDashDuration");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute7 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("direction");
				xmlAttribute7.Value = comboBox2.SelectedIndex.ToString();
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute7);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute8 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("duration");
				xmlAttribute8.Value = textBox4.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute8);
				break;
			}
			case "XYList":
			{
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute5 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("xvalues");
				xmlAttribute5.Value = "";
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute5);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute6 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("yvalues");
				xmlAttribute6.Value = "";
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute6);
				break;
			}
			default:
			{
				TextBox textBox = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("MinValue");
				TextBox textBox2 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("MaxValue");
				TextBox textBox3 = (TextBox)_parameterfield.FindName("DefaultValue");
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute3 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("min");
				xmlAttribute3.Value = textBox.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute3);
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute4 = _attrib.CreateAttribute("max");
				xmlAttribute4.Value = textBox2.Text;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute4);
				xmlElement.InnerText = textBox3.Text;
				break;
			}
			}
			_attrib.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			if (_parametereditor != null)
			{
				_parametereditor.Add(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		private void GetPoint(System.Windows.Point p, out double x, out double y)
		{
			x = p.X;
			y = p.Y;
			if (!_snap)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_isometric)
			{
				double num = _grid * _cosval;
				if (p.X > 0.0)
				{
					x = (double)(int)((p.X + num * 0.5) / num) * num;
				}
				else
				{
					x = (double)(int)((p.X - num * 0.5) / num) * num;
				}
				int num2 = (int)(x / num);
				double num3 = _grid * _sinval * 2.0;
				num = ((num2 % 2 == 0) ? 0.0 : (num3 / 2.0));
				if (p.Y > 0.0)
				{
					y = (double)(int)((p.Y + num3 * 0.5) / num3) * num3 + num;
				}
				else
				{
					y = (double)(int)((p.Y - num3 * 0.5) / num3) * num3 - num;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (p.X > 0.0)
				{
					x = (double)(int)((p.X + _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid;
				}
				else
				{
					x = (double)(int)((p.X - _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid;
				}
				if (p.Y > 0.0)
				{
					y = (double)(int)((p.Y + _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid;
				}
				else
				{
					y = (double)(int)((p.Y - _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid;
				}
			}
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_dragport != null)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(IconPanel);
				_portlocator.MovePort(position);
			}
			else if (_inDrag)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position2 = e.GetPosition(IconPanel);
				double num = Math.Abs(position2.X - _curX);
				double num2 = Math.Abs(position2.Y - _curY);
				double num3 = 1.0;
				double num4 = 1.0;
				if (_snap && _grid > num3)
				{
					num3 = _grid;
					num4 = ((!_isometric) ? num3 : (_grid * _cosval));
				}
				if (!(num > num4) && !(num2 > num3))
				{
					return;
				}
				double x = position2.X;
				double y = position2.Y;
				if (_current is Element)
				{
					((Element)_current).RevConvCoord(ref x, ref y);
				}
				num = x - _startX;
				num2 = y - _startY;
				if (_snap)
				{
					if (!_isometric)
					{
						num = ((!(_baseX + num < 0.0)) ? ((double)(int)((_baseX + num + _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseX) : ((double)(int)((_baseX + num - _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseX));
						num2 = ((!(_baseY + num2 < 0.0)) ? ((double)(int)((_baseY + num2 + _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseY) : ((double)(int)((_baseY + num2 - _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseY));
					}
					else
					{
						double num5 = _grid * _cosval;
						num = ((!(_baseX + num < 0.0)) ? ((double)(int)((_baseX + num + num5 * 0.5) / num5) * num5 - _baseX) : ((double)(int)((_baseX + num - num5 * 0.5) / num5) * num5 - _baseX));
						num2 = ((!(_baseY + num2 < 0.0)) ? ((double)(int)((_baseY + num2 + _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseY) : ((double)(int)((_baseY + num2 - _grid * 0.5) / _grid) * _grid - _baseY));
						int value = ((!(_baseX + num < 0.0)) ? ((int)((_baseX + num + num5 * 0.4) / num5)) : ((int)((_baseX + num - num5 * 0.4) / num5)));
						if (Math.Abs(value) % 2 == 1)
						{
							num2 += _grid * 0.5;
						}
					}
				}
				if (_current is Anchor)
				{
					switch (((Anchor)_current).Direction)
					{
					case 1:
						num = 0.0;
						break;
					case 2:
						num2 = 0.0;
						break;
					}
					((Anchor)_current).Drag(num, num2);
				}
				BaseObj baseObj = _current;
				if (_current is Element)
				{
					if (((Element)_current).OnLabel)
					{
						baseObj = (BaseObj)_current.Parent;
					}
					((Element)baseObj).UpdateValues(num, num2);
				}
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)baseObj.RenderTransform;
				int index = ((baseObj is Group) ? 2 : 0);
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[index];
				translateTransform.X = num;
				translateTransform.Y = num2;
				_curX = position2.X;
				_curY = position2.Y;
			}
			else if (_inResize)
			{
				System.Windows.Point position3 = e.GetPosition(_curLayer);
				if (_current != null)
				{
					GetPoint(position3, out var x2, out var y2);
					((Element)_current).Resize(ref _handle, x2, y2);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (_inInsert == null || _dragElement == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				System.Windows.Point position4 = e.GetPosition(IconPanel);
				GetPoint(position4, out var x3, out var y3);
				if (_inInsert == "Line")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)_dragElement;
					line.X2 = x3;
					line.Y2 = y3;
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Rectangle" || _inInsert == "Ellipse")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					polyline.Points.RemoveAt(1);
					polyline.Points.Insert(1, new System.Windows.Point(x3, polyline.Points[0].Y));
					polyline.Points.RemoveAt(2);
					polyline.Points.Insert(2, new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3));
					polyline.Points.RemoveAt(3);
					polyline.Points.Insert(3, new System.Windows.Point(polyline.Points[0].X, y3));
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Path" || _inInsert == "Path2")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline2 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					if ((x3 >= polyline2.Points[0].X && y3 >= polyline2.Points[0].Y) || (x3 < polyline2.Points[0].X && y3 <= polyline2.Points[0].Y))
					{
						double num6 = x3 - polyline2.Points[0].X;
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(1);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(1, new System.Windows.Point(x3, polyline2.Points[0].Y));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(2);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(2, new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(3);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(3, new System.Windows.Point(polyline2.Points[0].X - num6, y3));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(4);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(4, new System.Windows.Point(polyline2.Points[0].X - num6, polyline2.Points[0].Y));
					}
					else
					{
						double num7 = y3 - polyline2.Points[0].Y;
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(1);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(1, new System.Windows.Point(polyline2.Points[0].X, y3));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(2);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(2, new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(3);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(3, new System.Windows.Point(x3, polyline2.Points[0].Y - num7));
						polyline2.Points.RemoveAt(4);
						polyline2.Points.Insert(4, new System.Windows.Point(polyline2.Points[0].X, polyline2.Points[0].Y - num7));
					}
					if (_tempPath != null)
					{
						_tempPath.Resize(polyline2);
					}
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Polygon")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_dragElement;
					int count = polygon.Points.Count;
					polygon.Points.RemoveAt(count - 1);
					polygon.Points.Insert(count - 1, new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3));
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Polyline" || _inInsert == "PolyCurve")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline3 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					int count2 = polyline3.Points.Count;
					polyline3.Points.RemoveAt(count2 - 1);
					polyline3.Points.Insert(count2 - 1, new System.Windows.Point(x3, y3));
				}
			}
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_dragport != null)
			{
				_curport = _dragport;
				_dragport.SetAttrib();
				_dragport = null;
			}
			if (_inDrag)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)_current.RenderTransform;
				int index = ((_current is Group) ? 2 : 0);
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[index];
				double x = translateTransform.X;
				double y = translateTransform.Y;
				if (x != 0.0 || y != 0.0)
				{
					if (_current is Anchor)
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Move, _current);
					}
					else if (_current is LabelAt)
					{
