						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Label, HistorySubcode.Move, _current);
					}
					else
					{
						AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Move, _current);
					}
					_current.Move(x, y);
					if (_current is Element)
					{
						((Element)_current).UpdateClipping();
					}
					if (_current.Parent is Group)
					{
						((Group)_current.Parent).CalcSize(newgroup: false);
					}
				}
				if (_current is Anchor)
				{
					((Anchor)_current).EndMove();
				}
				else if (_current is LabelAt)
				{
					((LabelAt)_current).EndMove();
				}
				if (_current is Element && ((Element)_current).OnLabel)
				{
					Text text = (Text)_current.Parent;
					text.EndMove(this);
				}
				_inDrag = false;
				_portlocator.Reset();
			}
			if (_inResize)
			{
				((Element)_current).EndResize(_handle);
				((Element)_current).RefreshHandles();
				if (((Element)_current).OnLabel)
				{
					Text text2 = (Text)_current.Parent;
					text2.Resized();
				}
				_inResize = false;
				_portlocator.Reset();
			}
			if (_inInsert == null || !(_inInsert != "Polygon") || !(_inInsert != "Polyline") || !(_inInsert != "PolyCurve"))
			{
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(IconPanel);
			System.Windows.Point position2 = e.GetPosition(this);
			bool flag = Math.Abs(position2.X - _insertPos.X) + Math.Abs(position2.Y - _insertPos.Y) < 10.0;
			_curLayer.Children.Remove(_dragElement);
			Element element = null;
			if (!flag)
			{
				if (_inInsert == "Line")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Line elem = (System.Windows.Shapes.Line)_dragElement;
					element = new Line(elem, created: true);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Rectangle")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					element = new Rectangle(poly);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Ellipse")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly2 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					element = new Ellipse(poly2);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Path" || _inInsert == "Path2")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline poly3 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
					element = new Path(poly3, _inInsert == "Path2");
					if (_tempPath != null)
					{
						_curLayer.Children.Remove(_tempPath);
						_tempPath = null;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (_inInsert == "TextBox" || _inInsert == "Text")
			{
				GetPoint(position, out var x2, out var y2);
				element = ((!(_inInsert == "TextBox")) ? ((Element)new Text(this, null, x2, y2, _hysysicon)) : ((Element)new TextEntry(x2, y2)));
			}
			else if (_inInsert == "Cloud")
			{
				GetPoint(position, out var x3, out var y3);
				Cloud cloud = new Cloud(x3, y3);
				Text text3 = new Text(this, null, x3, y3, hysysicon: false, centered: true);
				cloud.AddText(text3);
				AddElement(text3);
				element = cloud;
			}
			if (element != null)
			{
				AddElement(element);
				SetCurrent(element);
				if (_inInsert == "Text")
				{
					((Text)element).SetFocus();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_curLayer.Children.Remove(_dragElement);
				if (_tempPath != null)
				{
					_curLayer.Children.Remove(_tempPath);
					_tempPath = null;
				}
			}
			if (_inInsert != "Port")
			{
				CancelInsert();
			}
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonDown2(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			FireEvent("MouseEvent", "LeftButtonDown");
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonUp2(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			FireEvent("MouseEvent", "LeftButtonUp");
			if (m_menupopup != null)
			{
				m_menupopup.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void CancelInsert()
		{
			_inInsert = null;
			_dragElement = null;
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
			FireEvent("Insert", "off");
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			Canvas curLayer = _curLayer;
			if (!_inDrag && !_inResize)
			{
				SetCurrent(null);
			}
			if (!Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftShift) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightShift) && !Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl))
			{
				Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl);
			}
			System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(IconPanel);
			if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				_insertPos = e.GetPosition(this);
				GetPoint(position, out var x, out var y);
				if (_inInsert == "Line")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					line.X1 = x;
					line.Y1 = y;
					line.X2 = x;
					line.Y2 = y;
					line.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
					_dragElement = line;
					curLayer.Children.Add(line);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Rectangle" || _inInsert == "Ellipse" || _inInsert == "Path" || _inInsert == "Path2")
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline();
					polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					if (_inInsert == "Path" || _inInsert == "Path2")
					{
						polyline.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
						polyline.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
					}
					else
					{
						polyline.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
					}
					_dragElement = polyline;
					curLayer.Children.Add(polyline);
					if (_inInsert == "Path" || _inInsert == "Path2")
					{
						_tempPath = new Path(polyline);
						curLayer.Children.Add(_tempPath);
					}
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Polygon")
				{
					if (_dragElement != null)
					{
						System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_dragElement;
						polygon.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
						return;
					}
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon2 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon();
					polygon2.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polygon2.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polygon2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
					_dragElement = polygon2;
					curLayer.Children.Add(polygon2);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Polyline" || _inInsert == "PolyCurve")
				{
					if (_dragElement != null)
					{
						System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline2 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
						polyline2.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
						return;
					}
					System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline3 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline();
					polyline3.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline3.Points.Add(new System.Windows.Point(x, y));
					polyline3.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
					_dragElement = polyline3;
					curLayer.Children.Add(polyline3);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Port")
				{
					FireEvent("TogglePort", "on");
					TogglePorts(bOn: true);
					_portcounter++;
					Port port = new Port(x, y, this, "p" + _portcounter, _portcounter);
					_ports.Add(port);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Port, HistorySubcode.Add, port);
					_portlist.AppendChild(port.GetXml(_wholelist));
					port.Render(onPfs: false, visible: true);
					IconPanel.Children.Add(port);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Anchor")
				{
					Anchor anchor = new Anchor(x, y, this, "a" + ++_anchorcounter);
					_anchors.Add(anchor);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Anchor, HistorySubcode.Add, anchor);
					_anchorlist.AppendChild(anchor.GetXml(_wholelist));
					anchor.Render(visible: true);
					IconPanel.Children.Add(anchor);
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Label" && _label == null)
				{
					FireEvent("TogglePort", "on");
					TogglePorts(bOn: true);
					_label = new LabelAt(x, y, this);
					_label.Render(visible: true);
					AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Label, HistorySubcode.Add, _label);
					IconPanel.Children.Add(_label);
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
					FireEvent("Insert", "off");
				}
				else if (_inInsert == "Icon")
				{
					LoadComponent(_insertIcon, x, y);
					_inInsert = null;
					base.Cursor = Cursors.Arrow;
				}
				return;
			}
			_curX = position.X;
			_curY = position.Y;
			_startX = position.X;
			_startY = position.Y;
			if (_current != null)
			{
				_current.GetBasePoint(out _baseX, out _baseY);
				if (_current is Element)
				{
					((Element)_current).RevConvCoord(ref _startX, ref _startY);
					((Element)_current).RevConvCoord(ref _baseX, ref _baseY);
				}
			}
		}

		private void IconCtrl_MouseRightButtonUp(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_inInsert == "Polygon" || _inInsert == "Polyline" || _inInsert == "PolyCurve")
			{
				if (_dragElement != null)
				{
					_curLayer.Children.Remove(_dragElement);
					if (_inInsert == "Polyline")
					{
						System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
						if (polyline.Points.Count > 1)
						{
							polyline.Points.RemoveAt(polyline.Points.Count - 1);
							Polyline polyline2 = new Polyline(polyline, created: true);
							AddElement(polyline2);
							SetCurrent(polyline2);
						}
					}
					else if (_inInsert == "PolyCurve")
					{
						System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline polyline3 = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)_dragElement;
						if (polyline3.Points.Count > 3)
						{
							polyline3.Points.RemoveAt(polyline3.Points.Count - 1);
							PolyCurve polyCurve = new PolyCurve(polyline3);
							AddElement(polyCurve);
							SetCurrent(polyCurve);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon polygon = (System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)_dragElement;
						if (polygon.Points.Count > 2)
						{
							polygon.Points.RemoveAt(polygon.Points.Count - 1);
							Polygon polygon2 = new Polygon(polygon, created: true);
							AddElement(polygon2);
							SetCurrent(polygon2);
						}
					}
				}
				CancelInsert();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
			else if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				CancelInsert();
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}

		private void IconCtrl_PreviewMouseWheel(object sender, MouseWheelEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Point position = e.GetPosition(this);
			bool flag = Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftShift) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightShift);
			bool flag2 = Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.LeftCtrl) || Keyboard.IsKeyDown(Key.RightCtrl);
			if (e.Delta < 0)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					Scroll(-10.0, 0.0);
				}
				else if (flag2)
				{
					Scroll(0.0, 10.0);
				}
				else
				{
					Zoom(0.9090909090909091, position);
				}
			}
			else if (e.Delta > 0)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					Scroll(10.0, 0.0);
				}
				else if (flag2)
				{
					Scroll(0.0, -10.0);
				}
				else
				{
					Zoom(1.1, position);
				}
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		private void IconCtrl_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			_bLoaded = true;
			if (KBase)
			{
				AdjustWholeList();
			}
			if (base.Parent is ScrollViewer)
			{
				ScrollViewer scrollViewer = (ScrollViewer)base.Parent;
				scrollViewer.ScrollChanged += scrview_ScrollChanged;
			}
			TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
			transformGroup.Children.Add(new ScaleTransform(_scale, _scale));
			if (_bx != 0.0 || _by != 0.0)
			{
				transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(_bx, _by));
			}
			else
			{
				transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(base.ActualWidth / 2.0, base.ActualHeight / 2.0));
			}
			if (_onPfs)
			{
				transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform(0.0, 0.0));
			}
			RootPanel.RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			if (_attrib == null)
			{
				_attrib = new XmlDocument();
				string attributes = global::IconCtrl.Properties.Resources.Attributes;
				_attrib.LoadXml(attributes);
			}
			if (_adsmap == null)
			{
				Assembly executingAssembly = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
				Stream manifestResourceStream = executingAssembly.GetManifestResourceStream("IconCtrl.Ads-Metadata.xml");
				StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(manifestResourceStream);
				_adsmap = new XmlDocument();
				_adsmap.LoadXml(streamReader.ReadToEnd());
			}
			IconPanel.DataContext = _attrib.DocumentElement;
			if (_gradientsnode == null)
			{
				string gradients = global::IconCtrl.Properties.Resources.Gradients;
				XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
				xmlDocument.LoadXml(gradients);
				_gradientsnode = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			}
			if (!_onPfs)
			{
				SetupGrid();
				_parametereditor = new ParameterEditor(_parameterfield, _adsmodelmap, _attrib, _adsmap, this);
				_portlocator = new PortLocator(IconPanel);
				base.ContextMenu = base.Resources["BackgroundMenu"] as ContextMenu;
				FireEvent("History", "UndoClear");
				FireEvent("History", "RedoClear");
			}
		}

		private void scrview_ScrollChanged(object sender, ScrollChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (_scaleon)
			{
				SetupScale();
			}
		}

		private void SetupWholeList()
		{
			string text = "<Root>";
			text += "<Visibility>----</Visibility>";
			text += "<Parameter>----</Parameter>";
			text += "<DashMotion>----</DashMotion>";
			text += "<Attributes>";
			text += "<Stroke>None</Stroke>";
			text += "<Stroke>@Color</Stroke>";
			text += "<Stroke>@CColor</Stroke>";
			text += "<Fill>None</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>@Color</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>@CColor</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Transparent</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Vertical</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Horizontal</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Sphere</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Top Half Sphere</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Bottom Half Sphere</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Left Half Sphere</Fill>";
			text += "<Fill>Right Half Sphere</Fill>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>0.2</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>0.5</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>1</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>2</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>3</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>4</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "<StrokeThickness>5</StrokeThickness>";
			text += "</Attributes>";
			text += "<Elements></Elements>";
			text += "<Ports></Ports>";
			text += "<PortTypes></PortTypes>";
			text += "<InPortNames></InPortNames>";
			text += "<OutPortNames></OutPortNames>";
			text += "<BiPortNames></BiPortNames>";
			text += "<Anchors></Anchors>";
			text += "<Commands></Commands>";
			text += "<Names></Names>";
			text += "</Root>";
			_wholelist = new XmlDocument();
			_wholelist.LoadXml(text);
			_attributelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Attributes");
			_elementlist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Elements");
			_portlist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Ports");
			_porttypelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/PortTypes");
			_inportnamelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/InPortNames");
			_outportnamelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/OutPortNames");
			_biportnamelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/BiPortNames");
			_anchorlist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Anchors");
			_commandlist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Commands");
			_namelist = _wholelist.SelectSingleNode("/Root/Names");
			_elementlist.OwnerDocument.NodeInserted += OwnerDocument_NodeInserted;
		}

		private void AdjustWholeList()
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = _attributelist.SelectNodes("Stroke");
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (item.InnerText == "@Color")
				{
					item.InnerText = "Default";
				}
				else if (item.InnerText == "@CColor")
				{
					item.InnerText = "Other...";
				}
			}
			xmlNodeList = _attributelist.SelectNodes("Fill");
			foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (item2.InnerText == "@Color")
				{
					item2.InnerText = "Default";
				}
				else if (item2.InnerText == "@CColor")
				{
					item2.InnerText = "Other...";
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddWholeList(string color)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.SelectSingleNode("Stroke[text()='" + color + "']");
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Stroke");
				xmlElement.InnerText = color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		private void OwnerDocument_NodeInserted(object sender, XmlNodeChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = e.Node is XmlElement;
		}

		private void CleanElementList()
		{
			int count = _elementlist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _elementlist.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.Name == "Element" || xmlNode.Name == "Group")
				{
					_elementlist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
			_namelist.RemoveAll();
		}

		public void AddAnimatedGradient(string name, GradientBrush brush, AnimatedBrush[] animbrushes, XmlNode[] animparams)
		{
			AnimatedBrush item = new AnimatedBrush(name, brush, animbrushes, animparams, this);
			_animatedbrushes.Add(item);
		}

		public void UpdateAnimatedGradient(AnimatedBrush brush0, bool runflag)
		{
			foreach (AnimatedBrush animatedbrush in _animatedbrushes)
			{
				if (animatedbrush != brush0)
				{
					animatedbrush.UpdateFromChild(brush0, runflag);
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddAnimatedBrush(XmlNode node)
		{
			AnimatedBrush item = new AnimatedBrush(node, this);
			_animatedbrushes.Add(item);
		}

		private void AddItemToList(string element, string value)
		{
			if (_wholelist == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = _wholelist.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _wholelist.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[i];
				if (xmlNode.Name == element)
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement(element);
					xmlElement.InnerText = value;
					_wholelist.DocumentElement.InsertAfter(xmlElement, xmlNode);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveItemFromList(string element, string value)
		{
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in _wholelist.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Name == element && childNode.InnerText == value)
				{
					_wholelist.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(childNode);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadGradientsList()
		{
			if (_attributelist == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = _attributelist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.Name == "Fill" && xmlNode.InnerText != "----" && xmlNode.InnerText != "None" && !xmlNode.InnerText.StartsWith("@") && xmlNode.InnerText != "Default" && xmlNode.InnerText != "Other...")
				{
					_attributelist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
			if (_gradientsnode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in _gradientsnode.ChildNodes)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = childNode.Attributes["name"].Value;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		public void LoadLocalGradients(XmlNodeList nodelist)
		{
			_localgradients = nodelist;
			foreach (XmlNode item in nodelist)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = item.Attributes["name"].Value;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		public GradientBrush CreateGradientBrush(string name, XmlNode root)
		{
			GradientBrush gradientBrush = null;
			if (AnimatedBrush(name) != null)
			{
				return (GradientBrush)AnimatedBrush(name).Brush;
			}
			if (root.Name == "LinearGradientBrush")
			{
				gradientBrush = new LinearGradientBrush();
				string value = root.Attributes["StartPoint"].Value;
				string value2 = root.Attributes["EndPoint"].Value;
				((LinearGradientBrush)gradientBrush).StartPoint = ConvertPoint(value);
				((LinearGradientBrush)gradientBrush).EndPoint = ConvertPoint(value2);
			}
			else if (root.Name == "RadialGradientBrush")
			{
				gradientBrush = new RadialGradientBrush();
				if (root.Attributes["GradientOrigin"] != null)
				{
					string value3 = root.Attributes["GradientOrigin"].Value;
					((RadialGradientBrush)gradientBrush).GradientOrigin = ConvertPoint(value3);
				}
				if (root.Attributes["Center"] != null)
				{
					string value4 = root.Attributes["Center"].Value;
					((RadialGradientBrush)gradientBrush).Center = ConvertPoint(value4);
				}
				if (root.Attributes["RadiusX"] != null)
				{
					string value5 = root.Attributes["RadiusX"].Value;
					((RadialGradientBrush)gradientBrush).RadiusX = double.Parse(value5, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
				if (root.Attributes["RadiusY"] != null)
				{
					string value6 = root.Attributes["RadiusY"].Value;
					((RadialGradientBrush)gradientBrush).RadiusY = double.Parse(value6, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
			}
			if (gradientBrush != null)
			{
				XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes(root.Name + ".GradientStops/GradientStop");
				AnimatedBrush[] array = new AnimatedBrush[xmlNodeList.Count];
				XmlNode[] array2 = new XmlNode[xmlNodeList.Count];
				int num = 0;
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
				{
					string value7 = item.Attributes["Offset"].Value;
					string value8 = item.Attributes["Color"].Value;
					GradientStop gradientStop = new GradientStop();
					if (value8.StartsWith("{Binding "))
					{
						string text = ExtractSubstring(value8, '=', '}');
						AnimatedBrush animatedBrush = AnimatedBrush(text);
						if (animatedBrush != null)
						{
							flag = true;
							array[num] = animatedBrush;
						}
						else
						{
							Binding binding = new Binding();
							binding.XPath = text;
							BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.ColorProperty, binding);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						gradientStop.Color = (System.Windows.Media.Color)System.Windows.Media.ColorConverter.ConvertFromString(value8);
					}
					if (value7.StartsWith("{Binding "))
					{
						string text2 = ExtractSubstring(value7, '=', '}');
						XmlNode xmlNode2 = AnimatedParameter(text2);
						if (xmlNode2 != null)
						{
							flag = true;
							array2[num] = xmlNode2;
						}
						else
						{
							Binding binding2 = new Binding();
							binding2.XPath = text2;
							BindingOperations.SetBinding(gradientStop, GradientStop.OffsetProperty, binding2);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						gradientStop.Offset = double.Parse(value7, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					}
					gradientBrush.GradientStops.Add(gradientStop);
					num++;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					AddAnimatedGradient(name, gradientBrush, array, array2);
				}
			}
			return gradientBrush;
		}

		private System.Windows.Point ConvertPoint(string value)
		{
			string[] array = value.Split(',');
			double x = double.Parse(array[0], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			double y = double.Parse(array[1], CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			return new System.Windows.Point(x, y);
		}

		public string ExtractSubstring(string str, char from, char to)
		{
			int num = str.IndexOf(from);
			int num2 = str.IndexOf(to);
			if (num >= 0 && num2 > num)
			{
				return str.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
			}
			return str;
		}

		public void UpdateDerivedGradient(XmlNode node)
		{
			string value = node.Attributes["base"].Value;
			string name = node.Name;
			XmlNode xmlNode = _gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + value + "']", _nsmgr);
			XmlNode xmlNode2 = _gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + name + "']", _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode == null || xmlNode2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode newChild = ConvertDerivedGradientContent(node, xmlNode);
			xmlNode2.ReplaceChild(newChild, xmlNode2.FirstChild);
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				element.UpdateGradientBrush(name, GradientsNode, _nsmgr);
			}
		}

		private void AddDerivedGradient(XmlNode node)
		{
			string value = node.Attributes["base"].Value;
			string name = node.Name;
			XmlNode xmlNode = _gradientsnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + value + "']", _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = name;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				XmlElement xmlElement2 = _gradientsnode.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Gradient", _nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				SetAttribute(xmlElement2, "name", name);
				SetAttribute(xmlElement2, "local", "True");
				XmlNode newChild = ConvertDerivedGradientContent(node, xmlNode);
				xmlElement2.AppendChild(newChild);
				_gradientsnode.AppendChild(xmlElement2);
			}
		}

		private XmlNode ConvertDerivedGradientContent(XmlNode node, XmlNode basenode)
		{
			string name = node.Name;
			XmlNode xmlNode = _gradientsnode.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(basenode.FirstChild, deep: true);
			string innerText = xmlNode.InnerText;
			bool flag = false;
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(innerText);
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.SelectNodes("//GradientStop");
			int num = 1;
			foreach (XmlNode item2 in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = node.Attributes["color" + num].Value;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = item2.Attributes["Color"];
				if (value[0] == '@')
				{
					xmlAttribute.Value = "{Binding XPath=" + value.Substring(1) + "}";
					if (AnimatedBrush(value.Substring(1)) != null)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					xmlAttribute.Value = value;
				}
				string value2 = node.Attributes["offset" + num].Value;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute2 = item2.Attributes["Offset"];
				if (value2[0] == '@')
				{
					xmlAttribute2.Value = "{Binding XPath=" + value2.Substring(1) + "}";
					if (AnimatedParameter(value2.Substring(1)) != null)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					xmlAttribute2.Value = value2;
				}
				num++;
			}
			xmlNode.InnerText = xmlDocument.InnerXml;
			if (AnimatedBrush(name) != null)
			{
				AnimatedBrush item = AnimatedBrush(name);
				_animatedbrushes.Remove(item);
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				CreateGradientBrush(name, xmlDocument.DocumentElement);
			}
			return xmlNode;
		}

		public void AddLocalColor(string color)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.SelectSingleNode("Stroke[text()='" + color + "']");
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				AddAttribute(xmlElement, "local", "true");
				xmlElement.InnerText = color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				XmlElement xmlElement2 = _wholelist.CreateElement("Stroke");
				AddAttribute(xmlElement2, "local", "true");
				xmlElement2.InnerText = color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement2);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveLocalColors()
		{
			int count = _attributelist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.ChildNodes[num];
				if ((xmlNode.Name == "Stroke" || xmlNode.Name == "Fill") && xmlNode.Attributes["local"] != null)
				{
					_attributelist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddColorToAttributeList(string color)
		{
			if (_wholelist != null)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = "@" + color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				XmlElement xmlElement2 = _wholelist.CreateElement("Stroke");
				xmlElement2.InnerText = "@" + color;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement2);
			}
		}

		private void RemoveColorFromAttributeList(string color)
		{
			int count = _attributelist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.InnerText == "@" + color)
				{
					_attributelist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
		}

		private void RemoveGradientFromAttributeList(string gradient)
		{
			int count = _attributelist.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _attributelist.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.Name == "Fill" && xmlNode.InnerText == gradient)
				{
					_attributelist.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			TogglePorts(bOn: false);
			_ports.Clear();
			_elements.Clear();
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				layer.Children.Clear();
			}
			CleanElementList();
			_portlist.RemoveAll();
			_portlocator.Reset();
		}

		public void LoadGlobalProperties(XmlNode root)
		{
			if (root == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in root.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (_attrib.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode(childNode.Name) == null)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode2 = _attrib.ImportNode(childNode, deep: true);
					_attrib.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
					_parametereditor.Add(xmlNode2);
					string value = xmlNode2.Attributes["type"].Value;
					if (value == "COLOR" || value == "ColorAnimation")
					{
						AddColorToAttributeList(xmlNode2.Name);
					}
					if (value == "Visibility")
					{
						AddItemToList(value, xmlNode2.Name);
					}
					switch (value)
					{
					case "string":
					case "int":
					case "double":
					case "DoubleAnimation":
						AddItemToList("Parameter", xmlNode2.Name);
						break;
					}
					if (value == "DashAnimation")
					{
						AddItemToList("DashMotion", xmlNode2.Name);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void SaveGlobalProperties(XmlNode root)
		{
			if (root != null)
			{
				root.RemoveAll();
				foreach (XmlNode childNode in _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
				{
					if (childNode.Attributes["type"] != null && (childNode.Attributes["global"] == null || !(childNode.Attributes["global"].Value != "True")))
					{
						root.AppendChild(root.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(childNode, deep: true));
					}
				}
			}
			ResetGradients();
		}

		public void LoadPortTypes(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (root == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_porttypelist.RemoveAll();
			_porttypenames = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Model", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = item.Attributes["name"].Value;
				string value2 = item.Attributes["displayName"].Value;
				_porttypenames.Add(value, value2);
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
				xmlElement.InnerText = value;
				XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = _wholelist.CreateAttribute("dispName");
				xmlAttribute.Value = value2;
				xmlElement.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
				_porttypelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		public string PortTypeDisplayName(string name)
		{
			if (name != null && _porttypenames.ContainsKey(name))
			{
				return _porttypenames[name];
			}
			return "";
		}

		public void SaveIcon(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			GetField(out var minx, out var miny, out var maxx, out var maxy);
			XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("y:Range", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				xmlNode = node.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Range", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				node.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			}
			SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MinX", minx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MaxX", maxx.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			if (KBase)
			{
				SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MinY", (0.0 - maxy).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MaxY", (0.0 - miny).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			else
			{
				SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MinY", miny.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				SetAttribute(xmlNode, "MaxY", maxy.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
			}
			SaveFields(node, nsmgr);
			if (_localgradients != null && _localgradients.Count > 0)
			{
				SaveGradients(node, nsmgr);
			}
			SaveContext(node, nsmgr);
			SavePorts(node, nsmgr);
			SaveAnchors(node, nsmgr);
			SaveCommands(node, nsmgr);
			SaveRepeaters(node, nsmgr);
			SaveClipElements(node, nsmgr);
			SaveAnimation(node, nsmgr);
			SavePlot(node, nsmgr);
			if (_label != null)
			{
				_label.Save(node, nsmgr);
			}
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Xaml | y:XamlText", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				node.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				string qualifiedName = "Xaml";
				if (layer.Type == 3)
				{
					qualifiedName = "XamlText";
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = node.OwnerDocument.CreateElement(qualifiedName, nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				if (layer.Type == 1 || layer.Type == 2)
				{
					SetAttribute(xmlNode2, "relative", layer.Relative.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				}
				if (layer.Type == 2)
				{
					SetAttribute(xmlNode2, "repeat", layer.Repeat.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat));
				}
				if (layer.growthmoniker != null)
				{
					SetAttribute(xmlNode2, "growthmoniker", layer.growthmoniker);
				}
				if (layer.labelinfo != null)
				{
					SetAttribute(xmlNode2, "labelinfo", layer.labelinfo);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(layer.visibility))
				{
					SetAttribute(xmlNode2, "Visibility", layer.visibility);
				}
				node.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
				string text = "";
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					text += element.GenXaml(bCurrent: false, ResolveAnchor: false);
				}
				XmlCDataSection newChild = node.OwnerDocument.CreateCDataSection(text);
				xmlNode2.AppendChild(newChild);
			}
		}

		public string GenCurrentXaml()
		{
			string text = "";
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					text += element.GenXaml(bCurrent: true, ResolveAnchor: false);
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string GenSvg(bool metadataonly = false)
		{
			string text = "";
			text += "<metadata>";
			GetField(out var minx, out var miny, out var maxx, out var maxy);
			text = text + "<left>" + (minx / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "</left>";
			text = text + "<top>" + (miny / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "</top>";
			text = text + "<right>" + (maxx / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "</right>";
			text = text + "<bottom>" + (maxy / 100.0).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat) + "</bottom>";
			if (_iconnode.Attributes["def"] != null)
			{
				text += "<autostream>true</autostream>";
			}
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Attributes["type"] != null && childNode.Attributes["global"] != null && childNode.Attributes["global"].Value == "False")
				{
					if (!flag)
					{
						flag = true;
						text += "<context>";
					}
					text += "<item ";
					text = text + "name=\"" + childNode.Name + "\" ";
					text = text + "type=\"" + childNode.Attributes["type"].Value + "\" ";
					if (childNode.Attributes["max"] != null)
					{
						text = text + "max=\"" + childNode.Attributes["max"].Value + "\" ";
					}
					if (childNode.Attributes["min"] != null)
					{
						text = text + "min=\"" + childNode.Attributes["min"].Value + "\" ";
					}
					text += ">";
					text += childNode.InnerText;
					text += "</item>";
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				text += "</context>";
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				text += port.GenSvg();
			}
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				text += anchor.GenSvg();
			}
			text += "</metadata>";
			if (metadataonly)
			{
				return text;
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					text += element.GenSvg();
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void SaveSmartIcon(string filename)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			string text = "<UserControl xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\" xmlns:local=\"http://localhost/pfs\">";
			text += "<UserControl.Resources>";
			text += "<XmlDataProvider x:Key=\"Data\" XPath=\"/root\">";
			text += "<x:XData>";
			text += "<root>";
			text += AttribNode.InnerXml;
			text += "</root>";
			text += "</x:XData>";
			text += "</XmlDataProvider>";
			text += "<local:Calc x:Key=\"Calc\" />";
			text += "<local:MCalc x:Key=\"MCalc\" />";
			text += "<local:EnumCalc x:Key=\"EnumCalc\" />";
			text += "</UserControl.Resources>";
			text += "<Canvas DataContext=\"{Binding Source={StaticResource Data}}\">";
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					string code = element.GenXaml(bCurrent: false, ResolveAnchor: true);
					text += ParseCode(code, _nsmgr);
				}
			}
			text += "</Canvas>";
			text += "</UserControl>";
			xmlDocument.LoadXml(text);
			xmlDocument.Save(filename);
		}

		private void SaveGradients(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Gradients", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
			root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
			foreach (XmlNode localgradient in _localgradients)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = root.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(localgradient, deep: true);
				xmlNode2.Attributes.RemoveNamedItem("new");
				xmlNode.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
			}
		}

		private void SaveContext(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Attributes["type"] != null && childNode.Attributes["global"] != null && childNode.Attributes["global"].Value == "False")
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (num == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode2 = root.SelectSingleNode("y:Context", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode2 == null)
			{
				xmlNode2 = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Context", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
				root.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
			}
			else
			{
				xmlNode2.RemoveAll();
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode2 in _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode2.Attributes["type"] != null && childNode2.Attributes["global"] != null && childNode2.Attributes["global"].Value == "False")
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode4 = root.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(childNode2, deep: true);
					if (xmlNode4.Attributes["override"] != null)
					{
						xmlNode4.Attributes.RemoveNamedItem("override");
					}
					xmlNode2.AppendChild(xmlNode4);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SaveFields(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			IconField iconField = new IconField(KBase, root, nsmgr);
			iconField.Load();
			iconField.CalcFields();
			iconField.SaveFields(root.OwnerDocument, nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
		}

		private void SavePorts(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (KBase)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = root.SelectSingleNode("y:Ports", nsmgr);
				if (xmlNode == null)
				{
					xmlNode = root.OwnerDocument.CreateElement("Ports", nsmgr.LookupNamespace("y"));
					root.AppendChild(xmlNode);
				}
				root = xmlNode;
			}
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.Save(root, nsmgr);
			}
			int count = root.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = root.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode2.Name == "Port")
				{
					if (xmlNode2.Attributes["updated"] == null)
					{
						root.RemoveChild(xmlNode2);
					}
					else
					{
						xmlNode2.Attributes.RemoveNamedItem("updated");
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void SaveAnchors(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				anchor.Save(root, nsmgr);
			}
		}

		private void SaveCommands(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Command|y:Menu", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				root.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Command command in _commands)
			{
				command.Save(root, nsmgr);
			}
		}

		private void SaveRepeaters(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Repeater", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				root.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					if (element is Group)
					{
						Group obj = (Group)element;
						obj.SaveRepeater(root, nsmgr);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadRepeaters(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Repeater", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = item.Attributes["index"].Value;
				((Group)FindElement(value, 0))?.LoadRepeater(item);
			}
		}

		private void SaveClipElements(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Clip", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				root.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					element.SaveClip(root, nsmgr);
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadClipElements(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Clip", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = item.Attributes["index"].Value;
				string value2 = item.Attributes["elements"].Value;
				Element element = FindElement(value, 0);
				element.LoadClip(value2);
			}
		}

		private void SaveAnimation(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Animation", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				root.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					element.SaveAnimation(root, nsmgr);
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadAnimation(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Animation", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = item.Attributes["index"].Value;
				Element element = FindElement(value, 0);
				element.LoadAnimation(item);
			}
		}

		private void SavePlot(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Plot", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				root.RemoveChild(item);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					if (element is Polyline)
					{
						Polyline polyline = (Polyline)element;
						polyline.SavePlot(root, nsmgr);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadPlot(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:Plot", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				string value = item.Attributes["index"].Value;
				Element element = FindElement(value, 0);
				if (element is Polyline)
				{
					Polyline polyline = (Polyline)element;
					polyline.LoadPlot(item);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetAttribute(XmlNode node, string attribname, string value)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes[attribname];
			if (xmlAttribute == null)
			{
				xmlAttribute = node.OwnerDocument.CreateAttribute(attribname);
				node.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			}
			xmlAttribute.Value = value;
		}

		public Rect GetSize()
		{
			GetField(out var minx, out var miny, out var maxx, out var maxy);
			return new Rect(minx, miny, maxx - minx, maxy - miny);
		}

		private void GetField(out double minx, out double miny, out double maxx, out double maxy)
		{
			minx = 10000000000.0;
			miny = 10000000000.0;
			maxx = -10000000000.0;
			maxy = -10000000000.0;
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				layer.GetField(ref minx, ref miny, ref maxx, ref maxy);
			}
		}

		public string GetXamlCode(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Xaml", nsmgr);
			string text = "";
			IEnumerator enumerator = xmlNodeList.GetEnumerator();
			try
			{
				if (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = (XmlNode)enumerator.Current;
					text += ParseCode(xmlNode.FirstChild.InnerText, nsmgr);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				IDisposable disposable = enumerator as IDisposable;
				if (disposable != null)
				{
					disposable.Dispose();
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void Load(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, bool hysysicon, List<string> inportnames, List<string> outportnames, List<string> biportnames, Dictionary<string, string> porttypes)
		{
			if (_iconnode != null && (m_history.Count > 0 || m_redoList.Count > 0))
			{
				MessageBoxResult messageBoxResult = MessageBox.Show("Save the icon?", "Icon Save", MessageBoxButton.YesNo);
				if (messageBoxResult == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
				{
					SaveIcon(_iconnode, _nsmgr);
				}
			}
			if (node.Name == "Model")
			{
				KBase = true;
			}
			_iconnode = node;
			_hysysicon = hysysicon;
			_groupcount = 0;
			_porttypes = porttypes;
			ClearHistory(m_history);
			ClearHistory(m_redoList);
			if (_bLoaded)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				ScrollViewer scrollViewer = (ScrollViewer)base.Parent;
				RootPanel.Width = scrollViewer.ViewportWidth;
				RootPanel.Height = scrollViewer.ViewportHeight;
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = 1.0;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = (_hysysicon ? (-1.0) : 1.0);
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
				translateTransform.X = scrollViewer.ViewportWidth / 2.0;
				translateTransform.Y = scrollViewer.ViewportHeight / 2.0;
			}
			if (!KBase)
			{
				FireEvent("TogglePort", "off");
			}
			_ports.Clear();
			_label = null;
			_animatedbrushes.Clear();
			ResetGradients();
			if (_attrib == null)
			{
				_attrib = new XmlDocument();
				string attributes = global::IconCtrl.Properties.Resources.Attributes;
				_attrib.LoadXml(attributes);
			}
			if (_gradientsnode == null)
			{
				string gradients = global::IconCtrl.Properties.Resources.Gradients;
				XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
				xmlDocument.LoadXml(gradients);
				_gradientsnode = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			}
			RemoveLocalColors();
			ClearAnchors();
			LoadContext(node, nsmgr);
			XmlNode xmlNode = LoadReferences(node, nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				node = xmlNode;
				LoadContext(node, nsmgr, 1);
			}
			LoadGradients(node, nsmgr);
			LoadLayers(node, nsmgr, designmode: true);
			LoadPorts(node, nsmgr);
			if (!ABE)
			{
				LoadPortNames(inportnames, outportnames, biportnames);
			}
			LoadAnchors(node, nsmgr, 0.0, 0.0, 0);
			LoadCommands(node, nsmgr);
			LoadRepeaters(node, nsmgr);
			LoadClipElements(node, nsmgr);
			LoadAnimation(node, nsmgr);
			LoadPlot(node, nsmgr);
			LoadNames();
			LoadLabelPosition(node, nsmgr);
			TogglePorts(KBase);
			SetupGrid();
			if (_portlocator != null)
			{
				_portlocator.Reset();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateIconReference(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			ClearAnchors();
			LoadContext(node, nsmgr, 2);
			LoadLayers(node, nsmgr, designmode: true);
			LoadPorts(node, nsmgr);
			LoadAnchors(node, nsmgr, 0.0, 0.0, 0);
			LoadCommands(node, nsmgr);
			LoadRepeaters(node, nsmgr);
			LoadClipElements(node, nsmgr);
		}

		public void LoadIcon(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, XmlNode context)
		{
			_nsmgr = nsmgr;
			_attrib = new XmlDocument();
			string attributes = global::IconCtrl.Properties.Resources.Attributes;
			_attrib.LoadXml(attributes);
			_hysysicon = node.SelectSingleNode("y:Port/attribute::moniker", nsmgr) != null;
			if (_hysysicon)
			{
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				((ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0]).ScaleY = -1.0;
			}
			if (context == null)
			{
				context = node.SelectSingleNode("y:Context", nsmgr);
			}
			if (context != null)
			{
				AddContext(context);
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode = LoadReferences(node, nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				node = xmlNode;
				LoadContext(node, nsmgr, 1);
			}
			LoadGradients(node, nsmgr);
			LoadLayers(node, nsmgr, designmode: false);
			LoadPorts(node, nsmgr);
			ClearAnchors();
			LoadAnchors(node, nsmgr, 0.0, 0.0, 0);
			LoadCommands(node, nsmgr);
			LoadRepeaters(node, nsmgr);
			LoadClipElements(node, nsmgr);
			LoadAnimation(node, nsmgr);
			LoadPlot(node, nsmgr);
			LoadLabelPosition(node, nsmgr);
		}

		private void LoadLayers(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, bool designmode)
		{
			int count = IconPanel.Children.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = IconPanel.Children[num];
				if (!(uIElement is Layer))
				{
					IconPanel.Children.Remove(uIElement);
				}
			}
			if (_layers.Count > 1)
			{
				for (int num2 = _layers.Count - 1; num2 >= 1; num2--)
				{
					IconPanel.Children.Remove(_layers[num2]);
					_layers.RemoveAt(num2);
				}
			}
			_curLayer = _layers[0];
			_curLayer.Children.Clear();
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Xaml | y:XamlText", nsmgr);
			count = 0;
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				if (count >= _layers.Count)
				{
					if (designmode)
					{
						foreach (Element element in _elements)
						{
							element.Enable(enable: false);
						}
					}
					Layer layer = new Layer(IconPanel);
					_layers.Add(layer);
					_curLayer = layer;
				}
				if (item.Name == "XamlText")
				{
					_curLayer.Type = 3;
				}
				else if (item.Attributes["repeat"] != null)
				{
					_curLayer.Type = 2;
					_curLayer.Relative = double.Parse(item.Attributes["relative"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					_curLayer.Repeat = double.Parse(item.Attributes["repeat"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
				else if (item.Attributes["relative"] != null)
				{
					_curLayer.Type = 1;
					_curLayer.Relative = double.Parse(item.Attributes["relative"].Value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				}
				else
				{
					_curLayer.Type = 0;
				}
				if (item.Attributes["growthmoniker"] != null)
				{
					_curLayer.growthmoniker = item.Attributes["growthmoniker"].Value;
				}
				if (item.Attributes["labelinfo"] != null)
				{
					_curLayer.labelinfo = item.Attributes["labelinfo"].Value;
				}
				if (item.Attributes["Visibility"] != null)
				{
					_curLayer.visibility = item.Attributes["Visibility"].Value;
				}
				_curLayer.Setup();
				string code = ParseCode(item.FirstChild.InnerText, nsmgr);
				LoadXaml(code, layermode: true);
				count++;
			}
			if (count == 0)
			{
				CleanElementList();
			}
			if (this.LayerReset != null)
			{
				this.LayerReset(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void ResetGradients()
		{
			if (_gradientsnode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int count = _gradientsnode.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = _gradientsnode.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode.Attributes["local"] != null)
				{
					_gradientsnode.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
					RemoveGradientFromAttributeList(xmlNode.Attributes["name"].Value);
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadGradients(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = root.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradients", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in xmlNode.ChildNodes)
			{
				XmlNode newChild = _gradientsnode.OwnerDocument.ImportNode(childNode, deep: true);
				_gradientsnode.AppendChild(newChild);
				XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Fill");
				xmlElement.InnerText = childNode.Attributes["name"].Value;
				_attributelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
			}
		}

		private XmlNode LoadReferences(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode result = null;
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = root.SelectNodes("y:IconReference", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				IconReference iconReference = new IconReference(item, nsmgr);
				_iconrefs.Add(iconReference);
				if (iconReference.IsDefault)
				{
					result = iconReference.IconNode;
					iconReference.IsCurrent = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void LoadContext(XmlNode root, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, int loadmode = 0)
		{
			if (loadmode != 1)
			{
				int count = _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
				for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = _attrib.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[num];
					if (xmlNode.Attributes["type"] != null && (loadmode != 2 || xmlNode.Attributes["common"] == null))
					{
						if (xmlNode.Attributes["global"] != null && xmlNode.Attributes["global"].Value == "False")
						{
							_attrib.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
							string value = xmlNode.Attributes["type"].Value;
							if (value == "COLOR" || value == "ColorAnimation")
							{
								RemoveColorFromAttributeList(xmlNode.Name);
							}
							if (value == "Visibility")
							{
								RemoveItemFromList(value, xmlNode.Name);
							}
							switch (value)
							{
							case "string":
							case "int":
							case "double":
								RemoveItemFromList("Parameter", xmlNode.Name);
								break;
							}
						}
						else if (xmlNode.Attributes["override"] != null)
						{
							_attrib.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			XmlNode xmlNode2 = root.SelectSingleNode("y:Context", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode2 != null)
			{
				AddContext(xmlNode2);
			}
			if (_parametereditor != null)
			{
				_parametereditor.Reload(_attrib);
			}
		}

		private void AddContext(XmlNode contextnode)
		{
			foreach (XmlNode childNode in contextnode.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode.Attributes["type"] == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string value = childNode.Attributes["type"].Value;
				if (value == "Gradient")
				{
					continue;
				}
				XmlNode xmlNode2 = _attrib.ImportNode(childNode, deep: true);
				XmlNode xmlNode3 = _attrib.DocumentElement.SelectSingleNode(xmlNode2.Name);
				if (xmlNode3 != null)
				{
					if (xmlNode3.Attributes["global"].Value == "True")
					{
						AddAttribute(xmlNode2, "override", "");
						xmlNode2.Attributes["global"].Value = "True";
						_attrib.DocumentElement.InsertBefore(xmlNode2, xmlNode3);
					}
					else
					{
						_attrib.DocumentElement.ReplaceChild(xmlNode2, xmlNode3);
					}
					continue;
				}
				_attrib.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlNode2);
				if (value == "COLOR" || value == "ColorAnimation")
				{
					AddColorToAttributeList(xmlNode2.Name);
				}
				if (value == "ColorAnimation")
				{
					AddAnimatedBrush(xmlNode2);
				}
				if (value == "Visibility")
				{
					AddItemToList(value, xmlNode2.Name);
				}
				switch (value)
				{
				case "string":
				case "int":
				case "double":
				case "DoubleAnimation":
					AddItemToList("Parameter", xmlNode2.Name);
					break;
				}
				if (value == "DashAnimation")
				{
					AddItemToList("DashMotion", xmlNode2.Name);
				}
				if (value == "enum")
				{
					string value2 = xmlNode2.Attributes["values"].Value;
					string[] array = value2.Split(',');
					string[] array2 = array;
					foreach (string text in array2)
					{
						AddItemToList("Visibility", xmlNode2.Name + "[" + text + "]");
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (XmlNode childNode2 in contextnode.ChildNodes)
			{
				if (childNode2.Attributes["type"] == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string value3 = childNode2.Attributes["type"].Value;
				if (!(value3 != "Gradient"))
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode5 = _attrib.ImportNode(childNode2, deep: true);
					_attrib.DocumentElement.AppendChild(xmlNode5);
					if (value3 == "Gradient")
					{
						AddDerivedGradient(xmlNode5);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadXaml(string code, bool layermode)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml("<root>" + code + "</root>");
			FilterCommentOut(xmlDocument);
			int count = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
			_elements.Clear();
			string text = "<Canvas xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\" ";
			text += "xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\" ";
			text += "xmlns:yy=\"clr-namespace:IconCtrl;assembly=IconCtrl\" >";
			text += "<Canvas.Resources>";
			text += "<yy:Calc x:Key=\"Calc\" />";
			text += "<yy:EnumCalc x:Key=\"EnumCalc\" />";
			text += "</Canvas.Resources>";
			text = text + code + "</Canvas>";
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
			streamWriter.WriteLine(text);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			Canvas canvas = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream) as Canvas;
			if (!layermode)
			{
				IconPanel.Children.Clear();
			}
			int count2 = canvas.Children.Count;
			Element lastelement = null;
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			for (int i = 0; i < count2; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = canvas.Children[0];
				canvas.Children.Remove(uIElement);
				XmlNode elemnode = documentElement.ChildNodes[i];
				Element element = LoadElement(uIElement, lastelement, elemnode);
				if (element != null)
				{
					_elements.Add(element);
					lastelement = element;
					_curLayer.Children.Add(element);
					if (i < count)
					{
						element.XmlNode(xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[i]);
					}
				}
			}
			CleanElementList();
			foreach (Element element2 in _elements)
			{
				XmlNode xml = element2.GetXml(_wholelist);
				_elementlist.AppendChild(xml);
			}
			_current = null;
			_curport = null;
		}

		private void FilterCommentOut(XmlDocument doc)
		{
			int count = doc.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
			for (int num = count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				XmlNode xmlNode = doc.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[num];
				if (xmlNode is XmlComment)
				{
					doc.DocumentElement.RemoveChild(xmlNode);
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadPorts(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			if (_portlist != null)
			{
				_portlist.RemoveAll();
			}
			_portcounter = 0;
			bool kbaseAutoStream = false;
			if (KBase)
			{
				if (node.Attributes["name"] == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				string value = node.Attributes["name"].Value;
				if (value.StartsWith("PIP_") || value.StartsWith("INS_CV"))
				{
					kbaseAutoStream = true;
				}
			}
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("./y:Port | ./y:Ports/y:Port", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				_portcounter++;
				Port port = new Port(item, this, _portcounter, kbaseAutoStream);
				_ports.Add(port);
				if (_portlist != null)
				{
					_portlist.AppendChild(port.GetXml(_wholelist));
				}
			}
			foreach (Port port2 in _ports)
			{
				port2.Render(_onPfs, _onPfs ? true : false);
				IconPanel.Children.Add(port2);
			}
		}

		private void LoadPortNames(List<string> inports, List<string> outports, List<string> biports)
		{
			_inportnamelist.RemoveAll();
			if (inports != null)
			{
				foreach (string inport in inports)
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
					xmlElement.InnerText = inport;
					_inportnamelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				}
			}
			_outportnamelist.RemoveAll();
			if (outports != null)
			{
				foreach (string outport in outports)
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement2 = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
					xmlElement2.InnerText = outport;
					_outportnamelist.AppendChild(xmlElement2);
				}
			}
			_biportnamelist.RemoveAll();
			if (biports == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string biport in biports)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement3 = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
				xmlElement3.InnerText = biport;
				_biportnamelist.AppendChild(xmlElement3);
			}
		}

		private void ClearAnchors()
		{
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				anchor.ClearBinding();
			}
			_anchors.Clear();
			if (_anchorlist != null)
			{
				_anchorlist.RemoveAll();
			}
			_anchorcounter = 0;
		}

		private void LoadAnchors(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr, double bx, double by, int elementoffset)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Anchor", nsmgr);
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				_anchorcounter++;
				Anchor anchor = new Anchor(item, this, "a" + _anchorcounter, nsmgr, elementoffset);
				_anchors.Add(anchor);
				if (_anchorlist != null)
				{
					_anchorlist.AppendChild(anchor.GetXml(_wholelist));
				}
				anchor.Shift(bx, by);
				anchor.Render(visible: true);
				if (_onPfs)
				{
					anchor.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				IconPanel.Children.Add(anchor);
			}
		}

		private void LoadCommands(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNodeList xmlNodeList = node.SelectNodes("y:Command|y:Menu", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNodeList.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (OnPfs)
			{
				m_menupopup = new Popup();
			}
			foreach (XmlNode item in xmlNodeList)
			{
				Command command = new Command(item, this, nsmgr);
				_commands.Add(command);
				if (_commandlist != null)
				{
					_commandlist.AppendChild(command.GetXml(_wholelist));
				}
			}
		}

		private void LoadNames()
		{
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(element.Name))
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement = _wholelist.CreateElement("Name");
					xmlElement.InnerText = element.Name;
					_namelist.AppendChild(xmlElement);
				}
			}
		}

		public void AddCommand(string type, XmlNode refnode)
		{
			if (type == "MenuItem")
			{
				if (refnode.Name == "Menu" || refnode.Name == "MenuItem")
				{
					XmlNode xmlNode = refnode;
					while (xmlNode.Name == "MenuItem")
					{
						xmlNode = xmlNode.ParentNode;
					}
					FindCommand(xmlNode)?.AddMenuItem(refnode);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Command command = new Command(type, this);
				_commands.Add(command);
				_commandlist.AppendChild(command.GetXml(_wholelist));
			}
		}

		public void ShowMenu(Menu menu, Element element)
		{
			if (m_menupopup != null)
			{
				m_menupopup.PlacementTarget = element;
				m_menupopup.Placement = PlacementMode.Relative;
				m_menupopup.Child = menu;
				m_menupopup.IsOpen = true;
			}
		}

		public void HideMenu()
		{
			if (m_menupopup != null)
			{
				m_menupopup.IsOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void LoadLabelPosition(XmlNode node, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			XmlNode xmlNode = node.SelectSingleNode("y:LabelAt", nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				_label = new LabelAt(xmlNode, this);
				_label.Render(visible: false);
				if (_onPfs)
				{
					_label.Visibility = Visibility.Hidden;
				}
				IconPanel.Children.Add(_label);
			}
		}

		private void LoadComponent(XmlNode iconnode, double bx, double by)
		{
			int count = _elements.Count;
			string xamlCode = GetXamlCode(iconnode, _nsmgr);
			XmlNode xmlNode = iconnode.SelectSingleNode("y:Context", _nsmgr);
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				AddContext(xmlNode);
				if (_parametereditor != null)
				{
					_parametereditor.Reload(_attrib);
				}
			}
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml("<root>" + xamlCode + "</root>");
			int count2 = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
			string value = "<Canvas xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\">" + xamlCode + "</Canvas>";
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
			streamWriter.WriteLine(value);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			Canvas canvas = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream) as Canvas;
			int count3 = canvas.Children.Count;
			Element lastelement = null;
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			for (int i = 0; i < count3; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = canvas.Children[0];
				canvas.Children.Remove(uIElement);
				XmlNode elemnode = documentElement.ChildNodes[i];
				Element element = LoadElement(uIElement, lastelement, elemnode);
				if (element != null)
				{
					_elements.Add(element);
					lastelement = element;
					element.Move(bx, by);
					_curLayer.Children.Add(element);
					if (i < count2)
					{
						element.XmlNode(xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[i]);
					}
					XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(_wholelist);
					_elementlist.AppendChild(xml);
				}
			}
			LoadAnchors(iconnode, _nsmgr, bx, by, count);
		}

		private Element LoadElement(UIElement elem, Element lastelement, XmlNode elemnode, int import = 0)
		{
			Element element = null;
			try
			{
				if (elem is Canvas)
				{
					Canvas canvas = (Canvas)elem;
					if (canvas.Tag != null && ((string)canvas.Tag).StartsWith("Arrow"))
					{
						lastelement?.SetArrow(canvas);
						return null;
					}
					element = LoadGroup(canvas, elemnode, import);
				}
				else
				{
					element = NewElement(elem, elemnode, import);
				}
				element.Control = this;
				element.AddHandles();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return element;
		}

		private Element LoadGroup(Canvas cv, XmlNode rootnode, int import)
		{
			int import2 = import;
			if (rootnode.ParentNode.Name != "root")
			{
				import2 = 0;
			}
			Element element = new Group(cv, this, ++_groupcount, import2);
			Group obj = (Group)element;
			int count = cv.Children.Count;
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = cv.Children[0];
				cv.Children.Remove(uIElement);
				XmlNode xmlNode = null;
				if (rootnode != null)
				{
					do
					{
						xmlNode = rootnode.ChildNodes[num++];
					}
					while (xmlNode is XmlComment || xmlNode.Name.StartsWith("Canvas."));
				}
				Element element2 = NewElement(uIElement, xmlNode, import);
				if (element2 is Group)
				{
					((Group)element2).Parent = obj;
				}
				element2.Control = this;
				obj.Add(element2);
			}
			obj.CalcSize(newgroup: false, initial: true);
			return element;
		}

		private Element NewElement(UIElement elem, XmlNode elemnode, int import)
		{
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)
			{
				return new Rectangle((System.Windows.Shapes.Rectangle)elem);
			}
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Path)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Path path = (System.Windows.Shapes.Path)elem;
				string source = path.Data.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
				if (import != 0 || (string)path.Tag == "Generic")
				{
					return new Path0(path, elemnode);
				}
				if (source.Contains('Q'))
				{
					return new PolyCurve(path);
				}
				return new Path(path);
			}
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Line)
			{
				return new Line(elem);
			}
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse)
			{
				return new Ellipse((System.Windows.Shapes.Ellipse)elem);
			}
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)
			{
				return new Polygon((System.Windows.Shapes.Polygon)elem);
			}
			if (elem is System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)
			{
				return new Polyline((System.Windows.Shapes.Polyline)elem);
			}
			if (elem is TextBox)
			{
				return new TextEntry((TextBox)elem);
			}
			if (elem is TextBlock)
			{
				if (OnPfs)
				{
					return new Text((TextBlock)elem, _hysysicon);
				}
				return new Text(this, (TextBlock)elem, _hysysicon);
			}
			if (elem is Viewbox)
			{
				Viewbox viewbox = (Viewbox)elem;
				TextBlock tb = viewbox.Child as TextBlock;
				if (OnPfs)
				{
					return new Text(tb, _hysysicon, viewbox);
				}
				return new Text(this, tb, _hysysicon, viewbox);
			}
			if (elem is Canvas)
			{
				return LoadGroup((Canvas)elem, elemnode, import);
			}
			return new Element(elem);
		}

		private void AddElement(Element element)
		{
			element.Control = this;
			element.AddHandles();
			_elements.Add(element);
			_curLayer.Children.Add(element);
			XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(_wholelist);
			_elementlist.AppendChild(xml);
			_portlocator.Reset();
			AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Add, HistorySubcode.Add, element);
		}

		public string ParseGradient(string code)
		{
			return ParseCode(code, _nsmgr);
		}

		private string ParseCode(string code, XmlNamespaceManager nsmgr)
		{
			int startIndex = 0;
			while (true)
			{
				startIndex = code.IndexOf("<!-- Gradient ", startIndex);
				if (startIndex < 0)
				{
					break;
				}
				startIndex = code.IndexOf('(', startIndex);
				int num = code.IndexOf(')', startIndex);
				string text = code.Substring(startIndex + 1, num - startIndex - 1);
				startIndex = code.IndexOf('>', startIndex);
				string text2 = "";
				XmlNode xmlNode = GradientsNode.SelectSingleNode("y:Gradient[@name='" + text + "']", nsmgr);
				text2 = ((xmlNode == null) ? ("<SolidColorBrush Color=\"" + text + "\"/>") : xmlNode.FirstChild.InnerText);
				code = code.Insert(startIndex + 1, text2);
				startIndex += text2.Length;
			}
			return code;
		}

		public void UpdateTextParam(string param)
		{
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					if (element is Text)
					{
						((Text)element).UpdateParam(param);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public void ToggleAnimation(bool run, string animparameter)
		{
			foreach (Anchor anchor in Anchors)
			{
				anchor.Run(run, animparameter);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					element.RunAnim(run, animparameter);
				}
			}
			foreach (AnimatedBrush animatedbrush in _animatedbrushes)
			{
				animatedbrush.Run(animparameter, run);
			}
		}

		public void UpdateAnimation(XmlNode node)
		{
			foreach (Anchor anchor in Anchors)
			{
				anchor.UpdateAnimation(node);
			}
			foreach (Layer layer in _layers)
			{
				foreach (Element element in layer.Elements)
				{
					element.UpdateAnimation(node);
				}
			}
			foreach (AnimatedBrush animatedbrush in _animatedbrushes)
			{
				animatedbrush.Update(node);
			}
		}

		public void TogglePorts(bool bOn)
		{
			foreach (Port port in _ports)
			{
				port.Visibility = ((!bOn) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
			}
			if (_label != null)
			{
				_label.Visibility = ((!bOn) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
			}
		}

		public void ToggleGrid(string grid)
		{
			_gridon = grid != "Off";
			_isometric = _gridon && grid == "Isometric";
			if (_gridon)
			{
				SetupGrid();
			}
			GridPanel.Visibility = ((!_gridon) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
		}

		public void ToggleSnap(bool bOn)
		{
			_snap = !_snap;
		}

		public void ToggleScale(bool bOn)
		{
			_scaleon = bOn;
			if (bOn)
			{
				SetupScale();
			}
			ScalePanel.Visibility = ((!bOn) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible);
		}

		public void ChangeGrid(string grid)
		{
			_grid = double.Parse(grid, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			SetupGrid();
		}

		public void ZoomFit()
		{
			GetField(out var minx, out var miny, out var maxx, out var maxy);
			if (minx != maxx && miny != maxy && !(minx > maxx) && !(miny > maxy))
			{
				ScrollViewer scrollViewer = (ScrollViewer)base.Parent;
				double num;
				double num2;
				if (Math.Abs(miny) < Math.Abs(maxy))
				{
					num = 0.0 - Math.Abs(maxy);
					num2 = Math.Abs(maxy);
				}
				else
				{
					num = 0.0 - Math.Abs(miny);
					num2 = Math.Abs(miny);
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num2 - num) > scrollViewer.ViewportHeight * 0.8)
				{
					miny = num;
					maxy = num2;
				}
				double num3;
				double num4;
				if (Math.Abs(minx) < Math.Abs(maxx))
				{
					num3 = 0.0 - Math.Abs(maxx);
					num4 = Math.Abs(maxx);
				}
				else
				{
					num3 = 0.0 - Math.Abs(minx);
					num4 = Math.Abs(minx);
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num4 - num3) > scrollViewer.ViewportWidth * 0.8)
				{
					minx = num3;
					maxx = num4;
				}
				SaveZoom();
				TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
				ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
				TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
				double num5 = scrollViewer.ViewportWidth * 0.8 / (maxx - minx);
				double num6 = scrollViewer.ViewportHeight * 0.8 / (maxy - miny);
				double num7 = ((num5 < num6) ? num5 : num6);
				double num8 = scrollViewer.ViewportWidth * num7;
				double num9 = scrollViewer.ViewportHeight * num7;
				RootPanel.Width = num8;
				RootPanel.Height = num9;
				scaleTransform.ScaleX = num7;
				scaleTransform.ScaleY = num7;
				translateTransform.X = num8 / 2.0;
				translateTransform.Y = num9 / 2.0;
				double num10 = (scrollViewer.ViewportWidth - (maxx - minx) * num7) / 2.0;
				double num11 = (scrollViewer.ViewportHeight - (maxy - miny) * num7) / 2.0;
				scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(num8 / 2.0 + minx * num7 - num10);
				scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(num9 / 2.0 + miny * num7 - num11);
			}
		}

		public void ZoomBack()
		{
			double savedScale = m_savedScale;
			double savedOffsetX = m_savedOffsetX;
			double savedOffsetY = m_savedOffsetY;
			double savedWidth = m_savedWidth;
			double savedHeight = m_savedHeight;
			double savedX = m_savedX;
			double savedY = m_savedY;
			SaveZoom();
			if (savedScale > 0.0)
			{
				ResetZoom(savedScale, savedOffsetX, savedOffsetY, savedWidth, savedHeight, savedX, savedY);
			}
		}

		private void SaveZoom()
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = (ScrollViewer)base.Parent;
			m_savedOffsetX = scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset;
			m_savedOffsetY = scrollViewer.VerticalOffset;
			m_savedWidth = RootPanel.Width;
			m_savedHeight = RootPanel.Height;
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
			m_savedScale = scaleTransform.ScaleX;
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
			m_savedX = translateTransform.X;
			m_savedY = translateTransform.Y;
		}

		private void ResetZoom(double scale, double offsetx, double offsety, double width, double height, double x, double y)
		{
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
			ScaleTransform scaleTransform = (ScaleTransform)transformGroup.Children[0];
			scaleTransform.ScaleX = scale;
			scaleTransform.ScaleY = scale;
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
			translateTransform.X = x;
			translateTransform.Y = y;
			RootPanel.Width = width;
			RootPanel.Height = height;
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = base.Parent as ScrollViewer;
			scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(offsetx);
			scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(offsety);
		}

		public void InsertElement(string element)
		{
			_inInsert = element;
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Cross;
		}

		public void InsertIcon(XmlNode iconnode)
		{
			_inInsert = "Icon";
			_insertIcon = iconnode;
			base.Cursor = Cursors.Cross;
		}

		public bool DragElement(Element element)
		{
			if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (element is Group && _current != null && _current != element)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (element.Parent is Group && _current != element)
			{
				Group obj = (Group)element.Parent;
				SetCurrent(obj);
				obj.ShowHandles(bShow: true);
				_inDrag = true;
				return false;
			}
			SetCurrent(element);
			_inDrag = true;
			return true;
		}

		public bool DragAnchor(Anchor anchor)
		{
			if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_current = anchor;
			_inDrag = true;
			return true;
		}

		public bool DragLabelAt(LabelAt label)
		{
			if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_current = label;
			_inDrag = true;
			return true;
		}

		public bool DragCursor(Cursor cursor)
		{
			if (_inInsert != null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			_current = cursor;
			_inDrag = true;
			return true;
		}

		public void ResizeElement(Element element, string id)
		{
			SetCurrent(element);
			if (element.OnLabel)
			{
				Text obj = (Text)_current.Parent;
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, obj);
			}
			else
			{
				AddToHistory(HistoryCode.Move, HistorySubcode.Resize, element);
			}
			_inResize = true;
			element.StartResize(id);
			_handle = id;
		}

		public void AnchorSelected(int index, bool reversedirection)
		{
			if (index >= _anchors.Count)
			{
				_toselectanchor.TagAnchor(_anchorselecthandle, null, reversedirection);
				return;
			}
			Anchor anchor = _anchors[index];
			_toselectanchor.TagAnchor(_anchorselecthandle, anchor, reversedirection);
		}

		public void HandleQuery(TagObj obj, string id)
		{
			if (_anchors.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (_popup == null)
			{
				_popup = new Popup();
				_popup.MouseLeave += _popup_MouseLeave;
				_anchorselector = new AnchorSelector(this);
				_anchorselector.Background = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.White;
				_anchorselector.BorderBrush = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
				_anchorselector.BorderThickness = new Thickness(1.0);
				_popup.Child = _anchorselector;
				_popup.Placement = PlacementMode.Mouse;
				_popup.HorizontalOffset = -30.0;
				_popup.VerticalOffset = -30.0;
			}
			_anchorselector.AnchorList.Items.Clear();
			foreach (Anchor anchor in _anchors)
			{
				_anchorselector.AnchorList.Items.Add(anchor.Name);
			}
			_anchorselector.AnchorList.Items.Add("---");
			_toselectanchor = obj;
			_anchorselecthandle = id;
			_popup.IsOpen = true;
		}

		public void RemoveLabel()
		{
			IconPanel.Children.Remove(_label);
			_label = null;
		}

		public void RestoreLabel(LabelAt label)
		{
			IconPanel.Children.Add(label);
			_label = label;
		}

		private void _popup_MouseLeave(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			_popup.IsOpen = false;
		}

		private void SetCurrent(Element element)
		{
			if (element == null && _curport != null)
			{
				_curport.Highlight(bOn: false, bLarge: false);
				_curport = null;
			}
			if (_current != null && _current is Element)
			{
				((Element)_current).ShowHandles(bShow: false);
				((Element)_current).SetSelected(bOn: false);
				FireEvent("Selected", "-1");
			}
			_current = element;
			if (element != null)
			{
				element.SetSelected(bOn: true);
				element.ShowHandles(bShow: true);
				if (element.OnLabel)
				{
					element = (Element)element.Parent;
				}
				FireEvent("Selected", ElementID(element));
			}
		}

		private string ElementID(Element element)
		{
			if (!(element.Parent is Group))
			{
				return _elements.IndexOf(element).ToString();
			}
			Group obj = (Group)element.Parent;
			return obj.ElementID(element);
		}

		private void Zoom(double ZoomIntoScale, System.Windows.Point p)
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = base.Parent as ScrollViewer;
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
			Transform transform = transformGroup.Children[0];
			TranslateTransform translateTransform = (TranslateTransform)transformGroup.Children[1];
			translateTransform.X *= ZoomIntoScale;
			translateTransform.Y *= ZoomIntoScale;
			double num = transform.Value.M11 * ZoomIntoScale;
			double scaleY = (_hysysicon ? (0.0 - num) : num);
			double num2 = RootPanel.ActualWidth * (ZoomIntoScale - 1.0);
			double num3 = RootPanel.ActualHeight * (ZoomIntoScale - 1.0);
			RootPanel.Width = RootPanel.ActualWidth * ZoomIntoScale;
			RootPanel.Height = RootPanel.ActualHeight * ZoomIntoScale;
			transformGroup.Children[0] = new ScaleTransform(num, scaleY);
			double num4 = num2 * (p.X / transform.Value.M11 + scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset) / RootPanel.ActualWidth;
			double num5 = num3 * (p.Y / transform.Value.M11 + scrollViewer.VerticalOffset) / RootPanel.ActualHeight;
			double num6 = scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset + num4;
			if (num6 < 0.0)
			{
				num6 = 0.0;
			}
			scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(num6);
			num6 = scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + num5;
			if (num6 < 0.0)
			{
				num6 = 0.0;
			}
			scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(num6);
			double num7 = 1.0 / num;
			XmlNode xmlNode = _attrib.SelectSingleNode("/Attributes/LabelScale");
			if (xmlNode != null)
			{
				xmlNode.InnerText = num7.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
			}
		}

		private void Scroll(double deltax, double deltay)
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = base.Parent as ScrollViewer;
			if (deltax != 0.0 && deltay != 0.0)
			{
				scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset + deltax);
				scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + deltay);
			}
			else if (deltax != 0.0)
			{
				scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset + deltax);
