				scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(scrollViewer.VerticalOffset);
			}
			else if (deltay != 0.0)
			{
				scrollViewer.ScrollToHorizontalOffset(scrollViewer.HorizontalOffset);
				scrollViewer.ScrollToVerticalOffset(scrollViewer.VerticalOffset + deltay);
			}
		}

		private void SetupScale()
		{
			ScrollViewer scrollViewer = base.Parent as ScrollViewer;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			TransformGroup transformGroup = (TransformGroup)RootPanel.RenderTransform;
			if (scrollViewer.ExtentHeight != 0.0 && scrollViewer.ExtentHeight < scrollViewer.ViewportHeight)
			{
				System.Windows.Point point = new System.Windows.Point((0.0 - (scrollViewer.ViewportWidth - scrollViewer.ExtentWidth)) * 0.5, (0.0 - (scrollViewer.ViewportHeight - scrollViewer.ExtentHeight)) * 0.5);
				System.Windows.Point point2 = transformGroup.Inverse.Transform(point);
				num = point2.X;
				num2 = point2.Y;
			}
			else
			{
				System.Windows.Point point3 = new System.Windows.Point(scrollViewer.ContentHorizontalOffset, scrollViewer.ContentVerticalOffset);
				System.Windows.Point point4 = transformGroup.Inverse.Transform(point3);
				num = point4.X;
				num2 = point4.Y;
			}
			double actualHeight = RootPanel.ActualHeight;
			double actualWidth = RootPanel.ActualWidth;
			ScalePanel.Children.Clear();
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
			line.Y1 = num2;
			line.Y2 = num2 + 10.0;
			line.X1 = 0.0;
			line.X2 = 0.0;
			line.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			line.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
			ScalePanel.Children.Add(line);
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line2 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
			line2.Y1 = 0.0;
			line2.Y2 = 0.0;
			line2.X1 = num;
			line2.X2 = num + 10.0;
			line2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
			line2.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
			ScalePanel.Children.Add(line2);
			int num3 = 0;
			for (double num4 = 10.0; num4 < actualWidth; num4 += 10.0)
			{
				num3++;
				double num5 = ((num3 % 5 == 0) ? 5.0 : 3.0);
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line3 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line3.X1 = num4;
				line3.X2 = num4;
				line3.Y1 = num2;
				line3.Y2 = num2 + num5;
				line3.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				line3.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				ScalePanel.Children.Add(line3);
				line3 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line3.X1 = 0.0 - num4;
				line3.X2 = 0.0 - num4;
				line3.Y1 = num2;
				line3.Y2 = num2 + num5;
				line3.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				line3.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				ScalePanel.Children.Add(line3);
				if (num3 % 5 == 0)
				{
					Label label = new Label();
					label.Content = num4.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					label.HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					label.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
					label.Width = 100.0;
					Canvas.SetTop(label, num2);
					Canvas.SetLeft(label, num4 - 50.0);
					ScalePanel.Children.Add(label);
					label = new Label();
					label.Content = (0.0 - num4).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					label.HorizontalContentAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					label.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
					label.Width = 100.0;
					Canvas.SetTop(label, num2);
					Canvas.SetLeft(label, 0.0 - num4 - 50.0);
					ScalePanel.Children.Add(label);
				}
			}
			num3 = 0;
			for (double num6 = 10.0; num6 < actualHeight; num6 += 10.0)
			{
				num3++;
				double num7 = ((num3 % 5 == 0) ? 5.0 : 3.0);
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line4 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line4.X1 = num;
				line4.X2 = num + num7;
				line4.Y1 = num6;
				line4.Y2 = num6;
				line4.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				line4.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				ScalePanel.Children.Add(line4);
				line4 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line4.X1 = num;
				line4.X2 = num + num7;
				line4.Y1 = 0.0 - num6;
				line4.Y2 = 0.0 - num6;
				line4.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
				line4.StrokeThickness = 1.0;
				ScalePanel.Children.Add(line4);
				if (num3 % 5 == 0)
				{
					Label label2 = new Label();
					label2.Content = num6.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					label2.VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					label2.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
					label2.Height = 50.0;
					Canvas.SetTop(label2, num6 - 25.0);
					Canvas.SetLeft(label2, num + 5.0);
					ScalePanel.Children.Add(label2);
					label2 = new Label();
					label2.Content = (0.0 - num6).ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture.NumberFormat);
					label2.VerticalContentAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
					label2.Foreground = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Red;
					label2.Height = 50.0;
					Canvas.SetTop(label2, 0.0 - num6 - 25.0);
					Canvas.SetLeft(label2, num + 5.0);
					ScalePanel.Children.Add(label2);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetupGrid()
		{
			double num = ((_grid < 5.0) ? 5.0 : _grid);
			double actualHeight = RootPanel.ActualHeight;
			double actualWidth = RootPanel.ActualWidth;
			GridPanel.Children.Clear();
			if (_isometric)
			{
				num *= _cosval;
			}
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
			line.Y1 = 0.0 - actualHeight;
			line.Y2 = actualHeight;
			line.X1 = 0.0;
			line.X2 = 0.0;
			line.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue;
			line.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
			GridPanel.Children.Add(line);
			for (double num2 = num; num2 < actualWidth; num2 += num)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line2 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line2.X1 = num2;
				line2.X2 = num2;
				line2.Y1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line2.Y2 = actualWidth;
				if ((int)Math.Abs(num2) % 50 == 0)
				{
					line2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
				}
				else
				{
					line2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
				}
				line2.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line2);
				line2 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line2.X1 = 0.0 - num2;
				line2.X2 = 0.0 - num2;
				line2.Y1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line2.Y2 = actualWidth;
				if ((int)Math.Abs(num2) % 50 == 0)
				{
					line2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
				}
				else
				{
					line2.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
				}
				line2.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line2);
			}
			if (_isometric)
			{
				num = ((_grid < 5.0) ? 5.0 : _grid);
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line3 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line3.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line3.X2 = actualWidth;
				line3.Y1 = (0.0 - actualWidth) * _tanval;
				line3.Y2 = actualWidth * _tanval;
				line3.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue;
				line3.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line3);
				for (double num3 = num; num3 < actualHeight; num3 += num)
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Line line4 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					line4.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
					line4.X2 = actualWidth;
					line4.Y1 = num3 - actualWidth * _tanval;
					line4.Y2 = num3 + actualWidth * _tanval;
					line4.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
					line4.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
					GridPanel.Children.Add(line4);
					line4 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					line4.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
					line4.X2 = actualWidth;
					line4.Y1 = 0.0 - num3 - actualWidth * _tanval;
					line4.Y2 = 0.0 - num3 + actualWidth * _tanval;
					line4.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
					line4.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
					GridPanel.Children.Add(line4);
				}
				num = ((_grid < 5.0) ? 5.0 : _grid);
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line5 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line5.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line5.X2 = actualWidth;
				line5.Y1 = actualWidth * _tanval;
				line5.Y2 = (0.0 - actualWidth) * _tanval;
				line5.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue;
				line5.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line5);
				for (double num4 = num; num4 < actualHeight; num4 += num)
				{
					System.Windows.Shapes.Line line6 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					line6.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
					line6.X2 = actualWidth;
					line6.Y1 = num4 + actualWidth * _tanval;
					line6.Y2 = num4 - actualWidth * _tanval;
					line6.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
					line6.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
					GridPanel.Children.Add(line6);
					line6 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
					line6.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
					line6.X2 = actualWidth;
					line6.Y1 = 0.0 - num4 + actualWidth * _tanval;
					line6.Y2 = 0.0 - num4 - actualWidth * _tanval;
					line6.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
					line6.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
					GridPanel.Children.Add(line6);
				}
				return;
			}
			System.Windows.Shapes.Line line7 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
			line7.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
			line7.X2 = actualWidth;
			line7.Y1 = 0.0;
			line7.Y2 = 0.0;
			line7.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightBlue;
			line7.StrokeThickness = 2.0;
			GridPanel.Children.Add(line7);
			for (double num5 = num; num5 < actualHeight; num5 += num)
			{
				System.Windows.Shapes.Line line8 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line8.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line8.X2 = actualWidth;
				line8.Y1 = num5;
				line8.Y2 = num5;
				if ((int)Math.Abs(num5) % 50 == 0)
				{
					line8.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
				}
				else
				{
					line8.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
				}
				line8.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line8);
				line8 = new System.Windows.Shapes.Line();
				line8.X1 = 0.0 - actualWidth;
				line8.X2 = actualWidth;
				line8.Y1 = 0.0 - num5;
				line8.Y2 = 0.0 - num5;
				if ((int)Math.Abs(num5) % 50 == 0)
				{
					line8.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
				}
				else
				{
					line8.Stroke = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.LightGray;
				}
				line8.StrokeThickness = 0.5;
				GridPanel.Children.Add(line8);
			}
		}

		private void AddAttribute(XmlNode node, string name, string value)
		{
			XmlAttribute xmlAttribute = node.Attributes[name];
			if (xmlAttribute == null)
			{
				xmlAttribute = node.OwnerDocument.CreateAttribute(name);
				node.Attributes.Append(xmlAttribute);
			}
			xmlAttribute.Value = value;
		}

		public void DoUndo(int steps)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < steps; i++)
			{
				List<string> modified = new List<string>();
				Undo(ref modified);
			}
		}

		public void DoRedo(int steps)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < steps; i++)
			{
				List<string> modified = new List<string>();
				Redo(ref modified);
			}
		}

		public void AddToHistory(HistoryCode type, HistorySubcode subcode, BaseObj obj, string description = null, bool append = false)
		{
			if (type == HistoryCode.Group && subcode == HistorySubcode.GroupEnd)
			{
				if (m_historyGroupDepth > 0)
				{
					m_historyGroupDepth--;
					return;
				}
				if (m_historyGroup.Empty)
				{
					History history = m_history[m_history.Count - 1];
					m_history.Remove(history);
					FireEvent("UndoRemove", history.Description);
				}
				else
				{
					if (description != null)
					{
						m_historyGroup.SetDescription(description);
					}
					FireEvent("AddHistory", m_historyGroup.Description);
				}
				m_historyGroup = null;
				return;
			}
			ClearHistory(m_redoList);
			History history2 = ((!append) ? new History() : ((m_historyGroup == null) ? m_history[m_history.Count - 1] : m_historyGroup.GetLast));
			if (!history2.Commit(type, subcode, obj, description, this, append))
			{
				ClearHistory(m_history);
				return;
			}
			if (!append)
			{
				if (m_historyGroup != null)
				{
					m_historyGroup.Add(history2);
				}
				else
				{
					if (m_history.Count == 0)
					{
						FireEvent("History", "UndoSet");
					}
					if (subcode != HistorySubcode.GroupBegin)
					{
						FireEvent("AddHistory", history2.Description);
					}
					m_history.Add(history2);
				}
			}
			if (type == HistoryCode.Group)
			{
				if (m_historyGroup != null)
				{
					m_historyGroupDepth++;
				}
				else
				{
					m_historyGroup = (HistoryGroup)history2.Item;
				}
			}
		}

		public void ClearHistory(List<History> list)
		{
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				if (list == m_history)
				{
					FireEvent("History", "UndoClear");
				}
				else
				{
					FireEvent("History", "RedoClear");
				}
			}
			list.Clear();
		}

		public void HistorySize(out int historysize, out int redosize)
		{
			historysize = m_history.Count;
			redosize = m_redoList.Count;
		}

		private void Undo(ref List<string> modified)
		{
			int count = m_history.Count;
			if (count != 0)
			{
				History history = m_history[count - 1];
				history.Undo(this, ref modified);
				m_history.Remove(history);
				FireEvent("Undo", history.Description);
				m_redoList.Add(history);
			}
		}

		private void Redo(ref List<string> modified)
		{
			int count = m_redoList.Count;
			if (count != 0)
			{
				History history = m_redoList[count - 1];
				history.Redo(this, ref modified);
				m_redoList.Remove(history);
				FireEvent("Redo", history.Description);
				m_history.Add(history);
			}
		}

		public void ContextMenu_Loaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			switch ((string)menuItem.Header)
			{
			case "Zoom Fit":
				menuItem.IsEnabled = Elements.Count > 0;
				break;
			case "Zoom Previous":
				menuItem.IsEnabled = m_savedScale > 0.0;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ContextMenu_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			MenuItem menuItem = (MenuItem)sender;
			switch ((string)menuItem.Header)
			{
			case "Zoom Fit":
				ZoomFit();
				break;
			case "Zoom Previous":
				ZoomBack();
				break;
			}
		}

		public void ImportXaml(string code)
		{
			List<Element> elements = new List<Element>();
			ImportElements(code, ref elements);
			foreach (Element item in elements)
			{
				_elements.Add(item);
				_curLayer.Children.Add(item);
			}
			CleanElementList();
			foreach (Element element in _elements)
			{
				XmlNode xml = element.GetXml(_wholelist);
				_elementlist.AppendChild(xml);
			}
			_current = null;
			_curport = null;
		}

		public void ImportElements(string code, ref List<Element> elements)
		{
			XmlDocument xmlDocument = new XmlDocument();
			xmlDocument.LoadXml("<root>" + code + "</root>");
			FilterCommentOut(xmlDocument);
			int count = xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes.Count;
			string text = "<Canvas xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\" ";
			text += "xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\" ";
			text += "xmlns:yy=\"clr-namespace:IconCtrl;assembly=IconCtrl\" >";
			text += "<Canvas.Resources>";
			text += "<yy:Calc x:Key=\"Calc\" />";
			text += "</Canvas.Resources>";
			text = text + code + "</Canvas>";
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(memoryStream);
			streamWriter.WriteLine(text);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			memoryStream.Position = 0L;
			Canvas canvas = XamlReader.Load(memoryStream) as Canvas;
			int count2 = canvas.Children.Count;
			Element lastelement = null;
			XmlNode documentElement = xmlDocument.DocumentElement;
			for (int i = 0; i < count2; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = canvas.Children[0];
				canvas.Children.Remove(uIElement);
				XmlNode elemnode = documentElement.ChildNodes[i];
				Element element = LoadElement(uIElement, lastelement, elemnode, 2);
				if (element != null)
				{
					elements.Add(element);
					lastelement = element;
					if (i < count)
					{
						element.XmlNode(xmlDocument.DocumentElement.ChildNodes[i]);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/IconCtrl;component/iconctrl.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				((MenuItem)target).Click += ContextMenu_Click;
				((MenuItem)target).Loaded += ContextMenu_Loaded;
				break;
			case 2:
				((MenuItem)target).Click += ContextMenu_Click;
				((MenuItem)target).Loaded += ContextMenu_Loaded;
				break;
			case 3:
				RootPanel = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			case 4:
				GridPanel = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				ScalePanel = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			case 6:
				IconPanel = (Canvas)target;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
